### [1] [Inferring Multi-Scale Cascade Structure from a Single Lagrangian Trajectory via Hidden Markov Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08971)
*Ziang Yang,Lei Fang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 单条拉格朗日轨迹即可编码湍流级串的空间结构，无需欧拉速度场；基于马尔可夫切换多重分形（MSM）框架，用精确贝叶斯滤波从单粒子轨迹推断隐藏的多尺度级串状态，并通过粒子对实验验证其携带真实空间信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流级串通常依赖空间分辨的欧拉速度场来诊断，但在粒子跟踪测速、大气与海洋追踪等场景中，只能获得稀疏或单条拉格朗日轨迹，瞬时欧拉场难以获取，因此需要一种仅从单轨迹推断级串结构的方法。

Method: 提出马尔可夫切换多重分形（MSM）框架，直接从单粒子轨迹推断隐藏的多尺度级串状态；利用 Kolmogorov 标度固定模型切换率，仅保留一个可标定的间歇性参数；采用精确贝叶斯滤波返回沿轨迹的逐层分辨级串状态后验。对粒子对中每条轨迹独立应用该框架，并通过随机化细尺度层来检验空间信息。

Result: 推断出的状态携带真实空间信息：与 Richardson 局域性图像一致，随机化细尺度层时 Kolmogorov r^{1/3} 标度保持不变，而一旦随机化跨越由粒子对间距设定的层，标度即崩溃。

Conclusion: 单条轨迹能够编码级串的空间组织，为以稀疏或单条拉格朗日轨迹为主要观测手段的湍流（如粒子跟踪测速、大气与海洋追踪）提供级串诊断途径。

Abstract: We show that a single Lagrangian trajectory encodes not only temporal but also spatial cascade structure--recoverable without access to a spatially resolved Eulerian velocity field. We report a Markov-Switching Multifractal (MSM) framework that infers hidden multi-scale cascade states directly from single-particle trajectories. Kolmogorov scaling fixes the model's switching rates, leaving a single calibrated intermittency parameter; exact Bayesian filtering then returns the posterior of the layer-resolved cascade state along the trajectory. When we apply the MSM framework independently to each trajectory of a particle pair, we find that the inferred states carry genuine spatial information: consistent with Richardson's locality picture, the Kolmogorov $r^{1/3}$ scaling is preserved when fine-scale layers are randomized, but collapses once the randomization crosses the layer set by the pair separation. Single trajectories thus encode the spatial organization of the cascade, opening a route to cascade diagnostics for turbulent flows accessible primarily through sparse or individual Lagrangian trajectories--from particle-tracking velocimetry to atmospheric and oceanographic tracking--where the instantaneous Eulerian field is out of reach.

</details>


### [2] [Coupled Thermochemical Ablation and Rarefied Particle Erosion during Mars Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09024)
*Hyeon Woo Nam,Sung Min Jo*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究将稀薄颗粒-连续流双向耦合框架扩展至退移与热解的热防护表面，应用于Schiaparelli轨迹，比较三种表面化学模型，首次定量对比稀薄与连续阻力关联式下的颗粒侵蚀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星尘埃颗粒直径与高超声速激波层气体平均自由程相当（Kn约0.2–100，处于过渡与自由分子区），而飞行器周围流动为连续流，因此需建立稀薄颗粒与连续流双向耦合模型；同时实际热防护表面存在炭化退移与热解，需要将其纳入耦合框架以评估表面化学与颗粒侵蚀对气动热的影响。

Method: 将作者此前针对双温度热化学非平衡流的双向流-颗粒耦合扩展至退移与热解的热防护表面；气-面相互作用与材料内部响应在不同时间尺度上求解，每一步流动更新表面平衡，内部响应在物理时间窗口内隐式耦合；流-材料耦合以石墨电弧加热器测量进行验证；将该框架应用于Schiaparelli轨迹50–30 km段，在固定外形、炭化退移、炭化烧蚀+颗粒侵蚀三种条件下比较平衡、Park-5与Zhluktov-Abe三种表面化学模型。

Result: 在轨迹末点，三种化学模型预测的驻点热流相差30–75%。对平衡模型，表面退移使热流变化小于1%，但降低壁温并增加热解气体注入。颗粒侵蚀造成1.73 mm驻点退移，超过炭化退移，并使三种模型的总退移量增加一倍以上。首次定量比较了稀薄与连续阻力关联式下的颗粒侵蚀，连续关联式低估驻点侵蚀率31–35%。

Conclusion: 表面化学模型选择显著影响驻点热流（30–75%差异）；炭化退移对热流影响有限但改变壁温与热解气注入；颗粒侵蚀是总退移的主要贡献者，可使退移量翻倍以上；连续阻力关联式会明显低估驻点侵蚀率，需采用适用于稀薄过渡区的关联式。

Abstract: Martian dust particles are a few micrometers in diameter, comparable to the mean free path of the gas in the hypersonic shock layer. Their Knudsen number ranges from approximately 0.2 to 100, placing them in the transitional and free-molecular regimes, whereas the flow around the vehicle is a continuum. The two-way flow-particle coupling previously developed by the authors for a two-temperature thermochemical nonequilibrium flow is extended to a receding and pyrolyzing heat shield surface. The gas-surface interaction and the in-depth material response are solved on separate time scales. The surface balances are exchanged at every flow step, while the in-depth response is coupled implicitly over physical time windows. The flow-material coupling is assessed against graphite arc-jet measurements. The framework is applied to the Schiaparelli trajectory from 50 to 30 km. Three surface chemistry models (equilibrium, Park-5, and Zhluktov-Abe) are compared for fixed geometry, char-induced recession, and combined char ablation and particle erosion. At the final trajectory point, the stagnation point heat flux differs by 30-75% among the chemistry models. For the equilibrium model, surface recession changes this heat flux by less than 1% but lowers wall temperature and increases pyrolysis gas injection. Particle erosion contributes 1.73 mm of stagnation point recession, exceeding the char-induced recession, and more than doubles the total recession for all three models. To the authors' knowledge, this study provides the first quantitative comparison of particle erosion between rarefied and continuum drag correlations, and the continuum correlation underestimates the stagnation point erosion rate by 31-35%.

</details>


### [3] [Extension of a mathematical analogy between vortical flows without surface tension and irrotational capillary flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10146)
*Valentin D. Kunz,Saleh Tanveer,Darren G. Crowdy*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将自由边界问题的数学类比从"常涡量自由面流 vs. 含表面张力无旋流"推广到"含三阶无旋应变的剪切流中的空心涡平衡"与"简单应变流中含表面张力的稳态气泡"，并在参数γ=0时给出孤立精确解，对非零γ给出数值解，同时发现毛细问题中出现的非平凡共形映射恒等式在参数适当约束下同样适用于空心涡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究揭示了无粘流体力学中两类二维自由边界问题（常涡量无表面张力自由面流，与无涡量但有表面张力/毛细作用的自由面流）之间的数学类比，并对若干物理上不同却数学等价的问题对给出实例。本文旨在检验该类比能否进一步扩展到新的问题对，从而加深对这两类问题内在数学等价性的理解。

Method: 作者选取两个物理上截然不同的问题：含三阶无旋应变的剪切流中的空心涡平衡，以及简单应变流中含表面张力的稳态气泡。对两者在参数γ=0时构造孤立的精确解，对非零γ则采用数值方法求解，并借助毛细问题类中的共形映射恒等式进行分析，在参数适当约束下将其迁移到空心涡问题。

Result: 证实在γ=0时两问题均存在孤立的精确解；对非零γ获得了相应的数值解，验证了类比在更广参数范围内的有效性。此外，发现源自毛细问题类的非平凡共形映射恒等式，在参数经适当约束后同样适用于空心涡问题，从而揭示了两个物理不同问题之间更深层的数学联系。

Conclusion: 该数学类比可进一步推广至"空心涡平衡—含表面张力稳态气泡"这一新问题对，扩大了类比的适用范围；共形映射恒等式的跨问题迁移表明毛细类问题与空心涡问题在数学结构上具有内在一致性，为后续自由边界问题的求解与类比研究提供了新的工具与思路。

Abstract: In a recent paper, a mathematical analogy was found between two physically distinct classes of two-dimensional free boundary problems in inviscid fluid mechanics. One class involves free surface flows with constant vorticity in the bulk but without boundary surface tension, the other comprises free surface flows without vorticity in the bulk but with non-zero boundary surface tension (capillarity). Several examples were given of pairs of physically-distinct free boundary problems that amount to solving the same mathematical problem, albeit with the possibility of a single constraint arising for the capillary flows. The present work shows that this analogy extends to another pair of physically-distinct problems: a hollow vortex equilibrium in a shear flow with a third order irrotational strain and a steady bubble with surface tension in a simple straining flow. For both problems, we construct an isolated exact solution when a parameter $γ=0$ and provide numerical solutions for non-zero $γ$. An interesting feature is that non-trivial conformal mapping identities emerge from the class of problems with capillarity that apply equally for the hollow vortex problems when the parameters are appropriately constrained.

</details>


### [4] [Machine-learning-assisted phase-amplitude reduction for fast synchronization of airfoil wakes with constrained fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09543)
*Daiki Beppu,Vedasri Godavarthi,Koichiro Yawata,Hiroya Nakao,Kai Fukami*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 结合相位-幅值约简与非线性机器学习稀疏传感重构，仅用三个传感器定位最优激励位置与时序，解析获得快速同步尾涡脱落频率且抑制升力脉动的最优波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速改变翼型绕流尾涡脱落频率，同时约束升力系数脉动，以避免对周期性气动/气弹系统造成有害波动，并减少对密集传感与试错激励的依赖。

Method: 将相位-幅值约简（phase-amplitude reduction）与基于非线性机器学习的稀疏传感器重构相结合；由约简模型导出时变相位与幅值灵敏度场，据此从三个传感器信息确定最优激励的空间位置与施加时序；进一步解析求解在幅值偏差惩罚下的最优激励波形，并与标准正弦激励对比。验证对象为多种NACA翼型在多个失速后攻角下的非定常周期涡脱落流动。

Result: 所识别的最优激励使尾涡频率的改变速度显著快于标准正弦激励；相比无幅值惩罚的最优激励，幅值惩罚激励额外实现升力系数脉动约20%的抑制。

Conclusion: 该幅值惩罚方法为存在流固耦合的周期性气动与气弹系统提供了一条快速流动调控且不引发有害脉动的有效路径。

Abstract: This study considers rapidly modifying the wake shedding frequency of the flow around an airfoil using sparse sensor information, subject to constraints on the lift coefficient fluctuations. This is achieved by combining phase-amplitude reduction with nonlinear machine-learning-based sparse sensor reconstruction. We derive time-varying phase and amplitude sensitivity fields that identify the optimal spatial locations and timing for actuation from merely three sensors. Through the sensitivity fields, we analytically obtain the optimal waveform for fast synchronization of wake shedding frequency while minimizing amplitude deviation of aerodynamic responses. The proposed approach is evaluated using flows over various NACA airfoils at several post-stall angles of attack, all of which exhibit unsteady periodic vortex shedding. With the identified optimal forcing, the wake frequency is altered much faster than with a standard sinusoidal actuation. Furthermore, the amplitude-penalized forcing achieves $20\%$ suppression of the lift coefficient fluctuation compared to the optimal forcing without amplitude penalty. The current amplitude-penalized technique may offer an efficient path for fast flow modification without causing detrimental fluctuations in periodic aerodynamic and aeroelastic systems with fluid-structure interactions.

</details>


### [5] [Steady translation of a chemically active viscous drop along a rigid wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10300)
*Nikhil Desai,Sébastien Michelin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究化学活性粘性液滴平行于刚性壁面的平动，比较界面Marangoni应力与扩散泳驱动的真实液滴与"各向同性活性粒子"模型的有效性。结果表明：液滴越稀（粘度越低）越靠近壁面、游动越快；在中等Pe下，游动速度对迁移率比依赖较弱，为用扩散泳活性粒子推断Marangoni驱动液滴运动提供了定量依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论研究常将活性液滴简化为扩散泳驱动的活性粒子，以便简化液滴表面边界条件的处理；但依赖此简化所获得的液滴运动物理认识，在真实流体液滴（尤其与周围环境发生化学-流体动力学相互作用时）可能适用性有限，因此需要检验该近似的有效性。

Method: 研究化学活性粘性液滴平行于刚性壁面的平动（常见实验构型），同时考虑界面Marangoni应力与扩散泳流两种推进机制；将结果与"各向同性活性粒子"模型进行对比，以检验后者的有效性。分析关注Peclet数Pe（对流与扩散溶质输运之比）、粘度比、迁移率比（扩散泳与Marangoni流动的相对强度）及受限力等参数。

Result: 在相同受限力下，粘度较低的液滴比粘度较高的液滴游动得更靠近壁面，从而因更强的浓度梯度驱动而游动更快；在中等Pe下，液滴游动速度对其迁移率比的依赖较弱。

Conclusion: 粘度较低的液滴因更靠近壁面而获得更强的浓度梯度与更快的游动速度；中等Pe下速度对迁移率比的弱依赖，为采用扩散泳驱动的各向同性活性粒子模型来推断Marangoni应力驱动活性液滴的运动提供了定量的合理性依据。

Abstract: Chemically active drops release a chemical solute, gradients in whose concentration cause interfacial flows that advect the emitted solute. This non-linear coupling between solute distribution and fluid flow causes the drop to swim via an advective instability, if the system's Peclet number Pe--ratio of advective-to-diffusive solute transport--is larger than a finite threshold. Theoretical analyses often model active drops as diffusiophoresis-driven active particles, primarily to simplify the implementation of boundary conditions at the drop surface. However, physical insights about drop motion that rely on this simplification risk having limited applicability to fluid drops, especially when the drop is interacting chemo-hydrodynamically with its environment. Towards this, we study here the translation of a chemically active viscous drop parallel to a rigid wall, as an example of a common experimental configuration. We consider a propulsion mechanism where the drop swims due to interfacial Marangoni stresses and diffusiophoretic flows, and compare our results to those of the ''isotropic active particle'' model, to examine the validity of the latter. Our analysis shows that, for the same confining force, a less viscous drop swims closer to the wall than a more viscous drop. This results in faster swimming, due to stronger concentration gradients driving the drop's motion. It is also shown that for moderate Pe, the swimming speed of the drop depends weakly on its mobility ratio (relative strength of diffusiophoretic and Marangoni flows). This weak dependence provides a quantitative justification for studying diffusiophoresis-driven active particles to infer the motion of Marangoni-stress-driven active drops.

</details>


### [6] [Conditional Flow Matching for Generation of 3D Multi-variable Instantaneous Urban Microclimate Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10430)
*Peng Liu,Shaoxiang Qin,Theodore Potsis,Lili Ji,Dingyang Geng,Liangzhu Leon Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出基于条件流匹配（CFM）的生成式框架，以建筑几何与平均流为引导，在数秒内生成城市微气候的三维瞬时风速与温度场，精度接近大涡模拟（LES）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市风场与温度场的快速准确预测对微气候设计和气候适应具有重要意义。大涡模拟（LES）虽能有效解析瞬时场，但计算成本高，难以用于迭代式设计；现有回归型数据驱动模型虽计算快速，但仅给出确定性点预测，无法表征湍流的随机性。

Method: 采用条件流匹配（CFM）生成框架，以建筑几何和平均流作为引导条件，生成合理的三维瞬时速度场与温度场。为突破像素空间三维生成的GPU显存瓶颈，模型在重叠的像素空间上并行运行，并通过共享噪声初始化保持整个域内流场结构的高空间连续性。

Result: 与参考LES数据对比，CFM代理模型可快速准确地还原一阶统计量（风速NRMSE 2.99%，温度NRMSE 1.77%）、二阶湍流指标（风速NRMSE 7.17%，温度NRMSE 8.84%）、湍动能（NRMSE 7%）、概率密度函数及代表性位置的垂直剖面。局部阵风预测的风工程应用进一步验证了CFM的速度与精度。

Conclusion: 该研究表明生成式AI可支持兼顾湍流特性的韧性城市设计与气候适应，使相关计算更加可行。

Abstract: Rapid and accurate prediction of urban wind and temperature fields is important for urban microclimate design and climate adaptation. Large-eddy simulation (LES) effectively resolves these instantaneous fields, but its application is limited in iterative design of urban microclimate applications due to high computational cost. Existing regressive data-driven models offers quick outputs, but they produce only deterministic point predictions that inherently fail to represent turbulent stochasticity. This paper adopts a novel generative framework of Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) that uses building geometry and mean flow as guidance to generate plausible three-dimensional instantaneous velocity and temperature fields for urban microclimate in seconds. To overcome the GPU memory bottleneck of pixel space 3D generation, the model operates in parallel on overlapping pixel space through a shared-noise initialization that preserves high spatial continuity of flow structure across the entire domain. Against reference LES data, the CFM surrogate can rapidly and accurately restore the first-order statistics with Normalized Root Mean Square Error (NRMSE) of 2.99% for wind and 1.77% for temperature, second-order turbulence metrics with NRMSE of 7.17% for wind and 8.84% for temperature, turbulent kinetic energy with NRMSE of 7%, probability density function and vertical profiles in representative locations. Wind engineering application of local gust prediction demonstrate that the speed and accuracy of CFM, supporting the use of generative AI for making turbulence-aware resilient urban design and climate adaptation more computationally feasible.

</details>


### [7] [SAGE-PINN: A Singularity-free Axisymmetric Geometry-Encoded Physics-Informed Neural Network for Axisymmetric Multiphysics Flow in Cylindrical Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10449)
*Raj Maurya,Joyprakash Akhuli,Doyel Pandey*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: SAGE-PINN：一种无奇点、轴对称几何编码的物理信息神经网络，用于求解轴对称柱坐标下的纳米流体流动与传热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴对称柱坐标下的流体流动因控制算子在轴线上奇异、动量与能量通过对流和浮力强耦合而研究不足，本文旨在解决该问题。

Method: 引入保奇偶变量 s=r^2 与改进流函数表示，从训练残差中消除 1/r 项并恒等满足质量守恒；采用贴体解析 ansatz 精确施加边界条件；结合分阶段动量-热联合训练、残差归一化、Péclet 与 Richardson 数延拓及物理标度漂移保护，实现无标签数据、仅用物理与边界约束的训练。

Result: 在狭窄动脉内稳态纳米流体流动与传热的有限元参考解上验证：质量守恒保持单精度舍入误差，核心工况下轴向速度和温度的相对 L2 误差分别为 1.6% 和 9.1%；主要退化仅出现在强浮力影响的慢流和强对流主导传热的极限工况。

Conclusion: 将柱几何、守恒结构和边界物理直接嵌入网络，为轴对称多物理场流动的 PINN 仿真提供了稳健途径。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a mesh-free framework for multiphysics modeling, yet fluid flow in axisymmetric cylindrical coordinates remains largely unexplored because the governing operators are singular at the axis and momentum and energy are strongly coupled through advection and buoyancy. We introduce SAGE-PINN, a Singularity-free Axisymmetric Geometry-Encoded Physics-Informed Neural Network for this class of problems. SAGE-PINN removes the coordinate singularity by construction: the parity-preserving variable $s=r^2$ and a modified stream-function representation eliminate explicit $1/r$ terms from the trained residuals and satisfy mass conservation identically. Boundary-fitted analytical ansätze enforce the boundary conditions exactly, while staged momentum-thermal-joint training, residual normalization, Péclet- and Richardson-number continuation, and a physics-scaled drift guard stabilize the coupled optimization. The framework is trained without labelled data, using only physics and boundary constraints, and its predictions are validated against finite-element reference solutions for steady nanofluid flow and heat transfer through a stenosed artery over a wide range of flow and thermal regimes, without case-specific tuning. Mass conservation remains at single-precision round-off, and over the central operating range the axial velocity and temperature are reproduced within $1.6\%$ and $9.1\%$ relative $L_2$ error, respectively. The main deterioration occurs only in the limiting regimes of strongly buoyancy-influenced slow flow and highly advection-dominated heat transfer. Method: 通过构造显式曲线 $y^2=t^{12h-5}+t$ 及其光滑射影模型上的 $S_3$ 作用（商亏格 $h$、单一歧化轨道、惯性阶为三）给出反例。正面结果方面：利用 A 型彩色路径（coloured paths of type $A$）与导出反射函子（derived reflection functors），在自然纤维积上构造核（kernels）；以惯性阶 $e_i$ 与共轭类 $[g]$ 同各分支惯性群 $H_i$ 的交的基数 $|[g]\cap H_i|$ 为不变量进行分析。

Abstract: We construct effective finite group actions on smooth projective complex curves for which Conjecture A of Polishchuk and Van den Bergh fails. More generally, when the coarse quotient has positive genus, we prove that the maximum number of pairwise completely orthogonal exceptional objects is $\sum_i\lfloor e_i/2\rfloor$, where $e_i$ are the inertia orders. We also characterize conjugacy-class decompositions with embeddings linear over the coarse quotient: such a decomposition exists if and only if $|[g]\cap H_i|\leq\lfloor e_i/2\rfloor$ for every nonidentity conjugacy class and every branch inertia group $H_i$. The sufficiency proof uses coloured paths of type $A$ and derived reflection functors, and produces kernels on the natural fibre products. The known ordered curve decomposition still yields a decomposition indexed by the connected components of the fixed quotients.

</details>

Abstract: We prove that a very general hypersurface of degree at least five and any positive dimension does not have a decomposition of the diagonal, over an uncountable algebraically closed field of characteristic not $2$. Abstract: For every positive integer $n$, we prove that there is a constant $C_n$, depending only on $n$, such that $Vol(-K_X) \leq C_n ε^{-(2^n-n-1)}$ whenever $X$ admits an $ε$-lc log Fano boundary. The exponent is optimal in every dimension at least two, even for toric Fano varieties of Picard number one. In particular, the optimal exponent for fourfolds is eleven. We also prove an anticanonical interpolation theorem with optimal exponent $2^n-1$. The proof combines signed discrepancy estimates under projection, finite morphisms to projective space, and the canonical bundle formula. A reduction to a base bounded independently of $ε$, followed by a volume estimate along a flag, yields the sharper volume exponent.

</details>

### [33] [Convergence of Roberts flow dynamos in Eulerian and Lagrangian codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08996)
*Nikyta Shchutskyi,Axel Brandenburg,Matthieu Schaller*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文将二维 Roberts 流 I–IV 确立为磁流体力学直接数值模拟中优异的 dynamo 基准问题，并系统研究其数值收敛性、跨代码一致性与可视化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MHD 数值代码基准多局限于一维、二维问题，忽略了 dynamo 现象（动能向磁能转化的指数不稳定性）以及周期域中磁螺度守恒的重要影响。磁扩散的存在对 dynamo 至关重要，而无磁扩散时用 Euler 势无法找到 dynamo 解。Roberts 流 I–IV 是支撑三维 dynamo 的二维流动，可弥补现有基准的不足。

Method: 在 Pencil Code 中采用网格宽度的二阶、六阶和十阶离散格式，研究 Roberts 流 dynamo 解的数值收敛性质；与基于 SPH 的 SWIFT 代码进行对比；提出生成全部 Roberts 流二维定常磁场可视化的方法，并进行逐像素对比。

Result: 展示了不同阶离散格式解的收敛特性，验证了 Pencil Code 与 SWIFT 代码结果的一致性，给出可用于表征解的一系列平均量清单，并实现了 Roberts 流的二维定常磁场可视化及逐像素比较。

Conclusion: Roberts 流兼具三维 dynamo、大尺度 dynamo 及非螺度流动等多种特征，是 MHD 方程直接数值模拟的理想 dynamo 基准问题。

Abstract: Standard benchmark tests for numerical magnetohydrodynamics codes focus on one- and two-dimensional problems that ignore the possibility of dynamos, i.e., an exponential instability converting kinetic energy into magnetic energy. They also tend to ignore magnetic helicity conservation that can have dramatic effects in periodic domains. The existence of magnetic diffusion is crucial for dynamos, as was exemplified by the impossi- bility of finding dynamo solutions when using Euler potentials, which is a perfectly valid approach in the absence of magnetic diffusion. The Roberts flows I, II, III, and IV are two- dimensional flows that support three-dimensional dynamos, all of which are also large- scale dynamos in the sense that their planar averages are of significant strengths, even though flow II is pointwise non-helical. Here we demonstrate the numerical convergence properties of solutions obtained using discretisation schemes of second, sixth, and tenth order in the mesh width in the Pencil Code, compare the results with the SPH- based SWIFT code, and present a list of averaged quantities that facilitate their use in characterising the solutions as benchmarks. In addition, we present a procedure to obtain two-dimensional time-independent magnetic field visualisation for all Roberts flows, and perform per-pixel comparison between the Pencil and SWIFT codes. The rich combination of features makes Roberts flows an excellent dynamo benchmark for the direct numerical simulations of magnetohydrodynamics equations.

</details>


### [34] [Reliable Uncertainty Estimation for Machine-Learned Multigroup Cross Sections Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09231)
*Joshua Nichols,Andrew Osborne,Valerio Mascolino*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文将不确定性预测神经网络与预训练的屏蔽因子神经网络相连，用其预测的残差标准差（模型与计数不确定性经平方和合成）作为保形预测的归一化因子，实现逐样本自适应预测区间，在核能应用中显著减小区间宽度并降低越界程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核能应用中机器学习模型需要不确定性量化，但联合训练点预测与不确定性预测的模型远比仅做点预测的模型困难。保形预测只保证边缘覆盖，需通过归一化非一致性分数来近似条件覆盖，因此需要一种有效的逐样本归一化因子。

Method: 将不确定性预测神经网络与估计多群中子截面屏蔽因子的预训练网络相连，预训练网络的权重和偏置固定，不确定性模型在相同的OpenMC数据上训练，输出残差标准差（分为模型不确定性与计数不确定性两部分），将两者方差按平方和合成作为保形预测的归一化因子，从而得到逐样本自适应的区间宽度。对比基线为Mondrian保形预测，以及使用OpenMC真值不确定性作为归一化因子。

Result: 与两类基线（Mondrian保形预测、OpenMC真值不确定性归一化）相比，本文方法同时大幅减小了预测区间宽度，并显著降低了预测值超出区间宽度的严重程度。

Conclusion: 将不确定性预测网络与固定预训练网络结合，并用其预测方差作为保形预测的归一化因子，是一种有效的逐样本自适应区间估计方法，在核能中子截面屏蔽因子应用中优于Mondrian保形预测等基线。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification in machine learning models is essential for nuclear energy applications. While machine learning models can make both uncertainty and point predictions, these models are often significantly more difficult to train jointly than models that make point predictions alone. Split conformal prediction is a family of methods for computing distribution-free prediction intervals by calculating nonconformity scores corresponding to a target quantile in a calibration dataset. Although conformal prediction only guarantees marginal coverage, normalizing nonconformity scores enables predictive interval widths to be calculated on a per-sample basis, providing an approximation to conditional coverage. In this work, we connect uncertainty predicting neural networks to pre-trained networks that estimate shielding factors in multigroup neutron cross sections. We train the uncertainty models on the same OpenMC data used by the pre-trained models, whose weights and biases remain fixed. The outputs of the uncertainty models are the standard deviations of the residual, separated into contributions from the model and tally uncertainty. Their corresponding variances are combined in quadrature to provide a normalizing factor for conformal prediction, providing interval widths that are adaptive on a per-sample basis. Baselines for comparison are established using Mondrian conformal prediction, and by using the ground truth OpenMC uncertainties as normalizers in the conformal framework. We use predicted uncertainty interval widths and the severity of predictions that overshoot the interval widths as bases for comparing our normalizer with the baselines. We find that our method dramatically reduces both interval widths and the degree to which predictions fall outside these widths.

</details>


### [35] [Self-Shielded Multigroup Microscopic Cross Section Generation Using Targeted Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10464)
*Joshua Nichols,Andrew Osborne,Ben Whewell,Nathan Gibson*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 训练神经网络可根据燃料核素原子浓度直接预测自屏微观截面，测试平均误差0.119%，用于多群k-eff计算时平均绝对误差约200 pcm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多群中子输运模型因内存与计算效率高而被广泛采用，但其依赖近似方法制备的核数据，需大量人工与专业知识处理自屏效应，且适用反应堆类型与运行工况有限。本文旨在用神经网络替代这一繁琐且受限的数据制备流程。

Method: 采用人工神经网络，输入仅为燃料组成核素的原子浓度；训练数据来自连续能量OpenMC pin-cell模拟，共8,704个训练与验证样本。网络输出CASMO-8能群结构下90种核素组合的自屏总截面、裂变、吸收、弹性散射与总散射截面；在固定几何与温度的二氧化铀燃料pin-cell中训练。使用1,500个训练中未见过的参考截面样本进行验证。

Result: 在测试集上，预测截面与参考真值平均一致度达0.119%；将预测截面用于多群本征值计算时，keff的平均绝对误差为200 pcm。模型在轻水堆的富集度与燃耗范围内均保持较高预测精度。

Conclusion: 神经网络能够以极简输入（原子浓度）准确预测自屏微观截面，验证了数据驱动方法替代传统多群核数据制备流程的可行性，可减少人工与专业依赖并提升多群码的适用范围，但当前局限于固定几何与温度的UO2 pin-cell。

Abstract: Multigroup neutron transport models are used widely in simulation codes due to their memory and compute efficiency compared to continuous energy methods. However, multigroup codes rely on nuclear data prepared using approximate methods that require significant manual effort and expertise to account for self-shielding effects. In addition, multigroup nuclear data libraries prepared in this way can be limited in their applicability for modeling diverse reactors and operating conditions. Here we show that trained artificial neural networks can predict self-shielded microscopic cross sections accurately in pincell simulations containing uranium dioxide fuel with a fixed geometry and temperature. The model's predictive accuracy remains high across fuel enrichment and burnup ranges encountered in light water reactors. Critically, the neural networks' predictions require only the atomic concentration of the fuel's constituent nuclides as inputs. The networks are trained using 8,704 samples of training and validation data generated using continuous energy OpenMC pincell simulations. The trained neural networks predict self-shielded total, fission, absorption, elastic, and total scattering cross sections for combinations of 90 nuclides in the CASMO-8 energy group structure. The neural networks' predictions of self-shielded cross sections are validated by comparison with 1,500 samples of reference cross sections unseen during training and tallied using OpenMC simulations. The predicted and ground truth cross sections in the test data set agree to within 0.119% on average. When the predicted cross sections are used in a multigroup eigenvalue run, they achieve a mean absolute error in keff of 200 pcm.

</details>

### [36] [A Bayesian Mirror Architecture for Emergent Consciousness: Circular Hierarchies, Self-Manifolds, and Hybrid Event-Self Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08792)
*Eduardo Righi Capanema de Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯镜像架构（BMA），一种通过闭环递归耦合感知抽象、元抽象与自我潜变量的自指生成框架，在2-Wasserstein几何下给出自我稳定性、混合相干性、因果学习机制与"选择"的形式化定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于意识、自我与生成模型的理论多将意识视为优化目标或语义标签，缺乏统一的形式化框架；同时，如何刻画自我表征与世界模型之间的持续耦合、稳定性与因果可学习性，尚缺少几何化、坐标无关的描述工具。

Method: 1) 定义闭环更新约束 S_t ← H_{t-1}，其中混合事件-自我潜变量 H_t 绑定自我表征与抽象世界模型并回注自我状态；2) 将内在状态空间设定为配备最优传输几何的概率测度空间，在 P_2 上以 2-Wasserstein 度量刻画自我稳定性与混合相干性；3) 通过Wasserstein信念漂移界与积分指数定义因果学习机制（CLR）；4) 在局部Wasserstein收缩性下，以不变测度支撑定义自我流形，识别Wasserstein ε-颈与传输瓶颈，并在电导趋于零的极限下导出唯一现实化延续；5) 以变分自由能最小化进行学习。

Result: BMA不要求全局严格收缩，可存在多个相干盆地；区域收缩性足以定义分区的自我流形。Wasserstein ε-颈刻画盆地间的解耦，其消失电导极限产生唯一实现路径，该选择被解释为内部决定但有限分辨率下外部不可预测的"选择"。CLR可诊断环境是否含可学习因果结构，但并非意识标志。

Conclusion: 在受限意义上，意识不是优化目标或语义标签，而是具备该闭环结构系统的架构属性；稳定性与能动性源自环境所提供的可学习性，自我稳定性与混合相干性由2-Wasserstein几何坐标无关地刻画。

Abstract: We present a foundational formulation of the Bayesian Mirror Architecture (BMA), a self-referential generative framework in which sensory abstractions, meta-abstractions, and a self-latent interact through circular recursion. The defining constraint is a closed update S_t <- H_{t-1}, where a hybrid event-self latent H_t binds self-representations to abstract world models and reinjects this coupling into the self-state. Consciousness, in a restricted sense, is not an optimization objective nor a semantic label, but an architectural property of systems possessing this circular structure.
  Because inference operates over posterior beliefs, BMA's intrinsic state space is a space of probability measures equipped with optimal-transport geometry. Stability and coherence are formulated in the 2-Wasserstein metric on P_2, yielding coordinate-free notions of self-stability and hybrid coherence along belief trajectories. We define a Causal Learning Regime (CLR) via bounds on Wasserstein belief drift together with an integration index capturing sustained coupling between self and world latents. CLR diagnoses whether the environment contains learnable causal structure; it is not a marker of consciousness.
  Global strict contractivity is not required: BMA may exhibit multiple coherent basins. We define self-manifolds basin-wise as supports of invariant measures under local Wasserstein contractivity. We identify Wasserstein epsilon-necks, transport bottlenecks where basins decouple, yielding a unique realized continuation in a vanishing-conductance limit. We interpret this selection as choice: internally determined yet externally unpredictable at finite resolution. Learning proceeds via variational free-energy minimization, with stability and agency emerging from what the environment affords to learn.

</details>

Result: Ridge回归在12周最优（RMSE 5.186，R² 0.474）；LightGBM在24周最优（RMSE 5.038，R² 0.525）。三项临床相关模式：基线严重度总体预测力最强；短期结局更依赖临床与医院情境；长期结局更依赖行为依从性与人口学因素。亚组分析显示预测因子在临床类别间及类别内差异显著。

Abstract: Depression severity among patients with chronic or acute medical conditions is influenced by a complex interaction of baseline psychological state, demographic characteristics, clinical context, and engagement with behavioral interventions. This paper presents an interpretable machine-learning analysis of a multi-center longitudinal clinical cohort to predict Beck Depression Inventory-II (BDI-II) scores at 12 and 24 weeks following mindfulness-based intervention participation. The study uses demographic variables, clinical condition information, hospital-center identifiers, baseline BDI-II scores, and therapy engagement measures to model short-term and long-term depression outcomes. Missing follow-up outcomes were addressed using a model-based stochastic imputation procedure to preserve the modest sample size while maintaining outcome variability. Five regression models were evaluated, spanning regularized linear regression and tree-based ensemble methods. Ridge Regression achieved the best 12-week performance with an RMSE of 5.186 and R^2 of 0.474, while LightGBM achieved the best 24-week performance with an RMSE of 5.038 and R^2 of 0.525. Beyond prediction accuracy, the analysis reveals three clinically relevant patterns: baseline severity remains the strongest overall predictor, short-term outcomes are more strongly associated with clinical and hospital context, and long-term outcomes show greater dependence on behavioral adherence and demographic factors. Disease-specific and hierarchical subgroup analyses further indicate that predictors differ substantially across and within clinical categories. These findings support the use of interpretable, context-aware modeling to inform personalized mental-health support following mindfulness-based interventions.

</details>

*Venkatesh Kolluru,Rajat Shinde,Abdelhak Marouane,Caden Helbling,Deepak Shah,Othneil Drew,Srinivas Kolluru,Iksha Gurung,Manil Maskey,Rahul Ramachandran*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 以广泛采用的GeoFM（Prithvi-EO-2.0）为对象，在三大洲12个国家、37个事件上评估分布外性能，并与区域参考产品对比验证。除总体精度（OA）外，评估五个方面：模型置信度是否指示失效、对观测窗口的敏感性、类别体系粗化的影响、对波段缺失的稳健性、对云与阴影污染的稳健性。采用预期校准误差（ECE）与蒙特卡洛dropout熵刻画置信度与分布偏移，并测试免重训练的调整方案（类别合并、观测窗口压缩）。

Abstract: Fine-tuned geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs) pretrained on large satellite archives have been shown to improve crop classification accuracy and geographic transferability. However, their operational performance beyond the training distribution remains poorly characterized. We evaluated the out-of-distribution performance of a widely adopted GeoFM [Prithvi-EO-2.0] across 37 events in 12 countries on three continents and validated against regional reference products. Results indicated that the mean overall accuracy (OA) declined from 0.65 in the United States to 0.40 in Europe. Beyond accuracy metrics, we assessed five key aspects of model performance: whether model confidence indicates signal failure, sensitivity to observation windows, the effect of coarsening class schemes, and robustness to both band loss and cloud- and shadow-contamination. Accuracy collapsed when the observation window misaligned with local crop phenology, while deterministic confidence remained high. Expected calibration error increased for seven of eight paired events, and 12-51% of each affected scene was confidently mislabeled at near-zero precision. Monte Carlo dropout entropy registered the shift in all eight, indicating that much of the apparent cross-continent decline reflected phenological misalignment rather than spatial transfer. Two adjustments recovered accuracy without retraining. Consolidating 13 classes into 10, based on the model's dominant confusions, raised the mean OA by 8.4 percentage points. Compressing the window toward near-real-time use preserved accuracy across a 45- to 90-day plateau, peaking near 75 days, though arms tighter than 30 days fell about 0.11 below that plateau. Fine-tuned crop GeoFMs therefore transfer usefully only where observation windows match local growing seasons. We translate these findings into operational guidance for the reliable deployment of the released model.

</details>


### [39] [KVFetch: Temporal Prefetching for the Missing Half of KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08811)
*Linfeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KVFetch，一个免训练、即插即用的框架，为任意基于分数的KV cache压缩器开辟"时序召回通道"，通过量化冷层、单调读指针检测与位置后继预取，解决压缩导致逐字复制中途断裂的"序列遗忘"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随上下文窗口扩展至数万乃至数十万token，KV cache压缩成为LLM高效推理的关键。现有方法（基于分数驱逐、摘要补偿、卸载召回）均以"内容相关性"决定保留/召回对象，忽略了缓存支持的另一种访问模式——按位置顺序遍历。该缺口在RAG、代码补全、结构化数据抽取等需逐字复现标识符、字段值或代码token的任务中引发致命问题：基于内容的驱逐保留了序列开头却丢弃后续部分，导致逐字复制中途不可逆地失败，即"序列遗忘"。

Method: KVFetch为免训练、即插即用框架，可嵌入任意基于分数的压缩器：(1) 将被驱逐的候选降级至量化冷层（cold tier）；(2) 通过单调递增的读指针检测模型正在进行的逐字复制行为；(3) 将位置后继（positional successors）预取到固定大小的热层槽位中，且不增加注意力计算开销。

Result: 在RULER-16K的等预算对照下，KVFetch将逐字复制能力从0.8恢复至78.4，13项任务平均提升+8.4，增益集中于需要顺序访问的任务；在LongBench上，由于没有任务需要顺序访问，该通道保持休眠且不产生额外开销。

Conclusion: 序列遗忘是压缩下剩余质量损失的主要来源，且无法通过更好的打分、更大的预算、摘要补偿或动态重打分解决。KVFetch以零训练成本补全了缓存的时序访问模式，仅在实际发生顺序复制时激活，兼顾效果与效率。

Abstract: As context windows scale to tens or hundreds of thousands of tokens, KV cache compression has become essential for efficient LLM inference. Existing methods fall into three families: score-based eviction, summary compensation, and offload-and-recall. Yet all three decide what to keep or recall by content relevance to the current query. We show this shared design is structurally incomplete. A cache supports two access modes: associative lookup by content and sequential traversal by position; current compressors implement only the first. The gap matters in practice: retrieval-augmented generation, code completion, and structured-data extraction all require the model to reproduce identifiers, field values, or code tokens verbatim from the context. Under compression, content-based eviction retains the head of such a sequence but discards its continuation, causing verbatim copying to break irreversibly midway, a failure we call sequential forgetting. This failure resists better scoring, larger budgets, summary compensation, and dynamic re-scoring; it is the dominant source of remaining quality loss under compression. We propose KVFetch, a training-free, drop-in framework that opens a temporal recall channel for any score-based compressor. It demotes evicted candidates to a quantized cold tier, detects active copying through a monotone read pointer, and prefetches positional successors into fixed-size hot-tier slots without increasing attention cost. On RULER-16K under an iso-budget control, KVFetch recovers verbatim copying from 0.8 to 78.4 and raises the 13-task average by +8.4, with gains concentrating on tasks that require sequential access. On LongBench, where no task requires sequential access, the channel remains dormant and imposes no cost.

</details>


### [40] [Bounded Autonomy and Verifiable Safety for Agentic AI Enabled Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08815)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Deveeshree Nayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BRaVeS有界推理与安全治理框架，将专家约束编码为不变量锚点，并用LBCF将连续认知风险映射到有限K-bag抽象、施加Lyapunov式能量下降的屏蔽状态转移；蒙特卡洛仿真显示有限步收敛且无安全护栏违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI在高风险环境中无法仅靠概率推理安全部署，存在"认知漂移"风险：推理越深，系统行为越可能偏离领域专家（SME）设定的安全运行约束。因此需要一种在推理过程中持续保持约束可见、并随认知风险升高而降低自主性的有界治理机制。

Method: 框架包含三部分：(1) 将SME定义的约束编码为不变量锚点(invariant anchors)；(2) 提出MoDA-Style（Mixture of Depths Attention）深度感知访问，作为在推理中保持锚点可见的候选机制；(3) 使用状态层级SMARtAutonomy，随认知风险上升而降低自主性。为形式化有界恢复，引入Lyapunov-Bounded Consensus Framework (LBCF)，把连续认知风险信号映射为有限K-bag抽象，并采用屏蔽状态转移，强制Lyapunov式能量下降或将系统导向人工介入的终止态。评估采用离散事件蒙特卡洛仿真，基于HAI 22.04工业控制系统时间序列数据，加入合成噪声与传感器退化情形。

Result: 在所测试的参数分组策略与阈值下，LBCF过程均实现有限步收敛，且未出现安全护栏违规。形式化收敛结论仅适用于固定阈值与可行屏蔽假设下的有限LBCF抽象，并不证明整个连续神经激活空间的安全性。

Conclusion: 仿真结果提供了基于抽象的证据，表明有界治理行为可被强制实现；但结论局限于该抽象层。未来工作包括已部署Transformer实现、实时人在回路验证以及更广泛的对抗性场景。

Abstract: Agentic AI-enabled automation cannot be safely deployed in high-stakes environments on probabilistic reasoning alone. A recurring risk is epistemic drift: as reasoning deepens, system behavior may move away from subject-matter-expert constraints for safe operation. This paper presents BRaVeS, a bounded reasoning and safety-governance framework termed the Defensible Next-Gen Reasoning System (DNRS). BRaVeS encodes SME-defined constraints as invariant anchors, proposes MoDA-Style (Mixture of Depths Attention) depth-aware access as a candidate mechanism for keeping these anchors visible during inference, and uses a state hierarchy (SMARtAutonomy) to reduce autonomy as epistemic risk increases. To formalize bounded recovery, we introduce the Lyapunov-Bounded Consensus Framework (LBCF), which maps continuous epistemic-risk signals into a finite K-bag abstraction and applies shielded state transitions that enforce Lyapunov-style energy descent or route the system to a human-mediated terminal state. The formal convergence result applies to the finite LBCF abstraction under fixed thresholds and feasible-shield assumptions; it does not prove safety of the full continuous neural activation space. We evaluate the framework through a discrete event Monte Carlo simulation using HAI 22.04 industrial-control-system time-series data with synthetic noise and sensor-degradation regimes. Across the tested parameter-grouping strategies and thresholds, the LBCF process achieved finite-step convergence and no safety-guard violations. These results provide simulation-based evidence that bounded governance behavior can be enforced under the stated abstraction, while motivating future work on deployed transformer implementations, live human-in-the-loop validation, and broader adversarial settings.

</details>


### [41] [The Cost of Long Memory: State, Context, and Stability Complexity in Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08816)
*Yuheng Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究长程时间依赖下的预测记忆资源问题，针对代数衰减记忆给出了指数模式与有限状态模式匹配的上下界，证明最优 r 模式预测误差按 e^{-Θ(√r)} 衰减，即达到误差 τ 需 r=Θ(log²(1/τ)) 状态；并进一步分析分数长记忆改变几何结构、有限上下文 1/L 主阶、d²与d⁴尺度及非线性递推的临界性必要条件，实验与理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程时间依赖给序列模型带来资源瓶颈：给定预测记忆律，究竟需要多少状态、上下文或动力学临界性才能准确预测尚不明确。作者希望在"预测风险"这一框架下直接回答该问题，并为稳定循环模型的记忆诅咒结果补充一个两侧匹配的典范目标。

Method: 1）对代数衰减的预测记忆，证明指数与有限状态模式下的匹配上、下近似界；2）引入真实分数长记忆，在分数积分后度量预测误差，推导有限上下文长度 L 的精确 1/L 主阶，并分析固定分数强度 d 的状态复杂度律及短记忆边界附近的 d²、d⁴ 尺度；3）对一致收缩状态动力学的非线性上下文递推，推导指数级首混沌包络与联系预测精度和收缩裕度的必要条件；4）用有限样本 KL 计算连接预测几何与统计信息；5）设计契约状态空间、门控循环与注意力模型进行定理匹配实验。

Result: 最优 r 模式预测误差以 e^{-Θ(√r)} 衰减，达到误差 τ 需要 r=Θ(log²(1/τ)) 个状态或模式，固定了该典范目标的最优资源指数；分数长记忆下预测误差经分数积分后度量，有限上下文预测有精确 1/L 主阶，固定分数强度 d 仍保持平方对数状态复杂度律；短记忆边界附近识别出 d² 与 d⁴ 尺度并给出中间区域的统一构造律；非线性收缩递推存在指数首混沌包络，代数目标上预测误差消失迫使递推定量趋向临界性（必要非充分）；有限样本 KL 计算建立了预测几何与统计信息的联系；实验复现了状态与稳定性预测。

Conclusion: 本文在预测风险框架中为代数衰减预测记忆给出了状态复杂度与预测精度的匹配界，明确了最优资源指数 r=Θ(log²(1/τ))；揭示了分数长记忆对预测几何的改变、有限上下文的 1/L 主阶与 d²/d⁴ 尺度规律；并通过非线性递推的临界性必要条件和 KL 信息联系，将预测几何、统计信息与模型稳定性统一起来，实验验证了理论预测。

Abstract: Long-range temporal dependence poses a resource question for sequence models: for a specified predictive-memory law, how much state, context, or dynamical criticality is required in order to forecast accurately? We study this question directly in forecasting risk. For algebraically decaying predictive memory, we prove matching upper and lower approximation bounds for exponential and finite-state modes. The best $r$-mode forecast error decays as $e^{-Θ(\sqrt r)}$, so reaching forecast error $τ$ needs $r=Θ(\log^2(1/τ))$ states or modes. Earlier curse-of-memory results establish broad limitations of stable recurrent models under different approximation notions; here both sides match for one canonical predictive target in forecast risk, which fixes the optimal resource exponent for that target. We then show that genuine fractional long memory changes the geometry itself. In particular, forecast error is measured after fractional integration, prediction from a finite context of length $L$ has an exact $1/L$ leading order, and a fixed fractional strength $d$ keeps the square-log state-complexity law. Near the short-memory boundary, we identify the relevant $d^2$ and $d^4$ scales and give a uniform constructive law in the intermediate regime. For nonlinear contextual recurrences with uniformly contractive state dynamics, we derive an exponential first-chaos envelope and an explicit necessary condition that relates forecast accuracy to the contraction margin. Vanishing forecasting error on an algebraic target forces the recurrence quantitatively toward criticality, a condition that is necessary and not by itself sufficient. Finite-sample Kullback--Leibler calculations further connect the predictive geometry to statistical information. Theorem-matched experiments with contractive state-space, gated recurrent, and attention models reproduce the state and stability predictions.

</details>


### [42] [DenoFlow: Flow Matching for SSVEP Denoising under Real Physiological Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08817)
*Zhentao He,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DenoFlow，将SSVEP去噪建模为整流流传输任务，用场网络回归污染与干净试次间的直线路径速度，并配合同步训练的分类器监督积分输出，在保持可解码性的同时提升信号保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSVEP脑机接口易受噪声与伪迹影响，解码精度显著下降。现有去噪方法缺乏成对真值监督、易退化为复现输入，且仅以波形距离优化，无法保证输出仍可解码。

Method: 将SSVEP去噪视为传输问题：不学习从污染试次到干净试次的直接映射，而用场网络按rectified-flow公式回归二者间直线路径的速度，去噪时从观测出发前向积分该速度场。场网络为编码器-解码器结构，每层接收污染试次，瓶颈层接收路径位置；同步训练的分类器监督积分输出。由于观测既是条件输入又是积分起点，模型不会从纯噪声生成试次，训练退化为回归，无需对抗式min-max博弈。为在无真值数据集上获得成对数据，在受控信噪比目标下注入录制的EMG与EOG生理伪迹。

Result: 在两个公开SSVEP数据集、五种主流SSVEP解码器上，DenoFlow在信号保真度与下游解码精度两方面均优于七种基线去噪模型。

Conclusion: DenoFlow通过整流流传输式去噪，规避了无配对真值与对抗训练的问题，并借助分类器监督确保去噪结果保持可解码性，验证了其在SSVEP去噪任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain-computer interfaces (BCIs), particularly steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) systems, are highly vulnerable to noise and artifacts, which severely degrade decoding accuracy. Although recent denoising approaches have shown promise, they are fitted without paired ground truth, can settle on reproducing their input, and are optimized on waveform distance alone, which says nothing about whether the output stays decodable. To address these issues, we propose DenoFlow, which casts SSVEP denoising as transport: instead of learning a direct map from a contaminated trial to a clean one, a field network regresses the velocity of the straight path between them, following the rectified-flow formulation, and denoising integrates that field forward from the observation. The field network is an encoder-decoder that sees the contaminated trial at every layer and the path position at its bottleneck, and a classifier trained alongside it supervises the integrated output. Because the observation itself is both the conditioning input and the starting point of the integration, the model never generates a trial from noise, and training reduces to regression, removing the adversarial min-max game. To obtain paired data on datasets with no ground truth, we injected physiological artifacts of the recorded electromyography (EMG) and electrooculography (EOG) signals under a controlled signal-to-noise target. Experiments on two public SSVEP datasets with five popular SSVEP decoders showed that DenoFlow outperformed seven baseline denoising models on both signal fidelity and downstream decoding accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/wzwvv/DenoFlow.

</details>

*Shuhao Li,Weidong Yang,Changan Liu,Wei Zhuo,Yingbo Zhou,Fan Zhang,Siqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GenST框架，将"预测未观测节点状态"（FUNS）重新定义为时空图上的条件生成任务，借助预训练大语言模型（LLM）提取节点描述（功能区、路网结构）的语义特征，并以"时空VAE+生成式Transformer（GenT）"两阶段架构从噪声重建未观测节点未来状态，在6个交通与2个非交通数据集上零样本预测显著优于基线。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器网络受部署成本与维护资源限制，空间覆盖不完整，导致存在大量无历史观测记录的节点，而传统模型依赖历史观测，在无历史记录节点上表现不佳，因此FUNS问题既关键又困难。作者希望利用节点语义信息弥补缺失的时空信号。

Method: 将FUNS重新建模为时空图上的条件生成任务；引入预训练并微调的LLM作为语义桥梁，从节点描述（功能区、路网结构等）中提取语义特征；设计两阶段生成架构：先由时空VAE将时空动态压缩到隐空间，再由生成式Transformer（GenT）在语义、地理坐标、邻域上下文等多模态条件引导下，从噪声重建未观测节点的未来状态。

Conclusion: 语义引导的生成式建模可有效应对未观测节点的时空预测难题，为缓解时空数据稀疏提供了有前景的新范式。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting is a cornerstone of logistics, urban planning, and intelligent transportation systems. However, constrained by deployment costs and maintenance resources, sensor networks often lack comprehensive spatial coverage, rendering Forecast Unobserved Node States (FUNS) a critical yet formidable challenge. Conventional models rely on historical observations and typically falter when encountering nodes without prior records. To address this, we redefine the problem as a conditional generation task on spatio-temporal graphs and propose GenST, a framework that introduces Large Language Models (LLMs) as a semantic bridge, leveraging a pre-trained LLM fine-tuned to extract rich semantic features from node descriptions, such as functional zones and road network structures, to compensate for missing spatio-temporal signals. Specifically, we design a two-stage generative architecture: a Spatio-Temporal VAE first compresses spatio-temporal dynamics into a latent space, followed by a Generative Transformer (GenT) that reconstructs the future states of unobserved nodes from noise, guided by multi-modal conditions including semantics, geographic coordinates, and neighborhood contexts. Experiments on six traffic and two non-traffic datasets show GenST significantly outperforms existing baselines in zero-shot prediction tasks, demonstrating the practical potential of semantic-guided generation for mitigating spatio-temporal data sparsity.

</details>

*Prabu,Fancy C,Suresh A,Srini Ramaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HydroSphere，一个面向水质实时监测、预测、处理优化与故障恢复的受治理数据驱动框架，基于 1940—2023 年间 282 万条水质测量数据进行评估，融合 TCN-LSTM 混合预测、PPO 自适应投药优化与 SHADE 深度自编码器异常检测三大模块，并辅以分层治理、确定性安全边界与人工监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速工业化与城市扩张加大了对水质和污水处理系统的压力，而传统处理厂多依赖静态监测与控制策略，难以适应不断变化的污染物状况，因此需要具备实时性、自适应性与安全可控性的智能污水管理框架。

Method: 框架包含三个主要组件：1）混合 TCN-LSTM 模型，对七个水质参数进行多步预测；2）自适应投药优化模块（Adaptive Dosage Optimization Module），采用 PPO 强化学习调整化学药剂投加量；3）SHADE 异常检测模块，使用深度自编码器识别传感器与工艺异常。同时引入分层治理、确定性安全边界与人工监督机制。评估使用 1940—2023 年间采集的 282 万条水质测量数据。

Result: TCN-LSTM 多步预测的 RMSE 为 0.1417、MAE 为 0.1047、R² 为 0.3596；PPO 投药优化模块的平均单步奖励为 1.059，高于固定投药基线的 1.017；无约束奖励优化会导致过度投药，表明需要显式运行安全约束；SHADE 在受控故障注入下的 F1 分数为 0.651。

Conclusion: HydroSphere 将上述能力与分层治理、确定性安全边界和人工监督相结合，可支撑更安全、更具自适应性的水务基础设施，为智能污水管理提供可扩展的基础，并有助于实现联合国可持续发展目标 6 与 13。

Abstract: Rapid industrialization and urban growth are increasing pressure on water quality and wastewater treatment systems, while conventional treatment plants often rely on static monitoring and control strategies that cannot easily adapt to changing pollutant conditions. This paper presents HydroSphere, a governed, data-driven framework for real-time water quality monitoring, forecasting, treatment optimization, and fault recovery. HydroSphere is evaluated using 2.82 million water-quality measurements collected between 1940 and 2023. The framework integrates three main components. First, a hybrid TCN-LSTM model performs multi-step forecasting across seven water-quality parameters, achieving an RMSE of 0.1417, MAE of 0.1047, and R2 of 0.3596. Second, the Adaptive Dosage Optimization Module uses PPO reinforcement learning to adjust chemical dosing, achieving a mean step reward of 1.059 compared with 1.017 for a fixed-dose baseline. The results also show that unconstrained reward optimization can lead to excessive dosing, demonstrating the need for explicit operational safeguards. Third, the SHADE anomaly detection module uses a deep autoencoder to identify sensor and process anomalies, achieving an F1 score of 0.651 under controlled fault injection. HydroSphere combines these capabilities with tiered governance, deterministic safety bounds, and human oversight to support safer and more adaptive water infrastructure. The framework provides a scalable foundation for intelligent wastewater management and supports the objectives of UN Sustainable Development Goals 6 and 13.

</details>


### [45] [PoreML: A Data-Driven Framework for Learning Multiphase Flow in Porous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10314)
*Chunyang Wang,Mingrui Zhang,Yuyan Zhang,Linqi Zhu,Xin Ju,Edo Sicco Boek,Martin J. Blunt,Gege Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开源框架PoreML，统一多孔介质多相流的数据生成、模型训练与评估，提供GPU格子玻尔兹曼求解器、3.3 TB/560次模拟/158,546时间步数据集及统一学习评估协议，并评测五种架构模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔微观结构多相流在CO2封存、燃料电池、倒装芯片封装中至关重要，但润湿性与复杂孔隙几何导致界面非线性演化，预测困难。机器学习有潜力，却受限于缺乏时间分辨的三维数据以及缺少统一的训练与评估流程。

Method: PoreML包含三部分：(a) 现代GPU原生格子玻尔兹曼求解器，经解析解与已发表实验验证，可复现地生成数据；(b) 3.3 TB数据集，涵盖560次模拟、158,546个存储时间步、四个应用驱动场景，横跨合成结构与真实材料micro-CT扫描几何，覆盖多种润湿条件与黏度比；(c) 统一学习框架，评估单步预测与自回归推演，并采用领域专用评估协议衡量预测精度与物理一致性。基于该协议评测五种不同架构模型，并通过两项互补挑战考察向更大区域及从合成结构到micro-CT结构的迁移。

Result: 构建并开放了PoreML框架，形成可复现的数据生成工具、大规模多场景时间分辨三维数据集，以及统一的模型训练与评估基准；在统一协议下完成五种架构模型的评测，并给出跨尺度与跨结构迁移的挑战性结果。

Conclusion: PoreML为多孔介质多相流的机器学习研究提供了共享基础，旨在帮助学界开发可靠的预测模型并推动领域发展。

Abstract: Multiphase flow in porous microstructures is central to CO$_2$ storage, fuel-cell operation, and flip-chip packaging. Predicting these flows remains challenging because wettability and complex pore geometry govern the nonlinear evolution of fluid interfaces. Machine learning holds substantial promise for advancing the field, but progress is constrained by scarce time-resolved 3D datasets and a lack of a unified workflow for training and evaluating models. To fill this critical gap, we introduce PoreML, an open-source framework unifying data generation, model training, and evaluation grounded in pore-scale physics. The framework comprises three core components. (a) A modern GPU-native lattice Boltzmann solver, validated against analytical solutions and published experiments, enables reproducible data generation. (b) A 3.3 TB dataset contains 560 simulation runs and 158,546 stored time steps across four application-driven scenarios. These trajectories span synthetic structures and geometries derived from micro-CT scans of real materials, covering diverse wetting conditions and viscosity ratios. (c) A unified learning framework evaluates one-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts. Its domain-specific evaluation protocols assess predictive accuracy and physical consistency. We evaluate five models of diverse architecture under these protocols. Two complementary challenges assess transfer to larger domains and from synthetic to micro-CT-derived structures. PoreML provides a shared foundation for machine-learning research on multiphase flow in porous media, with the aim of empowering the community to develop reliable predictive models and advance the field.

</details>


### [46] [Task-Oriented Key-Layer KV Communication for Efficient Latent Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08820)
*Dongsen Zhang,Peipei Li,Zekun Li,Wenjun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练的KITE框架，将潜在通信目标从发送端状态保真转为接收端任务充分性，通过关键层KV通信大幅降低开销并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统通过协作提升复杂问题求解能力，潜在通信直接传递模型内部状态以规避自然语言推理的高昂成本；但现有基于KV的潜在通信方法以发送端状态保真为优先，导致通信与计算开销巨大，并可能引入冗余信息。

Method: 从任务导向视角重新审视潜在通信，将目标由发送端状态保真转变为接收端任务充分性。提出KITE：用接收端轨迹扰动准则识别任务有效关键层，仅传输与该关键层相关的潜在工作记忆，并将同一层作为自回归潜在推理的入口。

Result: 在两个模型家族、三种模型规模的七个基准上，相比全层KV通信，KITE将通信量降低28–36倍，端到端推理最高加速3倍，精度最高提升23.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 以接收端任务充分性为目标的任务导向关键层KV通信，可在显著削减通信与计算开销的同时提升多智能体协作性能，为潜在通信提供了高效的新范式。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through collaboration, while latent communication directly transmits model internal states to avoid the high inference costs of natural language. However, existing KV-based latent communication methods prioritize sender-side state fidelity, leading to substantial communication and computation overhead and potentially introducing redundant information. To address these limitations, we revisit latent communication from a task-oriented perspective, shifting its objective from sender-side state fidelity to receiver-side task sufficiency. Under this formulation, we propose KITE, a training-free framework for task-oriented key-layer KV communication. KITE identifies a task-effective key layer using a receiver trajectory distortion criterion, transmits only the latent working memory associated with the key layer, and further uses the same layer as the entry point for autoregressive latent reasoning. Experiments on seven benchmarks across two model families and three model scales show that, compared with full-layer KV communication, KITE reduces communication volume by 28-36$\times$, achieves up to 3$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup, and improves accuracy by up to 23.3 percentage points.

</details>

*Zihao Liu,Daigo Kawabe,Jiaji Wang,Chul-Woo Kim,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出车辆集成数字孪生框架，融合物理建模与机器学习实现桥梁与道路状态的连续监测：以Fourier Neural Operator构建VBI/VRI代理模型实现毫秒级推理，经Bayesian优化设计电动检测车与传感/信号链，并采用对抗自编码器、矩阵轮廓与Transformer的无监督损伤评估流程，最终在澳大利亚与日本多站点现场试验中跨桥型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁老化是全球性关切，但传统结构健康监测成本高、难以规模化，人工目视巡检主观性强；"驱车式/间接"检测虽具可扩展性，却仍面临三大未解难题：从路面粗糙度中分离桥梁响应、正常交通流下检测损伤、以及跨不同桥型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出车辆集成数字孪生框架，统一物理建模与机器学习，含三大支柱：(1) 用Fourier Neural Operator构建VBI与VRI代理模型，学习从运营工况到车辆响应的函数到函数映射，基于仿真与实测数据训练，实现毫秒级推理以替代高成本全阶分析；(2) 通过Bayesian优化设计定制电动检测车、传感器布局与信号处理链，在抑制路面与车辆噪声的同时最大化桥梁信息产出，并开发基于对抗自编码器、矩阵轮廓与Transformer的无监督损伤评估流程；(3) 在澳大利亚与日本开展协调多站点现场试验，覆盖多种桥型、交通条件与环境设置，验证完整工作流。

Result: 构建的物理信息代理模型实现毫秒级推理，替代计算密集的全阶分析；经Bayesian优化的检测车与信号链可在抑制路噪与车噪条件下提升桥梁信息获取量；无监督损伤评估流程（对抗自编码器、矩阵轮廓、Transformer）已针对车辆数据完成开发与验证；完整流程通过澳、日多站点现场试验在多种桥型、交通与环境条件下得到验证。

Conclusion: 该车辆集成数字孪生框架通过物理建模与机器学习的深度融合，为桥梁与道路状态的连续、可扩展监测提供了可行路径，并在跨桥型、跨地域的现场试验中证明了工作流的有效性与泛化潜力。

Abstract: Ageing bridge infrastructure is a growing global concern, yet conventional Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) systems are costly and difficult to scale, and routine visual inspections remain subjective. Drive-by, or indirect, bridge inspection, in which a sensorised vehicle recovers structural information from vehicle-bridge interaction (VBI) and vehicle-road interaction (VRI) responses, offers a scalable alternative. However, key challenges remain unresolved, including separating bridge responses from road roughness, detecting damage under normal traffic, and generalising across diverse bridge types. This paper presents a vehicle-integrated digital twin framework that unifies physics-based modelling and machine learning for continuous monitoring of bridge and road conditions. The framework comprises three pillars. First, surrogate models of VBI and VRI are constructed using a Fourier Neural Operator that learns function-to-function mappings from operating conditions to vehicle responses. Trained on both simulated and field data, these surrogates deliver millisecond-scale inference, replacing computationally intensive full-order analyses. Second, the design of a custom electric inspection vehicle, its sensor layout, and signal processing chain are optimised through Bayesian optimisation to maximise bridge information yield while suppressing road and vehicle noise. Unsupervised damage-assessment pipelines based on adversarial autoencoders, matrix profiles, and transformer architectures have been developed and validated to process the resulting vehicle data. Third, the complete workflow is validated through coordinated multi-site field trials in Australia and Japan, covering a range of bridge types, traffic conditions, and environmental settings.

</details>


### [48] [HCPN-GCN: Scaling Hierarchical Prototype Networks with Cone Geometry for Continual Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08823)
*Sammuel R. Silva,Vander L. S. Freitas,Gladston Moreira,Eduardo J. S. Luz,Rodrigo Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HCPN-GCN，用 GCN 替换 HPN 的线性特征提取器并引入锥形原型与多样性正则，在六个 CGL 基准上提升平均精度、近零遗忘，且原子原型数量减少约 30 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续图学习（CGL）的核心难题是灾难性遗忘。层次原型网络（HPN）通过基于原型的记忆机制避免存储历史数据，但其线性特征提取器无法利用图拓扑结构，且点式原型在结构多样的图上易导致原型数量低效膨胀。

Method: 提出 HCPN-GCN，即 HPN 的图感知扩展：以图卷积网络（GCN）替代原线性特征提取器；引入锥形原型（cone-based prototypes）并配合多样性正则化目标，以更紧凑地建模嵌入空间。

Result: 在六个持续图学习基准上，平均分类准确率稳定优于原始 HPN 及代表性持续学习基线，同时保持近零遗忘；学习到显著更丰富的类级原型层次，原子原型数量约为原 HPN 的 1/30。

Conclusion: HCPN-GCN 在无需存储历史数据的前提下，兼顾图拓扑感知与紧凑记忆表示，为持续图学习提供了更紧凑、更有效的原型记忆方案。

Abstract: Continual Graph Learning (CGL) aims to incrementally learn from graph-structured data while preserving knowledge acquired from previous tasks. A major challenge in this setting is catastrophic forgetting, where learning new tasks degrades performance on previously learned ones. Hierarchical Prototype Networks (HPNs) address this problem through a prototype-based memory mechanism that avoids storing historical data, but their reliance on linear feature extractors limits their ability to exploit graph topology, while point-based prototypes often lead to inefficient prototype growth on structurally diverse graphs. In this work, we propose HCPN-GCN, a graph-aware extension of HPN that replaces the original linear feature extractors with Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) and introduces cone-based prototypes with a diversity regularization objective. The proposed design produces richer graph-aware representations while compactly modeling the embedding space, reducing prototype proliferation without sacrificing discriminability. Experimental results on six continual graph learning benchmarks demonstrate that HCPN-GCN consistently improves average classification accuracy over the original HPN and representative continual learning baselines while maintaining near-zero forgetting. Furthermore, our analysis shows that the proposed model learns substantially richer class-level prototype hierarchies using approximately $30\times$ fewer atomic prototypes than the original HPN, providing a more compact and effective memory representation for continual graph learning.

</details>


### [49] [FinVector-Market-4B: A Controlled Study of LoRA Adaptation for Structured Financial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08882)
*Alina Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FinVector-Market-4B 用 rank-16 LoRA 微调 Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B，在匹配显式提示下金融任务指标大幅提升，但增益受任务分布与提示契约限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验紧凑金融领域适配能否在输出格式学习之外带来实质性任务增益，并区分"格式合规"与"任务能力"两类提升。

Method: 基于 Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B，使用 rank-16 LoRA 在 22,000 条样本语料上微调；在 600 例基准上以隐式与显式 JSON-schema 契约评测基座与适配模型，并做事后策略评分审计（统一三目标类别）与数据一致性/重叠检查。

Result: 仅提供 schema 即将基座 JSON 有效率从 0% 提至 91.3%；匹配显式提示下，FinQA 精确匹配 14.7%→40.0%，计算器表达式正确率 48.0%→82.7%，情景分支标签一致率 20.1%→89.5%，蕴含方向一致率 52.4%→87.2%；统一标签集后 macro-F1 由 77.4% 升至 83.1%；存在申报文件重叠与计算器目标不一致问题。

Conclusion: 紧凑金融领域适配能在匹配提示契约下产生超越输出格式学习的显著任务增益，但增益上限受评测任务分布与提示契约约束，基准泛化结论需谨慎。

Abstract: FinVector-Market-4B adapts Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B with rank-16 LoRA on a 22,000-example corpus for structured financial tasks. We evaluate the base and adapted models on the same 600-example benchmark under implicit and explicit JSON-schema contracts. Supplying the schema alone raises base-model JSON validity from 0% to 91.3%. Under matched explicit prompting, the frozen scores improve from 14.7% to 40.0% for FinQA answer exact match, from 48.0% to 82.7% for calculator-expression correctness, from 20.1% to 89.5% for scenario branch-label agreement, and from 52.4% to 87.2% for implication-direction agreement. A post-hoc policy-scoring audit shows that the reported macro-F1 decline reflects a changing label set; using the same three target classes gives 77.4% for the base and 83.1% for the adapter. Filing overlap and calculator-target inconsistencies qualify the benchmark's generalization claims. The results show that compact financial domain adaptation can produce substantial task-specific gains beyond output-format learning under matched prompting, with gains bounded by the evaluated task distribution and prompt contract.

</details>


### [50] [GraphOPD: Graph-Augmented On-Policy Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08959)
*Bohan Lin,Liyi Chen,Zhuoning Guo,Muyang Li,Qimeng Wang,Yan Gao,Yao Hu,Yudong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GraphOPD，首个将图结构增强引入 on-policy distillation 的智能体蒸馏方法，通过环境状态变化记录构建步骤依赖图并以随机游走平稳分布打分，融合散度信号生成轨迹相对掩码，在 ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA 上超越最强基线最多 +5.8 pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy distillation 依赖单轮直觉，按 teacher-student 散度大小分配监督：认为散度大的步骤是值得纠正的错误。但在多轮决策链中该规则失效——早期漂移会进入后续所有上下文，导致 teacher 与漂移轨迹保持一致而不标记其成因；同时可互换步骤会产生大但无关结果的散度。作者在 agentic benchmark 上验证，蒸馏最高散度步骤相较随机选择并无稳定收益。

Method: GraphOPD 从环境自身状态变化记录中读取哪些步骤促成了哪些后续步骤，该信号不受污染 teacher-student 差距的漂移影响；将这些依赖组织为依赖图，用随机游走平稳分布为每步打分，并将该结构信用与散度信号融合为轨迹相对掩码，把监督集中在每次 rollout 中最高潜力的步骤上。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA 上，覆盖三个模型规模和十一个基线，GraphOPD 全程表现具竞争力，相较最强基线最高提升 +5.8 pp。执行回放审计显示，该结构信用分数追踪真实因果影响远超随机水平，两种融合信号各自独立必要，且同一信号可迁移至域外工具集成推理。

Conclusion: 按散度大小分配监督的规则在多轮智能体场景中失效；以环境状态变化为据的结构信用能可靠反映因果影响，与散度融合后可显著提升 on-policy distillation 效果，并具备跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation post-trains large language model agents by supplying dense, step-level guidance from a teacher policy when the reinforcement-learning reward is sparse and arrives only once per trajectory. Existing instantiations allocate this guidance by the size of the teacher-student divergence at each step, on the single-turn intuition that a large disagreement marks a mistake worth correcting. Once decisions chain over many turns, that rule misfires, since an early drift enters every later context both policies condition on, leaving the teacher consistent with the drifted trajectory instead of flagging its cause, while interchangeable steps register large but outcome-irrelevant divergences. We demonstrate this on an agentic benchmark, where distilling the highest-divergence steps brings no consistent benefit over random selection. To this end, we introduce GraphOPD, the first method to bring graph-based structural augmentation into on-policy distillation for agent capabilities. It reads which steps enabled which later ones from the environment's own record of state changes, immune to the drift that corrupts the teacher-student gap, organizes them into a dependency graph, scores each step by a random-walk stationary distribution over it, and fuses that structural credit with the divergence signal into a trajectory-relative mask concentrating supervision on each rollout's highest-aptitude steps. Across three model scales and eleven baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA, GraphOPD shows competitive performance throughout, improving over the strongest baseline by up to +5.8 pp. An executed-replay audit further shows that this structural credit score tracks true causal impact far above chance, that both fused signals are independently necessary, and that the same signal transfers to out-of-domain tool-integrated reasoning.

</details>


### [51] [Directed Temporal Representations for Offline Visual Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08960)
*Chenyang Yuan,Haoyu Wang,Zhuo Sun,Xiaoyuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Directed Temporal Representations for Control (DTRC)，在冻结的 LeWorldModel 特征之上，从离线视觉轨迹中学习一种与时间可达性对齐的有向时间拟度量，并将其变化作为时间评论家信号用于直接目标条件策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制任务需要的潜在几何结构应与时间可达性对齐，而非仅依赖预测相似性；现有世界模型表征往往只优化预测精度，缺少对时间到达成本与方向性的建模。

Method: DTRC 构建有向时间拟度量：用短程时间偏移校准距离尺度，用自举目标扩展长时域可达性，用动作条件一致性对齐局部转移动态；距离估计时间到达成本，其跨转移变化定义目标相对时间进度，作为时间评论家指导目标条件策略学习；并用受行为支持与动态一致性约束的模型辅助目标进行训练期精修。

Result: 在十个视觉控制任务上，DTRC 相对规划基线与直接策略基线取得较强的目标条件控制性能；在四个 LeWM 任务的留出诊断中，呈现一致的短程时间校准、任务相关的长程与方向结构，以及正向的转移级进度。

Conclusion: 时间监督可改善同一流策略参数化，并在四个 LeWM 任务上均有效；所得策略在测试时无需迭代轨迹搜索即可直接行动，验证了面向时间可达性的表示学习对目标条件控制的有效性。

Abstract: Predictive world models provide compact visual representations for control. Control requires a latent geometry aligned with temporal reachability rather than predictive similarity alone. We introduce Directed Temporal Representations for Control (DTRC), which learns such a geometry from offline visual trajectories on top of frozen LeWorldModel (LeWM) features. DTRC constructs a directed temporal quasimetric over the learned control representation. Short-range temporal offsets calibrate the distance scale. Bootstrapped targets extend temporal reachability across longer horizons. Action-conditioned consistency aligns the representation with local transition dynamics. The resulting distance estimates temporal reaching cost, and its change across a transition defines goal-relative temporal progress. We use this progress signal as a temporal critic for direct goal-conditioned policy learning. Model-assisted targets provide an additional training-time refinement under behavior-support and dynamics-agreement constraints. Across ten visual control tasks, DTRC achieves strong goal-conditioned control performance relative to planning and direct-policy baselines. Held-out diagnostics on the four LeWM tasks show consistent short-range temporal calibration, task-dependent long-range and directional structure, and positive transition-level progress. Temporal supervision improves the same flow-policy parameterization across all four LeWM tasks, while the resulting policy acts directly without iterative trajectory search at test time.

</details>


### [52] [On KL-Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08963)
*Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 KLPO 框架，将 KL 正则锚定在采样器上，导出闭式 Gibbs 解，用最小二乘拟合对数比最优条件，实现无 critic、每 prompt 单次 rollout 的异步 RL 更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步 RL 训练 LLM agent 时，rollout 来自陈旧 checkpoint，且推理引擎与训练器在相同参数下概率也不一致，存在采样-训练分布偏移。现有方法要么裁剪重要性比率（引入偏差），要么如 GRPO 每 prompt 采样一组回复（长 episode 下代价高）。

Method: KLPO 将 KL 正则项锚定在采样器处，正则化改进步具有闭式 Gibbs 解；通过对采样器自身轨迹做最小二乘拟合对数比最优条件，采样概率以对数比形式进入，无需重要性权重。消去回归截距后，用信号采样均值加采样器到训练器的 KL 散度替代难解的 log-partition。对 token 级策略镜像下降目标，证明梯度可由终端回报计算而无需 critic（采样器中心得分或单条轨迹残差），并适用于随机工具输出；进一步证明独立蒙特卡洛 KL 估计保持梯度无偏，导出 top-K 与二值近似的精确 KL 差距。

Result: KLPO 将 SPPO、GPO、REBEL、BPO 统一为特例；得到无需 critic、每 prompt 仅一次 rollout、既不需要学习归一化器也不需要一组回复的更新规则。

Conclusion: KLPO 为异步 LLM agent RL 提供统一且高效的无 critic 框架，规避重要性权重裁剪偏差与分组采样开销，并对 KL 近似误差给出理论刻画。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) for large language model (LLM) agents trains one policy on trajectories generated by another: rollouts come from stale checkpoints, and the inference engine's probabilities differ from the trainer's even at identical parameters. Standard remedies either clip importance ratios, which biases the update, or, as in GRPO, sample a group of responses per prompt, which is costly when episodes are long. We propose KL-Regularized Policy Optimization (KLPO), a framework that anchors the KL regularizer at the sampler. The regularized improvement step then has a closed-form Gibbs solution, and KLPO fits its log-ratio optimality condition by least squares on the sampler's own trajectories, so the sampler probability enters through a log-ratio and no importance weights are needed. Profiling out the regression intercept replaces the intractable log-partition function with the signal's sampler mean plus a sampler-to-trainer KL divergence. For token-level policy mirror descent targets, we show that the resulting gradient can be computed from terminal returns without a critic, via sampler-centered scores or a single trajectory residual, even under stochastic tool outputs. We further prove that independent Monte Carlo estimates of the KL term keep these gradients unbiased, derive the exact KL gap of cheaper top-$K$ and binary approximations, and show that SPPO, GPO, REBEL, and BPO arise as special cases of KLPO. The result is a critic-free update that uses one rollout per prompt and requires neither a learned normalizer nor a group of responses.

</details>


### [53] [Work While They Sleep: Exploiting Evaluation Latency for Fully Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08969)
*Gustavo Sutter,Alejandro Comas-Leon,David Holzmüller,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELF-BO利用目标函数评估的等待时间提前采样超参数后验，实现完全贝叶斯优化而不增加决策延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化中目标函数评估昂贵且耗时，评估时间主导执行；标准贝叶斯优化（BO）用点估计拟合代理模型超参数，而完全贝叶斯方法通过模型平均更好刻画超参数不确定性，在BO常见的低数据场景中尤其有用，但计算成本过高、很少实用。

Method: 提出ELF-BO：利用目标评估的延迟时间"抢跑"下一次建议的计算——在目标函数被评估的同时从超参数后验中采样，待观测到目标值后仅需对样本重新加权，从而以与标准BO相当或更低的决策延迟完成完全贝叶斯优化。

Result: 在合成函数和真实应用中，ELF-BO达到与完全贝叶斯方法相当的性能，而决策延迟与标准BO相当或更优。

Conclusion: ELF-BO使完全贝叶斯优化在实际应用场景中变得可行。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems are ubiquitous across science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions. This objective latency has two consequences during optimization: (i) the objective evaluation dominates execution time, and (ii) sample-efficient algorithms are crucial to accelerate development and avoid wasting resources. Bayesian optimization (BO) methods are the \textit{de facto} choice of planners for suggesting the next point to try. Standard BO fits the surrogate model's hyperparameters with a point estimate. Alternatively, a fully Bayesian approach uses model averaging to account for uncertainty over the hyperparameters, leading to better uncertainty estimates---useful in the low-data regime that is pervasive in BO. However, it is often prohibitively expensive and thus rarely used. In this work, we propose ELF-BO, an algorithm that uses the objective evaluation latency to headstart the computation of the next suggestion, allowing for fully Bayesian optimization without incurring substantial decision-time costs. This is done by sampling from the hyperparameter posterior \emph{while} the objective is being evaluated, only requiring reweighting of the samples once the objective value is observed. Across synthetic functions and real-world applications, we show that ELF-BO matches the performance of fully Bayesian methods while only incurring decision latency on par with or better than standard BO. Thus, ELF-BO makes fully Bayesian optimization practical in real-world use cases.

</details>


### [54] [The Best Optimizer Depends on Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08975)
*Xingyu Dang,Kaiyue Wen,Sadhika Malladi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑自适应优化器"最优选择不随批大小改变"的假设：Muon没有一致的缩放规则，且语言模型预训练中最优优化器会随批大小变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应优化器多设计用于高效估计并利用小批量梯度统计量来调整参数更新，但通常只在单一批大小下进行基准测试。超参数缩放规则声称能随批大小和梯度噪声变化保持性能，从而暗示某一批大小下的最优优化器在其他批大小下依然最优。作者认为这一优化器开发与评估范式需要被检验。

Method: 通过实验检验两类主张：(1) 是否存在适用于Muon的原则性缩放规则；(2) 在广泛超参数调优后，最优优化器是否仍随批大小改变。具体关注梯度统计量的估计与使用，以及批大小变化下的性能保持。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 对于Muon不存在跨训练设置一致的原则性缩放规则；(2) 即便经过大量超参数调优，语言模型预训练的最优优化器仍会随批大小变化。

Conclusion: 批大小是优化器选择的关键变量，不能依赖单一批大小基准或假设缩放规则能保持最优性；优化器的开发与评估应纳入批大小维度。

Abstract: A plethora of new adaptive optimizers are designed to efficiently estimate and use minibatch gradient statistics to shape parameter updates, but they are typically benchmarked at a single batch size. Hyperparameter scaling rules promise to preserve performance as batch size and gradient noise change, suggesting that the best optimizer at one batch size should remain the best at another. We challenge this approach to developing and evaluating optimizers by showing: (1) no principled scaling rule for Muon works consistently across training settings, and (2) the best optimizer for language model pretraining changes with batch size even after extensive hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [55] [SNR-Gated LSTM-Conditioned Diffusion Model for MIMO Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08977)
*Jixing Zhou,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种时序条件扩散信道估计框架，在角域去噪，结合LSTM条件编码、可学习SNR门控后融合捷径与SNR自适应DDIM推理，实现高精度低时延估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MIMO系统在移动场景下信道呈现结构化稀疏与强时间相关性，需高精度且低时延的信道估计；现有扩散类估计方法多基于单快照、未充分利用时间动态，且在高SNR下推理步数多、时延高。

Method: 从LS观测出发，在角域进行去噪；用LSTM在短观测序列上对条件信息编码，捕捉时间动态；引入可学习的SNR门控后融合捷径，通过带可训练中心与尺度的sigmoid门将网络输入注入最终解码阶段，以平衡观测保真与生成先验；采用确定性DDIM式反向更新，并进行SNR自适应截断与步数分配。

Result: 在时间演化的标准化信道模型仿真中，相比现有基于扩散的信道估计基线取得一致的性能增益，并借助SNR自适应推理保持低时延。

Conclusion: 该框架有效融合时序条件扩散与生成先验，兼顾宽SNR范围下的鲁棒性与推理效率，适用于高移动MIMO信道估计。

Abstract: Accurate and low latency channel estimation is critical for modern MIMO systems, particularly under mobility, where channels exhibit structured sparsity and strong temporal correlation. This paper proposes a time-series conditioned diffusion framework for channel estimation that performs denoising in the angular domain. Starting from least squares (LS) observations, we train a diffusion denoiser whose conditioning information is encoded by a long short-term memory (LSTM) network over a short observation sequence, enabling the model to exploit temporal dynamics beyond per-snapshot estimation. To robustly balance observation fidelity and learned generative priors across a wide signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range, we introduce a learnable SNR-gated late-fusion shortcut that injects the network input into the final decoding stage through a sigmoid gate with trainable center and scale. To reduce inference latency, we adopt deterministic denoising diffusion implicit model (DDIM) style reverse updates with SNR-adaptive truncation and step allocation, which significantly reduces the number of reverse diffusion steps at high SNR while maintaining strong performance in low SNR regimes. Simulations on time-evolving standardized channel models demonstrate that the proposed method achieves consistent performance gains over existing diffusion-based channel estimation baselines, while retaining low latency through SNR-adaptive inference.

</details>


### [56] [LASER: Latent Space Adjoint Matching for Support-Constrained Entropy-Regularized Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08989)
*Songyuan Zhang,Oswin So,Eric Yang Yu,Matthew Cleaveland,Peter Crowley-Dolen,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LASER 通过潜在空间伴随匹配实现熵正则化的潜在空间离线 RL，在 40 个 OGBench 任务上取得 SOTA 性能且无需逐任务调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线 RL 受限于分布外（OOD）动作的执行风险。近期方法通过流匹配学习行为克隆策略并在其约束的潜在空间内进行 RL 来缓解该问题。然而，朴素地优化潜在策略容易导致策略坍缩为脆弱的单一模态，或利用学习到的 critic 的尖锐伪影。本文发现熵正则化对解决这些潜在空间 RL 挑战至关重要。

Method: 提出 LASER，一种新型离线 RL 算法：在潜在空间中应用伴随匹配（adjoint matching），在保留富有表现力的流策略的同时实现熵正则化的潜在空间 RL，并避免时间反向传播（BPTT）。

Result: 在 40 个具有不同数据集质量的挑战性 OGBench 任务上进行了全面实验，LASER 取得了最先进性能。值得注意的是，LASER 在所有任务上使用固定的、方法特定的超参数，仍优于包括经过任务和数据集特定调参的基线方法。

Conclusion: 熵正则化是潜在空间 RL 的关键，LASER 通过潜在空间伴随匹配有效实现该目标，展现出稳健的适用性与优越性能。

Abstract: While offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables policy optimization from static datasets without costly online interaction, it remains bottlenecked by the risk of executing out-of-distribution (OOD) actions. Recent approaches mitigate this by learning a behavior-cloning policy through flow matching and then performing RL within its constrained latent space. However, naively optimizing the latent policy can easily cause the policy to collapse into a brittle mode or exploit sharp artifacts of the learned critic. In this work, we find that entropy regularization is essential in latent-space RL for addressing these challenges. We introduce LASER, a novel offline RL algorithm that applies latent-space adjoint matching to achieve entropy-regularized latent-space RL with expressive flow policies while avoiding backpropagation through time. Through comprehensive experiments on 40 challenging OGBench tasks with varying dataset qualities, we show that LASER achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, LASER uses fixed method-specific hyperparameters across all tasks and outperforms the evaluated baselines, including those with task- and dataset-specific tuning, which highlights the robust applicability of LASER. Project website: https://mit-realm.github.io/laser/.

</details>


### [57] [REFIT: Recognize, Fix, and Test Wearable Sensor Placement Shifts without Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08991)
*Bangxun Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFIT是一种针对冻结活动识别模型的输入校准方法，通过轴变换族（如反射和旋转）拟合用户数据并匹配训练数据统计量，实现无标签、无重训练的姿态/佩戴偏移校正，并配合无标签准确率估计决定是否提示用户重新佩戴传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时惯性传感器佩戴方式（如换腕、表带反向重新佩戴）与训练时不同，导致同一动作出现在改变的坐标轴上；现有方法需要标签或重训练，难以在冻结模型上直接纠正该偏移。

Method: 将佩戴偏移表示为由轴变换构成的族（如反射、旋转等），对每个族拟合用户数据，使简单统计量与训练数据匹配；以能最大程度消除不匹配的族作为偏移命名。校正时在冻结模型前应用该族最优成员，并重新估计归一化统计量。随后用无标签准确率估计测试校正后的模型，当估计值低时请求用户重新佩戴。另含对轴的所有带符号置换进行校正，并评估估计能否区分成功/失败校正。

Result: 在真实左右传感器配对以及真实与模拟重新佩戴场景中，REFIT在每个数据集上均优于无标签测试时自适应方法，并恢复了重新佩戴造成的大部分精度损失；在注入偏移命名上比基于置信度的选择器更可靠；在对轴进行所有带符号置换校正后，其估计能区分成功与失败的校正。

Conclusion: REFIT可在不访问标签、不重训练的情况下，对冻结活动识别模型进行佩戴偏移校正，并通过无标签精度估计触发用户干预，在多类真实与模拟场景中优于现有无标签测试时自适应方法。

Abstract: We present REFIT, an input calibration for frozen activity-recognition models whose inertial sensors are worn differently at deployment than in training. When users move a watch to the other wrist or put a strap sensor back on turned, the model sees the same motion on changed axes. REFIT undoes such shifts without labels or retraining. It describes them by families of axis transforms, such as reflections and rotations, and fits each family to the user's data so that simple statistics match those of the training data. The family that removes most of the mismatch names the shift. REFIT fixes the shift by applying the best member of that family before the frozen model and re-estimating its normalization statistics. It tests the fixed model with a label-free accuracy estimate and asks the user to re-wear the sensor when it is low. Experiments on real left/right sensor pairs and on real and simulated re-attachment show that REFIT outperforms label-free test-time adaptation methods on every dataset and restores most of the accuracy lost to re-attachment. It names injected shifts far more reliably than a confidence-based selector. After a correction over all signed permutations of the axes, the estimate separates successful from failed corrections.

</details>


### [58] [An extended deep energy method for thermo-mechanical crack propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09433)
*Han Zhang,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari,Babak Shahbodagh,Mohammad Vahab,Cosmin Anitescu,Timon Rabczuk,Elena Atroshchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出扩展深度能量方法（DEM），用两个神经网络分别表征温度与位移场，通过标量嵌入函数与Williams展开实现尖锐裂纹下的热力耦合裂纹扩展求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经能量求解器已用于相场断裂及尖锐裂纹表征，但尚未同时处理裂纹域上的热传导及其热应力驱动的裂纹扩展。

Method: 以标量嵌入函数将裂纹（保持为尖锐折线）输入网络，使温度与位移可跨裂纹不连续且无需正则化长度；位移在裂尖用Williams展开加可训练幅值进行富集。两场以交错序列分别最小化增量传导泛函与热弹性势能，采用分层蒙特卡洛积分并在裂尖加密、训练中重采样。由交互积分（含Wilson-Yu面积项）提取应力强度因子并用轮廓半径扫描校验；当裂尖温度下kink的能量释放率达临界值时，裂纹沿最大周向应力角扩展。

Result: 静止热边缘裂纹的应力强度因子与文献值吻合至0.11%；功能梯度剪切试验的起裂与独立尖锐裂纹有限元解相差不超过一个载荷步；缺口十字试样在机械、热及复合载荷下的裂纹路径与已发表解一致。
