Conclusion: 该扩展DEM可在无正则化长度下统一求解瞬态热传导、热弹性与尖锐裂纹扩展问题，精度经多类基准算例验证。

Abstract: Thermo-mechanical fracture couples transient heat conduction on a cracked domain with a crack that grows as the temperature and the displacement evolve. Neural energy solvers have been proposed for phase-field fracture and later extended to represent a sharp crack through the network input, but heat conduction on the cracked domain and crack propagation under the resulting thermal stresses have not yet been treated together in these solvers. We present an extended deep energy method for thermo-mechanical crack propagation in which the crack remains a sharp polyline. Two networks represent the temperature and the displacement and receive the crack through a scalar embedding function, discontinuous across the crack and smooth elsewhere, so that both fields can jump across it without a regularization length, and the displacement is enriched near the tip by the Williams expansion with trainable amplitudes. The two fields are obtained by minimizing an incremental conduction functional and the thermoelastic potential energy in a staggered sequence, with Monte Carlo integration on points stratified over background elements, densified near the tip and redrawn during training. The stress intensity factors are extracted by the interaction integral with the area term of Wilson and Yu and checked by a sweep of the contour radius, and the crack advances at the maximum hoop stress angle when the energy release rate of the kink reaches the critical value at the crack-tip temperature. On a stationary thermal edge crack the extracted stress intensity factor agrees with the published value to 0.11%, in a functionally graded shear test initiation agrees with an independent sharp-crack finite element solution to within one load step, and on a notched cruciform specimen the crack paths follow the published solutions under mechanical, thermal and combined loading.

</details>


### [59] [Algorithmic Scratchpads and Curriculum Staging for Arithmetic Reasoning in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09003)
*Sourabh Kasliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了小型Transformer（约10.6M非嵌入参数）在多步算法任务（加减乘除）中的机制，通过训练基础、算法草稿本设计、课程学习等改进，使单位数除法准确率从4.0%提升到86.7%，并揭示了多位数乘法、未见位数泛化及无缓冲训练导致遗忘的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型在确定性多步算法任务（如多位乘法、长除法）上表现不佳，需要理解其内部机制并寻找有效的训练与草稿本设计方法，以提升小模型在算术推理上的能力。

Method: 在合成数据上训练Tiny Transformer（约10.6M非嵌入参数，49.3M总参数），覆盖四种基本运算（+,-,*,/），采用逐步展开的scratchpad；建立训练基础：连续序列打包替代填充、语言预训练、现代架构组件（RoPE、RMSNorm、SwiGLU）及稀疏MoE；设计确定性的Digit-by-Digit Long Division scratchpad，并采用4阶段分层发展课程。

Result: 数据填充导致83%梯度饥饿，准确率从40%崩到1%，连续序列打包可修复；无语言预训练 <= 2.0%；现代架构与MoE显著提升加法推理。Digit-by-Digit Long Division scratchpad将单位数除法从4.0%提升至86.7%（4000题留出基准）。多位乘法仍困难：FOIL scratchpad要求单步同时求和多达九个多位项而无成对中间累积。未见4位操作数性能为0.00%；无缓冲训练导致灾难性遗忘，除法从86.7%降至0.00%。

Conclusion: 多步算术能力高度依赖数据打包、语言预训练、现代架构与草稿本算法设计；确定性逐步草稿本配合分层课程能显著提升除法准确率，但仍存在多位乘法困难、位数外推失败和灾难性遗忘等边界。

Abstract: Autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently struggle with deterministic multi-step algorithmic tasks such as multi-digit multiplication and long division. In this paper, we investigate the mechanics of multi-step arithmetic in compact "Tiny" Transformers (~10.6M non-embedding parameters, 49.3M total) trained on synthetic data across four basic operations (+, -, *, /) unrolled as step-by-step scratchpads. First, we establish the necessary training foundations: (1) dataloader sequence padding creates an 83% gradient starvation artifact that collapses accuracy from 40% to 1%, remediated via continuous sequence packing; (2) linguistic pretraining is an essential prerequisite (<= 2.0% without it); and (3) modern architectural primitives (RoPE, RMSNorm, SwiGLU) and Sparse Mixture of Experts (MoE) substantially improve additive reasoning over baseline GPT-2. Second, we demonstrate that algorithmic scratchpad formulation directly dictates success. Introducing a deterministic Digit-by-Digit Long Division scratchpad within a 4-stage Hierarchical Developmental Curriculum dramatically elevates single-digit division from 4.0% to 86.7% accuracy on a 4,000-problem held-out benchmark. In contrast, multi-digit multiplication remained challenging: detailed error analysis revealed that while the model correctly computed single-digit sub-products and place-value zeros, our FOIL scratchpad failed because it forced a simultaneous summation of up to nine multi-digit terms in a single step without pairwise intermediate accumulation. Finally, we identify two key boundaries: performance collapses to 0.00% on unseen 4-digit operands, and unbuffered training induces catastrophic forgetting, collapsing division accuracy from 86.7% down to 0.00%.

</details>


### [60] [Physics-Informed Neural Plasticity: PDE Solvers That Reshape Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09510)
*Chun-Wun Cheng,Bingcheng Hu,Angelica I. Aviles-Rivero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"物理信息神经可塑性"范式，其实现ReCAP通过动态重构表示容量（局部增强、残差导向分裂、门控剪枝、函数感知合并）求解异构复杂度PDE，在5个3D/4D基准上相对最强基线降低L²误差10.7%–27.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息神经PDE求解器的表示结构在训练中固定不变，难以为物理复杂区域分配足够容量，又在简单区域浪费容量，与解在空间及时空上的强异质复杂度不匹配。

Method: 提出ReCAP：基于高斯局部化的求解器，利用责任加权误差指标与残差能量几何来定位增强位置；通过局部增强、残差导向分裂、门控剪枝与函数感知合并动态重分配容量；并引入quiet-child精炼，通过传输父表示初始化新分量以控制瞬时功能扰动；同时建立条件性后验可靠性与结构稳定性保证。

Result: 在5个3D与4D PDE基准上对比11种物理信息求解器，ReCAP在每个问题均取得最低相对L²误差，较最强对比结果降低10.7%–27.5%。

Conclusion: 物理信息求解器不仅可学习参数，还可学习如何组织其表示容量，动态可塑性表示能有效应对PDE解的异质复杂度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural PDE solvers adapt their parameters to satisfy governing equations, yet their representational structure typically remains fixed throughout training. This rigidity is poorly matched to PDE solutions with strongly heterogeneous complexity across space and space--time, leaving capacity insufficient where the physics is difficult and redundant where it is simple. We introduce physics-informed neural plasticity, a paradigm in which the representation itself reshapes during optimization in response to unresolved physics. We instantiate this principle with Representation Capacity Adaptation for PDEs (ReCAP), a Gaussian-localized solver that dynamically redistributes capacity through local enrichment, residual-directed splitting, gate-based pruning, and function-aware merging. ReCAP uses responsibility-weighted error indicators and the geometry of residual energy to determine where and how to refine. To limit the disturbance introduced by splitting, we introduce quiet-child refinement, which initializes new components by transporting the parent representation while controlling instantaneous functional perturbation. We further establish conditional a posteriori reliability and structural-stability guarantees linking localized physics residuals to solution error and stable refinement. Across five challenging 3D and 4D PDE benchmarks against 11 physics-informed solvers, ReCAP achieves the lowest relative $L^2$ error on every problem, reducing error by $10.7\%$--$27.5\%$ relative to the strongest competing result. These results suggest that physics-informed solvers need not merely learn their parameters---they can learn how their representational capacity should be organized.

</details>


### [61] [Removing Information Content Does Not Certify Tamper Resistance in Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09004)
*Domenic Rosati,Alessa Carbo,Ali Dadsetan,Hong Huang,Matthew Young,Subhabrata Majumdar,Frank Rudzicz,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证仅凭发布时的互信息无法普遍地认证开放权重模型对微调攻击的抵抗性，因为保函数重参数化不改变信息却改变梯度下降几何结构，使不变量证书受限于最快可达的参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"移除有害信息即可使开放权重模型免于微调攻击"这一假设，指出现有基于发布时互信息的不变量认证存在根本性缺口。

Method: 将"保函数重参数化保持信息不变但改变梯度几何"这一原理应用于两类度量：训练数据过滤下的权重-数据互信息，以及能力移除下的标签-表示互信息；并通过显式构造与受控实验验证。

Result: 在权重-数据信息固定时，训练顺序可改变恢复时间；精确的表示级独立性仍可保留完整的参数雅可比矩阵；存在一个显式构造，其两类信息量均为零却能在一步梯度内恢复。受控实验展示了顺序依赖的恢复与参数化依赖的攻击速度。

Conclusion: 认证所缺失的必要条件是对攻击动力学的约束，而非仅对发布时互信息的约束。

Abstract: Does removing harmful information make open-weight models resistant to fine-tuning attacks? We show that mutual information at release alone cannot universally certify slow recovery. Function-preserving reparameterizations leave information unchanged while altering gradient-descent geometry, so an invariant certificate is bounded by the fastest reachable parameterization. We apply this principle to weight--data mutual information under training-data filtering and label--representation mutual information under capability removal. Training order can change recovery time at fixed weight--data information, while exact representation-level independence can preserve the entire parameter Jacobian. An explicit construction has both information quantities equal to zero and recovers in one gradient step. Controlled experiments illustrate order-dependent recovery and parameterization-dependent attack speed. These results identify the missing requirement for certification: constraints on attack dynamics beyond mutual information at release.

</details>


### [62] [CAFE+FNO: Fourier Kernel Generation via Multiplicative Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10105)
*Hyungjoon Juen,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAFE+FNO，将内容感知频率编码+（CAFE+）引入傅里叶核生成，通过并行的Fourier-Chebyshev特征组合建模频率交互，在五个PDE基准上验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO通过傅里叶空间核参数化学习PDE解算子，但频率截断限制了高频变化的学习；AM-FNO和SirenFNO等利用共享网络从谱坐标生成所有网格模式的核，使坐标编码与生成器设计变得关键。近期隐式神经表示（INR）提出通过显式特征组合构建频率交互，而非依赖后续MLP隐式形成。作者受此启发改进傅里叶核生成。

Method: 提出CAFE+FNO，将CAFE+融入傅里叶核生成。CAFE+通过并行仿射分支与Hadamard积组合Fourier–Chebyshev特征，在两族特征内部及跨族间形成交互；随后由核MLP将每个归一化谱坐标的表示映射为复值通道混合矩阵。每层的生成器在所有存储模式间共享，使固定架构下可训练参数量与模式数量无关。

Result: 在五个PDE基准上将CAFE+FNO与现有FNO变体进行对比，并针对基配置、乘法组合以及带宽可学习性开展消融实验。代码与实验配置已开源于GitHub。

Conclusion: CAFE+通过显式频率交互与共享生成器设计增强了FNO的核生成能力，为改进神经算子提供了有效途径，并将方法、基准对比与消融分析完整公开。

Abstract: The Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) learns solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs) through Fourier-space kernel parameterization, but frequency truncation can limit the learning of high-frequency variations. AM-FNO and SirenFNO generate kernels for all grid modes from spectral coordinates using shared networks, making coordinate encoding and generator design important. Recent work on implicit neural representations (INRs) has proposed constructing frequency interactions through explicit feature composition rather than relying on subsequent MLPs to form them implicitly. Building on this approach, we propose CAFE+FNO, which incorporates Content-Aware Frequency Encoding+ (CAFE+) into Fourier kernel generation. CAFE+ combines Fourier--Chebyshev features through parallel affine branches and a Hadamard product, forming interactions within and across the two feature families. A kernel MLP maps the resulting representation of each normalized spectral coordinate to a complex channel-mixing matrix. Each layer shares its generator across all stored modes, making the number of trainable parameters independent of the number of modes for a fixed architecture. We compare CAFE+FNO with existing FNO variants on five PDE benchmarks and conduct ablation studies on basis configuration, multiplicative composition, and bandwidth learnability. Code and experimental configurations are available at https://github.com/fabsk101/CAFEPlusFNO.git.

</details>


### [63] [Neighborhood Smoothing for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09020)
*Idan Horowitz,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将图平滑作为训练时校准的一般原则，通过在表示空间中惩罚近邻样本预测分布的Jensen-Shannon散度来实现，并给出理论边界分析；实验表明该方法提升预测质量，且与事后温度缩放互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络常出现过度自信导致校准不良。现有训练时校准方法主要修改任务损失或校准惩罚项，忽略了学习表示中的邻域结构，未被充分利用。

Method: 引入图平滑作为训练时校准的一般原则，鼓励表示空间中近邻样本具有相似的预测分布。分析其效果，推导边界，将近邻样本间的预测散度与局部置信度变化及逐点校准误差的传播相联系，并刻画平滑能或不能改善校准的条件。据此提出基于图的训练时正则化器（\modelNoSpace），惩罚近邻样本预测分布间的Jensen-Shannon散度。

Result: 在标准校准基准上，方法提升了预测质量；与事后校准互补：经温度缩放后，在8个图像与表格设置中的7个取得所有评估的训练时方法中最低的NLL。

Conclusion: 结果证明在学习表示上进行图平滑对神经网络校准具有价值。

Abstract: Modern neural networks are often miscalibrated, with a tendency to overconfidence. Existing train-time calibration methods largely modify task losses or calibration penalties, leaving neighborhood structure in learned representations underexploited. We introduce graph smoothing as a general principle for train-time calibration, which encourages similar predictive distributions across neighboring samples in representation space. We analyze the effects of graph smoothing, deriving bounds that connect predictive divergence between neighboring samples to local confidence variation and to the propagation of pointwise calibration error, and characterize the conditions under which smoothing can or cannot improve calibration. In light of this analysis, we propose \modelNoSpace, a graph-based train-time regularizer that penalizes the Jensen--Shannon divergence between predictive distributions of neighboring samples. We present a thorough empirical analysis, showing that across standard calibration benchmarks, \model improves predictive quality, and the improvement is complementary to post-hoc calibration: after temperature scaling, \model attains the lowest NLL of all evaluated train-time methods in seven of the eight image and tabular settings. These findings demonstrate the value of graph smoothing over learned representations for neural network calibration.

</details>


### [64] [SPIN: Shadow Predictive Indexer for Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09025)
*Yao Fu,Cyrus Chang,Ritchie Zhao,Bryce Long,Yueying Li,Mahdi Kamani,Samkit Jain,Rahul Raman,Tara Safavi,Shreya Gupta,Parsa Ashrafi Fashi,Minseok Lee,Julien Demouth,Bita Darvish Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SPIN（Shadow Predictive Indexer），用轻量级、基于历史的预测识别重要 KV 块，避免每步解码都对完整 KV 缓存打分，从而降低索引器开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于索引器的稀疏注意力虽只将少量重要 token 送入核心注意力，但索引器本身仍需在每个解码步对整个 KV 缓存打分；随上下文增长，该打分开销成为主要瓶颈。

Method: SPIN 采用轻量级、基于历史的预测来定位重要 KV 块，无需每步对全量 KV 缓存打分；并将 KV 块与投机解码作为一等设计与实现考量，进行协同优化。

Result: 在长上下文与智能体基准测试中，SPIN 在保持任务质量的同时实现 30–40% 稀疏度；在 vLLM 端到端服务中，输出吞吐量最高提升 14.9%，中位 token 间延迟最多降低 13.2%。

Conclusion: SPIN 通过历史预测式索引显著削减索引器开销，在不损失任务质量的前提下提升长上下文推理的效率与服务质量。

Abstract: Indexer-based sparse attention reduces the cost of core attention by passing only a fixed, small number of important tokens to it. However, the indexer must still score the entire KV cache at every decoding step. This scoring overhead becomes a major bottleneck as the context length grows. We propose SPIN (Shadow Predictive Indexer) to reduce this indexer overhead. SPIN uses lightweight, history-based prediction to identify important KV blocks, avoiding the need to score the full KV cache at every decoding step. SPIN treats KV blocks and speculative decoding as first-class design and implementation considerations. Across extensive evaluations on long-context and agentic benchmarks, SPIN achieves 30-40% sparsity while preserving task quality. In end-to-end vLLM serving, SPIN improves output throughput by up to 14.9% and reduces median inter-token latency by up to 13.2%.

</details>


### [65] [FedRSPO+: A Heterogeneity-aware Algorithm for Decision-focused Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09091)
*Konstantinos Ziliaskopoulos,Alexander Vinel,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedRSPO+，一个面向跨孤岛场景的异构感知决策聚焦联邦学习框架，基于正则化代理 RSPO+ 平滑决策映射，并给出决策误差/遗憾上界与跨客户端异构性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策聚焦学习（DFL）多假设数据集中，联邦场景下标准联邦方法只优化预测精度而忽略决策质量，且未处理下游目标与可行域的异构性；多面体问题中微小扰动会导致最优决策不连续变化，破坏客户端更新与聚合的稳定性。

Method: 以 RSPO+（通过投影平滑决策映射的正则化“先预测后优化”代理）为基础，构建异构感知的联邦 DFL 框架 FedRSPO+；采用退火式、模块化训练流程，兼容标准联邦个性化与聚合方法。

Result: 证明 RSPO+ 对正则化决策的决策误差与遗憾具有上界，并在精确正则化与一致 LP 解选择下对原始 LP 决策同样成立；推导出依赖目标异构性与可行域异构性的跨客户端异构性界，该界在同质时消失且不要求强凸性。在合成背包、最短路及真实能源定价任务上，于不同异构度与通信预算下与仅预测的联邦学习和 DFL 基线对比。

Conclusion: 平滑是稳定协同决策学习的有效要素，FedRSPO+ 为联邦 DFL 提供了异构感知的理论与方法基础。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning (DFL) trains predictive models for downstream optimization, but existing methods largely assume centralized data. In cross-silo settings, federated learning offers a natural alternative, yet standard federated methods optimize prediction over decision quality and do not address heterogeneity in downstream objectives or feasible sets. This heterogeneity is especially challenging for DFL because small perturbations in polyhedral problems can cause discontinuous changes in optimal decisions, destabilizing client updates and aggregation. We propose FedRSPO+, a heterogeneity-aware framework for decision-focused federated learning, built on RSPO+, a regularized predict-then-optimize surrogate that smooths the decision map through projection. We show that RSPO+ upper bounds decision error and regret for the regularized decision and, under exact regularization and consistent LP solution selection, for the original LP decision. We further derive cross-client heterogeneity bounds that depend on both objective and feasible-set heterogeneity, vanish at homogeneity, and require no strong convexity. FedRSPO+ uses an annealed, modular training procedure compatible with standard federated personalization and aggregation methods. Experiments on synthetic knapsack, shortest-path, and real-world energy pricing tasks compare against prediction-only federated learning and DFL baselines under varying heterogeneity and communication budgets. Results suggest that smoothing is a useful ingredient for stable collaborative decision learning and provide a heterogeneity-aware foundation for federated DFL.

</details>


### [66] [ORACLE: Optimizer-Relative Alignment for Constrained LEarning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09040)
*Utkarsh Grover,Wyatt Mackey,Kaixun Hua,J. Morris Chang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"优化器相对约束学习"框架，将约束兼容性评估放在优化器更新之后，并据此设计 ORACLE 方法；在 8 个 PDE 基准、4 类优化器上，94% 配置不劣于原生优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束处理方法通常在优化器作用之前介入，通过修改目标函数或梯度来实现约束满足。但动量、自适应缩放与结构化预条件会在信号变为参数更新之前显著重塑该信号，导致"更新前"的约束处理与实际参数更新不一致，约束兼容性评估失真。

Method: 提出优化器相对约束学习（optimizer relative constrained learning），在优化器更新之后评估约束兼容性。基于该视角提出 ORACLE：对原生优化器实际产生的步长，通过异构约束族的联合端点线性化进行评估；在优化器自身几何中构造对齐方向；对其权限（authority）进行界定；仅在验证通过后提交该修正。

Result: 在 8 个偏微分方程（PDE）基准和 4 种优化器（覆盖欧氏、对角自适应、结构化预条件几何）上评估。ORACLE 在 94% 的配置中优于或持平原生优化器；跨模型分析中 92% 的配置表现一致；在匹配比较中，相较在目标层、梯度层以及优化器后层介入的替代约束处理方法均有提升。

Conclusion: 约束处理应针对优化器实际实现的更新而非更新前的信号进行，ORACLE 通过在优化器几何中构造并验证约束对齐，可在多种优化器几何与 PDE 任务中稳定改善或保持原生性能，优于目标层、梯度层与优化器后层的替代方法。

Abstract: Constraint handling methods typically intervene before the optimizer acts, by modifying the objective or the gradient. Yet momentum, adaptive scaling, and structured preconditioning can substantially reshape that signal before it becomes a parameter update. We formulate optimizer relative constrained learning, where constraint compatibility is assessed on the post optimizer update. Building on this view, we introduce ORACLE, which evaluates the native optimizer's realized step through a joint endpoint linearization of heterogeneous constraint families, constructs the resulting alignment in the optimizer's own geometry, bounds its authority, and commits it only after validation. We evaluate ORACLE across eight Partial Differential Equation benchmarks and four optimizers spanning Euclidean, diagonal adaptive, and structured preconditioned geometries, where it improves or matches native optimizer in 94% of configurations. Cross model analysis shows the same behavior in 92% of configurations, while matched comparisons show improvements over alternative constraint-handling methods acting at the objective, gradient, and post-optimizer levels.

</details>


### [67] [Global Exponential Convergence of Two-Layer Linear Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09356)
*Stephen Y Zhang,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明了宽两层线性网络在光滑PL型预测损失下以显式速率全局指数（线性）收敛，通过对神经元律协方差的有限维Bures流刻画梯度流，并给出有限宽度、动量及深线性ResNet的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽网络训练动力学的理论收敛保证常缺乏显式速率，且对奇异初始化、动量优化和深层结构的刻画不足；本文旨在给出带显式速率的全局线性收敛结果并推广到多种设定。

Method: 将因子梯度流精确闭合为神经元律协方差的有限维Bures流，预测器动态由隐协方差块预条件；用平均场守恒律在初始协方差满足谱支撑间隙条件时给出隐预条件块的一致谱下界；进一步分析有限宽度采样、因子梯度下降、深线性ResNet（受残差路径界约束）以及heavy-ball动量（在提升相位协方差上闭合）等情形。

Result: 对初始协方差Σ₀=σ²Id，损失以至少4σ²κ的线性速率收敛到全局最小（κ为PL常数）；谱支撑间隙条件涵盖正定性并允许奇异初始化；证明该速率在有限宽度采样下的稳定性，并给出因子梯度下降的显式步长区间（依赖光滑性、初始损失与守恒谱裕度）；动量情形下线性收敛在能量与阻尼的显式条件下成立并给出可行阻尼窗口，双尺度白初始化下该区间在位置尺度足够大时非空；数值实验验证协方差几何并比较预测与观测速率。

Conclusion: 该工作通过Bures流与平均场守恒律为宽线性网络提供了显式全局线性收敛率，并一致地扩展到有限宽度、离散梯度下降、深线性ResNet与动量优化，数值实验支持理论预测。

Abstract: We prove global exponential (linear) convergence with an explicit rate in the rich scaling for wide two-layer linear networks trained with smooth Polyak-Lojasiewicz predictor losses. Gradient flow in the factors closes exactly in terms of a finite-dimensional Bures flow of the neuron law covariance, in which the predictor dynamics are preconditioned by hidden covariance blocks. Mean-field conservation laws provide uniform spectral lower bounds on the hidden preconditioning blocks when the initial covariance satisfies a spectral support gap condition. This condition encompasses positive definiteness while still allowing for singular initializations. For an initial covariance $Σ_0 = σ^2 \mathrm{Id}$, the loss converges to the global minimum with linear rate at least $4σ^2κ$, where $κ$ is the PL constant. We establish stability of this rate under finite-width sampling, as well as global convergence of factor gradient descent for an explicit stepsize interval depending on smoothness, the initial loss, and conserved spectral margins. Our argument extends layerwise to deep linear ResNets, subject to a residual-path bound. In the case of heavy-ball momentum, training dynamics close instead over positions and velocities in terms of a lifted phase covariance. Linear convergence holds under an explicit condition on the energy and damping, specifying a window of admissible dampings. For two-scale white initializations, this interval is nonempty for sufficiently large position scales, with a fixed initial loss gap and velocity covariance. Numerical experiments illustrate the covariance geometry and compare the predicted and observed rates.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Financial World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09048)
*Humzah Merchant,Alec Guthrie,Simon Mahns,Randall Balestriero,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Market-1T数据集(2008-2025年美国股票、1Hz、近万亿观测)，建立严格评估协议，并对18种编码器训练策略进行大规模系统性研究，发现预测性能相近的编码器对市场状态的组织方式可能截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建世界模型需要可用于规划和决策的状态表征，且可能涉及训练时未知的任务。在金融市场情境下，这要求模型能推理市场整体状况、资产特定预期收益、流动性、波动率及跨资产关系。然而既有的金融表征学习多只在单个预测任务、单一时间段的较窄数据集上评估，缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 三项主要贡献：(1) 构建Market-1T数据集，涵盖2008至2025年美国股票、1Hz分辨率、近万亿观测；(2) 设计并实现严格的评估协议；(3) 开展大规模系统性研究，在近二十年不同市场制度下比较18种编码器训练策略。评估方式包括常见金融任务上的预测效用，以及对潜在结构的探针分析。

Result: 在近二十年市场制度下对18种编码器训练策略的系统比较表明：预测性能相近的编码器可能以非常不同的方式组织市场状态。数据集与评估协议为金融表征学习提供了统一的大规模测试基础。

Conclusion: 该工作为训练与评估金融市场表征奠定了基础，以支撑世界模型(如DINO-WM、V-JEPA 2、LeWM)的构建。其核心启示是：仅凭预测性能不足以刻画表征质量，潜在结构的组织方式同样是关键评估维度。

Abstract: Building a world model requires a state representation useful for planning and decision-making---potentially over tasks unknown at training time. In the context of financial markets, planning and decision-making may require a model to reason about market-wide conditions, asset-specific expected returns, liquidity, volatility, and cross-asset relationships. Yet financial representation learning has largely been evaluated on individual predictive tasks, oftentimes on a single time period using comparatively narrow datasets. We address this through three primary contributions. First, we introduce Market-1T, a dataset containing nearly one trillion observations across U.S. equities from 2008 to 2025 at 1 Hz resolution. Second, we develop and implement a rigorous evaluation protocol. Third, we conduct a systematic large-scale study of financial representation learning, comparing 18 encoder-training strategies across nearly two decades of market regimes. We evaluate learned representations both by their predictive utility on common finance tasks and through probes of latent structure. We find that encoders with similar predictive performance can organize market state very differently. Collectively, we establish a foundation for training and evaluating financial market representations in support of world models such as DINO-WM, V-JEPA 2, and LeWM.

</details>


### [69] [Identifiability of a dissipative knowledge-dynamics model: exact recovery under designed excitation, degeneration on observational data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09889)
*Arman Kostanian,Armen Beklaryan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将人类学习建模为非线性耗散常微分方程组，参数具有机制意义（概念迁移矩阵、逐概念遗忘率、饱和练习响应增益），并研究其逆问题的结构可辨识性。在显式激励条件下证明了可辨识定理并给出双概念情形的闭式恢复，同时提出L稳定的半隐式数值格式与批处理求解器（比特精确、梯度达1e-10、快两个数量级，可支持10^5量级学习者）。合成数据下恢复精确（参数达机器精度、先决结构F_1=1.0），但在大规模观测基准上失效；四个独立控制实验表明看似强的恢复源于模型平稳结构导致的退化，即定理预测的无激励情形不可辨识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类学习是耗散动力学过程：掌握随练习积累、随遗忘衰减、并沿相互依赖的概念传播。作者希望建立一个参数具有机制可解释性的学习动力学模型，并回答核心问题：这些机制参数在什么条件下能从数据中被真正恢复（辨识），从而为可解释性主张提供验证边界与协议。

Method: 构建非线性耗散ODE系统：概念迁移矩阵（编码先决耦合）、逐概念遗忘率、饱和练习响应增益。针对逆问题，在显式激励条件下证明结构可辨识性定理，给出双概念情形的构造性闭式恢复，并证明单调性、鲁棒性与L稳定性。数值上提出耗散子系统的半隐式L稳定格式与批处理求解器。实证采用双侧设计：合成数据验证定理条件；大规模观测基准上设置四个独立控制（破坏时间顺序、经典贝叶斯基线、移除真实时间戳等）检验恢复的真实性。

Result: 在定理的激励条件下，合成恢复是精确的：参数达机器精度，先决结构F_1=1.0。但在大规模观测基准上并非如此：一个表面很强的恢复——遗忘率与主题难度的Spearman相关达ρ=0.83——被四个独立控制推翻：该相关在破坏数据时间顺序后仍然存在、被经典贝叶斯基线匹配、且移除真实时间戳后不受影响。作者将其归因于模型的平稳结构，证明这正是定理在缺乏设计激励时所预测的退化现象。

Conclusion: 研究划定了可辨识与不可辨识区域之间的清晰边界，并给出面向可解释性主张的验证协议：只有在满足激励条件时机制参数的恢复才可信，否则表面相关性可能只是模型平稳结构导致的退化假象。

Abstract: Human learning is a dissipative dynamical process: mastery accumulates through practice, decays through forgetting, and propagates across interdependent concepts. We model it as a nonlinear dissipative system of ordinary differential equations whose parameters are mechanistically meaningful (a concept-transfer matrix encoding prerequisite coupling, per-concept forgetting rates, and a saturating practice-response gain), and we study when those parameters can actually be recovered from data. We prove a structural identifiability theorem for the associated inverse problem under explicit excitation conditions, with constructive closed-form recovery for the two-concept case, together with monotonicity, robustness and L-stability results. We derive a semi-implicit L-stable scheme for the dissipative subsystem and a batched solver numerically equivalent to the per-trajectory formulation (bit-exact predictions, gradients to $10^{-10}$) yet two orders of magnitude faster, making estimation feasible on cohorts of $10^5$ learners. The empirical study is two-sided. Under the theorem's excitation conditions, synthetic recovery is exact: parameters to machine precision, prerequisite structure at $F_1 = 1.0$. On large observational benchmarks it is not. An apparently strong recovery, with forgetting rates correlating with topic difficulty at Spearman $ρ= 0.83$, is refuted by four independent controls: it survives destroying the temporal order of the data, is matched by a classical Bayesian baseline, and is unaffected by removing real timestamps. We trace this to the stationary structure of the model and show that it is the degeneration the theorem predicts in the absence of designed excitation. The result delineates a sharp boundary between identifiable and unidentifiable regimes and yields a validation protocol for interpretability claims.

</details>


### [70] [A Self-Pruning Transformer: Extreme KV-Cache Compression with Universal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09051)
*Davis Wertheimer,Haochen Shen,Ahan Gupta,Derrick Liu,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Mudhakar Srivatsa,Raghu K. Ganti,Minjia Zhang,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Universal Attention，一种统一框架，将多种互补的新型衰减机制与RoPE嵌入及Softmax注意力相结合，实现端到端可训练的高表达性注意力架构，通过自适应剪枝在自然语言与合成任务上达10倍KV-cache压缩，长上下文16k长度下压缩达25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM的KV-cache规模庞大，阻碍高效部署。已有工作用基于衰减的机制替换RoPE位置嵌入以便在推理时剪枝KV-cache，但这类衰减函数表达性有限，实际退化为类滑动窗口的驱逐模式。

Method: 提出统一的互补衰减机制框架，能够刻画复杂的键统计与交互，同时保留表达性强的RoPE嵌入与Softmax注意力；由此构建Universal Attention——高表达性、端到端可训练，其复合衰减机制作为天然的自适应剪枝准则，剔除对注意力计算贡献最小的token。

Result: 在自然语言和合成任务数据上实现最先进的10倍压缩，下游性能优于最先进基线和未剪枝oracle；在16k长度上展现优越的长上下文泛化能力，压缩率达25倍。

Conclusion: 该统一框架克服了现有衰减机制表达性不足的问题，将衰减机制转化为自适应KV-cache剪枝准则，在保持甚至提升性能的同时实现高倍压缩，并对长上下文具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: The large KV-cache size of modern LLMs creates a barrier to efficient deployment. Recent work has explored replacing attention layers' RoPE positional embeddings with alternative decay-based mechanisms, which can then be used to prune KV-cache during inference. However, these decay functions have limited expressivity, and in practice devolve into sliding-window-like eviction patterns. In this work, we propose a unifying framework for complementary and novel decay mechanisms, capturing complex key statistics and interactions while preserving expressive RoPE embeddings and Softmax attention. The resulting Universal Attention is a highly expressive and end-to-end trainable architecture, whose composite decay mechanism acts as a natural, $\textit{adaptive}$ pruning criterion, removing tokens that contribute least to attention computation. Experimentally, Universal Attention achieves state-of-the-art $10\times$ compression on natural language and synthetic task data, while $\textit{improving}$ downstream performance compared to both state-of-the-art baselines and unpruned oracles. It further demonstrates superior long-context generalization with unprecedented $25\times$ compression at length 16k.

</details>


### [71] [Eigenvalues of the Hessian in Deep Learning: The Origin of Symmetry and Its Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09919)
*Yossi Arjevani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将训练后深度网络Hessian谱的“大块近零+少数离群值”聚类现象，归因于对某个隐藏的高对称参考构型的偏离：参考构型处Hessian具有不由权值对称性解释的丰富不变性，从而迫使高维核与大重数特征值；回到原构型时对称性被破缺，产生所观察到的簇与离群层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型训练后的Hessian谱存在稳定模式（近零大块与孤立离群值），但缺乏统一解释。作者希望找到一个自然机制，说明这些谱结构并非偶然，而是与某个邻近的、高度对称的隐藏参考构型相关。

Method: 提出一般性框架：通过修改架构、数据分布或参数度量，暴露邻近参考构型，其Hessian具有丰富不变性；用对称性精确刻画谱，导出高维核与大重数特征值。对三层ReLU网络做详细分析，并推广到卷积、图、Transformer模型及NTK；进一步分析逐层Hessian与Gauss-Newton矩阵。

Result: 在该框架下，参考构型的Hessian对称性可精确描述谱结构，产生高维核和大重数特征值；偏离参考构型后对称性破缺，形成近零大块与少数离群值的层次。该机制在多种模型（卷积、图、Transformer、NTK）以及逐层Hessian和Gauss-Newton矩阵中均呈现类似谱结构。

Conclusion: 训练后深度网络Hessian谱的聚类与离群现象可由“隐藏高对称参考构型的偏离”统一解释，且该解释具有跨架构与跨矩阵类型的普适性。

Abstract: Hessian spectra at trained models in deep learning exhibit a persistent pattern: eigenvalues organize into distinct clusters, including a large bulk near zero and a few isolated outliers. This paper shows that a natural account of these spectral phenomena emerges when the original setting is understood as a departure from a nearby, otherwise hidden, highly symmetric reference.
  Modifications, including changes to the architecture, data distribution, or parameter metric, expose a nearby reference configuration whose Hessian exhibits rich invariances-ones not accounted for by weight symmetries. There, symmetry enables a precise description of the spectra, forcing high-dimensional kernels and eigenvalues of large multiplicity. Returning to the original configuration breaks the Hessian symmetry and thereby produces the observed hierarchy of clusters and outliers.
  The framework is developed in some generality, with a detailed analysis of three-layer ReLU networks and applications to convolutional, graph, and transformer models, as well as to the NTK. The same mechanism is further shown to yield analogous spectral structures in layerwise Hessians and the Gauss-Newton matrix.

</details>


### [72] [BeatFlow-ECG: Rectified Flow for ECG Reconstruction from Indirect Wearable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09052)
*Mohamed Kamel,Sahar Selim,Walaa Medhat,Tamer Nadeem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BeatFlow-ECG，一种条件整流流模型，从同步PPG与IMU信号重建单通道ECG，在两个数据集上取得最优波形与节拍时序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床外连续心脏监测需兼顾信息量与日常可采集性：ECG信息丰富但难以连续获取，PPG易连续采集但为间接测量且对运动敏感。因此需从PPG重建ECG。

Method: 将重建建模为从噪声到ECG的条件传输，采用卷积编解码器+Transformer瓶颈及显式流时间条件；通过IMU条件特征、运动相关损失加权与由易到难训练课程引入运动信息。

Result: 在PPG-DaLiA与WESAD的留一被试协议下全面优于确定性、对抗及扩散基线；Pearson相关系数0.983与0.986，R峰F1为0.946与0.955；相较Conditional DDPM-1D，L1误差降至0.062与0.055。固定R峰相对波形区域相关性更高，低/中/高运动子集重建误差均更低。

Conclusion: BeatFlow-ECG验证了条件整流流在PPG→ECG重建中的有效性，并显示其在运动鲁棒性上的优势。

Abstract: Continuous cardiac monitoring outside clinical settings requires signals that are both informative and practical to collect during daily life. Electrocardiography (ECG) provides rich information about cardiac rhythm and waveform morphology, while wearable photoplethysmography (PPG) is easier to acquire continuously but is only an indirect cardiovascular measurement and is highly sensitive to motion. We present BeatFlow-ECG, a conditional rectified-flow model for reconstructing single-channel ECG from synchronized PPG and inertial measurements. BeatFlow-ECG models reconstruction as conditional transport from noise to ECG using a convolutional encoder-decoder with a transformer bottleneck and explicit flow-time conditioning. Motion information is incorporated through IMU-derived conditioning features, motion-dependent loss weighting, and an easy-to-hard training curriculum. We evaluate the model under leave-one-subject-out protocols on PPG-DaLiA and WESAD. BeatFlow-ECG achieves the best results among the evaluated deterministic, adversarial, and diffusion-based baselines across all reported waveform and beat-timing metrics, with Pearson correlations of 0.983 and 0.986 and R-peak F1 scores of 0.946 and 0.955, respectively. Compared with Conditional DDPM-1D, L1 error decreases from 0.085 to 0.062 on PPG-DaLiA and from 0.074 to 0.055 on WESAD. Additional analyses on PPG-DaLiA show higher correlation in fixed R-peak-relative waveform regions and lower reconstruction error across low-, medium-, and high-motion subsets.

</details>


### [73] [Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning for Multichain MDPs: A Hierarchical Decomposition Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10326)
*Huizhen Yu,Isaiah Heidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究平均奖励多链MDP的最优策略学习，提出基于异步值迭代的无模型RL算法，利用Bather分解分层划分状态空间，并给出三种算法的几乎必然收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多链MDP中，最优增益可能依赖初始状态，且不同策略下的循环结构不同，这给强化学习带来挑战。既有方法通常需归约为折扣问题，缺乏真正无模型的平均奖励多链RL算法。

Method: 提出基于异步值迭代的RL算法，仅需MDP转移图知识，借助Bather分解将状态空间分为通信子系统与瞬态状态，并将全局决策问题重铸为结构化子问题。在此基础上发展两个算法：一个近似求解多链平均最优方程以获得近增益最优策略；另一个近似最优偏差函数，并利用基础算法求解诱导的平均奖励多链MDP。

Result: 基础算法收敛到最优增益并在有限时间内输出增益最优策略；三个算法均具几乎必然收敛保证。实验比较表明，后两个算法相对基础算法能持续改善瞬态性能。

Conclusion: 提出了首批不归约为折扣问题的、面向一般多链MDP的近乎无模型平均奖励RL算法，并分析了增益最优与近偏差最优的性能权衡。

Abstract: We study learning optimal policies in average-reward multichain Markov decision processes (MDPs), where the optimal gain may depend on the initial state and recurrence structures vary across policies, creating challenges for reinforcement learning (RL) methods. We propose an asynchronous value-iteration-based RL algorithm that requires no model knowledge beyond the MDP's transition graph and leverages Bather's decomposition to hierarchically partition the state space into communicating subsystems and transient states. This decomposition induces a recasting of the global decision problem into structured subproblems, which our algorithm exploits. We show that the algorithm converges to the optimal gain and produces gain-optimal policies after finite time. Building on this base algorithm, we develop two further algorithms: one approximately solves the multichain average optimality equations to obtain near gain-optimal policies, and another targets near bias-optimality by approximating the optimal bias function and solving an induced average-reward multichain MDP using the base algorithm. We provide almost-sure convergence guarantees for all three algorithms and empirically compare their tradeoffs, showing that the latter two also consistently improve transient performance relative to the base algorithm. To our knowledge, these are the first essentially model-free average-reward RL algorithms for general multichain MDPs without reductions to discounted problems.

</details>


### [74] [A Geometry-Based Capacity Theory for Finite-Feature Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09056)
*Jianhai Zhang,Donghao Zhang,Pattarawut Charatpangoon,Bijoy Menon,M. Ethan MacDonald,Wu Qiu,Aravind Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种基于几何的容量理论，用于压缩有限特征Hebbian联想记忆中的精确键检索，将表征几何与记忆容量直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解联想记忆中表征几何如何决定记忆容量，特别是区分哪些性能瓶颈可通过增加特征预算改善、哪些必须改变表征本身，是核心动机。

Method: 将检索干扰分解为随特征维度下降的有限特征噪声与由键间核重叠平方决定、在无限特征极限下持续存在的结构干扰；对相关值情形，引入键核与值Gram矩阵的联合作用，推导有限特征近似。

Result: 理论给出无需拟合的检索质量预测、几何相关的容量上限及达成目标检索质量所需的特征预算；在合成、视觉与医学图像表征上验证，预测的检索曲线与经验行为高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该框架将表征几何直接与记忆容量挂钩，并正确识别偏好记忆设计的变化，明确了何时增加特征预算有效、何时需更换表征。

Abstract: We develop a geometry-based capacity theory for exact-key retrieval in compressed finite-feature Hebbian associative memory. For random or approximately isotropic values, retrieval interference separates into finite-feature noise, which decreases with feature dimension, and structural interference, which is determined by squared kernel overlap among stored keys and persists in the infinite-feature limit. This yields a fit-free prediction of retrieval quality, reveals a geometry-dependent capacity ceiling, and predicts the feature budget required for a target retrieval quality. When stored values are correlated, we show that retrieval depends jointly on the key kernel and value Gram matrix, and derive finite-feature approximations that account for this interaction. We validate the theory on synthetic, visual, and medical-image representations. Overall, the framework links representation geometry directly to memory capacity and distinguishes when performance can be improved by increasing the feature budget and when the representation itself must be changed. Across these settings, the predicted retrieval curves closely match empirical behavior and correctly identify changes in the preferred memory design.

</details>


### [75] [Learning Cross-Model Activation Alignments with Explicit Many-to-Many Layer Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09058)
*Alina Sudakov,Guy Bar-Shalom,Fabrizio Frasca,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MATCHA，通过联合学习层对应关系与层共享特征映射，实现不同LLM之间激活空间的对齐，并支持激活干预的跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM发布速度极快，但两个独立训练的模型之间在层对应关系与特征变换方式上的关系尚不明确。已有工作大多预先固定层对应（按相对深度粗略配对），缺乏对层间映射的可学习、可解释建模。

Method: MATCHA学习一个从源模型逐层激活到目标模型激活的对齐映射，并将其分解为两部分：(1) 层映射，输出可提取、可检查的target-by-source显式矩阵；(2) 隐空间之间的层共享特征映射。两部分均从prompt中联合学习，而非预先固定层对应。

Result: 在三个模型族、七个模型构成的42个模型对上，MATCHA比先前方法更忠实地重建目标激活，并显著提升检索类指标。恢复的映射在深度上总体单调，但呈现一致的多对多结构——每个目标层对应源模型的一个层带。对齐结果还支持激活空间干预的迁移，使为一个模型开发的steering vector与probe可迁移至另一模型。

Conclusion: 跨独立训练LLM的激活对齐可以被联合学习，且其层对应本质上是多对多的；该对齐不仅提升激活重建与检索指标，还打通了激活干预的跨模型迁移路径。

Abstract: LLMs are released at a rapid pace, raising a natural question: how do two independently trained models relate, both in which layers correspond and in how features transform between them? We study this by learning an activation alignment, a map from a source model's layerwise activations to a target's. Our method, MATCHA, factors this map into a layer map, whose output is an explicit target-by-source matrix that can be extracted and inspected, and a layer-shared feature map between hidden spaces. Most of prior work fixes the layer correspondence in advance, pairing layers at roughly the same relative depth; in contrast, we learn both factors jointly from prompts. Across 42 pairs of seven models spanning three different families, MATCHA reconstructs the target's activations more faithfully and improves retrieval-based metrics substantially, w.r.t. previous approaches. The recovered maps are broadly monotone in depth but, in contrast with most previous approaches, are consistently many-to-many: each target layer draws on a band of source layers. Our alignments also enable transfer of activation-space interventions, allowing steering vectors and probes developed for one model to transfer to another.

</details>


### [76] [EDiS: Edge Disjoint Subgraph Sparsification Framework for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09059)
*Sai Karthik Navuluru,Siddhartha Shankar Das,Franck Dernoncourt,S M Ferdous,Ryan A. Rossi,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Baris Coskunuzer,Alex Pothen,Lakshman Tamil,Mahantesh M Halappanavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDiS是一个边不相交子图稀疏化框架：一次性将图分解为可缓存的边不相交子图，再按边预算跨epoch重组训练图，无需重复提取结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏GNN训练可降低计算量，但选择保留哪些边代价高。复用单一稀疏图虽廉价却将训练锁定在固定拓扑，而每epoch改变拓扑又需反复采样或重计算。作者希望在低成本与拓扑多样性之间取得平衡。

Method: EDiS将结构提取与每epoch图组合分离：先一次性把图分解为可缓存的边不相交子图，再在边预算约束与保留比例下跨epoch重组为训练图，无需重新提取结构。默认构造使用基于特征的分数和连续最大分数覆盖森林；同一组合机制也支持其他边选择规则。作者对每epoch采样器（从缓存分解中抽取训练图的组合步骤）进行了组合分析。

Result: 在默认覆盖森林选择器下，存储的分解确定性地保留高分割边；并推导出与选择器无关的条件界，刻画组合训练图中高分割边的存活率。在19个同质、异质及大规模节点分类基准上，与17个基线在相同边预算下比较，EDiS取得最高平均基准分数（accuracy/ROC-AUC）以及最低平均排名和与最优的差距。消融实验表明，结构分解与epoch变化在紧边预算下收益最明显。

Conclusion: EDiS通过结构提取与图组合的分离，在不重复提取结构的前提下实现逐epoch拓扑变化，兼顾低开销与稀疏训练效果，并在多类基准上优于既有方法。

Abstract: Sparse GNN training reduces computation, but deciding which edges to keep can be costly. Reusing one sparse graph is cheap, but locks training to a fixed topology, while varying it across epochs can require repeated sampling or recomputation. We introduce EDiS (Edge-Disjoint Subgraph sparsification framework), which separates one-time structural extraction from per-epoch graph composition. EDiS decomposes the graph once into cacheable edge-disjoint subgraphs, then recombines them into graphs with edge-budget constraints across epochs and retention ratios without re-extracting structure. Our default construction uses feature-based scores and successive maximum score covering forests, while the same composition mechanism also supports alternative edge selection rules. We provide a combinatorial analysis of the per-epoch sampler, the composition step that draws a training graph from the cached decomposition. We show that, under the default covering-forest selector, the stored decomposition deterministically preserves high-score cut edges, and we derive a selector-agnostic conditional bound on high-score cut survival in composed training graphs. Across 19 homophilic, heterophilic, and large-scale node classification benchmarks against 17 baselines under the same edge budget, EDiS achieves the highest mean benchmark score (accuracy/ROC-AUC) and the lowest average rank and gap-to-best among ranked methods. Ablations show the clearest benefits of structural decomposition and epoch variation at tight edge budgets.

</details>


### [77] [Exact Dynamics and Finite-Sample Trajectory Recovery of Linear Recursive Feature Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09196)
*Andrew Cheng,Bobak T. Kiani,Yue M. Lu,Adityanarayanan Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将线性递归特征机（RFM）从插值设定推广到含噪岭正则化多输出回归，证明每步迭代的特征矩阵以高概率逼近其无限数据理想形式，误差衰减率为 \(O(\sqrt{d/n})\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGOP 与神经网络特征学习存在联系，线性 RFM 为分析表示在训练中如何演化提供了简化设定，但已有关于线性 RFM 与迭代重加权最小二乘的联系仅限插值情形，缺乏对带噪、正则化回归的刻画。

Method: 研究各向同性亚高斯输入、由 \(d\) 维低秩教师矩阵生成目标的多输出含噪回归；将线性 RFM 与迭代重加权最小二乘的联系扩展到岭正则化情形，并分析特征矩阵相对无限数据理想对应物的逐迭代偏差。

Result: 对 \(n\) 个样本，证明特征矩阵误差以高概率按 \(O(\sqrt{d/n})\) 衰减；并在真实文本与单细胞基因表达数据上展示该线性模型学到的特征。

Conclusion: 线性 RFM 在含噪岭正则化多输出回归中具有良好的统计稳定性，其特征学习动态可被理论刻画并得到有限样本保证。

Abstract: Recursive feature machines (RFMs) learn representations of data by alternating between fitting a predictor to a dataset and updating features of that predictor using the average gradient outer product (AGOP). Connections between AGOPs and feature learning in neural networks motivate linear RFMs as a simple setting for analyzing how representations evolve during training. Here, we study the dynamics and statistics of linear RFM in noisy multi-output regression with isotropic sub-Gaussian input data and targets generated by a low-rank teacher matrix of dimension $d$. We extend the known connection between linear RFM and iteratively reweighted least squares from the interpolating setting to ridge-regularized multi-output regression with noise. We show that the learned feature matrix remains close to its infinite-data ideal counterpart at every iteration. Namely, for $n$ samples, we show the error in the feature matrix decays as $O(\sqrt{d/n})$ with high probability. Experiments on real-world text and single-cell gene-expression data illustrate the features learned by this simple linear model.

</details>


### [78] [Multi-Label Topic Assignment via LLM Distillation: A Comparative Analysis of Generative vs. Discriminative Student Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09063)
*Sourabh Kasliwal,Shubhranshu Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM蒸馏后学生模型在多标签主题分类（电商UGC）中参数量（1B/4B/8B）与架构范式（因果生成式 vs 双向判别式）的选择，发现效果取决于数据类型：结构化评论上判别式更优，复杂多轮对话上最小1B生成式即超越判别式基线，并在大规模标签集与长尾分布下更稳健（判别式Macro-F1下降35%），最终完成大规模生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商场景下，用户生成内容（产品评论与买卖对话）的多标签主题标注面临语言非正式、标签极度稀疏、分类体系快速演化的可扩展性挑战。业界常用LLM作为标注预言机蒸馏训练数据以规避高昂人工标注成本，但蒸馏所得学生模型应采用何种低延迟最优架构尚无定论，该问题亟待系统性研究。

Method: 构建基于LLM标注预言机的蒸馏数据，开展跨规模与跨范式的对比实验：小语言模型参数量覆盖1B、4B、8B；架构涵盖因果生成式（text-to-label生成分类器）与双向判别式（DeBERTa-V3、ModernBERT基线）。在结构化产品评论与复杂多轮对话两类数据上评测，并考察标签集扩展（至112个主题）与严重长尾分布下的表现。

Result: 呈现数据依赖的关键权衡：在结构化产品评论上判别式模型优于超轻量生成式模型；但在复杂多轮对话数据上，最小的1B生成式模型即超过判别式基线。在标签集大幅扩展（至112主题）与严重长尾分布下，生成式模型性能保持稳健，而判别式基线在规模扩展时Macro-F1下降约35%。

Conclusion: 研究明确了多标签主题分类中学生模型的规模与架构选择应依数据类型与标签体系复杂度而定，生成式范式在复杂对话与大规模长尾场景更具优势。所优化模型已在产品评论与对话两类域成功落地生产，满足严格延迟要求并在全球电商规模化场景中产生实际业务价值。

Abstract: Multi-label topic assignment for user-generated content (UGC) -- including product reviews and buyer-seller conversations -- poses unique scalability challenges in large-scale e-commerce due to informal language, extreme label sparsity, and rapidly evolving taxonomies. While utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) as labeling oracles to distill ground-truth data has emerged as an industry standard to bypass prohibitive manual annotation costs, determining the optimal, low-latency architecture for the resulting student models remains an open challenge. To address this, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation across Small Language Model (SLM) parameter scales (1B, 4B, and 8B) and architectural paradigms (causal generative versus bidirectional discriminative). Comparing generative text-to-label classifiers against discriminative baselines (DeBERTa-V3 and ModernBERT), our analysis reveals a crucial data-dependent trade-off: while discriminative models outperform ultra-lightweight generative models on structured product reviews, even the smallest 1B generative model surpasses discriminative baselines on complex, multi-turn conversational data. Furthermore, generative models maintain robust performance under massive label-set expansion (up to 112 topics) and severe long-tail distributions, whereas discriminative baselines suffer a 35% drop in Macro-F1 at scale. Finally, we detail the successful production deployment of these optimized models across both product review and conversational domains, demonstrating strict latency compliance and tangible business impact at a global marketplace scale.

</details>


### [79] [An Accuracy--Information Tradeoff for Loss-Difference Conditional Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09206)
*Hazar Yueksel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在特定线性预测器、光滑凸损失与幂次正则化设定下，精度会迫使损失差条件互信息（ld-CMI）达到Θ(n)比特量级，因此范围缩放的ld-CMI界在这些分布上无法消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作知道精度会迫使信息进入模型（模型级信息有下界），但数据处理不等式不能把这种下界传递到损失上。作者希望澄清：精度是否也会迫使损失差条件互信息（ld-CMI）下界，从而影响基于ld-CMI的泛化界能否消失。

Method: 通过界定损失差的三个矩来建立下界。考虑维度至少线性于n的缩放符号立方体上的乘积分布，线性预测器配光滑凸损失（在零点斜率非零，如logistic损失），再加曲率与增长率均为幂r≥2的正则化项；分析最优样本量n≍ε^{-2+2/r}下的任意proper learner。也考察无正则化（n≍ε^{-2}）和损失差上加高斯噪声的情形。

Result: 在所述设定下，任何期望超额风险至多ε的proper learner，其最坏情况ld-CMI为Θ(n)比特；在高斯噪声（标准差τ）下为Θ(n/(1+(τ/ε)^2))比特。无正则化时结论同样成立。因此范围缩放的ld-CMI界在这些分布上不能消失，尽管每个proper learner的泛化差距仅为O(n^{-1/2})。此外，模型级信息不能决定带噪损失差信息；增长率、零点斜率与维度条件是必要的（维度条件可放松至对数因子）。

Conclusion: 精度不仅迫使模型级信息，也迫使损失差条件互信息存在下界。这表明基于范围缩放ld-CMI的泛化界在这些分布上具有本质局限：即使泛化差距很小，ld-CMI仍可保持Θ(n)量级。同时明确了所需条件的必要性。

Abstract: Loss-difference conditional mutual information (ld-CMI) uses the smallest of the standard observations in the supersample hierarchy of generalization bounds: it measures what a learner's loss differences reveal about which candidate of each pair it was trained on. Accuracy is known to force information into the model; data processing does not carry such lower bounds to losses. We show, by bounding three moments of the loss differences, that accuracy also forces ld-CMI. For linear predictors with a smooth convex loss of nonzero slope at zero, such as the logistic loss, plus a regularizer whose curvature and growth are both of power $r\ge2$, on product distributions over a scaled sign cube in dimension at least linear in $n$, every proper learner with expected excess risk at most $\varepsilon$ on these distributions at the optimal sample size $n\asymp\varepsilon^{-2+2/r}$ has worst-case ld-CMI of order $n$ bits, and $Θ(n/(1+(τ/\varepsilon)^2))$ bits under Gaussian noise of standard deviation $τ$ on the loss differences. The same holds without a regularizer, at $n\asymp\varepsilon^{-2}$. Consequently, range-scaled ld-CMI bounds cannot vanish on these distributions, although every proper learner's generalization gap is $O(n^{-1/2})$. We also show that model-level information does not determine noisy loss-difference information, and that the growth, slope and dimension conditions are needed, the last up to a logarithm.

</details>


### [80] [TAP: Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Pruning via Trajectory-Anchored Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09074)
*Yuanzhe Li,Pengxin Wang,Yuxin Ren,Jianing Deng,Jingtong Hu,Song Wang,Jingdi Chen,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TAP，首个面向RL训练智能体的结构化剪枝框架，将结构剪枝与高效on-policy恢复耦合，并基于恢复目标梯度迭代重评通道重要性。移除60% FFN通道后，在ALFWorld/WebShop上分别保留99.2%/88.0%成功率，每成功任务GPU时间降低约22%/17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体任务需反复调用模型，推理成本高。现有为问答任务设计的剪枝方法直接迁移到智能体模型会严重损害任务表现，其失败源于两个决策：剪什么（一次性重要性估计无法跟踪剪枝后的模型适应）与如何恢复（离线蒸馏只覆盖教师前缀，全轨迹on-policy蒸馏导致学生误差逐轮累积）。

Method: TAP将结构化剪枝与高效on-policy恢复耦合：把交互锚定在教师轨迹上，但由学生生成每个推理-动作响应；冻结的稠密教师监督学生响应的前缀，缓解响应内训练-推理不一致，同时阻止学生引起的偏离跨训练轮次传播。不同于一次性剪枝，TAP利用恢复目标在已恢复学生上的梯度重评通道，将迭代通道选择与演进中的策略相连接。

Result: 在移除60% FFN通道的条件下，TAP在ALFWorld与WebShop上分别保留稠密7B智能体99.2%与88.0%的任务成功率，并将每成功任务的GPU时间分别降低约22%与17%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在有限恢复预算下可对长程智能体实现有效的结构化压缩，验证了面向RL智能体的剪枝-恢复协同设计思路。

Abstract: Emerging long-horizon agentic tasks require repeated model calls, worsening the inference cost of already-costly language models. While narrow agentic tasks suggest potential for aggressive model pruning without performance drop, empirical results show existing methods proposed for question answering tasks severely degrade task performance when applied to agentic models. We trace this failure to two decisions: what to prune and how to recover. For pruning, one-shot importance estimates fail to track how the pruned model adapts. For recovery, offline distillation covers only teacher prefixes, while full-trajectory on-policy distillation causes student errors to compound across turns. In this work, we propose Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP), the first structural pruning framework for reinforcement learning (RL)-trained agents. TAP couples structural pruning with efficient on-policy recovery, anchoring interactions to teacher trajectories while allowing the student to generate each reasoning-action response. A frozen dense teacher supervises the student's response prefixes, addressing within-response training-inference mismatch while preventing student-induced deviations from propagating across training turns. Instead of one-shot pruning, TAP re-scores channels using gradients of the recovery objective on the recovered student, connecting iterative channel selection to the evolving policy. With 60% of FFN channels removed, TAP retains 99.2% and 88.0% of the dense 7B agents' task success rates on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively, while reducing GPU time per successful task by approximately 22% and 17%. These results demonstrate effective structural compression of long-horizon agents under a limited recovery budget.

</details>


### [81] [Efficient Best-of-N policy evaluation for inference-time alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09250)
*Jonas Schweisthal,Yuxin Wang,Athiya Deviyani,Stefan Feuerriegel,Dennis Frauen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出仅基于样本的 BoN 策略评估与选择框架，通过分数-排名概率表达密度比，构造双稳健估计量 BoN-DR，并给出两种预算选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BoN 是常见的推理时对齐方法，从日志数据评估 BoN 策略需要依赖响应似然的密度比，而仅有样本访问时这些似然不可得，标准离策略估计量失效。

Method: 利用 BoN 的次序统计量结构，将所需密度比表示为可由样本估计的分数-排名概率；提出双稳健估计量 BoN-DR，在候选预算间共享辅助样本池以提升复用效率；在奖励模型误设定下建立渐近推断并证明估计量效率；给出两条选择规则：最大化估计策略值，以及最大化相对参考策略改进的下置信界（含 no-harm 保证）。

Result: 在合成实验及 GSM8K（多种参考模型与奖励模型）上，框架能准确估计 BoN 策略值并选出有效的采样预算。

Conclusion: 该工作表明无需响应似然即可实现 BoN 策略的可扩展评估与预算选择，兼顾稳健性与效率，并缓解大 N 带来的奖励过优化问题。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) is a common inference-time alignment method that selects the highest-scoring response among N samples from a reference model. Evaluating BoN policies from logged data is challenging under sample-only access because standard off-policy estimators require density ratios that depend on unavailable response likelihoods. In this paper, we propose a sample-only framework for evaluating and selecting BoN policies without access to these likelihoods. We show that the order-statistic structure of BoN allows the required density ratios to be expressed through score-rank probabilities that are estimable from samples alone. We then develop a doubly robust estimator of the BoN policy value (BoN-DR) that efficiently reuses a shared auxiliary sample pool across candidate budgets. We establish valid asymptotic inference even under reward estimator misspecification and prove the efficiency of our BoN-DR estimator. Since larger budgets can amplify errors in the score function and lead to reward overoptimization, we derive two selection rules: (i) maximizing the estimated policy value and (ii) maximizing a lower confidence bound on the improvement over the reference policy, which accounts for estimation uncertainty and provides a no-harm guarantee. Across synthetic experiments and GSM8K with multiple reference and reward models, our framework accurately estimates BoN policy values and selects effective sampling budgets.

</details>


### [82] [The Symbol of the Surrogate: Measuring Numerical Provenance in Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09255)
*Ridham Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种经验性 Fourier 符号诊断方法，通过探测神经 PDE 代理模型线性化单步算子的频谱特性，区分其究竟学习到的是精确物理演化还是训练所用数值格式的离散误差，实验表明代理模型主要复制训练格式的误差特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经 PDE 代理模型在数值求解器输出上训练，训练数据同时包含真实物理演化与求解器特有的离散误差；且评估也使用同一求解器的留存轨迹，因此标准基准无法区分模型是拟合精确演化还是模仿数值格式。需要一种能直接辨别数值来源的诊断手段。

Method: 提出经验性 Fourier 符号诊断：用单个 Fourier 模探测已训练代理模型的线性化单步算子，并将其与精确演化和训练格式两种参考进行比较。为消除架构频谱偏置，在具有正交耗散与色散特征的格式上训练相同网络，比较其学到的算子。验证覆盖线性平流、非局部 Fourier 神经算子以及非线性 Burgers 动力学的算子层面。

Result: 在线性平流中，学习到的代理模型复现了训练格式的振幅与相位误差，双格式差异达到解析预测的完全模仿上限的 99.8% 以上；非局部 Fourier 神经算子表现一致，非线性 Burgers 动力学在算子层面亦如此。

Conclusion: 与求解器生成测试数据的一致本身并不能证明对精确演化的保真度；Fourier 符号测量为数值来源提供了一种直接诊断工具。

Abstract: Neural PDE surrogates are trained on numerical solver outputs that contain both physical evolution and solver-specific discretization errors. Because surrogates are also evaluated against held-out trajectories from the same solver, standard benchmarks cannot distinguish fidelity to the exact evolution from imitation of the numerical scheme. We introduce an empirical Fourier-symbol diagnostic that probes a trained surrogate's linearized one-step operator with individual Fourier modes and compares it with both exact-evolution and training-scheme references. To address architectural spectral bias, we train identical networks on schemes with orthogonal dissipative and dispersive signatures and compare their learned operators. In linear advection, the learned surrogates reproduce the training schemes' amplitude and phase errors, with the twin-scheme difference reaching more than 99.8\% of the analytically predicted full-imitation ceiling. The same behavior occurs for a non-local Fourier neural operator and at the operator level for nonlinear Burgers dynamics. These results show that agreement with solver-generated test data does not by itself establish fidelity to the exact evolution. Fourier-symbol measurements provide a direct diagnostic of numerical provenance.

</details>


### [83] [Tucker Bottleneck Attention for Multi-Dimensional Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09090)
*Ryan Solgi,Parsa Madinei,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TuBA（Tucker瓶颈注意力）利用低秩张量结构实现高效的全局token混合，将隐藏张量投影到紧凑的Tucker核心上执行多头自注意力，从而将计算降至亚二次复杂度，并在视频预测与天气预测任务上取得更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力计算成本随序列长度呈二次增长，限制了其在多维数据长序列上的可扩展性，因此需要一种高效且能保持全局建模能力的注意力机制。

Method: 提出Tucker瓶颈注意力（TuBA）：将隐藏张量投影到紧凑的Tucker核心，在核心上执行多头自注意力与线性投影，再将更新写回原始空间，实现亚二次计算；其自回归扩展在核心内部采用双向交互，在核心之间采用因果注意力。

Result: 在视频预测和全球天气预测任务上，TuBA相较标准与高效注意力及任务专用模型取得更好的精度-效率权衡。相比标准自注意力，视频预测误差与计算量最多降低24.7%和66.6%，自回归天气预测降低37.1%和85.1%，加速最高达4.27倍；低秩Tucker核心与多帧生成分别优于全秩注意力和逐帧生成。

Conclusion: 利用Tucker低秩张量结构进行注意力瓶颈设计，可在多维长序列任务中实现显著的精度提升与计算节省，验证了低秩张量分解作为高效注意力机制的有效性。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of self-attention limits scalability to long sequences from multidimensional data. We introduce Tucker bottleneck attention (TuBA), which exploits low-rank tensor structure for efficient global token mixing. TuBA projects hidden tensors into compact Tucker cores, performs multi-head self-attention and linear projections on the cores, and writes updates back to the ambient space, enabling subquadratic computation. Its autoregressive extension combines bidirectional interactions within cores with causal attention across cores. On video prediction and global weather forecasting, TuBA achieves favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over standard and efficient attention and task-specific models. Compared to standard self-attention, TuBA reduces error and computation by up to 24.7% and 66.6% for video prediction and 37.1% and 85.1% for autoregressive weather forecasting, with speedups up to 4.27 times. Low-rank Tucker cores and multi-frame generation also outperform full-rank attention and frame-by-frame generation, respectively.

</details>


### [84] [MaRK: Markov-adapted Recurrent Kernels for Dynamic Operator Conditioning in State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09092)
*Syed Ibrahim Omer,Ginny Y. Wong. Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaRK将上下文向量映射为冻结SSM递推参数的动态调制，用6.3–11M可训练参数实现从双向目标到迭代扩散的高效适配，Chebyshev核表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练SSM用于迭代生成时，条件机制通常置于循环算子之外（输入注入或激活调制），虽引入条件信息却无法改变底层时间动态，限制了模型对上下文的动态适应能力。

Method: 提出MaRK动态算子条件框架，将上下文向量映射为冻结SSM递推（A）、读入（B）、读出（C）、跳连（D）与离散化（Δ）参数的有界调制；以LPV-SSM视角构造上下文索引的Markov参数序列族，使每个扩散时间步重塑输入-输出记忆核。在111M Hydra SSM骨干上实例化Hypernet、Chebyshev多项式与DCT三种适配器几何，通过冻结骨干上的低秩辅助映射实现参数高效微调，并给出调制递推的解析仿射二次稳定性证明。

Result: 合成LPV恢复实验与Markov算子诊断表明，MaRK在匹配假设下可恢复坐标不变的时间算子，并生成各异且稳定的时间步条件记忆剖面。Chebyshev变体验证损失最低（2.55），DCT次之（2.59），Hypernet为3.77。

Conclusion: 将条件作用内置于SSM递推参数可实现时间动态的动态化，且参数高效性源于适配机制本身的结构性结果；有界递推参数化附带稳定性证书，Chebyshev几何为最优适配器选择。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) offer an efficient alternative to Transformers for sequence modeling, yet conditioning pre-trained SSMs for iterative generation typically operates outside the recurrent operator, through input injection or activation modulation. While such mechanisms expose the model to conditioning information, they leave the underlying temporal dynamics fixed. We introduce MaRK (Markov-adapted Recurrent Kernels), a dynamic operator-conditioning framework that maps context vectors directly into bounded modulations of a frozen SSM's recurrence ($A$), read-in ($B$), read-out ($C$), skip ($D$), and discretization ($Δ$) parameters. Viewed through the lens of LPV-SSM systems, MaRK induces a context-indexed family of Markov parameter sequences, allowing each diffusion timestep to reshape the model's input-output memory kernel. We instantiate MaRK on a frozen 111M-parameter Hydra SSM backbone and study three adapter geometries: Hypernet, Chebyshev polynomial, and Discrete Cosine Transform kernels. Since these adapters modify the Markov parameter sequence through low-rank auxiliary maps on the frozen backbone, parameter-efficient fine-tuning arises as a structural consequence of the adaptation mechanism itself, requiring only 6.3--11M trainable auxiliary parameters to transition from a bidirectional objective to an iterative diffusion regime. The bounded recurrence parameterization further yields an analytic Affine Quadratic Stability certificate for the modulated recurrence. Through synthetic LPV recovery experiments and Markov-operator diagnostics, we show that MaRK recovers coordinate-invariant temporal operators under matched assumptions and produces distinct, stable timestep-conditioned memory profiles. Empirically, the Chebyshev variant yields the strongest performance, achieving an average validation loss of 2.55, followed by the DCT (2.59) and Hypernet (3.77) geometries.

</details>


### [85] [Benign Overfitting under Heterogeneous Input Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09340)
*Houzhen Liu,Xiaobo Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究异构输入融合下的 benign overfitting（良性过拟合）问题，针对最小范数线性插值在异构高斯设计下的行为，刻画回归与分类两侧的融合何时保持良性、何时转为有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 良性过拟合在单一高维输入下已有广泛研究，但在异构输入融合（heterogeneous input fusion）下的行为尚不清楚。论文旨在厘清：当把两个统计相依的输入块融合时，边缘分布的良性是否足以保证联合问题的良性。

Method: 在保持总体任务固定的前提下，比较两个统计相依输入块及其融合，采用异构高斯设计、最小范数线性插值。回归侧引入全谱协方差证书（full-spectrum covariance certificate）；分类侧考虑 one-sparse 高斯分类，用存活预测信号与噪声污染的平衡来刻画良性。

Result: 回归中给出全谱协方差证书，其渐近状态与截断阈值无关，并被所有与两个边缘一致的正半定联合协方差所保持，但该保护是精确的、不覆盖全部良性回归问题——证书之外存在两个良性边缘却融合有害的情形。分类中，良性由存活信号与噪声污染的平衡决定，融合可令二者反向变动，且该模型类中所有边缘到联合的良性/非良性模式均可实现；同一融合输入对回归与分类可产生本质不同的影响。

Conclusion: 异构融合下的良性过拟合由输入交互所形成的联合信号与谱几何决定，而非仅由边缘良性决定。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is extensively studied when learning from a single high-dimensional input, but its behavior under heterogeneous input fusion remains largely unexplored. We study this question for minimum-norm linear interpolation under a heterogeneous Gaussian design, comparing two statistically dependent input blocks with their fusion while holding the underlying population task fixed. For regression, we identify a full-spectrum covariance certificate whose asymptotic status is independent of the cutoff threshold and prove that it is preserved by every positive-semidefinite joint covariance consistent with the two marginals. This protection is sharp, yet it does not extend to all benign regression problems: outside the certified regime, two benign marginals can have a harmful fusion. For one-sparse Gaussian classification, benignity in the regular regime is characterized by the balance between surviving predictive signal and nuisance contamination. Fusion can move these two quantities in opposite directions, and within this model class every marginal-to-joint benign/non-benign pattern is attainable. We further show that the same fused input can have qualitatively different effects on regression and classification. These results establish that benign overfitting under heterogeneous fusion is determined by the joint signal and spectral geometry created by input interaction, rather than by marginal benignity alone.

</details>


### [86] [Are We Really Benchmarking Forecasting Models? The Impact of Preprocessing on Time Series Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09096)
*Guilherme Afonso Galindo Padilha,Paulo Salgado Gomes de Mattos Neto,Rafael Menelau Oliveira e Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个"预处理感知"基准，在29,000条M4时间序列上评估11个预测模型与16种可逆预处理流程，发现预处理是预测性能的关键驱动因素，逐序列优化预处理可使各模型提升约27%–87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献虽强调预处理对预测精度的重要作用，但当前研究普遍忽视该环节。主流基准通常仅使用简单的缩放，未能处理非平稳性等关键变换（如差分），这造成结构性的预处理偏差：偏向内置数据处理机制的模型，同时掩盖了较简单架构的真实潜力。

Method: 构建一个预处理感知的基准，评估11个预测模型在16种可逆预处理流程下的表现，覆盖29,000条M4时间序列；并以逐序列方式优化预处理配置。

Result: 预处理被识别为预测性能的关键驱动因素。逐序列优化预处理使所有被评估模型获得约27%至87%的性能提升，其中缺乏内置预处理的架构改进最为显著，从而使较简单的架构在现代基准中可与复杂的最先进模型高度竞争。所有资源与实验结果被整合为一个综合元数据集以支持未来元学习任务。

Conclusion: 当前基准因忽视预处理而产生结构性偏差；预处理优化能显著提升预测性能并释放简单架构的潜力，所构建的元数据集可为后续元学习研究提供支持。

Abstract: While established literature underscores the pivotal role of preprocessing in forecasting accuracy, this stage remains largely overlooked in current research. Modern benchmarks typically resort to simple scaling, failing to account for critical transformations required to address nonstationarity, such as differencing. This omission creates a significant structural preprocessing bias that favors models with built-in data treatments while obscuring the true potential of simpler architectures. We study this effect through a preprocessing-aware benchmark that evaluates 11 forecasting models across 16 reversible preprocessing pipelines on 29,000 M4 time series. Our results identify preprocessing as a key driver of forecasting performance. Optimizing preprocessing per series yields gains of approximately 27\% to 87\% across all evaluated models, with architectures lacking internalized preprocessing experiencing the most substantial improvements. This allows simpler architectures to become highly competitive with complex, state-of-the-art models in modern forecasting benchmarks. All resources and experimental results from this benchmark are stored in a comprehensive metadataset to support future metalearning tasks.

</details>


### [87] [Convex-Concave Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09108)
*Shripad V. Deshmukh,Yaswanth Chittepu,Dhawal Gupta,Philip Thomas,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示策略优化在log密度比坐标下具有DC结构，用SCP求解并提出CCRL方法，性能优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略学习的核心优化问题（最大化期望回报）本质非凸，领域普遍回避直接求解，转而优化凸替代目标（如NPG、TRPO、PPO、AWR的信任域约束近似），导致近似误差。作者希望证明该问题并非无结构，从而摆脱替代近似。

Method: 在log密度比坐标y = log[π/π_n]下，将每轮精确目标（经PDIS计算）表述为差凸约束差凸（DC-constrained DC）规划；用序列凸规划（SCP）求解该每轮问题，并在温和条件下给出收敛保证。该方法沿可解释的轴统一了CPI、NPG、TRPO、AWR为特例，并引入耦合相邻决策的多步轴k。产物为multi-step Convex-Concave RL（CCRL）。

Result: 在多步信用传播的诊断MDP上CCRL获胜，优势随依赖长度增长；在经典控制任务上与调参后的PPO相当；在真实、随机、中等时长医疗域上，收敛到相同近优生存率的速度明显快于调参PPO，训练曲线面积（AUC）高11.3%。

Conclusion: 将RL策略优化识别为DC规划，桥接了差凸优化与RL文献；通过多步SCP求解可超越替代近似，在需要跨时域信用分配的随机任务上优于PPO。

Abstract: Policy learning drives many of the most consequential and heavily-invested applications of reinforcement learning today. Yet the core optimization problem it rests on (maximizing expected return) is notoriously non-convex, even under a direct policy parameterization, and the field has largely responded by avoiding it: optimizing convex surrogate approximations of the return under trust-region constraints (NPG, TRPO, PPO, AWR). We show that this seemingly unstructured problem is not actually structureless. In log-density-ratio coordinates $y := \log[π/π_n]$, the exact per-iteration objective, computable via per-decision importance sampling (PDIS), is a difference-of-convex-constrained difference-of-convex (DC-constrained DC) program. This structure lets us move beyond surrogate approximations: it recovers CPI, NPG, TRPO, and AWR as special cases along interpretable axes, and it opens a multi-step axis $k$ that couples consecutive decisions. We solve the per-iteration program with sequential convex programming (SCP), the standard solver for difference-of-convex problems, and give convergence guarantees under mild conditions, bridging the difference-of-convex optimization and RL literatures. Empirically, multi-step Convex-Concave RL (CCRL) wins on diagnostic MDPs where credit must propagate across a horizon (its advantage growing with the dependency length), is competitive with a tuned PPO on classic control, and on a realistic, stochastic, mid-horizon healthcare domain converges markedly faster than tuned PPO to the same near-optimal survival, with an 11.3% higher area under the training curve.

</details>


### [88] [DSReg: Provably Recovering Individual World Latents without Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09457)
*Yujia Zheng,David Klindt,Randall Balestriero,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在无重建、无解码器、无标签条件下可证明恢复世界潜在变量的方法DSReg，通过"结构多样性"条件将线性可辨识表示进一步提升为符号置换可辨识的个体潜在变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恢复世界个体潜在变量的方法（非线性ICA、字典学习、因果表示学习）依赖重建、辅助监督或分布非对称性等锚定机制；而无锚定方法（如JEPA）只能将潜在状态辨识到线性变换，个体潜在变量仍然混合。论文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出核心条件"结构多样性"（Structural Diversity）：不同潜在变量在观测上留下不同的依赖足迹。基于LeJEPA提供的线性可辨识性，证明在结构多样性下，依赖-稀疏正则化（DSReg）可将个体世界潜在变量恢复至符号置换（signed permutation），无需重建或解码器。DSReg可事后应用于任何线性可辨识表示，复用已训练检查点，且不损失联合训练性能。

Result: 建立了首个完全可辨识的JEPA，能恢复每个世界潜在变量。结构多样性作为依赖足迹条件，严格弱于以往可辨识潜在变量模型的所有结构条件。在合成场景、世界模型探针、学习视觉编码器和外部渲染器上，DSReg在保持密集预测性能的同时，随规模提升改进了个体潜在变量恢复及下游使用效果。

Conclusion: 个体世界潜在变量可在无重建、无解码器、无标签条件下被可证明恢复；DSReg作为通用后处理正则化方法，为JEPA类架构提供了完全可辨识性，并具有更弱的条件要求与良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Methods that recover individual latent variables of the world, from nonlinear ICA to dictionary learning and causal representation learning, anchor the latents to observations through reconstruction, auxiliary supervision, or distributional asymmetries such as non-Gaussianity. Methods without these anchors, including joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs), identify the latent state only up to a linear transformation, so individual latents remain mixed. We close this gap: individual world latents can be provably recovered with no reconstruction, no decoder, and no labels. The key condition is Structural Diversity: different latents leave distinct dependency footprints on observations, just as no two snowflakes are alike. Building on the linear identifiability that LeJEPA provides, we prove that under Structural Diversity, DSReg (Dependency-Sparsity Regularization) recovers individual world latents up to signed permutation, without reconstruction or a decoder. It applies post hoc to any linearly identified representation, reusing trained checkpoints at no loss over joint training, and establishes the first fully identifiable JEPA that recovers every world latent. Moreover, as a condition on dependency footprints, Structural Diversity is strictly weaker than all structural conditions of prior identifiable latent variable models. Across synthetic regimes, world model probes, learned visual encoders, and external renderers, DSReg preserves dense prediction while improving individual-latent recovery and downstream use with scales.

</details>


### [89] [Breaking Adversarial Transferability in Fine-Tuned Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09109)
*Mojtaba Nafez,Aref Mousavi,Mohammad Ebrahim Mahdavi,Mobina Poulaei,Kiarash Kiani Feriz,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 组织在公开预训练ASR模型上微调并黑盒部署，原以为有限访问即安全，但研究表明在公开基模型上构造的对抗扰动可有效迁移到微调目标模型，严重破坏性能。为此提出TransferBreaker统一微调框架以抑制对抗迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织基于公开的预训练ASR模型进行微调并在黑盒环境下部署，假设限制访问即可获得安全性。该研究旨在揭示这一假设的脆弱性：在公开基模型上制作的对抗扰动能够迁移到微调后的目标模型上，大幅降低其性能，从而对安全关键应用构成威胁。

Method: 提出TransferBreaker统一微调框架，包含三个组件：1）Base Adversarial Fine-Tuning（基础对抗微调），将对抗训练限制在基模型有效的扰动上；2）Latent Jacobian Regularization（潜空间雅可比正则化），通过抑制对抗敏感方向强制潜空间不变性；3）HybridGrad-AFT，通过插值来自基模型和目标模型梯度的可迁移扰动，提升对自适应攻击的鲁棒性。作者对各个组件均给出了理论证明。

Result: 在三种语言和四个大型ASR模型上评估TransferBreaker，将对抗词错误率（adversarial WER）从92.6降低至27.8。代码已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: 黑盒部署的假设并不安全，公开基模型上构造的对抗扰动可迁移到微调目标模型。TransferBreaker通过对抗微调、潜空间正则化和混合梯度插值，有效抑制对抗迁移性并显著提升ASR模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Many organizations fine-tune publicly available pretrained Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) models and deploy them in black-box settings, assuming limited access provides protection. We show this assumption is fragile: adversarial perturbations crafted on the public base model transfer effectively to fine-tuned target models, severely degrading performance and posing concerns for safety-critical applications. We propose TransferBreaker, a unified fine-tuning framework that suppresses adversarial transfer by integrating Base Adversarial Fine-Tuning, which restricts adversarial training to base-effective perturbations; Latent Jacobian Regularization, which enforces latent-space invariance by suppressing adversarially sensitive directions; and HybridGrad-AFT, which improves robustness against adaptive attacks by interpolating transferable perturbations from base and target gradients. We theoretically justify all components and evaluate TransferBreaker across three languages and four large ASR models, reducing adversarial WER from 92.6 to 27.8. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/rohban-lab/TransferBreaker.

</details>


### [90] [Gauss-Newton Accuracy and Indefinite Hessians: Uniform Coexistence in Low-Cost Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09675)
*Kihun Rhee,Hanjoon Byun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究岭正则化非线性最小二乘中Gauss-Newton曲率的精度，证明了在局部正则性与水平集曲率持续性条件下，存在两种曲率机制的均匀共存，并给出尖锐的相对误差界与秩结构结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gauss-Newton方法以忽略Hessian中的二阶曲率项为代价换取计算效率，但其在岭正则化非线性最小二乘中的近似精度缺乏统一、定量的刻画；作者希望明确该近似何时可靠、何时失效，并厘清岭权重、预测水平集曲率与Jacobian秩对误差的影响。

Method: 在局部正则性与精确拟合段上水平集曲率幅值的持续性假设下，分析岭正则化非线性最小二乘的Hessian与Gauss-Newton近似之差；构造基于当前预测水平集的逐点证书，分别控制Hessian修正的法向、混合与切向分量；并通过解析例子讨论输出对齐、曲率方向与持续性所起的作用。

Result: 证明了两种曲率机制的均匀共存：全局极小值存在，且每个全局极小值的相对Hessian误差低于(1+√2)/8，而同一低成本集合中存在Hessian不定、相对误差至少为15/8的点。单一正岭上界对固定邻域内所有独立中心与标签扰动、以及所有不超过该上界的正岭权重均适用，且这些邻域不随岭权重趋于零而收缩。在所述逐点类上给出预测映射与岭变化时的尖锐相对误差界。另有结构性结果：低成本集合上Jacobian行满秩，且在秩亏参考点附近精确插值。

Conclusion: Gauss-Newton曲率在岭正则化非线性最小二乘中并非一致可靠：低成本集合内可同时出现近优的良性极小值与高度不定的近似失败点，二者共存且边界由水平集曲率持续性决定；所提供的逐点证书与误差界给出量化判据，但该现象不随岭权重减小而消失。

Abstract: We study the accuracy of Gauss-Newton curvature in ridge-regularized nonlinear least squares. Under local regularity and persistence of level-set curvature magnitude along an exact-fit section, we prove uniform coexistence of two curvature regimes. Global minimizers exist, and every global minimizer has relative Hessian error below $(1+\sqrt2)/8$, while the same low-cost set contains a point with an indefinite Hessian and relative error at least $15/8$. One positive ridge cap works for all independent center and label perturbations in fixed neighborhoods and every positive ridge weight up to the cap. These neighborhoods do not shrink as the ridge weight tends to zero. A pointwise certificate based on the current prediction level set controls the normal, mixed, and tangent parts of the Hessian correction. We prove a sharp relative-error bound over the stated pointwise class when the prediction map and ridge vary. Analytic examples describe the roles of output alignment, curvature orientation, and persistence. A separate structural result gives full Jacobian row rank throughout low-cost sets and exact interpolation near a rank-deficient reference.

</details>


### [91] [The Deceptive Bandit Problem: Exploratory Coupling and the Fragility of Multi-Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09120)
*Michael Tang,Mahmoud Abdelgalil,Jorge I. Poveda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示随机探索的独立性与隐私对多智能体学习的安全性至关重要，证明欺骗者可利用与受害者探索相关的泄露信号，将学习动力学导向"欺骗性纳什均衡"（DNE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机探索（randomized exploration）广泛用于bandit学习、多智能体强化学习与零阶策略搜索，但其独立性和隐私通常仅被视为技术性假设，其安全意义未被重视。作者希望说明这些性质对安全性具有关键作用，并刻画对手如何利用关于他人探索的特权信息。

Method: 在最小化的双人强单调（strongly monotone）设定中分析"欺骗者—受害者"对：欺骗者仅获得与受害者探索相关的泄露信号，通过将自身探索动作与该信息耦合，向学习动力学注入外部性。作者定义欺骗性纳什均衡（DNE），证明欺骗性bandit学习（DBL）动力学收敛到DNE的任意小邻域，并在放宽标准bandit优化文献中二阶光滑性条件的前提下达到最优收敛速率。进一步刻画欺骗严格改变稳态的条件及其对欺骗者成本的影响，并在资源分配博弈中示例。

Result: DBL动力学以最优收敛速率收敛至DNE的任意小邻域，且该最优速率是在放宽二阶光滑性假设下取得的；给出了欺骗严格移动稳态的条件及其对欺骗者成本的影响，并在资源分配博弈中加以说明。

Conclusion: 探索的独立性与隐私并非无关紧要的技术假设，而是多智能体学习的安全关键属性；泄露的探索信息足以让欺骗者操纵学习收敛点。

Abstract: Randomized exploration is central to bandit learning, multi-agent reinforcement learning, and zeroth-order policy search, yet its independence and privacy are usually only treated as technical assumptions. We show that these properties are critical for security purposes and demonstrate how an adversarial agent can exploit privileged information on another agent's exploration. We analyze a deceiver-victim pair in the minimal two-player strongly monotone setting, where a deceptive player obtains leaked signals that are merely correlated with the victim's exploration. We show that, by coupling their own exploratory action with this information, the deceptive player injects an externality that steers the learning dynamics to a new steady state, called the deceptive Nash equilibrium (DNE). We prove that the deceptive bandit learning (DBL) dynamics converge to an arbitrarily small neighborhood of the DNE while retaining optimal convergence rates. Interestingly, our analysis attains these optimal rates while relaxing second-order smoothness conditions from standard bandit optimization literature. We characterize conditions under which deception strictly shifts the steady state and its effect on the deceiver's cost, illustrating the results in a resource-allocation game.

</details>


### [92] [EntroPrefill: Renyi-Guided Context Pruning with Conditional Stability Guarantees for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09757)
*Inbasekaran S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 EntroPrefill 形式化为一种基于 Renyi 熵引导的提议机制，并结合对丢弃注意力质量的显式约束，从理论层面界定中间预填充剪枝的可行性与形式化边界，但不报告实测加速或任务精度保持结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间预填充剪枝能减少深层 transformer 处理的序列长度，但仅凭注意力集中现象无法证明被丢弃上下文确实可弃；缺乏对丢弃注意力质量的显式约束与可行性上界，也缺乏剪枝层自适应选择下的统计保证与未来输出扰动的形式化分析，因此需要一套严格的理论刻画。

Method: 提出 EntroPrefill：以 Renyi 熵引导的提议机制耦合对丢弃注意力质量的显式约束；采用 sink 隔离、正则化的头池化以兼容分组查询注意力（GQA），并暴露专业化与最差头覆盖之间的定量权衡。推导混合到头的删除包络（可行 token 移除的可计算上界）、剪枝层自适应选择下仍成立的有限样本观测者保证、带显式充分 Lipschitz 常数的条件 transformer 扰动界及首 token 决策边界推论；用反例说明浅层观测无法推出无条件未来输出保证。系统分析区分查询头并集、物理页分配与 KV 传输负载，并给出剪枝的算术盈亏平衡条件。

Result: 建立了删除包络（可行 token 移除上界）、自适应剪枝层下的有限样本观测者保证、含显式充分 Lipschitz 常数的条件扰动界与首 token 决策边界推论；反例表明浅层观测不能推出无条件未来输出保证；系统分析给出剪枝的算术盈亏平衡条件。论文为理论性质，未报告实测加速或任务精度保持数据。

Conclusion: EntroPrefill 明确了方法流程、假设前提与形式化极限，界定了中间预填充剪枝的理论边界；实验留待后续验证假设、近似紧致性及端到端资源权衡。

Abstract: Mid-prefill pruning can reduce the sequence processed by deeper transformer layers, but attention concentration alone does not certify that discarded context is dispensable. We formulate EntroPrefill as a Renyi-guided proposal mechanism coupled to explicit constraints on discarded attention mass. Sink-isolated, regularized head pooling respects grouped-query attention while exposing a quantitative trade-off between specialization and worst-head coverage. We derive a mixture-to-head deletion envelope, a computable upper bound on feasible token removal, and a finite-sample observer guarantee that remains valid when the pruning layer is selected adaptively. We then establish a conditional transformer perturbation bound with explicit sufficient Lipschitz constants and a first-token decision-margin corollary. A counterexample shows why shallow observations alone cannot imply an unconditional future-output guarantee. The systems analysis distinguishes query-head unions, physical page allocation, and KV-transfer payload, and gives an arithmetic break-even condition for pruning. This manuscript is theoretical in scope: it defines the procedure, its assumptions, and its formal limits, but does not report measured acceleration or task-accuracy preservation. Experiments are reserved for subsequent validation of the assumptions, approximation tightness, and end-to-end resource trade-offs.

</details>


### [93] [Are Parameter-Efficient Fine-tuning Methods Really Different?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09122)
*Yikuan Li,Pinyan Lu,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统比较六种PEFT方法在语言与扩散模型中的参数化差异，发现谱保持并非适配与保持能力的关键，主导谱分量的功能后果才是核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEFT方法众多，但其参数化方式与功能差异（任务性能、遗忘、预训练权重几何变化）之间的关系尚不清晰；尤其OFT以谱保持为设计动机，但谱保持本身是否必要缺乏验证。

Method: 在语言模型与扩散模型上对比六种PEFT方法，考察其参数化与任务性能、遗忘、预训练权重几何变化的关系；通过恢复漂移的奇异值谱、以及分别恢复主导/中间/尾部谱分量的干预实验进行功能验证。

Result: LoRA族方法也近似保持预训练几何，恢复其轻微漂移的奇异值谱基本不损任务性能，说明显式几何保持非必需；LoRA最稳定地限制遗忘，DoRA在多数比较中平均任务分更高，PiSSA保持代价更大；恢复主导谱分量带来的通用文本NLL上升或基础图像漂移的平均减少最大。
