Conclusion: 应通过几何约束的功能后果而非单纯保持性来评估PEFT方法；不同方法的性能增益源于对不同谱分量组的修改，且主导谱分量最关键。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) offers many parameterizations, yet their methodological and functional differences remain unclear. We compare six methods in language and diffusion models to examine how their parameterizations relate to task performance, forgetting, and changes in pretrained weight geometry. Motivated by the spectrum-preserving design of orthogonal fine-tuning (OFT), we first ask whether spectral preservation is itself important for adaptation and retention. We find that the selected LoRA-family methods also approximately preserve pretrained geometry, and that restoring their slightly drifted singular-value spectra largely preserves task performance, questioning the necessity of explicit geometric preservation. Beyond this, we observe that some methods exhibit distinct adaptation--retention trade-offs that vary across settings: LoRA most consistently limits forgetting at competitive performance, DoRA achieves higher mean task scores than LoRA in most comparisons, while PiSSA often incurs greater retention costs. Further intervention experiments suggest that while performance gains from different PEFT methods can be attributed to modifications in different groups of spectral components, we consistently find that restoring dominant rather than intermediate or trailing components produces the largest mean reduction in general-text NLL or base-image drift. Together, these results motivate evaluating geometric constraints through their functional consequences rather than preservation alone. Code is available at https://github.com/Kuaaannn/PEFT_methods.

</details>


### [94] [Leaner Transformers Can Easily Learn to Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09760)
*Charlotte Park,Kenneth L. Clarkson,Lior Horesh,Takuya Ito,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作改进了Transformer执行k-means（Lloyd算法）的构造，将嵌入维度从d+k降至d+⌈log₂k⌉，并研究其学习、泛化与失败情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明Transformer可在前向传播中精确执行Lloyd算法，但所需嵌入维度为d+k，注意力投影矩阵为(d+k)²，规模较大；同时对这些可学习聚类算法的收敛性、泛化性和局限性缺乏系统理解。

Method: 构造一个表达能力相同但更小的Transformer，用嵌入维度d+⌈log₂k⌉执行Lloyd算法；在聚类任务分布上训练这些Transformer以学习聚类算法；基于随机梯度的学习算法，理论刻画并实验验证影响收敛与分布内泛化的因素；进一步探测其一般聚类能力，分析成功与失败情形。

Result: 提出了更紧凑的d+⌈log₂k⌉嵌入构造（相比d+k显著降低参数量，如注意力矩阵从(d+k)²降至(d+⌈log₂k⌉)²）；给出了影响收敛与分布内泛化的理论刻画并获实验验证；识别了学习到的Transformer聚类算法的成功与失败场景。

Conclusion: Transformer可在更小嵌入下精确执行Lloyd算法，且通过训练可获得具泛化性的聚类能力；其学习算法的收敛与泛化受随机梯度相关因素影响，并存在特定的成功与失败边界。

Abstract: Transformers have in-context learning capabilities, where some known learning algorithms can be executed in the forward pass through the model. Recent work shows that transformers can exactly perform Lloyd's algorithm for $k$-means clustering with $n$ points in $d$ dimensions with an embedding size $d_{\textsf{emb}} = d+k$ (thus, requiring attention projection matrices of size $(d+k)^2$). In this work, we build upon this result in the following ways: First, we present an equally expressive but smaller transformer that executes Lloyd's algorithm with embedding size $d_{\textsf{emb}} = (d + \lceil \log_2 k \rceil)$. Next, we train these transformers to learn the clustering algorithms given a distribution of clustering tasks, and theoretically characterize and empirically validate the factors affecting the convergence and in-distribution generalization of learning algorithms based on stochastic gradients. Finally, we probe the general clustering abilities of these learned algorithms (in the form of transformers), and try to understand situations where they succeed and fail.

</details>


### [95] [Spatial Induction Heads: In-Context Learning of Multidimensional Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09124)
*Kimia Kazemian,Menghan Xu,John Thickstun,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"空间归纳头"（spatial induction heads）：在将多维元胞自动机轨迹展平为序列、缺乏显式坐标偏置的条件下，两层Transformer通过第一层"聚集"重构局部空间邻域、第二层"匹配"历史构型，实现对未知局部规则的上下文学习，其路由所需位置维度仅取决于局部邻域与空间维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归纳头（induction heads）理论通常假设与预测相关的上下文构成连续块。但在多维数据中，序列化会把空间邻居分散到序列中相距很远的位置，产生"路由问题"。作者希望研究Transformer如何克服这一障碍。

Method: 以多维随机与确定性元胞自动机为对象，每条轨迹由未知局部规则生成并展平为序列且无坐标基空间归纳偏置。提出空间归纳头：两层gather-and-match电路，第一层聚集重构相关空间邻域，第二层将所得构型与先前出现进行匹配。给出gather的两种显式实现，并构造实现贝叶斯计数的匹配层。

Result: 端到端电路对随机规则可任意逼近贝叶斯后验，对确定性规则可精确预测；空间路由所需的位置维度仅取决于局部邻域与空间维度，与网格体积和轨迹长度无关。实验中训练的两层Transformer能泛化到一维与二维中未见规则，确定性rollout近乎完美，随机规则上与贝叶斯最优预测器的KL散度小于0.005 nats。注意力模式与逐层探针与预测的gather-and-match计算一致。

Conclusion: Transformer能通过空间归纳头机制解决多维数据的序列化路由问题，实现对未知局部规则的上下文学习，并具备理论可证的贝叶斯近似能力与可解释的机制证据。

Abstract: Induction heads provide a mechanistic account of in-context learning in sequential data, but existing theory largely assumes that the context relevant to a prediction forms a contiguous block. In multidimensional data, serialization breaks this assumption by scattering spatial neighbors across distant positions in the token sequence. We study how transformers overcome this routing problem in multidimensional stochastic and deterministic cellular automata, where each trajectory is generated by an unknown local rule and presented as a flattened sequence without an explicit coordinate-based spatial inductive bias. We introduce spatial induction heads, two-layer gather-and-match circuits in which the first layer reconstructs the relevant spatial neighborhood and the second matches the resulting configuration against earlier occurrences. We give two explicit realizations of the gather and show that the positional dimension required for spatial routing depends only on the local neighborhood and spatial dimension, not on grid volume or trajectory horizon. We further construct a matching layer which implements Bayesian counting. The end-to-end circuit can approximate the Bayesian posterior arbitrarily closely for stochastic rules and can predict exactly for deterministic rules. Empirically, trained two-layer transformers generalize to unseen rules in one and two dimensional settings, achieving near-perfect deterministic rollouts and less than 0.005 nats KL from the Bayes-optimal predictor on stochastic rules. Attention patterns and layerwise probes align with the predicted gather-and-match computation, providing mechanistic evidence for spatial induction in trained transformers.

</details>


### [96] [Fluctuations of Nonlinear Observables in Mean Field Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09768)
*Arnaud Descours,Geoffrey Lacour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究浅层神经网络经SGD训练时平均场涨落向有限维非线性可观测量的传播，建立了中心极限定理与显式协方差公式，并在常秩假设下给出了可观测量可否由观测泛函局部因子化(可辨识性)的充要微分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场极限已通过泛函中心极限定理刻画参数经验分布的渐近涨落，但实际关注的量(如损失、物理或统计指标)是参数分布的非线性可观测泛函而非分布本身。因此需要弄清平均场涨落如何传播到这些有限维非线性量，并判断所选观测是否足以辨识目标量。

Method: 在构造极限涨落过程的加权Sobolev空间中工作，采用普通Fréchet可微性下的泛函Delta方法，避免使用对测度变量的Lions导数；针对由SGD训练的浅层网络，推导可观测量的CLT，并在其微分具有合适表示时给出继承自平均场涨落理论的显式协方差公式；进一步在常秩假设下分析可辨识性。

Result: 得到了非线性可观测量的中心极限定理及显式协方差表达；证明在常秩假设下，目标量在局部可通过观测泛函因子化，当且仅当在某个邻域内观测泛函微分的核包含于目标量微分的核中，即用一个直接定义在环境Sobolev空间上的微分条件给出了精确的非线性局部因子化刻画。

Conclusion: 该框架既能量化有限宽度下具有统计或物理意义的可观测量的不确定性，又能评估所选观测是否包含辨识目标量所需的信息，为宽网络训练动力学的可观测层面分析提供了统一工具。

Abstract: Mean field limits describe the training dynamics of wide neural networks through the evolution of the empirical distribution of their parameters. Although functional central limit theorems characterize the asymptotic fluctuations of this distribution, quantities of practical interest are typically nonlinear observables of the parameter distribution rather than the distribution itself. In this work, we show how these mean field fluctuations propagate to finite dimensional nonlinear observables for shallow neural networks trained by stochastic gradient descent. Working in the weighted Sobolev space in which the limiting fluctuation process is constructed, we apply a functional Delta method under ordinary Fr{é}chet differentiability, without requiring Lions derivatives with respect to the measure variable. We obtain a central limit theorem for the observables and, under a suitable representation of their differentials, an explicit covariance formula inherited from the underlying mean field fluctuation theory. We also study whether prescribed quantities of interest can be recovered from the selected observations. Under a constant rank assumption, we prove that a quantity of interest factors locally through the observation functional if and only if, throughout a neighborhood, the kernel of the differential of the observation is contained in that of the quantity of interest. Thus, a differential condition expressed directly in the ambient Sobolev space yields an exact nonlinear local factorization. These results provide a framework both for quantifying finite-width uncertainty on observable, statistically or physically meaningful quantities and for assessing whether the chosen observations contain the information required to identify them.

</details>


### [97] [Domain-informed Adaptive Sampling for Generalizable PINNs in Metal Additive Manufacturing via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09126)
*Hyeonsu Lee,Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对金属增材制造中物理信息神经网络(PINN)热建模依赖手工静态配点采样、泛化性差的问题，通过经验风险最小化理论证明过程条件感知的自适应采样优于静态采样，并提出两阶段自适应采样框架：条件流匹配模型学习高残差分布+混合采样生成配点。实验表明相对L2误差平均降低62.1%，为金属AM中首个PINN自适应采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属增材制造的热建模对理解"工艺-结构-性能"链条至关重要，PINN可作为有效的代理热模型。然而现有工作通常依赖手工设计、静态的配点(collocation)采样策略，既缺乏理论依据，也难以在不同工艺条件下扩展，限制了其泛化能力。

Method: 先从经验风险最小化(ERM)角度进行理论分析，证明过程条件感知的自适应采样在泛化性上严格优于传统静态采样。基于此提出两阶段自适应采样框架：(1) 条件流匹配(Conditional Flow Matching)模型，学习不同工艺条件下的近似高残差分布；(2) 混合采样策略，将该分布与领域先验基础分布结合，生成自适应配点以精炼PINN预测器。

Result: 在金属AM数值基准上，该方法在相同配点预算下持续优于最先进的PINN基线，相对L2误差平均降低62.1%，有效捕捉文献中常被忽略的工艺相关散热区域。

Conclusion: 这是金属增材制造中首个面向PINN的自适应采样策略，提升了模型泛化能力与更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Accurate thermal modeling is essential in metal additive manufacturing (AM) for understanding the process-structure-property chain. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer effective surrogate thermal modeling by minimizing physics-based residual losses at collocation points. However, prior works typically rely on manually-crafted, static collocation sampling strategies, which are neither principled nor scalable across process conditions, hindering their generalization capability. In this work, we provide theoretical analysis through empirical risk minimization, showing that process condition-aware adaptive sampling is strictly more favorable than conventional static sampling for generalization. Building on this insight, we propose an adaptive sampling strategy within a two-stage framework: (1) a conditional Flow Matching model that learns approximate high-residual distributions across different process conditions, and (2) a mixed sampling strategy combining this distribution with a domain-informed base distribution to generate adaptive collocation points for refining the PINN predictor. Experiments on metal AM numerical benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art PINN baselines, achieving an average 62.1\% reduction in relative $L_2$ error under an identical collocation budget, by capturing process-dependent heat dissipation regions often overlooked in the literature. To the authors' knowledge, this is the first adaptive sampling strategy for PINNs in metal AM, contributing to the enhanced generalization and broader applicability.

</details>


### [98] [AdaPS-LiNGAM: Adaptive Predecessor Selection for Linear Non-Gaussian Acyclic Models under Small-Sample Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09782)
*Shun Yanashima,Kentaro Kanamori,Hirofumi Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示DirectLiNGAM在小样本高维下反复残差化必然退化的结构性缺陷，提出基于"活跃边界"的AdaPS-LiNGAM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现在小样本、变量数较多的情形下极具挑战，线性非高斯无环模型（LiNGAM）同样如此。DirectLiNGAM通过依次识别外生变量并从其余变量中剔除其线性效应来估计因果顺序，但当变量数超过样本量时，其反复残差化过程存在固有局限，作者希望刻画这一限制并加以克服。

Method: 论文首先从理论上建立DirectLiNGAM的结构性局限：当变量数大于样本量时，反复残差化在确定完整因果顺序之前必然退化。分析进一步表明，每个残差仅可由已排在前面的因果顺序中、由图结构确定的变量子集（称为活跃边界，active boundary）重构。据此提出AdaPS-LiNGAM（自适应前驱选择LiNGAM），直接用原始观测、通过自适应选取的稀疏前驱子集来重构每个残差；同一子集选择原则也被用于边估计的最终剪枝步骤。

Result: 合成数据实验表明，AdaPS-LiNGAM在样本受限（小样本）情形下能准确恢复因果结构，且随样本量减少性能退化更为平缓，优于基准方法。

Conclusion: DirectLiNGAM的反复残差化在高维小样本下必然失效；利用"活跃边界"的自适应稀疏前驱选择可有效重构残差并实现稳健的因果发现，AdaPS-LiNGAM在小样本场景下具有更好的结构恢复能力与退化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Causal discovery becomes particularly challenging when the available sample size is small relative to the number of variables. This challenge also arises in the linear non-Gaussian acyclic model (LiNGAM), an identifiable framework for causal discovery from observational data. DirectLiNGAM estimates a causal order, which arranges variables so that causes precede their effects, by sequentially identifying an exogenous variable and removing its linear effect from the remaining variables. We establish a structural limitation of this procedure: when the number of variables exceeds the sample size, repeated residualization necessarily becomes degenerate before the full causal order can be determined. Our analysis further reveals that each residual can be reconstructed using only a graph-determined subset of variables already placed earlier in the causal order, termed the active boundary. This result motivates AdaPS-LiNGAM (Adaptive Predecessor Selection LiNGAM), which reconstructs each residual directly from the original observations using an adaptively chosen sparse subset of those earlier variables. The same subset-selection principle is also applied to the final pruning step for edge estimation. Experiments on synthetic data demonstrate that AdaPS-LiNGAM provides accurate causal-structure recovery in sample-limited settings and degrades more gradually as the sample size decreases.

</details>


### [99] [Directional Evidence Guided Search-Space Reduction for Exact DAG Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09136)
*Upala Junaida Islam,Abdelmonem Elrefaey,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出非参数混合框架DECO，利用数据中的依赖与方向证据构建可接受父集，缩小精确DAG学习的搜索空间，在保持结构恢复性能的同时显著降低计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据学习有向无环图（DAG）因候选父集配置数随节点数指数增长而成为困难的组合问题。现有精确评分法需高强度组合搜索，约束法在图规模和条件集复杂度增大时不可靠或计算代价高，亟需在无需固定参数结构模型的前提下降低精确优化负担。

Method: 提出非参数混合框架DECO（Directional Evidence-guided Configuration Optimization）：先从观测数据中提取依赖性与方向性证据，筛选出经验支持的父集配置，据此构建可接受父集，再进行精确优化；剔除经验上不支持的父配置，同时保留所有合理边方向的灵活性。理论上分析可接受父集配置空间的指数级缩减，并刻画有界边级遗漏对保留真实父结构概率的影响。

Result: 在基准贝叶斯网络及合成的离散与连续DAG上的实验表明，DECO显著缩减搜索空间，同时结构恢复性能具有竞争力，在多数评估设置下取得较优的结构汉明距离（SHD）。

Conclusion: 方向性证据可作为有效的预处理机制，在不依赖固定参数结构模型的情况下降低精确DAG学习的计算负担。

Abstract: Learning a directed acyclic graph (DAG) from observational data is a challenging combinatorial problem due to the exponential growth in the number of candidate parent-set configurations. Existing exact score-based methods often require computationally intensive combinatorial search, whereas constraint-based methods can become unreliable or computationally demanding as graph size and conditioning-set complexity increase. We develop a non-parametric hybrid framework, referred to as DECO (Directional Evidence-guided Configuration Optimization), that extracts dependency and directional evidence from observation data to construct admissible parent sets prior to exact optimization. It reduces the optimization search space by eliminating empirically unsupported parent configurations while preserving flexibility for all plausible edge orientations. Theoretical analysis establishes an exponential reduction in the admissible parent-set configuration space and quantifies how bounded edge-level omission affects the probability of retaining the true parent structure. Experiments on benchmark Bayesian networks and synthetic discrete and continuous DAGs demonstrate substantial search-space reduction while achieving competitive structure-recovery performance, with favorable structural Hamming distance across many evaluated settings. These results show that directional evidence can provide an effective preprocessing mechanism for reducing the computational burden of exact DAG learning without requiring a fixed parametric structural~model.

</details>


### [100] [A Cognitive-Aware QML-CRL Framework for Detecting Affinity and Romance-Investment Fraud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09141)
*Bibhas Adhikari,Ramya Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典框架，将操纵性对话中的认知偏差建模为结构化参数化量子电路中的专用量子比特，并结合经典强化学习智能体逐轮决策是否标记对话（建模为最优停止问题），在含硬负例的合成对话上进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"情感与恋爱投资类诈骗"（杀猪盘类）检测，传统方法难以刻画操纵性对话中的认知偏差及其时间顺序与共现结构，作者希望借助量子电路的表征能力与强化学习的序贯决策能力来提升检测效果。

Method: 混合量子-经典框架：1）用专用量子比特编码该类诈骗核心的认知偏差；2）设置"框架量子比特"（frame qubit），使编码对操纵性重构的时间顺序敏感；3）设置"叙事量子比特"（narrative qubit），通过可训练纠缠层聚合共现信息；4）电路参数与经典强化学习智能体联合训练，智能体逐轮决定是否标记对话，建模为最优停止问题。

Result: 在包含硬负例（合法但紧急的对话、合法但强推的销售对话）的合成对话数据集上评估了模型性能（摘要未报告具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 该框架为情感与恋爱投资诈骗检测提供了一种将认知偏差编码、时序敏感性与序贯决策相结合的量子-经典混合建模范式；但当前仅在合成数据上验证，尚需在真实场景与量化基准上进一步检验。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical framework that detects affinity and romance-investment fraud by modelling the cognitive biases in a manipulative conversation. In our proposed framework, cognitive biases central to this fraud class are carried by dedicated qubits in a structured parameterized quantum circuit, together with a frame qubit makes the encoding sensitive to the temporal order of manipulative reframing, and a narrative qubit that aggregates co-occurrence through a trainable entanglement layer. The circuit parameters are trained jointly with a classical reinforcement-learning agent that decides, turn by turn, whether to flag the conversation, modeled as an optimal stopping problem. We evaluate the model's performance on synthetic conversations that include hard negatives, legitimate but urgent, and legitimate but pushy sales conversations.

</details>


### [101] [Noise Your Prompt: Noising Conditioning Tokens in Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09145)
*Justin Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在连续扩散语言模型训练中，对条件提示词元也施加噪声（而非保持干净），仅需一行代码修改，即可在组合推理任务上大幅提升求解率与多样性，并在部分自然语言生成任务上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型文献中的标准做法是在训练时将条件提示词元（conditioning prompt tokens）固定为干净状态。作者质疑这一惯例，认为其可能限制模型的泛化能力与生成多样性，因此探索在训练阶段也对提示词元加噪的可行性。

Method: 对标准训练目标做极简单修改：在训练时不仅对生成序列加噪，也对条件提示词元加噪（noise the conditioning prompt tokens）。该方法为训练目标层面的单行改动，默认不增加推理成本，并支持受无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）启发的引导采样。

Result: 在组合推理任务上取得更好泛化：Sudoku Hard 求解率从3.73%提升至24.65%，10×10 N-Queens 覆盖率从50.60%提升至73.79%（多样性提升）。在中小规模数据集上，Gigaword 摘要的自然语言生成质量有可测量改进；但增益并非普遍适用，例如开放式对话生成未见提升。

Conclusion: 对条件提示词元加噪是一种简单有效的训练目标改进，能显著提升组合推理的求解率与解多样性，并改善部分自然语言生成任务，但效果不具普适性。方法易于实现、无额外推理开销，并兼容引导采样，代码已公开。

Abstract: We revisit a standard accepted practice in the continuous diffusion language model
  literature of fixing conditioning prompt tokens clean during training.
  We make a very simple modification: also noise the conditioning prompt tokens during training.
  We demonstrate that under this modified training objective, we achieve better generalization
  in combinatorial reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and N-Queens, with the largest gains on harder variants
  ($3.73\% \to 24.65\%$ solve rate on Sudoku Hard), and increased diversity of generated solutions ($50.60\% \to 73.79\%$ coverage on
  10x10 N-Queens). We also show measurable improvements to natural language generation quality
  in modest dataset regimes with Gigaword summarization, but notably demonstrate that gains do not
  transfer to all natural language tasks (e.g open ended dialogue generation).
  Our method is a single line change to the training objective, requires no additional inference costs by default,
  and provides the flexibility of classifier-free guidance inspired guided sampling. Our
  \href{https://github.com/LateralIntelligence/noise-your-prompt} {code} is publicly available.

</details>


### [102] [Expected Sample Complexity in Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09929)
*Nadav Sukenik,Nadav Merlis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机多臂老虎机问题的样本复杂度，提出"期望近似正确"(ACE)框架，分析已知与未知ε下的随机算法并给出近乎匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本复杂度是序贯决策问题的常用指标，但现有框架多依赖高概率保证，缺乏对期望样本复杂度的系统分析。作者希望引入新的性能度量ACE，以刻画期望意义下的近似正确性，并建立其与最优期望收益收敛性及遗憾界之间的联系。

Method: 提出ACE（approximately correct in expectation）框架来分析期望样本复杂度。证明ACE保证蕴含几乎必然收敛到最优期望收益，并给出将ACE保证转化为显式期望遗憾界的方法。分析确定性算法无法获得有利ACE界，随后在两种设定下研究随机算法：（1）算法已知子优水平ε时，设计explore-then-ε-greedy算法；（2）ε未知时，分析Thompson采样的期望样本复杂度。最后推导两种设定的下界。

Result: ACE保证可推出几乎必然收敛到最优期望收益，且可转化为显式期望遗憾界；确定性算法无法获得有利ACE界；已知ε时explore-then-ε-greedy算法有效，未知ε时Thompson采样有效；两设定的下界近乎匹配，证明算法在ε上紧致，并揭示两种情形之间存在性能分离。

Conclusion: ACE为随机多臂老虎机的期望样本复杂度提供了统一分析框架，兼顾收敛性与遗憾界，且明确划分了ε已知与未知两种情形的性能边界。

Abstract: Sample complexity is a widely used metric in sequential decision-making problems, defined as the number of suboptimal decisions during the interaction between the agent and an environment. We study the sample complexity of stochastic multi-armed bandit problems and introduce the expected sample complexity performance measure, analyzing it in a novel framework called approximately correct in expectation (ACE). We show that ACE guarantees imply almost sure convergence to the optimal expected reward, in contrast to high-probability guarantees found in other frameworks, and also show how to convert ACE guarantees into explicit expected regret bounds. We further show that, in contrast to existing measures, deterministic algorithms cannot obtain favorable ACE bounds, and analyze stochastic algorithms in two settings: when the allowed suboptimality level $ε$ is known to the algorithm and when it is unknown. In the former, we devise an explore-then-$ε$-greedy algorithm, and in the latter, we analyze the expected sample complexity of Thompson sampling. Finally, we establish nearly matching lower bounds for both settings, showing that the algorithms are tight in $ε$ and proving a performance separation between the two regimes.

</details>


### [103] [Context-aware Attention-based Gaussian Mixture Models for Vehicular Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09174)
*Arash Raftari,Babak Ebrahimi Soorchaei,Yaser P. Fallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAA-GMM：一种上下文感知注意力高斯混合模型，用于多模态、不确定性感知的轨迹预测，在nuScenes与Argoverse 2上兼顾精度与低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同驾驶与自动驾驶在复杂不确定环境中需要可靠且可解释的轨迹预测，现有栅格化方法在多模态建模、交互融合与通信/感知退化条件下的鲁棒性方面仍有不足。

Method: 以场景上下文与智能体动力学为条件，将未来运动建模为概率混合分布，用可解释的高斯分量刻画多种行为模式；引入轻量注意力机制自适应编码智能体间交互与上下文显著性，从而高效融合栅格化环境线索与运动历史。

Result: 在nuScenes与Argoverse 2上，精度与最先进栅格基线相当或更优，同时保持低计算复杂度；消融实验验证注意力模块对上下文推理与预测精度的关键作用；在通信与感知不完善条件下仍表现出较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CAA-GMM是一种高效、可扩展的协同轨迹预测方案，兼具多模态不确定性建模能力与可解释性，适用于智能交通系统。

Abstract: Reliable and interpretable trajectory prediction is critical for cooperative and autonomous driving in complex and uncertain environments. This paper introduces a Context-Aware Attention-based Gaussian Mixture Model (CAA-GMM) for multimodal, uncertainty-aware motion forecasting. The proposed approach models future motion as a probabilistic mixture conditioned on both scene context and agent dynamics, capturing diverse behavioral modes with interpretable Gaussian components. A lightweight attention mechanism adaptively encodes inter-agent interactions and contextual salience, enabling efficient fusion of rasterized environment cues and motion history in dense traffic scenes. Comprehensive evaluations on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that CAA-GMM achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared with state-of-the-art raster-based baselines, while maintaining low computational complexity. Ablation analyses confirm the importance of the attention module for robust contextual reasoning and predictive precision. Furthermore, evaluations under imperfect communication and perception conditions highlight the framework's resilience to uncertainty, establishing CAA-GMM as an efficient and scalable solution for cooperative trajectory prediction in intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [104] [m-Set Adversarial Bandits with Winner Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10128)
*Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Matteo Papini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了$m$-set对抗性bandit问题，在不同效用（winner reward或sum of rewards）与反馈模型（winner index、winner reward、sum of rewards及其组合）下给出了遗憾的上界和下界，揭示了设定中细微变化对学习率的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合bandit与MNL bandit已有标准遗憾界，但$m$-set设定中效用定义和反馈模型的不同组合可能导致学习率发生剧烈变化，这一影响尚未被系统刻画，因此需要建立相应的上下界来量化这一问题。

Method: 针对$m$-set对抗性bandit，按效用类型（winner reward或sum of rewards）与反馈模型（winner index、winner reward、sum of rewards及其组合）分类，推导遗憾的上界与下界，核心技术上采用信息论下界方法，并与组合bandit和MNL bandit的标准界进行对比；同时在合成数据上进行实验验证。

Result: 给出了各设定下遗憾的上界与下界，其中主要技术贡献为信息论下界；通过与组合bandit和MNL bandit标准界的比较，显示设定中的细微变化会导致学习率出现显著差异；合成数据实验与理论分析结论一致。

Conclusion: 效用与反馈模型的组合选择对$m$-set对抗性bandit的学习率有决定性影响，信息论下界是刻画该问题的关键工具，理论结果得到合成实验的支持。

Abstract: We show upper and lower bounds on the regret of $m$-set adversarial bandits for different utilities (winner reward or sum of rewards) and feedback models (winner index, winner reward, sum of rewards, and their combinations). By comparing to standard bounds for combinatorial and MNL bandits, our results reveal how subtle changes in the setting can have a dramatic impact on the learning rates. Our main technical contributions are the information-theoretic lower bounds on the regret. Experiments on synthetic data confirm our theoretical analyses.

</details>


### [105] [LayerRoPE: Dynamic Depth-wise Magnitude & Angular Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09179)
*Shikhar Srivastava,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文挑战"深度诅咒"被视为病态现象的主流观点，指出Transformer隐藏态范数随深度增长实际上是一种涌现的深度位置编码，由归一化权重γ承载；作者提出LayerRoPE将其显式化，在参数更少、FLOPs几乎不变的情况下持续优于多种归一化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据在Transformer中传播，隐藏态范数随深度呈数量级增长，这一"深度诅咒"现象几乎被普遍视为需要抑制的病态。作者反向思考：这种增长可能并非缺陷，而是模型自发形成的一种深度位置编码机制，值得被理解和显式化，而非简单压制。

Method: 作者分析了9个家族、16个预训练LLM（涵盖稠密、MoE、混合架构及Pre/Peri/Post-Norm设计），发现残差流上唯一可学习的逐层增益——归一化权重γ——随深度增大并在方向上旋转，共同编码层索引。据此提出LayerRoPE，作为RoPE沿深度轴的隐式类比，用单个共享向量加深度条件标量替换所有逐层γ向量。

Result: 在扩展至100B+ token的模型阶梯上，LayerRoPE一致优于Pre-/Post-/Peri-Norm及Layer-Norm Scaling，以3.4倍更少算力达到Pre-Norm 1.3B的损失；是唯一在深度扩展至512层时仍保持强收敛且近乎单调改进的方法；学习率敏感性改善3–10倍，并可朴素迁移至循环隐模型和ViT。

Conclusion: LayerRoPE并未缩小残差流，反而拓宽它，抑制每个块的读取、放大其写入，从而颠覆了主流前提。作者认为深度稳定性不应靠压制残差流，而应对其驱动的计算块进行深度条件化调节。

Abstract: As data propagates through a Transformer, the norm of its hidden states grows by orders of magnitude with depth, a phenomenon framed as 'curse of depth' and nearly universally treated as a pathology to be suppressed. We take the opposite view. Across 16 pre-trained LLMs from 9 families, spanning dense, mixture-of-experts and hybrid architectures and Pre-, Peri- and Post-Norm designs, we find that this growth reflects an emergent depth-positional encoding, carried by the only learned per-layer gain on the residual stream, the normalization weight $γ$: with depth, $γ$ grows in magnitude and rotates in direction, jointly encoding the layer index. We make this depth-conditioned encoding explicit with LayerRoPE, an implicit analog of RoPE along the depth axis, which replaces all layerwise $γ$ vectors with a single shared vector and depth-conditioned scalars, at a net reduction in parameters and $<0.02\%$ change in FLOPs. Across a model ladder scaled up to $100$B+ tokens, LayerRoPE consistently outperforms Pre-, Post- and Peri-Norm and Layer-Norm Scaling, reaching Pre-Norm's 1.3B loss with $3.4\times$ less compute; LayerRoPE is the only approach that shows strong convergence and improves near monotonically as depth scales to 512 layers. It improves learning-rate sensitivity by $3$-$10\times$, and transfers naively to and consistently improves looped latent models and Vision Transformers. Inspecting its learned schedule inverts the prevailing premise: LayerRoPE does not shrink the residual stream but widens it, damping what each block reads while amplifying what it writes. Depth stability, our results suggest, calls not for suppressing the residual stream, but for depth-conditioned regulation of the computational blocks it feeds.

</details>


### [106] [Pre-training of Bayesian Optimization Algorithm through Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10186)
*Satoshi Katayama,Shoyo Hunt,Shintaro Masuda,Masayuki Karasuyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出利用GP样本路径优化BO超参数的框架，通过外层BO最小化估计的期望累积遗憾，实验证明能有效选出高性能参数配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BO算法通常包含需预先指定的参数，其性能对这些参数选择高度敏感，而现有做法缺乏基于当前信息的自适应选择手段。

Method: 以BO启动时可用信息推断的高斯过程（GP）为对象，从中抽样得到样本路径；将候选参数配置下的BO算法在这些样本路径上运行，得到期望累积遗憾的经验估计；再以另一层BO（outer BO）为优化器，最小化该估计以筛选参数配置。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能在候选参数配置集合中有效选取取得优异性能的参数配置。

Conclusion: 通过GP样本路径上的经验遗憾估计与外层BO优化，可实现BO超参数的信息驱动自适应选择，从而提升BO算法性能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is widely used as a standard approach for expensive black-box optimization. However, BO algorithms often involve parameters that must be specified in advance, and their performance can strongly depend on these choices. We propose a framework for optimizing such parameters using sample paths drawn from a Gaussian process (GP) inferred from the information available at the start of BO. We use cumulative regret as the performance metric for a BO algorithm. By running the BO algorithm on the generated sample paths, we obtain an empirical estimate of its expected cumulative regret for a given parameter configuration. Optimizing this estimate allows us to identify parameter configurations that, given the currently available information, are expected to achieve low cumulative regret. Since this parameter optimization is itself a black-box optimization problem, we employ another BO procedure to solve it, which we refer to as outer BO. Through experiments, we demonstrate that the proposed framework can effectively select parameter configurations that achieve strong performance among a range of candidate configurations.

</details>


### [107] [Q-PACE: Dynamic Precision Allocation for Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09183)
*Alexandra Volkova,Matin Ansaripour,Erik Schultheis,Christoph H. Lampert,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-PACE方法，通过二阶灵敏度模型预测量化噪声引起的损失增量，实现LLM混合精度训练的精度分配，在显著降低内存预算下达到相当损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化感知训练（QAT）可降低LLM部署成本，但激进量化会损害模型性能。常见的缓解手段是混合精度训练，即对部分层保留高精度以维持性能并约束成本，但这需要为各层分配精度，现有方法缺乏有效的分配依据。

Method: Q-PACE由二阶灵敏度模型构成，将损失增量预测为量化噪声MSE的加权和，权重为逐层曲率系数。训练过程中周期性地通过跨层扰动重新计算这些曲率系数，并据此重新分配精度。

Result: 在参数量高达4B的LLM上进行预训练和有监督微调实验，Q-PACE持续优于现有混合精度训练方案，并在显著更低的总内存预算下达到相当的损失。

Conclusion: 量化灵敏度可由层深度和层类型高度预测，且其在训练过程中的稳定性支持低频、低成本的重校准，从而提高混合精度QAT的实用性与效率。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) leverages lower-precision arithmetic to reduce the cost of LLM deployment, but aggressive quantization degrades final model performance. A common remedy is mixed-precision training, in which high precision is assigned to some of the layers to maintain performance while keeping the cost constrained. This approach then requires precision assignments for model layers during training. We provide a new approach, called Q-PACE, consisting of a second-order sensitivity model that predicts the loss increase as a sum of quantization noise MSE weighted by per-layer curvature coefficients. During training, we periodically re-compute these coefficients using perturbations across layers, and re-assign precision. Pretraining and supervised fine-tuning experiments on LLMs of up to 4B parameters show that Q-PACE consistently improves over existing mixed-precision training recipes, and achieves comparable loss at substantially lower total memory budgets. We further find that quantization sensitivity is highly predictable by depth and layer type, and its stability during training allows for infrequent, cheap recalibration.

</details>


### [108] [Finite-Sample Approximation of Hessian-Guided Perturbed Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10218)
*Ryotaro Kawata,Atsushi Nitanda,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究PWGF（Wasserstein梯度流的Hessian引导扰动变体）在有限粒子近似下的长时间跟踪精度，证明在累积曲率条件下粒子与目标值跟踪界成立，并在方差加余弦模型与正则化矩阵分解模型中验证条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein梯度流将梯度下降推广到概率测度空间，其扰动变体PWGF通过加入高斯扰动以逃逸非凸问题中的鞍点。然而用有限多个相互作用粒子近似时，近似能否在渐长时间尺度上保持准确尚不清楚，尤其是曲率沿路径累积可能放大或抑制误差。因此需要刻画临时不稳定与长期精确跟踪可以共存的场景。

Method: 分析保留了沿种群驱动参考路径累积的曲率：负曲率会放大近似误差，随后的正曲率可抑制其影响。在正则性假设与给定共同扰动计划下，对满足累积曲率显式条件的参考路径，证明高概率事件上的粒子与目标值跟踪界。为处理状态依赖的高斯跳，构造"种群优先"耦合，保持参考粒子的条件独立性并将跳误差归约为协方差比较。

Result: 在方差加余弦模型中验证所提条件，曲率恢复带来渐长时间跟踪保证；在正则化矩阵分解模型的两个区域建立局部吸引、横向下降与正二阶变分，从而给出正-负-正曲率模式。

Conclusion: PWGF的有限粒子近似可在参考路径累积曲率满足显式条件时，于随时间增长的时域上保持高概率粒子与目标值跟踪精度；临时的不稳定可由后续正曲率补偿，且该条件在具体模型中可实现，为分析非凸优化中扰动梯度流的长期行为提供了框架。

Abstract: Wasserstein gradient flow extends gradient descent to probability measures. Its Hessian-guided perturbed variant (PWGF) adds Gaussian perturbations to escape saddle points in nonconvex problems. We investigate when its approximation by finitely many interacting particles remains accurate over growing time horizons. Our analysis retains the curvature accumulated along the population-driven reference path: negative curvature can amplify approximation errors, while subsequent positive curvature can damp their influence. This captures favorable scenarios in which temporary instability is compatible with accurate tracking over growing horizons. Under regularity assumptions and a prescribed common perturbation schedule, we prove particle and objective-value tracking bounds on a high-probability event for reference paths satisfying explicit conditions on accumulated curvature. To handle state-dependent Gaussian jumps, we construct a population-first coupling that preserves the reference particles' conditional independence and reduces jump errors to covariance comparison. We verify the conditions in a variance-plus-cosine model, where curvature recovery yields a growing-horizon tracking guarantee. We also establish local attraction, transverse descent, and positive second variation in two regions of a regularized matrix-factorization model, motivating a positive-negative-positive curvature pattern.

</details>


### [109] [The Dichotomy Between Pattern Recognition and Step-by-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09186)
*Amrut Nadgir,Pratik Chaudhari,Vijay Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"模式识别"与"逐步推理"是同一光谱的两端：当下一token仅依赖极少量前文时，推理轨迹构成De Bruijn图的DAG子图，学习该子图的边即可组合解决更长的新任务。理论证明边数远小于推理轨迹数，实验显示训练样本需求与边数呈幂律关系；状态频率决定上下文窗口c，小c准确但脆弱，大c仅相当于模式识别，中等状态密度在准确性与鲁棒性间取得平衡。真实数据亦具De Bruijn结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM究竟如何"推理"缺乏统一理论框架。作者希望从上下文依赖长度这一视角，将模式识别与逐步推理统一为一个连续谱，并给出可度量、可解释的形式化刻画。

Method: 将下一token仅依赖最近c个token的情形建模为De Bruijn图，节点为c长度上下文、边为next-token转移；任务的所有推理轨迹构成该图的DAG子图。通过引入"状态"使未来推理与过去独立来控制c，并对Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct在解方程和故事问答任务上微调，考察不同状态频率（c大小）下的表现；另在GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond上限制Qwen3-14B/32B的注意力窗口以验证真实数据的De Bruijn结构。

Result: 理论证明边的数量相对推理轨迹数可忽略不计；实证上transformer所需训练样本数与边数呈幂律关系，即逐步推理学习是样本高效的。微调实验表明：频繁状态（小c）带来更高准确率但对测试扰动更脆弱；大c模型仅相当于无推理的模式识别；中等状态密度可平衡准确性与鲁棒性。真实数据中，Qwen3-14B与Qwen3-32B在注意力窗口不到完整推理轨迹15%时，仍保留GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond上超过75%的准确率。

Conclusion: 模式识别与逐步推理是同一光谱的两端，由下一token所依赖的上下文长度c决定。学习De Bruijn子图的边使逐步推理变得样本高效，状态密度在准确性与鲁棒性之间存在权衡，且真实推理数据具备De Bruijn结构，支持该理论框架的普适性。

Abstract: We argue that pattern recognition and step-by-step reasoning are two ends of a spectrum. A large language model (LLM) learns to reason step-by-step when data is structured such that the next token depends on a small amount of preceding context. Inference in LLMs resembles pattern recognition when the next token depends on a large amount of preceding context. If the next token depends on only the $c$ most recent tokens, reasoning traces are paths on a De Bruijn graph whose nodes are $c$-length contexts and edges are next-token transitions between contexts. The set of reasoning traces of a task forms a directed acyclic subgraph of the De Bruijn graph. An LLM that has learned all edges of this subgraph can compose them to solve longer, unseen tasks, i.e., it reasons step-by-step. We prove that the number of edges is vanishingly small compared to the number of reasoning traces. Empirically, the number of training samples a transformer needs is a power law in the number of edges, so learning to reason step-by-step is sample efficient. We can induce De Bruijn structure in any task by maintaining a ``state'' that makes future reasoning independent of the past. The frequency of states in the reasoning trace determines $c$. We show, by fine-tuning Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct to solve equations and answer questions about stories, that frequent states (small $c$) result in higher accuracy but greater fragility to perturbations at test time. LLMs trained with a large $c$ are only as good as models that perform pattern recognition without reasoning. A moderate density of states balances accuracy and robustness. We show that real-world data has De Bruijn structure: Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B retain over 75% of their accuracy on GSM8K, MATH-500 and GPQA-Diamond when attention is restricted to a sliding window less than 15% as long as the full reasoning trace.

</details>


### [110] [Patient, Place, Prior (P$^3$): What Counts as Personalization in Medical World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09194)
*Xingrui Gu,Hanxue Gu,Yuxiang Zhang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出P³审计框架与Cancer JEPA模型，检验纵向影像预测是否真正利用患者个体信息而非仅依赖群体平均模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向模型能预测患者影像状态演变，但预测准确性本身无法证明预测是由患者个体的观测轨迹驱动的；群体平均预测可能有效，却无法建立患者特异性世界模型的论断。

Method: 引入P³（Patient, Place, Prior）审计，分别检验预测是否受益于患者纵向影像历史（Patient）、是否受益于患者匹配的外部提供空间支持（Place）、以及在匹配支持与情境下是否超越群体平均预测（Prior）；并提出Cancer JEPA，一种单步模型，预测新辅助治疗期间乳腺DCE-MRI检查的冻结表征，在患者条件的低复杂度降秩回归基线上加入病灶约束的神经校正，采用基于遮挡的潜空间目标训练，从而支持对冻结模型的事后P³审计。

Result: 在曾用于开发的验证队列中，当神经校正接收患者自身历史而非其他患者历史、接收患者匹配病灶占据图而非替换图时，预测误差更低；但比较由患者历史计算的校正与群体平均神经校正的描述性95%区间包含零。

Conclusion: P³将输入使用与患者特异性预测价值的证据区分开来，即超越群体水平模式的预测价值并未得到确证。

Abstract: Longitudinal models forecast how a patient's imaging state evolves, but accuracy does not show whether the patient's observed trajectory drives the prediction. A population-average forecast may be useful but cannot establish a patient-specific world-model claim. We introduce Patient, Place, Prior (P$^3$), an audit asking whether a forecast benefits from the patient's longitudinal imaging history (Patient), benefits from patient-matched externally supplied spatial support (Place), and gains predictive value beyond a population-average prediction under matched support and context (Prior). We also propose Cancer JEPA, a one-step model that forecasts frozen representations of future breast dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI examinations during neoadjuvant therapy. It adds a lesion-constrained neural correction, trained with an occlusion-based latent objective, to a patient-conditioned low-complexity reduced-rank regression baseline. This factorization permits a post-hoc P$^3$ audit of the frozen model. In a validation cohort previously used in development, forecast error is lower when the neural correction receives the patient's history rather than another patient's and patient-matched lesion occupancy maps rather than substituted maps. However, the descriptive 95% interval comparing the correction computed from patient history with the population-average neural correction includes zero. P$^3$ thus separates input use from evidence of patient-specific predictive value beyond a population-level pattern.

</details>


### [111] [Shared Gaussianization: What Gaussian Regularizers Certify About Contrastive Learning, and What They Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10299)
*Ruoyu Zhao,Yuting Chen,Jinheng Zhang,Zhehao Zou,Tong Che*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出共享高斯化(SG)正则项，通过两视图平均的特征函数高斯性检验统一检测错位与非均匀性；SG在InfoNCE总体最优处严格为零，并以√SG速率给出InfoNCE超额风险的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布匹配正则项(如LeJEPA中的SIGReg)在对比学习中到底能提供何种可证明的保证尚不清楚，尤其缺少SG损失与InfoNCE目标之间的定量联系。

Method: 对两个归一化视图的平均施加特征函数高斯性检验，并以独立χ_d半径缩放，构造SG正则项；利用特征函数分析证明SG在总体InfoNCE对齐且均匀最优解处消失；推导SG对InfoNCE超额风险的界；分析旋转不变均匀性检验的线性界条件(谱支配e^{βu^T v})；研究各向同性干扰通道下的局部极小值与非对角U统计量修正有限批偏差。

Result: SG超额界为4·3^{3/4}β·√(SG)加线性项，平方根速率与维度无关常数均为紧的，且无任何视图对的平方均值嵌入距离能达到更快速率；高斯核e^{-γ||u-v||^2}当且仅当γ≥β/2时合格，矩匹配则永不合格；纯SG在非均匀代码下会通过增加逐视图干扰降低损失；LeJEPA存在随批大小递减的临界SIGReg权重。

Conclusion: SG可同时对错位与非均匀性提供可证保证并刻画与InfoNCE的差异；在三视图风格保留实验中，仅高于实测增益的对齐权重能去除风格，该增益可区分保留与不保留风格的编码器；InfoNCE训练比从头SG训练达到更低的SG₀.₂损失，揭示存在优化间隙。

Abstract: What can a distribution-matching regularizer such as SIGReg in LeJEPA certify about contrastive learning? We study shared Gaussianization (SG), a characteristic-function Gaussianity test on the average of two normalized views, scaled by an independent $χ_d$ radius. Because disagreeing views shorten the average, one test detects both misalignment and non-uniformity. SG vanishes exactly at the aligned, uniform minimizers of population InfoNCE, and under equal marginals it bounds the InfoNCE excess by $4\cdot 3^{3/4}β$ times the square root of the SG loss, plus a term linear in the loss. The square-root rate and this dimension-free constant are sharp, and no squared mean-embedding distance on view pairs achieves a faster rate. With an explicit alignment term, a rotation-invariant uniformity test gives a linear bound if and only if its spectrum dominates that of InfoNCE's kernel $e^{βu^\top v}$; SG's own test does, Gaussian kernels $e^{-γ\|u-v\|^2}$ qualify exactly when $γ\ge β/2$, and moment matching never does. Away from the optimum, the objectives differ. Along an isotropic nuisance channel, pure SG lowers its loss by adding per-view nuisance whenever the shared code is non-uniform. An alignment weight above the channel's gain makes the nuisance-free solution a strict local minimizer; for LeJEPA, the same rule gives a critical SIGReg weight that decreases with the batch size. At finite batch size, an off-diagonal U-statistic removes a plug-in bias toward misalignment. In controlled latent-variable models, pure SG retains per-view style, an alignment weight above the measured gain removes it, and for LeJEPA at three batch sizes the measured gain separates the encoders that retain style from those that do not. InfoNCE training also reaches a lower SG$_{0.2}$ loss than SG$_{0.2}$ training from scratch, which points to an optimization gap.

</details>


### [112] [Revisiting Explainable AI through Model-Independent Concept Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10301)
*Thomas Schnake,Doreen Schöppenthau,Alexander Meyer,Jacques Corbeil,Klaus-Robert Müller,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DictXAI 通过在输入域中定义可解释的字典元素并计算输入的稀疏编码，将模型预测归因于这些元素，实现可解释、可行动且与架构无关的 AI 解释，优于经典 XAI 和现有概念方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 模型日益复杂，XAI 旨在提升模型透明度。但现有 XAI 方法通常假设输入特征本身可解释，或依赖难以刻画、高度架构相关的中间内部抽象，导致跨模型使用难以一致。

Method: 提出 DictXAI，直接在输入域中通过字典定义概念——字典是一组庞大、可能过完备的预定义元素，每个元素具有可解释含义。技术上，先计算输入的稀疏编码，再将模型预测归因于相关字典元素。

Result: DictXAI 解释具有可行动性，可将 AI 故障（如 Clever Hans 效应）直接归因于数据中可识别的伪影模式，并促进人机在复杂生物医学信号上的对齐。方法可跨多种字典运行，包括学习到的图像基、心电图的解析定义波形以及实验获取的字典元素。

Conclusion: 相比经典 XAI 或现有概念方法，DictXAI 提供了更具可解释性、可行动性和架构无关性的洞察。

Abstract: Modern applications of AI rely on increasingly complex models. Explainable AI (XAI) has emerged as a set of techniques aimed at improving model transparency. However, existing XAI methods typically assume input features to be inherently interpretable, or they rely on intermediate internal abstractions that are difficult to characterize and highly architecture-specific, hindering consistent use across models. To address these limitations, we propose DictXAI, a method that defines concepts directly in the input domain via a dictionary---a large, potentially overcomplete set of predefined elements, each carrying an interpretable meaning. Technically, DictXAI first computes a sparse code of the input and then attributes the model's prediction to the associated dictionary elements. We demonstrate the actionable nature of DictXAI explanations, showing that they can attribute AI malfunctions (e.g., Clever Hans effects) directly to identifiable artifact patterns in the data, while fostering human-AI alignment on intricate biomedical signals. We further demonstrate our method's ability to operate across a wide variety of dictionaries, including learned image bases, analytically defined waveforms for electrocardiography, and experimentally acquired dictionary elements. Overall, our results show that DictXAI provides more interpretable, actionable, and architecture-agnostic insights than classical XAI or existing concept-based approaches.

</details>


### [113] [Koopman Observers for Diffusion Acceleration: Correcting Feature Forecasts with Shallow Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10366)
*Hanru Bai,Yuanchao Xu,Fengyi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出观测校正Koopman框架，用新计算的浅层特征作为观测来校正深层特征预测，从而加速冻结扩散模型采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征缓存方法仅依赖过去特征预测，无法纳入当前去噪状态的变化；作者探究用廉价、新计算的浅层特征作为观测来校正预测。

Method: 利用校准轨迹辨识有限维、时变的Koopman近似，联合描述浅层与深层网络特征的增量；加速采样时用算子预测昂贵深层特征演化，用浅层特征的观测新息校正预测状态，并周期性全量评估刷新观测器，生成模型参数保持不变。

Result: 在每数据集三次各10,000张图像的运行中，相比同四部分步调度下的通道级仿射预测，成对Inception特征MSE在CIFAR-10降低19.9%、在十类ImageNet子集降低11.9%；匹配消融显示观测校正分别额外贡献4.54%和4.67%的降低；相对DDIM-50实现1.89倍和1.85倍实测加速。

Conclusion: 该框架在不重训去噪器的前提下提升了参考采样器保真度，并支持对时间预测与观测校正进行受控比较。

Abstract: Feature caching accelerates diffusion sampling by replacing expensive network evaluations with predictions from previously computed activations. However, forecasts based only on past features cannot directly incorporate changes in the current denoising state. We investigate whether inexpensive, freshly computed features can serve as observations for correcting these predictions. We introduce an observation-corrected Koopman framework for accelerating frozen diffusion models. Using calibration trajectories, we identify finite-dimensional, time-dependent Koopman approximations that jointly describe the increments of shallow and deep network features. During accelerated sampling, these operators predict the evolution of expensive deep features, while innovations in the observed shallow features correct the predicted state. Periodic full evaluations refresh the observer, and all generative-model parameters remain unchanged. This formulation enables controlled comparisons of temporal prediction and observation correction. Across three 10,000-image runs per dataset, our method reduces paired Inception-feature MSE by $19.9\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $11.9\%$ on a ten-class ImageNet subset relative to channelwise affine prediction under the same four-partial-step schedule. Matched ablations attribute additional reductions of $4.54\%$ and $4.67\%$ to observation correction. The observer achieves $1.89\times$ and $1.85\times$ measured speedups over DDIM-50, supporting improved reference-sampler fidelity without retraining the denoiser.

</details>


### [114] [CurveTQ: Rotation-Free Trellis Quantization of LLM Weights via Curvature-Weighted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09212)
*Guanhua Ding,Zi Wang,Ruichao Li,Jack Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CurveTQ，一种无需旋转的两比特权重量化框架，将Hessian的LDL对角权重注入Viterbi分支度量，使搜索在编码块内跟随曲率，从而在多个模型上超越QTIP和Proteus的下游精度，并具有最快的解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优的两比特权重量化器（如QTIP和Proteus）先用随机正交变换旋转权重矩阵，解码时需撤销该旋转，并在欧氏搜索下用trellis或lattice码编码；Hessian仅通过编码块间的误差反馈起作用，导致Hessian的一部分信息未被充分利用。作者希望挖掘被忽略的Hessian信息以提升量化精度。

Method: 作者证明误差反馈将损失转化为逐坐标舍入误差的加权和，其权重为Hessian的LDL分解对角元，而现有量化器计算了这些权重却未加利用。作者将这些权重引入Viterbi分支度量，使搜索在编码块内跟随曲率；并指出旋转的作用正是去除块内变化，因此在本征基下加权与旋转互为替代。基于此构建CurveTQ：无旋转的trellis编解码器，用因子化尺度场处理权重幅度与边缘形状，用闭式分位数表，并为每个编码块存储起始状态以适配误差反馈传入的残差。

Result: 在三个模型上，加权本征搜索与全维随机Hadamard旋转的下游精度差距约在1个百分点以内，而旋转后再加权增益甚微。在两比特下，CurveTQ在三个4-8B Instruct模型上的平均下游精度比QTIP和Proteus高1-3个百分点，即使给两者加上本文的起始状态，它们也各自提升1-3个百分点。在35B混合专家模型上CurveTQ同样领先，据作者所知这是首个在该类模型上的trellis编码结果。由于无需撤销旋转，其解码器在所有测试批大小和比特宽度下都是三者中最快的。

Conclusion: Hessian的LDL对角权重是现有量化器中被忽视的有效信息；将其纳入Viterbi搜索可在块内跟随曲率，使加权本征基搜索可替代随机旋转。基于此的CurveTQ无需旋转、精度领先且解码最快，验证了该思路的有效性与效率优势。

Abstract: The best two-bit weight quantizers for large language models, such as QTIP and Proteus, rotate each weight matrix by a random orthogonal transform, which must be undone at every decoding step, then encode it with a trellis or lattice code under a Euclidean search; the layer Hessian enters only through error feedback between coding blocks. We show that this leaves part of the Hessian unused. Error feedback turns the loss into a weighted sum of per-coordinate rounding errors whose weights, the diagonal of the Hessian's LDL factorization, existing quantizers compute but never read. We put these weights into the Viterbi branch metric, so the search follows the curvature within each coding block. This also explains the rotation: it removes this within-block variation, so weighting in the native basis and rotating are substitutes. On three models the weighted native search matches a full-dimension randomized Hadamard to within about one point of downstream accuracy, and weighting after the rotation gains little. Around this search we build CurveTQ, a trellis codec with no rotation, which handles the weights' amplitude and marginal shape with a factored scale field and a closed-form quantile table, and stores a start state per coding block so the trellis can adapt to the residual that error feedback carries into it. At two bits CurveTQ is 1-3 points higher in mean downstream accuracy than QTIP and Proteus on three 4-8B Instruct models, even after both are given our start state, which alone lifts either baseline by 1-3 points. It also leads on a 35B mixture of experts, to our knowledge the first trellis-coded result on such a model. With no rotation to undo, our decoder is the fastest of the three at all tested batch sizes and bit widths.

</details>


### [115] [Training Parallel Speculative Draft Models by Directly Minimizing Expected Decoding Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10411)
*Yunxiao Zhao,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将推测解码建模为马尔可夫奖励过程，提出EDR（期望解码轮数）训练目标，无需额外超参数，并推导精确时序差分梯度与离线评估器，微调DSpark和DFly后在九项基准上提升平均接受长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行与半自回归草稿模型通过单次前向提出整块token以提升起草效率，但其训练存在难点：某位置的草稿分布依赖于解码轮次的起点，而轮次起点又取决于此前轮次接受的token数量，形成跨轮耦合。现有训练目标多依赖块局部代理指标，忽略该耦合，未能直接优化全局解码效率。

Method: 将推测解码形式化为马尔可夫奖励过程，导出EDR目标：以状态占用度对局部拒绝代价加权，恰等于期望解码轮数，且不引入辅助超参数；进一步推导精确的时序差分梯度，支持从目标模型rollout进行无偏随机优化；同一框架给出精确的离线轮数评估器，可在共享rollout上配对比较草稿模型而无需实际运行推测解码。

Result: 以EDR微调两个当前最优草稿模型DSpark与DFly，在数学推理、代码生成与对话三类共九项基准上，平均接受长度稳定提升，并优于现有训练目标。

Conclusion: 通过马尔可夫奖励过程视角，EDR为并行/半自回归草稿模型提供了直接优化全局解码效率的无超参目标、无偏随机优化梯度及高效离线评估手段，实证表明其一致优于既有代理目标。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a low-cost draft model to propose tokens that the full-size target model verifies in parallel. Parallel and semi-autoregressive (semi- AR) drafters improve drafting efficiency by proposing an entire block in a single forward pass, but training them raises a new difficulty: the draft distribution for a given position depends on where the decoding round starts, and where rounds start depends on how many tokens earlier rounds accepted. Existing training objectives typically rely on block-local surrogates that ignore this cross-round coupling, and therefore do not directly optimize the global decoding efficiency. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for training and evaluating these drafters by representing speculative decoding as a Markov reward process. This formulation yields the Expected Decoding Rounds (EDR) objective, which weights local rejection costs by state occupancies and exactly equals the expected number of decoding rounds. Unlike prior surrogate objectives, EDR introduces no auxiliary hyperparameters. We then derive an exact temporal-difference gradient that supports unbiased stochastic optimization from target-model rollouts. The same framework also yields an exact offline evaluator for round counts, enabling paired drafter comparisons on shared target rollouts without running speculative decoding. Finetuning two state-of-the- art drafters, DSpark and DFly, with EDR consistently improves mean accepted length and outperforms existing training objectives across nine benchmarks spanning math reasoning, code generation, and chat.

</details>


### [116] [Consistent Distribution Matching for Data-Free Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09221)
*Yuxiang Fu,Qi Yan,Zike Wu,Yongxing Zhang,Purang Abolmaesumi,Lele Wang,Renjie Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Consistent Distribution Matching，一种无需模拟、无需数据的蒸馏方法，用于加速扩散与流模型，统一学生网络同时完成样本生成与得分估计，仅需冻结教师与可训练学生两个模型即可优化单一目标，并证明其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型因数值积分计算昂贵而推理缓慢；现有蒸馏方法依赖精心筛选的数据集、昂贵的教师采样轨迹或辅助代理网络，导致训练与扩展复杂。

Method: 核心洞察是用单一学生网络统一样本生成与得分估计，框架仅包含一个冻结教师模型和一个可训练学生模型，优化单一目标函数，实现免模拟且免数据的蒸馏。

Result: 证明最小化该目标意味着学生流映射推前分布到教师边缘分布的 Wasserstein 收敛；在 ImageNet 256×256 上，1-NFE 达到 FID 2.04，训练 40 轮内 4-NFE FID 为 1.37，超越无数据条件下的最先进蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种简洁高效的无数据蒸馏范式，在保持强生成能力的同时显著降低推理开销与训练复杂度。

Abstract: Flow and diffusion models suffer from slow inference due to computationally expensive numerical integration. Distillation provides a promising way for a student model to learn from a teacher's dynamics, enabling one-step or few-step generation. However, existing methods often depend on curated distillation datasets, costly teacher rollouts, or auxiliary proxy networks, which complicate model training and scaling. In this work, we propose Consistent Distribution Matching, a simulation-free and data-free distillation method for accelerating diffusion and flow models while preserving strong generative capacity. Our key insight is to unify sample generation and score estimation with one student network. Thus, our framework uses only two models, a frozen teacher and a trainable student, and optimizes one objective. We prove that minimizing our objective indicates Wasserstein convergence of the student flow-map pushforwards to the teacher marginals. On ImageNet 256$\times$256, our method attains an FID of 2.04 with a single function evaluation (1-NFE) and a 4-NFE FID of 1.37 within 40 epochs of training, surpassing the state-of-the-art distillation baselines without data. Our code code and model are available at https://consistentdmd.github.io/.

</details>


### [117] [Two-Level Softmax Sampling Done Right: Correcting Bias from Size Imbalance and Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10483)
*Walid Bendada,Guillaume Salha-Galvan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出两级softmax（2LS）采样存在系统性偏差，源于忽略簇大小不平衡和簇内相似度离散度，提出S-2LS与SD-2LS两种校正方法，在几乎无额外开销下提供更好的softmax近似，并在五个大规模数据集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从softmax分布中采样是机器学习的基础操作，但其复杂度随候选项数量线性增长，大规模下难以精确执行。两级softmax（2LS）采样通过先采簇再采簇内项实现次线性采样，被广泛采用。然而其采样偏差问题此前未被充分认识。

Method: 作者首先理论分析2LS的偏差来源，指出其因忽略簇大小不平衡（cluster size imbalance）和簇内相似度离散度（intra-cluster similarity dispersion）而对簇进行了错误加权，导致系统性偏差。据此提出两种校正方法：Size-Corrected 2LS（S-2LS）校正簇大小不平衡；Size- and Dispersion-Corrected 2LS（SD-2LS）同时校正簇大小与离散度。

Result: 理论上证明两种方法可提供可证明更优的softmax近似，且计算开销可忽略甚至为零。在五个大规模数据集上的深入实验验证了改进后的采样性质。

Conclusion: 2LS存在由簇大小不平衡与簇内离散度引起的系统性采样偏差；S-2LS与SD-2LS能有效校正该偏差、提升近似质量且开销极小。作者建议未来工作中以二者一致替代标准2LS。

Abstract: Sampling from a softmax distribution is a fundamental operation in machine learning, but its linear complexity in the number of items makes exact sampling impractical at scale. Two-level softmax (2LS) sampling is a popular alternative enabling sublinear-time sampling. Assuming items are partitioned into clusters, 2LS first samples a cluster and then an item within it. In this paper, we show that, despite its advantages, 2LS introduces systematic and undesirable sampling biases, which arise from misweighting clusters by ignoring both cluster size imbalance and intra-cluster similarity dispersion. We propose two sampling methods, Size-Corrected 2LS (S-2LS) and Size- and Dispersion-Corrected 2LS (SD-2LS), which correct these biases and provide provably better softmax approximations with negligible to non-existent computational overhead. In-depth experiments on five large-scale datasets validate the improved sampling properties of our methods. We recommend their consistent use in place of standard 2LS in future work.

</details>


### [118] [Conditional Accuracy Profiles: Diagnosing LLM Judges across Deployment Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09229)
*Wenqi Li,Bin Liu,Mindi Ruan,Chuanbo Hu,Minglei Yin,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出条件准确率画像（CAP）框架，将成对LLM评判准确率分解为内容敏感性、鲁棒性与理由质量三类共八个条件，用于诊断评判器在不同部署条件下的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-judge评估多以单一准确率概括性能，掩盖了评判器在特定部署条件下成功或失败的情形，缺乏可操作的选择依据。

Method: CAP为基准无关的事后诊断框架：在基准提供注释时可直接应用，亦可经任务子集代理近似，或通过生成扰动对进行受控增强。作者在六个成对评判基准、七个LLM评判器上实例化CAP，并构建了支持全部八个条件的受控测试平台judgerEva-Standard。

Result: CAP揭示了聚合准确率所掩盖的画像差异：在judgerEva的评判器无关Hard-Constructed子集上，对遗漏限定条件最敏感的两个评判器总体准确率排名位于七者中的后三位，说明遗漏敏感性无法由聚合准确率预测。跨基准中位置鲁棒性排名稳定性最强（平均Spearman ρ̄=0.87），但在JudgeBench-Pro对抗压力下最为脆弱，平均准确率下降幅度在共享条件中最大，且主导退化通道因评判器而异。

Conclusion: 条件级画像比聚合准确率更能为LLM评判器的选择提供可操作依据。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge is now a standard tool for scalable evaluation, but judge performance is still often summarized by a single accuracy number. This aggregate view hides the deployment conditions under which a judge succeeds or fails. We introduce \textbf{Conditional Accuracy Profiling} (CAP), a post-hoc diagnostic framework that decomposes pairwise LLM-judge accuracy into eight conditions organized into content sensitivity, robustness, and rationale quality. CAP is benchmark-agnostic: it can be applied directly when a benchmark provides the required annotations, approximately through task-subset proxies, or through controlled augmentation when perturbation pairs can be generated. We instantiate CAP on seven LLM judges across six pairwise judging benchmarks, including \textsc{judgerEva-Standard}, a controlled testbed we created to support all eight conditions. CAP exposes profile differences hidden by aggregate accuracy: on \textsc{judgerEva}'s judge-independent Hard-Constructed subset, the two judges most sensitive to omitted qualifications rank in the bottom three of seven by overall accuracy, so omission sensitivity is not predicted by aggregate accuracy. Across benchmarks, Position Robustness shows the strongest rank stability (mean Spearman $\barρ{=}0.87$) but is itself fragile under JudgeBench-Pro adversarial stress, showing the largest mean accuracy drop among the shared conditions, though the dominant degradation channel varies by judge. Condition-level profiles provide a more actionable basis than aggregate accuracy for selecting LLM judges.

</details>


### [119] [Oracle-Efficient and Parameter-Free Agnostic Smoothed Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10499)
*Sasha Voitovych,Adam Block,Alexander Rakhlin,Abhishek Shetty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究平滑在线学习，首次提出无需已知基测度μ的无参数oracle高效算法，在agnostic设定下达到次线性遗憾，遗憾界为Õ(d√(T/σ))，每轮仅需一次ERM oracle调用，并引入若干新技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习虽通用但存在统计与计算瓶颈。平滑在线学习通过假设条件协变量分布密度有界，在对抗与随机设定之间取得平衡，能兼顾经典学习的统计与计算保证及在线学习的灵活性。然而现有oracle高效算法需要(i)对基测度μ的采样访问，或(ii)标签可被固定假设完美预测，这两点限制了适用性。经典统计学习中ERM可在agnostic设定下无需知道数据分布即高效学习，因此本文旨在消除上述两个假设。

Method: 提出基于高斯Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader的算法，该算法为parameter-free：不需要知道基测度μ、平滑参数σ或时间跨度T；每轮仅需一次ERM oracle调用。在建立遗憾界过程中引入若干可能具有独立价值的新技术。

Result: 给出首个在agnostic设定下、无需知道μ且达到次线性遗憾的oracle高效算法；对VC维为d的二分类，遗憾界为Õ(d√(T/σ))，在√d因子内为最优。

Conclusion: 现有oracle高效算法的两个假设均非必要；本文算法无需μ知识即可在agnostic设定下实现oracle高效学习，达到接近最优的遗憾界，并贡献了若干新技术。

Abstract: Online learning is an attractive framework in many domains because it permits well-defined learning even when data are dependent or chosen adversarially. This generality, however, comes at a steep price, introducing significant statistical and computational barriers. Recently, smoothed online learning has emerged as a promising framework that interpolates between the fully adversarial and fully stochastic settings by assuming that the conditional law of each covariate has density at most $1/σ$ with respect to some fixed base measure $μ$, and it is known to match the statistical and computational guarantees of classical learning while still allowing for much of the flexibility of online learning. However, existing oracle-efficient algorithms require either (i) sampling access to the base measure $μ$ or (ii) labels that are perfectly predicted by a fixed hypothesis. Both assumptions limit the applicability of these algorithms, in contrast to statistical learning, where empirical risk minimization (ERM) learns efficiently in the agnostic setting without any knowledge of the data distribution. We show that neither assumption is necessary, giving the first oracle-efficient algorithm that achieves sublinear regret in the agnostic setting without knowledge of $μ$. Our algorithm, based on Gaussian Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader, is parameter-free: it requires no knowledge of $μ$, the smoothing parameter $σ$, or the horizon $T$, and it achieves regret $\widetilde O(d\sqrt{T/σ})$ for binary classes of VC dimension $d$ with a single call to an ERM oracle per round, which is optimal up to a $\sqrt{d}$ factor. En route to establishing the regret bound, we introduce several new techniques that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [120] [Symmetry-Informed Causal Partial Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09230)
*Uzair Akbar,Zulfiqar Zaidi,Niki Kilbertus,Krikamol Muandet,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将数据对称性（因果效应在特定数据变换下的不变性）作为部分识别（PI）的新约束来源，通过对因果函数施加形状约束和测度变换两种方式实现，并在理论与实验中证明其能收紧经典PI模型的因果效应界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分识别通过将关于数据生成的假设编码为约束优化问题来估计因果效应的界，即使因果效应本身不可识别，这些界也足以指导政策决策。但PI界在实践中常常是"空泛的"（vacuous），因此研究者希望尽可能多地引入领域知识作为额外约束以使界更具信息量。论文旨在寻找一个新且未被充分利用的约束来源。

Method: 论文将已知的数据对称性——即因果效应在某些数据变换下的不变性——作为PI的新约束来源。具体操作化为两种方式：（1）对因果函数施加形状约束；（2）通过数据预处理进行测度变换（change of measure），在此基础上重新表述PI问题。

Result: 在两种经典的PI模型下，两种方法都被证明能收紧因果效应的界；该结论在总体（population）情形下得到理论证明，并在有限样本情形下通过实验验证。

Conclusion: 该框架将数据对称性确立为稳健因果推断中一种自然且尚未被充分利用的背景知识来源，扩展了部分识别的约束体系。

Abstract: Partial identification (PI) entails estimating bounds on causal effects by encoding different assumptions on data generation as a constrained optimization problem. Such bounds can suffice to inform policy decisions even if the causal effect itself is not identifiable. Often vacuous in practice, practitioners seek to exhaustively encode domain knowledge as additional constraints to make the PI bounds more informative. We introduce known data symmetries -- invariance of the causal effect under certain data transformations -- as a new source of constraints to inform PI. We operationalize this as a shape constraint on the causal function, and via a change of measure against which PI is posed using simple data pre-processing. Both approaches are shown to sharpen bounds under two canonical PI models. This is shown both theoretically for the population case, and via experiments in the finite-sample case. More broadly, our framework establishes data symmetries as a natural, underutilized source of background knowledge for robust causal inference.

</details>


### [121] [Why Forget-Only Unlearning Needs Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10519)
*Luka Radić,Vikrant Singhal,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"仅遗忘式"机器遗忘（仅给定训练模型与待遗忘样本，无保留数据或额外训练信息），揭示了其可行性与学习算法相关，并给出了匹配重训练精度的下界及所需记忆信息量的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘希望删除样本后的模型接近从头重训，而"仅遗忘式"设定最为受限（无保留数据、无训练信息）。作者追问两个核心问题：(1) 仅遗忘式遗忘是否总是可能？(2) 若可行，算法必须记忆哪些训练信息？动机在于厘清普通学习中丢弃的信息是否对后续删除是必需的。

Method: 通过在"不同数据集产生相同训练模型、但删除同样样本后需要不同输出"的情形上构造分析，推导遗忘输出匹配重训练精度的下界，并针对若干标准学习算法具体化；进一步推导算法在应对任意删除请求时必须记忆训练数据的信息量下界，并以简单阈值学习器为实例进行分析。

Result: 仅遗忘式遗忘的可行性依赖于具体学习算法；针对多种标准学习算法得到了匹配重训练精度的下界；对简单阈值学习器，所需记忆的信息量可能高达整个数据集，尽管普通训练仅保留一个边界点。

Conclusion: 普通学习中丢弃的信息在后续删除时可能仍被需要，因此面向仅遗忘式遗忘设计的模型可能需要比标准训练保留更多信息。

Abstract: Machine unlearning asks for a deletion algorithm whose output is close to retraining from scratch without the selected forget examples. In this work, we study forget-only unlearning, where the deletion algorithm receives only the trained model and the examples to forget, with no retained data or extra training information. We ask whether forget-only unlearning is always possible. We first show that this depends on the learning method: different datasets can produce the same trained model but require very different outputs after the same examples are removed. Using this observation, we derive lower bounds on how accurately unlearning can match retraining and instantiate them for several standard learning algorithms. We then ask what must be true when forget-only unlearning succeeds. To this end, we derive lower bounds on what an algorithm must memorize about the training data to handle arbitrary deletion requests. For simple threshold learners, the required information can be as large as the entire dataset, even though ordinary training keeps only one boundary point. Overall, our results show that information discarded during ordinary learning may be needed later for deletion, so models designed for forget-only unlearning may need to retain more information than standard training does.

</details>


### [122] [An Informational Curse of Horizon in Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09247)
*John L. Zhou,Yuxuan Dong,Jonathan C. Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示目标条件策略学习中还存在一种"信息层面的horizon诅咒"：延长目标重标注horizon会显著降低策略泛化与性能，其机理是动作与事后重标注目标间条件互信息随horizon下降，导致策略敏感性由目标信息转向状态信息；通过将短horizon策略的输入Jacobian蒸馏到长horizon策略可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往将目标到达策略的学习困难归因于"horizon诅咒"，表现为时序差分备份中的偏差累积和优势估计噪声。作者认为还存在一种此前未被识别的信息层面的horizon诅咒：在目标条件策略学习中，增大目标重标注horizon会显著损害策略的泛化能力和性能。这一现象在从离线数据学习通用策略时尤为重要，但此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 作者使用oracle规划器进行一系列受控实验，将训练时采样的目标horizon与测试时要求策略到达的目标horizon解耦。即便只在邻近子目标序列上评估，目标条件行为克隆（BC）策略仍表现出严重且依赖于训练horizon的性能退化，而强化学习（RL）目标可缓解该问题。作者从理论上将该现象解释为动作与事后重标注目标之间条件互信息随horizon的下降，并通过策略输入Jacobian实证测量，发现BC和RL策略在长horizon目标上训练后都会出现从目标信息到状态信息的敏感性偏移。基于此，提出将短horizon策略的输入Jacobian蒸馏到长horizon策略中，并验证其在组合操作任务上的效果。

Result: 1）即使仅在邻近子目标序列上评估，目标条件BC策略仍出现严重的、随训练horizon增长而加剧的性能退化，RL目标可部分缓解；2）该现象可由动作与事后重标注目标间条件互信息随horizon下降来解释；3）BC与RL策略训练于长horizon目标时，其输入Jacobian显示敏感性从目标信息向状态信息偏移；4）将短horizon策略的输入Jacobian蒸馏到长horizon策略能带来显著性能提升，尤其在组合操作任务中效果突出。

Conclusion: 目标重标注horizon是离线数据学习通用策略时需要重点考虑的关键因素。研究揭示了信息层面的horizon诅咒及其机制，并表明通过Jacobian蒸馏可有效缓解该问题，为通用目标条件策略学习提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: The difficulty of learning goal-reaching policies is often attributed to a "curse of horizon" that manifests as bias accumulation in temporal-difference backups and noisy advantage estimates. In this work, we identify an additional informational curse of horizon in goal-conditioned policy learning, where increasing the goal relabeling horizon can significantly reduce policy generalization and performance. Through a series of controlled experiments with oracle planners, we decouple the goal horizons sampled during training from those that the policy is asked to reach at test time. Even when evaluated only on a sequence of nearby subgoals, goal-conditioned behavioral cloning (BC) policies suffer from severe, training horizon-dependent performance degradation that is mitigated by reinforcement learning (RL) objectives. We explain this phenomenon as a horizon-dependent decrease in the conditional mutual information between actions and hindsight-relabeled goals, and find empirically that both BC and RL policies trained on longer-horizon goals exhibit a shift in sensitivity from goal to state information, as measured by the policy's input Jacobians. Motivated by this observation, we find that distilling the input Jacobians of short-horizon policies into long-horizon policies yields significant performance gains, especially in combinatorial manipulation tasks. Taken together, our results highlight goal relabeling horizon as an important consideration when learning generalist policies from offline data.

</details>


### [123] [Evaluating Trajectory Features for Routing Final-Layer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09272)
*Yupeng Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在冻结的 SmolLM3-3B-Base 与 Qwen3.5-4B-Base 上，用配对执行获得的下一 token 损失差监督注意力路由器，评估隐藏状态外推误差、曲率与误差变化是否优于不确定性、一步位移、位置与状态投影等基线。结果：六项预设比较经族系校正后均无正向增益，轨迹类摘要的增量价值有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力路由需要一个能预测当前前缀上注意力价值的信号。已有基线（不确定性、一步位移、位置、状态投影）之外，本文检验轨迹类摘要（隐藏状态外推误差、曲率、误差变化）是否能提供额外预测力，并区分"分配质量"与"实际推理收益"。

Method: 在冻结的 SmolLM3-3B-Base 与 Qwen3.5-4B-Base 检查点上，对最终注意力层做配对执行（paired executions），得到带符号的下一 token 损失差作为监督信号，训练效用监督路由器。在 100 本留出 PG-19 书籍上，以相同的因果 20% 调用配额进行评测。设置六项预设比较，进行族系校正（familywise correction），并加入参数匹配的固定投影控制组、冻结阈值长视野漂移分析、实际选中查询执行的延迟与 NLL 度量。

Result: 六项预设比较在族系校正后均无正向增益。在 Qwen3.5 中，参数匹配的固定投影控制相比轨迹路由器将 NLL 降低 0.00356 nats/token（95% 区间 0.00218–0.00487）。次要结果依赖于移除的操作、特征位置与打分视野；冻结阈值在更长视野下也显著漂移。实际选中查询执行可带来小幅长序列延迟下降，但 NLL 上升；而学习型路由器在缓存续写时仍更慢。

Conclusion: 该研究界定了轨迹类摘要（外推误差、曲率、误差变化）增量价值的边界：它们未能超越更简单的基线信号，且分配质量不等同于实际推理收益。路由信号设计与推理加速的收益需分开评估。

Abstract: Attention routing requires a signal that predicts the value of attention on the current prefix. We evaluate whether hidden-state extrapolation error, curvature and error change improve this prediction beyond uncertainty, one-step displacement, position and state projections. Paired executions of the final attention layer supply signed next-token loss differences in frozen SmolLM3-3B-Base and Qwen3.5-4B-Base checkpoints. Utility-supervised routers are tested on 100 held-out PG-19 books at an identical causal 20 percent invocation quota. None of six prespecified comparisons shows a positive gain after familywise correction. In Qwen3.5, a parameter-matched fixed-projection control lowers NLL by 0.00356 nats/token relative to the trajectory router (95 percent interval 0.00218 to 0.00487). Secondary results depend on the operation removed, feature location and scoring horizon; frozen thresholds also drift substantially at longer horizons. Actual selected-query execution yields small long-sequence latency reductions with increased NLL, while learned routers remain slower during cached continuation. The study identifies limits on the incremental value of these trajectory summaries and separates allocation quality from measured inference benefit.

</details>


### [124] [Twist Flow for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09281)
*Shiqin Zeng,Zijun Deng,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 joint twist-flow，一种增强流匹配方法，通过从增强源状态 (z_x, y) 到增强终端状态 (x, z_y) 的连续传输学习后验采样，缓解条件生成模型因配对训练导致的过度确定性映射问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯反问题中的后验采样需在与观测一致的同时刻画合理解的范围。直接条件生成模型引入潜在噪声建模模糊性，但配对反问题训练仍可能倾向于学习从观测到目标的近乎确定性映射，导致样本虽观测一致却低估后验变异性，尤其在后验多峰时出现覆盖不足、模态失真或不同可行解间的人为过渡。

Method: joint twist-flow 采用增强流匹配公式，学习从增强源状态 (z_x, y) 到增强终端状态 (x, z_y) 的连续传输。其中 x 为目标变量，y 为观测，z_x 为后验采样的高斯参考坐标，z_y 为观测分支对应的高斯似然侧坐标。在高斯观测模型下，z_y 由与观测兼容性相关的归一化观测残差启发得到；其作用不是替代 x 的不确定性，而是将 x 的生成样本与观测一致性耦合，在减少似然不一致变异的同时保留弱约束方向上的变异性。

Result: 在具有参考后验样本的低维反问题上验证，joint twist-flow 比直接条件流基线更好地保持多峰后验支撑；在图像恢复与地震地下速度模型反演上进一步评估，显示在保持观测一致性的同时提高了后验变异性。

Conclusion: joint twist-flow 通过增强流匹配的双向坐标耦合机制，有效缓解条件生成模型在后验采样中的变异性低估问题，在低维与高维实际反演任务中均表现出更好的后验支撑保持与变异性刻画能力，同时维持观测一致性。

Abstract: In Bayesian inverse problems, posterior sampling requires generating samples that are consistent with given observations while capturing the range of plausible solutions. Direct conditional generative models introduce latent noise to model this ambiguity, but paired inverse-problem training can still encourage an almost deterministic map from the observation to the target. As a result, generated samples may be observation-consistent while under-representing posterior variability, especially when the posterior is multimodal, leading to undercoverage, mode distortion, or artificial transitions between distinct feasible solutions. We propose joint twist-flow, an augmented flow-matching formulation that learns a continuous transport from the augmented source state $(z_x, y)$ to the augmented terminal state $(x, z_y)$. Here x is the target variable, $y$ is the observation, $z_x$ is the Gaussian reference coordinate for posterior sampling, and $z_y$ is a Gaussian likelihood-side coordinate associated with the observation branch. Under a Gaussian observation model, $z_y$ is motivated by the normalized observation residual associated with observation compatibility. Its role is not to replace uncertainty in $x$, but to couple generated samples of x to observation consistency, helping reduce likelihood-inconsistent variation while preserving variability in weakly constrained directions. We validate the method on low-dimensional inverse problems with reference posterior samples, where joint twist-flow better preserves multimodal posterior support than a direct conditional-flow baseline. We further evaluate the method on image restoration and seismic subsurface velocity-model inversion, showing increased posterior variability while maintaining observation consistency.

</details>


### [125] [Self-attention summary networks for subsurface velocity-model building from common-image gathers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09282)
*Shiqin Zeng,Yunlin Zeng,Abhinav Prakash Gahlot,Zijun Deng,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多尺度自注意力摘要网络，将高维3D CIG体映射为紧凑条件嵌入，用于概率速度反演的流匹配生成建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIG蕴含速度模型误差信息，但传统成像流程只将其作诊断工具，未充分利用；直接以原始CIG作为条件会带来高维、背景速度不匹配导致的变异性问题。

Method: 构建多尺度自注意力摘要网络，将3D CIG体压缩为保留偏移相关运动学结构与空间连贯性的条件嵌入；以该嵌入为条件，用流匹配模型学习从高斯源分布到速度场后验分布的输运映射。

Result: 数值实验中，相比直接以原始CIG为条件，摘要网络提升了后验速度推断效果；多尺度注意力设计对背景模型不匹配更鲁棒，后验重建更准确、预测不确定性更低。

Conclusion: 将CIG通过多尺度注意力摘要为紧凑嵌入并作为生成式反演条件，可有效利用CIG信息，提升概率速度反演的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Common-image gathers (CIGs) contain physically meaningful information about velocity-model errors through reflector focusing and residual moveout, but in conventional imaging workflows they are typically used only as diagnostic tools. In this work, we propose a multiscale self-attention summary network that maps high-dimensional 3D CIG volumes into compact conditioning embeddings for probabilistic subsurface velocity inversion. These learned embeddings preserve offset-dependent kinematic structure and spatial coherence while reducing variability caused by background-velocity mismatch. Conditioned on these summary embeddings, a flow-matching model learns a transport from a Gaussian source distribution to the posterior distribution of plausible velocity fields. Numerical experiments show that, compared with direct conditioning on raw CIGs, the proposed summary network improves posterior velocity inference. In particular, the multiscale attention design provides greater robustness to background-model mismatch, yielding more accurate posterior reconstructions and lower predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [126] [LeCuration: A Tiny World Model as a Data Curation Multi-Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09285)
*Mayank Sengupta,Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以单个数据集的独特设定与物理规律为中心的数据策展新思路，训练小型世界模型LeCuration（基于LeWM潜变量编码器/预测器并加装DiT解码器），用于服务下游更大的模型。在CS:GO游戏数据上，其嵌入可用于异常检测与内容聚类，自回归预测游戏状态可用于定性检查动作-状态一致性。当前仅为定性概念验证，尚未报告定量策展指标或下游训练结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI（如仓库机器人、游戏内智能体）多运行于物理规律有限的封闭物理世界中，针对这类应用进行数据的组织、筛选与策展需要贴合各数据集特有的设定与物理规律。

Method: 训练小型世界模型LeCuration作为独立下游大模型的数据策展工具；以LeWorldModel（LeWM）作为潜变量编码器与预测器，并引入扩散Transformer（DiT）解码器，为自回归游戏rollout补充视觉画面。

Result: 模型嵌入可作为异常检测信号和基于内容的聚类启发式；用该模型自回归预测游戏状态可对动作-状态一致性进行定性检查。实验为CS:GO游戏数据的定性概念验证案例研究。

Conclusion: 该方法在小世界模型驱动的数据策展上具备可行性，但尚未报告定量策展指标与下游训练结果，这被列为关键的下一步工作。

Abstract: Many applications of physical AI run within finite or closed physical worlds with a limited set of physical laws governing object behavior. Examples include robots working in a warehouse and agents moving around in a video game. In order to better organize, filter, and curate data for physical AI applications, we propose a new approach centered on the unique settings and physical laws of individual datasets. We train LeCuration, a small world model intended to serve as a data curation tool for a separate, larger downstream model. To build this model, we choose LeWorldModel (LeWM)as our latent encoder and predictor, adding a diffusion transformer (DiT) decoder to add visuals to autoregressive gameplay rollout. We find that the embeddings of this model can be used as an anomaly detection signal and as a content-based clustering heuristic, and that auto-regressively predicting the game state with this model allows us to qualitatively check for action-state consistency. This paper presents a qualitative, proof-of-concept case study on CS:GO gameplay data; we do not yet report quantitative curation metrics or downstream training results, which we identify as the key next step.

</details>


### [127] [Node-level Graph Neural Architecture Search Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09297)
*Lintao Yanga,Sirui Lia,Yaqing Wang,Pietro Liò,Xu Shen,Baisong Liu,Chengbin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出节点级图神经架构搜索算法N-GNAS，为不同节点子集自动选择合适架构，并引入对比学习损失，在八个节点/图分类数据集上超越现有GNAS与人工设计GNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN与架构搜索框架对所有节点施加统一卷积操作，忽视节点在结构和特征上的差异，导致模型性能下降，且随层数增加易出现过平滑问题。

Method: N-GNAS在更新节点特征时，为每个节点子集自动选择适配的网络架构，实现节点级架构搜索；同时引入对比学习损失，拉近同类样本特征、推远异类样本特征。

Result: 在八个节点分类与图分类数据集上，方法优于当前主流GNAS技术及传统人工设计GNN；例如在CiteSeer数据集上达到78.26%的准确率。

Conclusion: 节点级架构搜索结合对比学习能有效缓解统一卷积与过平滑问题，验证了为不同节点自适应选择架构的优越性。

Abstract: In recent years, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and architecture search frameworks have gained extensive application in non-Euclidean data processing, attributable to their superior capacity in managing unstructured data. Nevertheless, traditional approaches typically apply uniform convolution operations to all nodes, regardless of their varying structural and feature characteristics, which can undermine model performance and result in over-smoothing issues as the number of layers increases. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we propose a \textbf{N}ode-Level \textbf{G}raph \textbf{N}eural \textbf{A}rchitecture \textbf{S}earch (N-GNAS) algorithm. It can automatically choose an appropriate network architecture for each subset of nodes when updating node features. N-GNAS also introduces a contrastive learning loss to separate sample features from different categories and vice versa. In experiments conducted on eight datasets for node and graph classification, our methodology outperforms current leading GNAS techniques and traditional human-designed GNNs. For example, it achieves an accuracy rate of 78.26\% on the CiteSeer dataset.

</details>


### [128] [Kuration SDK: Addressing the Virtual2Real Gap via Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09305)
*Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Mayank Sengupta,Tejal Bedmutha,Siri Reddy,Sahiti Dharmavaram,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出以动作条件世界模型（action-conditioned world model）的现有基准正从视觉相似度转向动作语义和物理接地指标，但对领域与任务无关的训练仍信号不足。作者在CounterStrike游戏数据上训练并评估扩散世界模型，发现可玩性与FVD、LPIPS、JEDi等指标不对应，称之为Virtual2Real gap；提出通过数据策划（curation）与多项诊断属性度量来提供更稳健的信号，并开源了通用物理AI数据策划工具包Kuration SDK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型的基准仍在演化，虽然已从视觉相似度指标转向动作语义和物理接地指标，但对领域与任务无关（domain and task-agnostic）的训练而言，现有基准提供的信号有限。作者观察到视觉相似度指标无法反映实际可玩性，因此需要一种在训练开始前就能提供可靠信号的方法来弥合虚实差距。

Method: 作者在CounterStrike游戏数据上训练并评估扩散世界模型，对比定性可玩性与FVD、LPIPS、JEDi等指标的对应关系。在此基础上提出多种数据策划（curation）策略，并开发通用物理AI数据策划工具包Kuration SDK，用于策划原始游戏数据并测量多种诊断属性。

Result: 实验证实定性可玩性与FVD、LPIPS、JEDi等指标不对应，即存在Virtual2Real gap。Kuration SDK成功揭示了该差距在特定案例中的根因：两个在相同游戏地图、动作与状态分布上训练的世界模型，尽管LPIPS和FVD分数非常接近，实际游玩时表现却差异显著。

Conclusion: 在缺乏可靠基准的情况下，策划原始游戏数据并测量多种诊断属性，能在训练开始前提供更稳健的信号以弥合Virtual2Real gap。Kuration SDK有潜力揭示特定数据集中Virtual2Real gap的根因，并加速高样本效率训练数据集的开发。

Abstract: Benchmarks for measuring the quality of action-conditioned world models are still evolving and shifting away from visual similarity-based metrics to action-semantic and physically-grounded metrics. However, for domain and task-agnostic action-conditioned world model training, existing benchmarks provide a limited signal.
