CERO instead adapts which prompts are selected, how often they are revisited across rounds, and how many groups are generated in each round. A compact Fenchel representation linearizes the dependence on cumulative exposure, while projected online gradient descent updates prompt-specific supporting slopes and a shared budget price using reward-variation feedback and budget deviations. We establish pathwise guarantees for the surrogate allocation objective against fixed-rate and same-path time-varying benchmarks, with explicit terms for proxy discrepancy and rate variation. Under matched training-response budgets, CERO attains the highest avg@16 macro-average on each of three backbones across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Mechanistic analyses link CERO's prompt choices to within-group reward contrast, while multi-seed ablations show gains from adaptive pacing over both uniform and preset spending schedules.

</details>


### [163] [Few-Shot Learning for Personalised Automated Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09692)
*Heinke Hihn,Ibrahim Eisawy,Patrick Thiam,Hans A. Kestler,Friedhelm Schwenker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自动疼痛评估中从群体级到个体级的评估转变重新解释为任务域偏移，采用少样本学习（元学习子领域）实现个性化分类器。在BioVid、SenseEmotion和PMED三个数据集上验证，结合k-shot条件化样本可进一步提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疼痛感知在个体间差异显著，导致基于群体的分类器难以在数据集内所有被试上泛化，因此需要个性化分类器来应对被试间变异性。

Method: 将群体级到个体级评估的转变重新表述为任务域偏移（观测类别固定、目标被试变化），采用少样本学习进行个性化，并使用support样本实现k-shot条件化；在BioVid、SenseEmotion及PMED数据集上以留一被试交叉验证（LOSO-CV）评估，并进行消融实验及个性化效应分析。

Result: 在LOSO-CV协议下，二进制与多类设置中BioVid达85.75%和35.49%，SenseEmotion达82.37%和41.88%，PMED达90.47%（仅二进制基准）；使用k-shot条件化后分别提升至86.25%、40.06%、83.43%、44.08%和91.25%。

Conclusion: 支持集条件化的少样本自适应能在被试间变异性下提升平均性能，表明少样本学习是自动疼痛评估个性化的有效途径。

Abstract: Pain perception varies substantially across individuals, making it difficult for population-based classifiers to generalise across all subjects in a dataset. One way to account for subject variability is to train personalised classifiers. In this work, we evaluate Few-Shot Learning, a sub-area of Meta-Learning, as an approach to personalisation in automated pain assessment. We re-interpret the shift from population-level to subject-level evaluation as a task-domain shift, where the observed classes remain fixed but the target subject changes. We evaluate our method on the BioVid Pain Database, the SenseEmotion Database, and the PainMonit Experimental Dataset (PMED), reaching 85.75% and 35.49% accuracy on BioVid and 82.37% and 41.88% on SenseEmotion in the binary and multi-class settings under a Leave-One-Subject-Out CV protocol respectively, and 90.47% on PMED, for which only a binary benchmark exists. Using samples to implement k-shot conditioning, the accuracies can be improved to 86.25%, 40.06%, 83.43%, 44.08%, and 91.25%, respectively. To further evaluate the effects and robustness of our method, we provide additional ablation experiments and investigate the personalisation effects. Our results suggest that support-conditioned few-shot adaptation can improve average performance under inter-subject variability.

</details>


### [164] [Pretraining Shapes Spectral Structure: Architecture- and Strategy-Conditional Prediction of OOD Robustness in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09709)
*Sangyoon Bae,Sk Miraj Ahmed,Shinjae Yoo,Jiook Cha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明基础模型的OOD鲁棒性在无目标数据时即可由预训练权重的谱结构预测：架构决定信息存储方式、预训练策略决定奖励导向，二者共同构成"谱几何"；在按(架构×策略)"cell"分组后，单一谱统计量可在组内对92%的模型对正确排序，方向在跨模态验证中全部成立，并可将OOD差距缩小24%（ID保持率87.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD诊断要么需要源/目标数据（目标域出现前不可用），要么对所有权重套用同一统计量、不区分鲁棒与脆弱模型。因此本文追问：能否仅凭预训练权重、在任何目标数据之前判断模型是否会OOD泛化。

Method: 从理论上建立OOD精度差距与源表示集中度的上界关系，提出由预训练权重单独计算、作为集中度代理的谱统计量（spectral concentration）。关键发现是该统计量的方向在不同架构族间发生反转，故按(架构×策略)划分为最细的"cell"分组，在cell内选择对应方向的统计量。跨116个模型、7种模态验证，并在EEG、基因组、蛋白质等矩阵外模型族上预注册cell、指标与符号后再做OOD评估。

Result: 整体池化时单一统计量排序能力弱（因方向相反的cell相互抵消）；cell内选定统计量在样本内对92%的模型对正确排序OOD鲁棒性。在四个矩阵外模型族（EEG、基因组、蛋白质）上，事先记录的预测方向全部成立。据此干预谱集中度可将OOD差距缩小24%，同时保持87.5%的ID精度。

Conclusion: OOD鲁棒性可编码于预训练权重的谱结构中，且仅依赖已发布权重即可检出，使OOD鲁棒性在模型选择阶段、尚未投入目标域数据与算力之前就可被检验。

Abstract: Can we determine whether a foundation model will generalize out-of-distribution (OOD) before any target data is available? Existing diagnostics require source or target data, which rules them out before a target domain exists. Those that use the weights alone apply one statistic to every architecture, and do not separate robust models from fragile ones. We show the answer is encoded in the spectral structure of pretrained weights. Two forces shape that structure. Architecture determines how information is stored in weight matrices. Pretraining strategy determines what is rewarded. Together they set a spectral geometry that governs OOD robustness. We prove that the OOD accuracy gap is bounded by how tightly the source representations concentrate. A statistic computed from the pretrained weights alone serves as a proxy for that concentration. The direction of that proxy reverses between architecture families. We operationalize it: the direction is stable within one (architecture X strategy) combination, the finest grouping we test, which we call a cell. Pooled over 116 models spanning 7 modalities, a single statistic ranks OOD robustness weakly, because cells of opposite direction cancel. Within a cell, the statistic selected for it orders 92% of model pairs by OOD robustness in-sample. The selection does not leak the target: for each model family outside the matrix we logged the cell, metric and sign before running its OOD evaluation, and the predicted direction held in every case: EEG, genomic and protein. Acting on spectral concentration narrows the OOD gap by 24% at 87.5% ID retention. The diagnostic operates on released weights alone, so OOD robustness becomes checkable at model-selection time, before data or compute is committed to a target domain.

</details>


### [165] [EC-EarthFlow: Probabilistic emulation of daily transient global climate model simulations with flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09715)
*Kirien Whan,Nikolaj T. Mücke,Karin van der Wiel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EC-EarthFlow生成式流匹配模型，以低成本复现EC-Earth3气候模拟的温度场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理气候模型计算成本高昂，需探索低成本代理模型以复现其模拟结果。

Method: 基于EC-Earth3的瞬变模拟（1950-2166，SSP2-4.5），训练流匹配生成模型，用前几日温度场及年均温预测次日温度场，并进行一个月至更长季节的自回归推演。

Result: 仅用目标变量即可复现EC-Earth3的日变率、空间型态、年循环与长期趋势，计算成本显著低于物理模型；长时段推理稳定，并能学习其模拟的物理关系。

Conclusion: EC-EarthFlow可作为EC-Earth3的高效稳定代理模型，验证了流匹配在气候场模拟中的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce EC-EarthFlow, a generative flow matching model that emulates simulations from the physical climate model EC-Earth3. The model is trained on transient simulations from EC-Earth3 (1950-2166, SSP2-4.5) to predict the day ahead temperature field from the previous days temperature as well as annual mean temperature. Predictions are made auto-regressively with rollout periods of between a month and an extended season. Using only this variable of interest, we are able to reproduce the daily variability, spatial patterns, annual cycle and long-term trend from EC-Earth3 at a substantially lower computational cost than the physical model. We demonstrate that EC-EarthFlow is stable for long inference periods, and that it can learn the physical relationships as simulated in EC-Earth3.

</details>


### [166] [SoftSEEPS improves ML-based precipitation forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09752)
*Jost Arndt,Utku Isil,Noelia Otero,Rodrigo Almeida,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微的SEEPS评分近似SoftSEEPS，使机器学习模型能直接以降水预报为目标训练；在IMERG数据集上训练预训练低分辨率预报模型潜空间的降水解码器，SoftSEEPS与RMSE联合优化可兼顾两指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEEPS是降水预报中常用且受认可的评分，但不可微，无法直接作为损失函数用于机器学习模型的端到端训练，因此需要构造其可微近似，以实现对降水的直接优化。

Method: 设计SEEPS的可微近似SoftSEEPS；在IMERG数据集（0.1°分辨率）上，基于预训练低分辨率预报模型的潜空间训练一个降水解码器；并将SoftSEEPS与RMSE组合成联合目标函数进行优化。

Result: SoftSEEPS可用于直接训练降水预报模型；联合使用SoftSEEPS与RMSE时，两个指标仅有轻微权衡（marginal trade-offs）。

Conclusion: 可微的SoftSEEPS为以降水评分直接驱动机器学习预报训练提供了可行途径，且与RMSE联合优化具有实用性。

Abstract: In this paper we have developed a differentiable approximation of the well-known SEEPS score, which we name SoftSEEPS. This allows the training of a Machine Learning model to forecast precipitation directly. We test SoftSEEPS on the IMERG dataset (0.1 degree resolution) by training a decoder for precipitation on the latent space of a pre-trained low-resolution forecasting model. Combining SoftSEEPS and RMSE in a joint objective is possible with marginal trade-offs in either metric.

</details>


### [167] [Unrolled Flow Models for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09759)
*Faissal Izermine,Hanru Bai,Oscar Davis,T. Konstantin Rusch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论与实证两方面研究流匹配（flow matching）模型在推理任务中的表现，证明两层Transformer流可解图可达性，并提出"沿自身潜变量轨迹训练"等方法使推理准确率随积分步数增加而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配能以少量积分步完成语言生成，但增加积分步数是否提升推理能力尚不清楚。标准流语言模型的训练目标独立监督每个时间点，未显式要求后续步以前一步为基础，导致模型难以从更多积分步中获益。

Method: 理论上证明由两层Transformer参数化的流可求解图可达性，所需积分步数随目标节点到根的距离增加。方法上改为在[0,1]的随机采样子区间上沿模型自身的潜变量rollout进行训练，仅在端点解码；对Sudoku、Maze等长rollout任务，将潜变量状态投影回球面以稳定动力学；并采样多条rollout，配合无参数选择评分进行筛选。

Result: 在ProsQA上准确率提升至97%，且性能随积分步数增加而改善；在Sudoku和Maze上，球面约束带来的增益显著超过参数量三倍以上的基线；多rollout采样结合无参数评分可进一步提升性能，但对较长答案的可靠选择仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 研究为流模型的推理能力建立了理论基础，并表明rollout训练、稳定的潜变量动力学与rollout筛选三者结合能在实践中实现该能力。

Abstract: Flow matching enables language generation in few steps, but whether additional integration steps improve reasoning remains unclear. We prove that a flow parameterized by a two-layer Transformer can solve graph reachability, with the required number of integration steps increasing with the target's distance from the root. Yet, standard flow language models can fail to benefit from additional steps on reasoning tasks. We attribute this limitation to objectives that supervise each time point independently, without explicitly training successive steps to build on one another. To address this, we instead train through the model's own latent rollout over a randomly sampled subinterval of [0, 1], decoding only at the endpoint. On ProsQA, this raises accuracy to 97% and enables performance to improve with additional integration steps. For the longer rollouts required by reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and Maze, retracting the latent state onto a sphere stabilizes the dynamics and yields substantial gains over baselines with more than three times as many parameters. Sampling multiple rollouts further improves performance when paired with a parameter-free selection score, although reliable selection remains challenging for longer answers. Together, these results establish a theoretical basis for reasoning with flows and show how rollout training, stable latent dynamics, and rollout selection help realize this capacity in practice.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Policy Support: Interaction Constrained Offline Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09763)
*Mahmoud Selim,Cristina Cipriani,Karl Henrik Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出离线强化学习框架ICDP，显式约束交互级分布偏移（IDS），通过对比密度比估计分离出残差交互支撑成分，在nuPlan、InterPlan及真实卡车实验中抑制高价值但交互不支持的轨迹，提升交互关键场景表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习无需在线探索即可从固定数据集中改进策略，适用于自动驾驶等安全关键领域。核心挑战是分布偏移：策略优化可能偏好离线数据支撑薄弱的动作，导致价值估计不可靠。现有方法主要在自车动作空间控制偏移，但在交互环境中不足——候选自车轨迹在边缘行为分布下支撑良好，却可能与日志交互中周围智能体行为联合支撑不佳。作者将这种交互支撑退化称为交互分布偏移（IDS）。

Method: 从自车与周围智能体未来轨迹的联合数据分布出发，证明联合支撑退化可精确分解为自车支撑成分与残差交互支撑成分。通过对比密度比估计恢复残差交互支撑成分，从而分离交互兼容性，无需显式联合密度建模、周围智能体预测，也无需在策略优化中使用反应式模拟器或学习世界模型进行rollout。

Result: 在nuPlan、InterPlan和真实卡车实验的闭环评估中，ICDP能抑制高价值但交互不支持的轨迹选择，并在交互关键驾驶场景中提升性能。

Conclusion: ICDP通过显式控制交互级分布偏移，弥补了传统方法仅约束自车动作空间的不足，在交互关键驾驶场景中有效提升离线强化学习策略的可靠性与性能。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning enables reward-driven policy improvement from fixed datasets without requiring online exploration, making it particularly attractive in safety-critical domains. A central challenge, however, is distribution shift: policy optimization may favor actions that are weakly supported by the offline data, rendering value estimates unreliable. Existing approaches primarily control this shift in the policy's own action space. In interactive environments such as autonomous driving, this can be insufficient: a candidate ego trajectory may remain well supported under the marginal behavior distribution while being poorly supported jointly with the surrounding-agent behavior observed in the logged interaction. We refer to this degradation in interaction support as \emph{interaction distribution shift} (IDS), and introduce \emph{Interaction-Constrained Drive Policy} (ICDP), an offline reinforcement learning framework that explicitly controls interaction-level distribution shift. Starting from the joint data distribution over ego and surrounding-agent futures, we show that joint-support degradation decomposes exactly into an ego-support component and a residual interaction-support component. We recover the latter through contrastive density-ratio estimation, isolating interaction compatibility without explicit joint-density modeling, surrounding-agent prediction, or rollouts in reactive simulators or learned world models during policy optimization. Closed-loop evaluations on nuPlan, Interplan and real-world truck experiments show that ICDP suppresses high-value yet interaction-unsupported trajectory selections and improves performance in interaction-critical driving scenarios. Project webpage: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/ICDP/

</details>


### [169] [A Proof-of-Concept Study of Weakly Supervised Labeling of Fine-Grained EEG Components for Artifact Attenuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09792)
*Lu Wang-Nöth,Hai Huang,Philipp Heiler,Shuqiong Wu,Liyun Zhang,Helmut Mayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合频率感知高维表示与多实例学习的框架，用于解决多通道头皮脑电中肌电伪迹与神经活动混叠且缺乏可靠成分级标注的问题，实现细粒度肌电伪迹检测与分数引导的衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电对肌电伪迹高度敏感，其时间异质性及与神经活动在空间-频谱上的重叠，导致盲源分离后仍残留混合源。现有去伪迹方法受限于缺乏可靠的成分级真值：专家标注昂贵且主观，且尚无成熟方法为多通道头皮脑电中的肌电污染提供真实的仿真真值。

Method: 提出频率感知高维表示与多实例学习相结合的框架。该表示将分离出的成分展开为频率分辨的"成分内单元"（intra-components），构建使神经与肌肉活动更可分的空间；利用弱监督学习形式，从epoch级标签学习各成分内单元的伪迹似然分数，无需更细粒度真值。所得成分内分类器支持细粒度肌电伪迹检测与分数引导的衰减。

Result: 在留出受试者上的实验表明，该框架能学到有信息量的成分内分数，并最明显地降低下颌紧绷相关的频谱偏差，抬眉效果中等，皱眉效果有限。

Conclusion: 该框架通过频率分辨的成分内表示与弱监督多实例学习，在缺乏成分级真值的条件下实现了细粒度肌电伪迹检测与衰减，对不同肌电来源的去除效果存在差异。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is highly susceptible to electromyographic (EMG) artifacts, whose temporal heterogeneity and spatial-spectral overlap with neural activity can leave mixed sources after blind source separation. Existing artifact-removal methods are further limited by scarce reliable component-level ground truth: expert annotations are costly and subjective, while no established method provides realistic simulation-based ground truth for EMG contamination in multichannel scalp EEG. To address these limitations, we propose a framework combining a frequency-aware high-dimensional representation with Multi-Instance Learning. The representation unfolds separated components into frequency-resolved intra-components, creating a space in which mixed neural and muscular activity becomes more separable, while the weakly supervised learning formulation enables artifact-likelihood scores for individual intra-components to be learned from epoch-level labels without finer-grained ground truth. The resulting intra-component classifier supports fine-grained EMG artifact detection and score-guided attenuation. Experiments on held-out subjects show that the framework learns informative intra-component scores and reduces artifact-related spectral deviations most clearly for jaw tension, with moderate effects for raising eyebrows and limited effects for frowning.

</details>


### [170] [BoT-GRPO: Efficient Process-Reward RL for Reasoning via Bag-of-Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09804)
*Yingxiang Yang,Weihang Xiao,Zhunxuan Wang,Joshua Flashner,Niresh Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BoT-GRPO，用长度不变的"词袋"聚合把 GRPO 扩展到 token 级奖励，无需 critic，收敛更快且最终质量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 中 rollout 内每个 token 共享同一优势值，缺乏过程监督；而引入 value network 代价高。作者希望在不增加价值网络成本的前提下，实现高效的 token 级过程监督，从而加速收敛并提升最终质量。

Method: BoT-GRPO 将 GRPO 扩展到 token 级奖励模型：收集所有 rollout 中的 token 级奖励，按来源序列长度取倒数加权，并基于加权组统计量计算每 token 优势。该方法为 critic-free，可直接替换 GRPO，只需具备 token 级奖励即可使用。

Result: React 前端代码生成任务上，BoT-GRPO 达到 80% 编译率，且比 GRPO 快至多 1.9 倍，收敛快于 GSPO、DAPO、PURE 等 GRPO 变体，并取得更高的最终编译率与 VLM 评判胜率。AIME 数学推理任务上，以一半步数取得相对 GRPO 最高 8.1% 的绝对 Pass@k 提升。两个任务均在 Qwen2.5-3B、SmolLM3-3B、Phi-4-mini-reasoning 上对比了推理与非推理基座模型家族。

Conclusion: BoT-GRPO 以无 critic 的方式高效实现 token 级过程监督。实验还给出奖励模型设计的实用准则：奖励的稳定性比丰富性更重要，干净、有界、稳定的细粒度信号能持续加速学习，而噪声较大的替代方案则会停滞。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now central to eliciting reasoning in large language models, while in the popular algorithm Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) every token in a rollout receives the same advantage. We ask how to make process supervision efficient: accelerating convergence and improving final quality without the cost of value networks. We propose Bag-of-Tokens Group Relative Policy Optimization (BoT-GRPO), which extends GRPO to token-level reward models through a length-invariant "bag of tokens" aggregation: it collects all token-level rewards across rollouts, weights each by the inverse of its source sequence length, and computes per-token advantages relative to weighted group statistics. BoT-GRPO is critic-free, and is a drop-in replacement wherever GRPO is used when token-level reward is available. On React front-end code generation, BoT-GRPO reaches $80\%$ compile rate up to $1.9\times$ faster than GRPO and converges faster than modern GRPO variants (GSPO, DAPO, PURE) while reaching higher final compile and VLM-judged win rates. On a second task, AIME mathematical reasoning, BoT-GRPO delivers absolute Pass@$k$ gains up to $8.1\%$ over GRPO in half the steps. For both tasks we compare the algorithm's performance on reasoning vs. non-reasoning base-model families (Qwen2.5-3B, SmolLM3-3B, Phi-4-mini-reasoning). Our experiments also yield a practical recipe for the reward model itself: reward stability matters more than richness: clean, bounded, stable fine-grained signals consistently accelerate learning where noisier alternatives stall.

</details>


### [171] [Homogenization in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09824)
*Prakhar Ganesh,Kyra Wilson,Luca Zappella,Barry-John Theobald,Nicholas Apostoloff,Lucas Monteiro Paes,Nivedha Sivakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多智能体系统(MAS)中智能体间的交互会导致"同质化"，即智能体行为趋于相似。本文用三个指标刻画同质化，并在代码生成、招聘、同行评审任务中证明其带来系统性风险，且简单的多样性提升方法无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注MAS通过智能体交互完成复杂任务的成功表现，但忽视了交互可能带来的负面效应——智能体行为趋同（同质化），进而削弱智能体多样性、放大共享失败。作者希望将同质化作为一种MAS的失效模式加以系统刻画和实证。

Method: 提出用三个指标操作化同质化：对多数派的趋同(conformity to the majority)、向极端方向的极化(polarization towards extremes)、以及随交互次数增加对变化的惰性增长(growing inertia against changes)。在教育场景之外，选取代码生成、招聘、科学同行评审三类任务对MAS中的同质化进行评测，并检验两类简单增多样性方法（采样随机性、混合模型MAS）的效果。

Result: 跨任务发现同质化会转化为具体的下游风险：代码生成中隐藏并放大相关误差，形成系统性漏洞；招聘中使有偏智能体的影响在其被移除后长期持续；同行评审中造成不同研究领域间评估标准不均衡。简单增加多样性的方法（采样随机性、混合模型MAS）未能降低同质化风险。

Conclusion: 同质化是MAS的一种失效模式，因此MAS评估不能仅停留在聚合性能指标上，而必须深入分析交互动态；同时，仅靠采样随机性或混合模型等简单手段不足以缓解同质化，亟需能够有效利用智能体多样性的策略。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) leverage interactions between agents to perform complex tasks. Despite their success, we show that these interactions can also lead to homogenization, i.e., agents converging to similar behaviors. Homogenization in MAS can reduce agent diversity and reinforce shared failures. In this paper, we operationalize homogenization using three metrics: conformity to the majority, polarization towards extremes, and growing inertia against changes over subsequent interactions. We evaluate homogenization in MAS for code generation, hiring, and scientific peer review. Across these tasks, we show that homogenization translates to concrete downstream risks: in code generation, it hides and amplifies correlated errors which can create systemic vulnerabilities; in hiring, it allows the influence of biased agents to persist long after their removal; and in peer review, it creates uneven evaluation standards across research areas. Our results establish homogenization as a failure mode of MAS, demonstrating that MAS evaluations must move beyond aggregate performance to carefully analyze interaction dynamics. Finally, we show that simple approaches to increase diversity---leveraging sampling stochasticity and mixed-models MAS---fail to reduce homogenization risks, highlighting the need for strategies to effectively leverage agent diversity.

</details>


### [172] [Dual-QK: Sharp Queries and Flat Keys for Prunable 2-bit KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09827)
*Sunjoo Whang,Jungjun Oh,Minsung Kim,Dongho Seo,Jisu Shin,Gregory Kielian,Hoi-Jun Yoo,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dual-QK 提出成对的非正交 query/key 变换，结合部分 key 白化与 query 对齐基，在保持低位量化的同时恢复 query 能量的通道集中性，实现 KV-cache 压缩与动态通道剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输入与长生成任务使 KV cache 的存储和访问开销显著增加。低位量化可降低存储与内存流量，query 通道剪枝可减少 key cache 读取。但基于旋转的量化虽能将 key 离群值能量重新分配到各通道，为保持计算不变性需对 query 施加正交变换，这会分散 query 能量，削弱"少数大分量保留、多数小分量剪枝"的可分离性。因此，量化所需的旋转与剪枝所需的能量集中之间产生冲突。

Method: Dual-QK 采用成对的非正交 query 与 key 变换：基于校准得到的 query 和 key 统计量，将部分 key 白化与 query 对齐基相结合，一方面平衡 key 的尺度以适配 INT2 量化，另一方面集中 query 能量以支持动态通道剪枝。此外引入 channel-0 保护机制和 bucket-relative RoPE，以在长上下文下维持低位精度。

Result: 在 5 个生成基准和长上下文检索任务、4 个模型上，40% query 通道稀疏度下，Dual-QK 在多数任务上优于 OSCAR。在 128K 上下文下，实现 $6.8\times$ 的 KV-cache 压缩，相比未剪枝 BF16 估计降低 $8.3\times$ 的 KV 读取量，SGLang 实现达到最高 $3.75\times$ 的解码吞吐。

Conclusion: 通过解耦旋转量化与 query 剪枝之间的正交性约束，Dual-QK 以非正交变换同时满足 key 尺度平衡与 query 能量集中，在长上下文场景下兼顾低位精度、压缩率与推理吞吐。

Abstract: Long inputs and extended generation increase the storage and access costs of the key-value (KV) cache. Low-bit quantization reduces storage and memory traffic, while query-channel pruning can further reduce key-cache reads. Rotation-based quantization redistributes the energy of key outliers across channels. To maintain computational invariance, the same orthogonal transform must be applied to queries, preserving query-key dot products. However, this rotation can disperse query energy, weakening the separation between a few large components to retain and many small ones to prune. We introduce Dual-QK, which uses paired non-orthogonal query and key transforms to address this conflict. Using calibrated query and key statistics, Dual-QK combines partial key whitening with a query-aligned basis to balance key scales for INT2 quantization and concentrate query energy for dynamic channel pruning. Channel-0 protection and bucket-relative RoPE support low-bit accuracy over long contexts. Experiments on four models across five generative benchmarks and long-context retrieval tasks show improved accuracy over OSCAR on most tasks at 40% query-channel sparsity. At a 128K context, Dual-QK provides $6.8\times$ KV-cache compression and an estimated $8.3\times$ reduction in KV read volume relative to unpruned BF16. Under the evaluated configurations, our SGLang implementation achieves up to $3.75\times$ the decoding throughput of unpruned BF16.

</details>


### [173] [Fully Interpretable Minimal Transformers: From Geometry to Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09838)
*Raneem Mahajne,Toviah Moldwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个构建与解释极简Transformer的框架：将嵌入维度和注意力头大小限制为2，实现内部表征的完全二维可视化，并主张"学习到的几何结构隐含一种算法"，即R^2中点与边界的排布可读作逐步计算过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer内部计算机制缺乏可解释性，高维表征难以直观理解。作者希望通过极端降维（2维）构建一个可完整可视化、可逐步追踪的极简模型，作为教学与实验测试平台，探究Transformer如何利用信息几何实现下一词元预测。

Method: 将Transformer的embedding维度与head size均限制为2，使嵌入、Q/K/V变换、注意力输出、残差流、决策边界均可直接可视化。在简单任务上训练该模型：在数字序列中当"+"算子出现时，输出最近观察到偶数。训练后逐步可视化其全部计算流程：token及位置嵌入、Q/K/V变换、Q与K点积形成注意力矩阵、用注意力矩阵选择value以将各输入token表征移动到输出层定义域中能正确预测下一词元的区域。并提出一套可解释性可视化工具使算法解释显式化。

Result: 成功训练并完整呈现模型每一步计算：模型能嵌入token及其位置，经Q/K/V矩阵变换，通过Q与K点积构造注意力矩阵，并借助该矩阵选择value，将每个输入token的表征移至输出层定义域中可正确预测下一token的区域。所提可视化套件使该过程的算法解释变得明确。

Conclusion: 该框架为探索Transformer如何利用信息几何实现下一词元预测提供了教学性与实验性测试平台，验证了在2维约束下可通过几何结构直接解读模型所学算法。

Abstract: We present a framework for building and interpreting minimal transformer models. By constraining a transformer's embedding dimension and head size to 2, we enable full two-dimensional visualization of its internal representations. Embeddings, query/key/value transforms, attention outputs, residual streams, and decision boundaries can all be seen directly. Our central claim is that the learned geometry implies an algorithm; the arrangement of points and boundaries in R^2 can be read as a step-by-step procedure. We train a transformer on a simple task where it must produce the most recently observed even number whenever the '+' operator appears in a sequence of digits. Once trained, we visually walk through every step of the transformer's computation. We show how the model embeds the tokens and their respective positions in the sequence, transforms them via the Q, K, and V matrices, uses the dot product between the Q and K representations to form the attention matrix, and uses the attention matrix to select values that move the representation of each input token to the region of the domain of the output layer that will correctly predict the next token. We introduce a suite of interpretability visualizations that make the algorithmic interpretation of this procedure explicit. Our framework offers a pedagogical and experimental testbed to explore how transformers use informational geometry to implement next-token prediction.

</details>


### [174] [For Those Who Believe in Faithfulness: Optimizing the Area Under Insertion and Deletion Curves for Ranking Relative Feature Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09844)
*Bjørn Leth Møller,Bulat Ibragimov,Christian Igel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从忠实性（插入/删除曲线AUC）推导目标函数并随机化近似其梯度，结合神经解释掩码框架得到Ra-NEM，可在任意可微模型上生成高忠实性、稳健且高效的归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向社会相关任务的机器学习需要有效的可解释AI方法来理解模型行为。归因方法（热图反映输入特征对预测的相对重要性）是主流XAI途径，但其质量评估常依赖插入/删除曲线下面积衡量的忠实性。现有方法在忠实性优化、稳健性与效率上仍存在不足，作者希望建立忠实性与特征选择之间的理论联系并据此优化归因。

Method: 从忠实性（插入/删除曲线）概念出发推导目标函数及其梯度近似。建立插入曲线与top-k特征选择的联系，得到衡量归因质量的损失函数；通过对损失进行随机化（randomization）高效近似其梯度。将该损失与神经解释掩码（neural explanation mask）框架结合，形成Ra-NEM方法，可用于任意可微模型且不影响模型性能。

Result: 实验表明Ra-NEM能稳健、高效地提供准确归因。与其他算法相比，其归因不仅忠实性更高，在其他XAI指标上也表现良好；推理速度快，适合在线应用。代码已开源（https://github.com/baerminator/Ra_Nem）。

Conclusion: 通过将插入曲线与top-k特征选择关联并随机化近似梯度，可将忠实性目标转化为可优化的损失，配合神经解释掩码得到Ra-NEM，兼顾归因质量、稳健性与在线推理效率。

Abstract: The adoption of machine learning for socially relevant tasks requires effective explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) methods to better understand the behavior of machine learning models. Attribution methods are a popular XAI approach in which input-output relationships are characterized by heat maps that reflect the relative importance of input features for a particular prediction. The quality of such maps is often assessed by measuring faithfulness based on the area under insertion and deletion curves, which measures changes in the model output as features are added and removed. In this study, we derive an objective function from this notion of faithfulness and a way to approximate its gradient. We establish the connection between insertion curves and top-$k$ feature selection, which leads to a loss function measuring the quality of attributions. Randomization of the loss allows us to efficiently approximate its gradient. To show the effectiveness of the general approach, we combine the loss function with the neural explanation mask framework. The resulting method, termed Ra-NEM, can be used with any differentiable model without affecting the model's performance. Experiments demonstrate that Ra-NEM provides accurate attributions robustly and efficiently. Compared to other algorithms, the attributions have not only higher faithfulness but also perform well in terms of other XAI metrics. The high inference speed of Ra-NEM makes the method suitable for online applications. The code is available online: https://github.com/baerminator/Ra_Nem

</details>


### [175] [Stream-Based Active Learning with Cooperative Neural Networks for Data-Efficient Partial Inverse Design: An Automotive Glass Run Channel Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09848)
*Agung Nugraha,Hyerin Kwon,Heungjun Im,Gian Antariksa,Jihwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoNN-AL框架，将流式主动学习与CoNN-DAE结合，用于数据高效的部分逆向设计，仅需约2.3%标注样本即可达到R² 0.967–0.982。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计中，每个带标签训练样本需通过昂贵的仿真获得，构建大规模数据集既慢又昂贵；尤其是部分逆向设计（仅给定部分设计变量，需推断其余变量以达到目标性能）面临数据效率瓶颈。

Method: 在CoNN-DAE（带去噪自编码器的协作神经网络）基础上加入基于流的主动学习：利用蒙特卡洛dropout估计预测不确定性，实时判断到来的候选样本是否值得标注，从而将有限标注预算用于信息量最大的设计。

Result: 在超过90万个仿真设计的真实汽车玻璃导槽数据集上验证：仅用2万个主动选择的标签（约训练池的2.3%），CoNN-AL在各缺失变量水平下R²达0.967–0.982，接近使用远更多数据训练的上界模型；在较难缺失变量水平下，比随机采样少用30–40%标签即可达到R²≥0.95；在最难水平下是唯一达到R² 0.98的策略。同时公开该数据集。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性驱动的流式主动学习，CoNN-AL显著降低部分逆向设计所需标注量并保持高精度，为数据驱动的工程设计提供了高效方案，并公开数据集支持后续研究。

Abstract: Inverse design in engineering often runs into a simple problem. Each labeled training sample must be produced through expensive simulation, so building a large dataset is slow and costly. This study addresses that problem for partial inverse design, where only some design variables are specified and the rest must be inferred to reach a target performance value. We propose CoNN-AL, a framework for data-efficient partial inverse design that adds stream-based active learning to the Cooperative Neural Network with Denoising Autoencoder (CoNN-DAE). The model estimates predictive uncertainty through Monte Carlo dropout and uses it to decide, in real time, which incoming candidate samples are worth labeling, so the limited labeling budget is spent on the most informative designs. We validate the framework on a real-world automotive glass run channel dataset of more than 900,000 unique simulated designs. With only 20,000 actively selected labels, about 2.3% of the training pool, CoNN-AL reaches R-squared values of 0.967 to 0.982 across all missing-variable levels, approaching the upper-bound models trained on far more data. It reaches R-squared of at least 0.95 with 30 to 40% fewer labels than random sampling at the more difficult missing-variable levels and, at the most challenging level, is the only strategy in this study to reach R-squared of 0.98. Together with this work, we publicly release the dataset to support future research on data-driven design.

</details>


### [176] [MUNITE: Unified Multimodal Latent Inference for Any-to-Any Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09866)
*Kyeongmin Yeo,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUNITE将多模态生成中的编码与潜在生成统一为同一推断问题，通过条件流模型和自蒸馏训练实现灵活的任意到任意多模态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生成方法通常将编码与生成割裂处理，难以在不同观测证据量下灵活地进行任意到任意的模态生成，且训练中面临不完整样本的挑战。

Method: 提出MUNITE潜在变量框架，将编码与潜在生成建模为不同观测证据量下的同一推断问题：完整观测对应确定性编码，无观测对应潜在边缘，中间子集对应条件潜在推断，统一于单一条件流模型。共享潜变量捕获跨目标一致的变化，模态特定解码器建模剩余不确定性。通过自蒸馏扩展条件流匹配，用更丰富观测条件下的预测监督同一中间潜在状态下的小子集条件预测。

Result: 在PolyMNIST-D-Q、FFHQ64和图像-文本-音频任务上，MUNITE取得竞争性或更优的生成质量与源目标对齐，联合生成一致性更高；在所有一对多及无条件图像-文本-音频比较中取得最高一致性。

Conclusion: 统一的潜在推断框架MUNITE能有效适用于多样多模态场景，验证了跨观测证据量统一潜在推断的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce MUNITE, a latent-variable framework for flexible any-to-any multimodal generation that treats encoding and latent generation as the same inference problem under different amounts of observed evidence. Given any subset of modalities, MUNITE models the conditional distribution over the latent representation associated with the complete observation. Full observation recovers deterministic encoding, no observation recovers the latent marginal, and intermediate subsets define conditional latent inference, all within a single conditional flow model. A shared latent sample captures variation that must remain consistent across generated targets, while modality-specific generative decoders model the remaining uncertainty independently. To learn these conditional distributions from incomplete training examples, we extend conditional flow matching through self-distillation: predictions conditioned on richer available observations supervise the same model conditioned on smaller subsets at the same intermediate latent state. When the richer-evidence trajectory follows the exact conditional flow, this provides the same expected learning signal as full-target denoising. Across PolyMNIST-D-Q, FFHQ64, and image-text-audio, MUNITE achieves competitive or better generation quality and source-target alignment, with higher joint-generation coherence. In particular, it attains the highest coherence in all one-to-many and unconditional image-text-audio comparisons, showing the effectiveness of unified latent inference across diverse multimodal settings.

</details>


### [177] [Layerwise Error Attribution for Fast and Robust Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09877)
*Samy Houache,Yann Traonmilin,Jean-François Aujol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合精度训练后量化方法，通过层级量化误差的概率分析实现快速、稳健的比特分配，无需外部求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度训练后量化需在全局内存预算和小校准集下逐层分配比特，难点在于分配问题的组合复杂性以及对小型且可能被损坏的校准数据的敏感性。因此需要一种计算快速、且在校准数据损坏时仍能保持模型质量的高效分配方法。

Method: 对量化误差进行层级概率分析，将传播误差与特定层引入的局部扰动分离；利用该局部项构建可分离评分，用于一种简单的分配算法，无需外部求解器。概率化处理带来对损坏数据的稳健性。

Result: 在DRUNet去噪任务、平均每权重4比特预算下，干净校准时匹配或优于当前最优混合精度基线；校准数据损坏时更稳健，PSNR提升最高达7.5 dB；比特分配相对基线加速28倍至2570倍。在量化扩散模型中，直接应用该框架也提升了当前最优结果。

Conclusion: 该方法兼具高效性、无需求解器、对损坏校准数据的稳健性，并在去噪与量化扩散模型任务上取得优于或匹配当前最优的表现。

Abstract: Mixed-precision post-training quantization is a network compression method that assigns bits layer by layer, under a global memory budget using a small calibration set. The main difficulties are to overcome the combinatorial nature of the allocation problem and to manage the sensitivity to small, potentially corrupted databases. Hence, an efficient allocation method should be fast to compute and preserve model quality when calibration data are corrupted. To design such a method, we derive a layerwise probabilistic analysis of the quantization error that separates propagated error from the local perturbation introduced at a given layer. We use this local term to build a separable score for a simple allocation algorithm, that requires no external solver. The probabilistic nature of our approach brings robustness to corrupted data. On denoising tasks with DRUNet, with an average budget of 4 bits per weight, our method matches or improves state-of-the-art mixed-precision baselines under clean calibration, and is more robust to corrupted calibration, with PSNR gains of up to 7.5 dB under the tested corruptions. Experiments show bit-allocation speed-ups from 28x to 2,570x over the studied baselines. For quantized diffusion models, our experiments show that a direct application of our framework also improves the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [178] [RollVerify: Bridging Efficiency and Accuracy in Long-Tail Rollout Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09914)
*Yongqiang Yao,Jinru Tan,Kaihuan Liang,Zixin Yin,Yazhe Niu,Ruihao Gong,Dahua Lin,Ningyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RollVerify，一个基于 partial rollout 的轻量级 RL 框架，通过 OPS 度量主动验证并修复样本，在保持 partial rollout 效率的同时达到接近全 on-policy 训练的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾 rollout 在 on-policy 训练中造成 GPU 气泡，降低系统利用率并限制 RL 扩展性；异步/部分 rollout 虽提升吞吐，但引入的陈旧 off-policy 样本会损害最终精度。现有方法仅被动重加权，仍存在与全 on-policy 训练的精度差距。

Method: 提出 RollVerify，构建于 partial rollout 之上，在样本进入训练前主动验证与修复。引入 off-policy shift 度量 OPS 量化部分生成轨迹的 off-policy 偏差；在 OPS 约束指导下，进行序列级与 token 级双重验证，识别并截断轨迹中无效后缀，从而产出高质量样本。

Result: 在数学推理与工具辅助数学推理任务上，RollVerify 达到与 on-policy 训练相当的精度，同时降低训练成本；额外的代码生成结果提供了数学领域之外的初步证据。

Conclusion: 主动验证与修复样本优于被动重加权，可在保留 partial rollout 效率收益的同时保护模型精度，为可扩展的 LLM 强化学习提供可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is crucial for improving large language models' reasoning and generalization. It relies on massive rollouts whose lengths become increasingly long-tailed as context windows grow. In on-policy training, these long-tail rollouts can result in GPU bubbles, reducing system utilization and limiting RL scalability. Asynchronous or partial-rollout methods improve throughput by relaxing synchronization, but inevitably introduce stale off-policy samples (trajectories) that may hurt final accuracy. Existing approaches mainly mitigate this off-policy issue by reweighting off-policy samples during training, yet they can still leave a performance gap compared to fully on-policy training. In this work, rather than passively reweighting samples during training, we propose RollVerify, a lightweight RL framework built on partial rollout that actively verifies and repairs samples before they enter training. Specifically, it introduces an off-policy shift metric OPS, to quantify the off-policy deviation of partially generated trajectories. Guided by the OPS constraint, RollVerify performs both sequence-level and token-level verification to identify and truncate invalid suffixes of trajectories. This yields high-quality samples that protect the models' accuracy while preserving the efficiency gains of partial rollout. Experiments on mathematical and tool-assisted mathematical reasoning show that RollVerify achieves accuracy comparable to on-policy training while reducing training cost. Additional code-generation results provide preliminary evidence beyond mathematics.

</details>


### [179] [NeuralZip: Reusable Setup for Fast Lossless Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09916)
*Martín Bravo,Samuel Horváth,Gonzalo Navarro,Andrés Abeliuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeuralZip无损压缩框架，通过一次性构建并复用指数分布统计结构（分组、共享Huffman码、打包指数元组），实现浮点模型权重的快速无损压缩，且该结构可跨兼容架构迁移复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损压缩可减少模型权重的存储与传输且不改变浮点数值，但重复的统计分析与编码构造带来额外计算开销。作者研究指数（exponent）的统计结构能否"一次准备、多次复用"，以摊薄并规避这种setup成本。

Method: 提出NeuralZip：将指数分布相似的chunk分组，共享Huffman码，并对重复出现的指数元组用打包指数（packed exponents）表示以获得额外中等压缩率。setup阶段在后续编码之前选定这些表示，而每次编码仍处理当前张量值；进一步验证setup可预计算并从一个兼容架构迁移到另一个。

Result: 在浮点模型checkpoint上，setup后压缩比基线快1.81–21.33倍，并实现精确的逐位重建；setup可跨兼容架构迁移，保留相近压缩率并免除setup成本摊还；训练checkpoint证明权重演化过程中可持续复用；GPU实验中活跃显存最多降低27.5%，且logits完全一致。

Conclusion: 压缩适配（compression adaptation）是可迁移且可复用的：指数统计结构一次构建后可跨架构与训练过程反复使用，在保证无损（逐位/逐logit精确）的前提下显著提升压缩速度并降低显存占用。

Abstract: Lossless compression can reduce the storage and movement of model weights without changing their floating-point values, but repeated statistical analysis and code construction add computational overhead. We study whether the statistical structure of exponents can be prepared once and reused. For this, we introduce NeuralZip, which groups chunks with similar exponent distributions, shares Huffman codes, and selectively represents recurring exponent tuples using packed exponents, thereby achieving additional moderate compression ratios. A setup chooses these representations before subsequent encodings, while every encoding still processes the current tensor values. In floating-point model checkpoints, post-setup compression is 1.81-21.33$\times$ faster than the baselines and achieves exact bit-to-bit reconstruction. We show that this setup can be precomputed and transferred from another compatible architecture, preserving similar compression ratios and avoiding the need to amortize setup costs. Therefore, compression adaptation is transferable and reusable. Training checkpoints demonstrate continued reuse as the weights evolve. Finally, GPU experiments reduce active memory usage by up to 27.5$\%$ while reproducing the logits exactly.

</details>


### [180] [KGATE : a Knowledge Graph Embedding Training Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09927)
*Benjamin Loire,Galadriel Brière,Célia Brahimi,Antoine Toffano,Anaïs Baudot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KGATE是一个基于PyTorch Geometric与TorchKGE的模块化Python库，用于知识图谱自编码器嵌入训练，支持组件化装配、防数据泄露预处理、内置训练流程与可复现性设计，其训练时间与最快的现有库相当且功能更全面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱嵌入（KGE）模型多采用自编码器架构，编码器与解码器的结合需求日益增长，但现有库很少支持完整的自编码器，且常缺乏维护、依赖未文档化的默认超参数、跨库结果无法比较。

Method: 提出KGATE（Knowledge Graph Autoencoder Training Environment）：基于PyTorch Geometric和TorchKGE构建的模块化Python库，用户可将初始化器、编码器、解码器、损失函数、负采样器和评估指标作为构建块自由组装，或插入自定义模块；同时提供控制数据泄露的预处理流程、内置训练管线，并以设计层面保证可复现性。

Result: 与六个现有KGE库的基准对比表明，KGATE的训练时间与最快的库相当，同时提供更广泛的功能集。

Conclusion: KGATE通过模块化设计、防数据泄露预处理、内置训练管线与可复现性保障，弥补了现有KGE库在完整自编码器支持、可维护性与结果可比性方面的不足，并在训练效率上保持竞争力。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) models encode the entities and relations of a knowledge graph into a low-dimensional latent space, enabling tasks such as classification or link prediction. Most KGE models follow an autoencoder architecture, in which an encoder projects the knowledge graph into the latent space and a decoder reconstruct it. Combining both encoder and decoder components is increasingly needed, yet existing libraries rarely support complete autoencoders, are often unmaintained, rely on undocumented default hyperparameters, and produce results that cannot be compared across libraries. Here we present KGATE (Knowledge Graph Autoencoder Training Environment), a modular Python library built on PyTorch Geometric and TorchKGE. KGATE lets users assemble initializers, encoders, decoders, losses, negative samplers, and evaluation metrics as building blocks, or plug in their own block. KGATE includes a preprocessing procedure that controls data leakage, a builtin training pipeline, and reproducibility by design. Benchmarks against six existing KGE libraries show that KGATE training time is comparable with the fastest libraries while offering a broader set of features.

</details>


### [181] [Learning Traffic Flow Dynamics with Stochastic Physics-Informed Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09946)
*Federica Bragone,Matthieu Barreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息神经细胞自动机（PI-NCA）用于数据驱动交通流建模，通过物理一致性架构保证车辆数守恒，并扩展至随机动力学；在NaSch与KKW模型场景上优于标准NCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通流建模对理解路网车辆集体动力学至关重要。细胞自动机以局部交互规则提供简单、可解释且能复现复杂宏观交通现象的框架。但从数据中学习局部转移规则并同时保持物理约束仍具挑战，对随机模型尤为困难。

Method: 在标准神经细胞自动机（NCA）基础上构建PI-NCA：设计与道路拓扑物理一致、并保证车辆总数守恒的神经架构，将学习到的转移规则约束为物理上可接受的动力学；进一步通过参数化概率转移规则，在保持相同物理约束的前提下扩展至随机动力学。

Result: 在由经典Nagel-Schreckenberg与Kerner-Klenov-Wolf细胞自动机生成的多种交通场景上评估。结果表明PI-NCA能成功学习两种交通模型的动力学，并持续优于标准NCA；随机扩展能在不破坏所施加物理约束的情况下捕捉概率转移规则。

Conclusion: 将物理约束嵌入神经细胞自动机的架构设计，可实现兼顾数据驱动与物理可容许性的交通流建模，并自然推广至随机情形。

Abstract: Traffic flow modeling is essential for understanding and predicting the collective dynamics of vehicles on road networks. Cellular automata provide a simple, interpretable yet powerful framework for representing these dynamics via local interaction rules, while retaining the ability to reproduce complex macroscopic traffic phenomena. However, learning local transition rules from data while preserving physically meaningful constraints remains challenging, particularly for stochastic models. In this work, we propose a physics-informed neural cellular automaton (PI-NCA) for data-driven traffic flow modeling. Building on the standard neural cellular automaton (NCA), we design a neural architecture that is physically consistent with the road topology and guarantees conservation of the total number of vehicles, thereby constraining the learned transition rules to physically admissible dynamics. We further extend this framework to stochastic dynamics by parameterizing probabilistic transition rules while preserving the same physics-informed constraints. We evaluate the proposed models on multiple traffic scenarios generated by the well-established Nagel-Schreckenberg and Kerner-Klenov-Wolf cellular automata. The results demonstrate that the PI-NCA successfully learns the dynamics of both traffic models and consistently outperforms a standard NCA, while the stochastic extension captures probabilistic transition rules without compromising the imposed physical constraints.

</details>


### [182] [Force without transmission: a depth-induced rank collapse that no loss on the representation reopens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09958)
*Martin Hofmann,Patrick Mäder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探究有界修正力的损失项能否修复Transformer的秩崩塌（token表示退化到单一方向、学习停滞）问题，通过削弱跳跃连接制造秩崩塌进行实验。结果显示：没有任何新增损失项能修复崩塌，原因不在修正力度（最强的修正项施加了约十分之一的任务梯度），而在梯度传播路径——任务梯度不再到达决定注意力指向的query和key权重，新增项的梯度也在崩塌形成的层前就已衰减；恢复跳跃连接（不改变任何权重）可立即重新打通该路径，秩随之恢复；高学习率爆发后路径保持畅通，秩可在无干预下恢复；但所有情况下秩恢复后损失仍高于健康网络。可修复性取决于梯度是否仍能到达必须改变的权重，而非损失项施力强弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练可使Transformer发生秩崩塌，即所有token表示指向同一方向、学习停止。已有研究表明，在一种相关的注意力崩塌中，带界限修正力的损失项可在训练中修复网络。作者由此提出问题：这类损失项是否也能修复秩崩塌？

Method: 通过削弱跳跃连接（skip connection）使小型Transformer发生秩崩塌，并处理崩塌网络的多个副本；尝试加入不同强度的损失项作为修正；进一步检查梯度传播路径，观察任务梯度与新增项梯度能否到达query和key权重及崩塌形成的层；随后恢复跳跃连接（不改变任何权重）或施加强高学习率以检验路径与秩的恢复。

Result: 没有任何新增损失项修复崩塌，即使较强项的推力约为任务梯度的十分之一。原因在于路径而非强度：任务梯度不再到达query和key权重，新增项的梯度在崩塌形成的层之前便已衰减。恢复跳跃连接可立即重新打通该路径，秩随之恢复，但恢复尺度远高于崩塌尺度。高学习率爆发后路径保持畅通，秩在无干预下恢复。基于路径的注册预测能对恢复时间排序，但无法迁移至本成因。所有情况下，秩恢复后损失仍高于健康网络。

Conclusion: 崩塌网络能否被修复，取决于梯度是否仍能到达必须改变的权重，而不取决于损失项施力强弱。

Abstract: Training can drive a transformer into a rank collapse: all token representations point in one direction, and learning stops. In a related collapse of attention, a loss term with a bounded corrective force repairs the network during the run. We ask whether such a term repairs rank collapse. We collapse small transformers by weakening their skip connection and treat copies of the collapsed network. No added loss term repaired the collapse, although the stronger kind pushed with about a tenth of the task gradient. The reason was the path, not the strength. The task gradient no longer reached the query and key weights, which decide where attention looks, and the added term's gradient faded before the blocks where the collapse forms. Restoring the skip connection, which changes no weight, reopened this path at once. The rank then recovered, but only far above the scale of collapse. After a burst of high learning rate the path stayed open and the rank recovered untreated. Registered predictions from the path ranked recovery times but did not transfer to this cause. In every case the loss stayed above that of a healthy network after the rank recovered. Whether a collapsed network can be repaired depends on whether the gradient still reaches the weights that must change, not on how strongly a loss term pushes.

</details>


### [183] [TR-PTQ: High-Accuracy Integer-Only Transformer Post Training Quantization via Taylor Region Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09969)
*Eliyahu Levy,Adam Teman,Yoni Pugachov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TR-PTQ，一种全整数后训练量化框架，通过共享Taylor Region指数/对数原语在 log 域完成除法、开方等昂贵运算，并配合免校准的异常值感知LayerNorm优化，消除非线性层的浮点回退，在视觉与语言基准上绝对精度损失小于1.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer非线性层难以量化，现有方法将精度损失归因于数值精度不足，常需浮点回退。本文指出退化实际源于特定结构性误差源：归一化层中学得的缩放参数，以及GELU中的复合近似，而SoftMax本身对激进量化具有鲁棒性。

Method: 定位主要误差来源为LayerNorm缩放参数与GELU复合近似；提出统一的整数化公式TR-PTQ，基于共享Taylor Region（TR）指数与对数原语，将除法、平方根等在log域用标准整数算术执行；并对LayerNorm参数采用免校准、异常值感知的优化。

Result: 在视觉与语言基准上实现绝对精度损失小于1.5%，且非线性运算无需浮点硬件单元。

Conclusion: 通过针对结构性误差源而非盲目提升数值精度，TR-PTQ实现了全整数、无浮点回退的Transformer后训练量化，兼顾精度与部署效率。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables efficient deployment, yet transformer architectures remain challenging to quantize due to nonlinear layers. While existing methods attribute accuracy loss to insufficient numerical precision, often necessitating floating-point fallbacks, we demonstrate that degradation is actually driven by specific structural error sources. We find that learned scale parameters in normalization layers and compounded approximations in GELU are the primary error contributors, whereas SoftMax remains inherently robust to aggressive quantization. To address these bottlenecks, we introduce TR-PTQ, a unified integer-only formulation using shared Taylor Region (TR) exponential and logarithm primitives. This approach allows computationally expensive operations, including division and square roots, to be performed entirely in the log-domain via standard integer arithmetic. Combined with a calibration-free, outlier-aware optimization for LayerNorm parameters, our method eliminates the need for floating-point hardware units for nonlinearities, achieving less than 1.5\% absolute accuracy degradation across vision and language benchmarks.

</details>


### [184] [Efficient Patch-Based Anomaly Detection Fused with Diffusion Driven Generative Modeling for Semiconductor Wafer Bin Map Open Set Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09993)
*Limon Bin Hossain,Md Sadib Rahman Ananta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EfficientAD与DDPM部分扩散重建的混合单类框架，仅用700个正常晶圆训练，融合检测器AUROC达0.9985并降低误分类至618，但固定权重融合仅能恢复oracle可用纠正量的40-70%，指出需样本自适应融合与不平衡感知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶圆图（WBM）空间缺陷特征可追溯良率损失至特定工艺故障，但监督分类器只能识别训练中见过的缺陷类型；而基于单一机制的单类检测器往往只能捕捉局部结构偏差或全局分布异常中的一种，难以兼顾两者。

Method: 构建混合单类框架：结合基于图块的student-teacher检测器（EfficientAD）与用于部分扩散重建的去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM），通过固定凸组合融合二者经百分位校准的异常分数；仅在WM-38K混合类型数据集的700个正常晶圆上训练，在18,658个留出晶圆上评估。

Result: 融合检测器AUROC达0.9985，误分类由DDPM的852、EfficientAD的1,412降至618，所有成对差异p < 0.001显著。增益源于两模块误差弱重叠，但固定权重融合仅恢复oracle选择器可用纠正量的40-70%。在基准的反向类平衡下，平均精度与F1饱和，而MCC与NPV暴露出正常预测不可靠。像素级图谱显示强图像级可分性不等于空间定位能力，扩散模块作为局部密度先验而非全局几何推理发挥作用。

Conclusion: 混合单类检测器通过互补误差融合可显著提升晶圆异常检测性能，但固定权重融合存在上限；研究建议采用样本自适应融合策略以及对类别不平衡敏感（imbalance-aware）的评估方式。

Abstract: Spatial defect signatures on wafer bin maps (WBMs) trace yield loss to specific process faults, yet supervised classifiers recognize only the defect types seen during training, and one-class detectors built on a single mechanism tend to capture either local structural deviations or global distributional violations, but rarely both. This work proposes a hybrid one-class framework that couples a patch-based student-teacher detector (EfficientAD) with a denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) used for partial-diffusion reconstruction, and fuses their percentile-calibrated scores through a fixed convex combination. Trained on only 700 normal wafers from the WM-38K mixed-type dataset and evaluated on 18,658 held-out wafers, the fused detector reached an AUROC of 0.9985 and reduced misclassifications from 852 (DDPM) and 1,412 (EfficientAD) to 618, with all pairwise differences significant at p < 0.001. Beyond aggregate accuracy, the analysis shows that the gain arises from weakly overlapping errors between the two modules, yet fixed-weight fusion recovers only 40-70% of the correction available to an oracle selector. Under the benchmark's inverted class balance, average precision and F1 saturate, while the Matthews correlation coefficient and negative predictive value expose unreliable normal predictions. Pixel-level maps further show that strong image-level separability does not imply spatial localization, and the diffusion module succeeds as a local density prior rather than through global geometric reasoning. These findings motivate sample-adaptive fusion and imbalance-aware evaluation of hybrid wafer anomaly detectors.

</details>


### [185] [Temporal Predictive Multiplicity: Equally Accurate Time Series Models Yield Different Forecast Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09994)
*Emanuele Albini,Francesca Toni,Saumitra Mishra,Francesco Leofante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"时间预测多重性"框架，刻画在预测性能几乎相同的模型间，完整预测轨迹层面的分歧，并证明仅约束性能或逐时域多重性都无法消除轨迹级分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有预测多重性研究多停留在单个标量输出层面；而在时间序列预测中，各时域的预测共同构成一条轨迹，逐时域比较可能掩盖预测行为的重要差异。

Method: 提出时间预测多重性（temporal predictive multiplicity）框架，将模型分歧定义在完整预测轨迹上，而非逐时域输出；并在性能约束、逐时域多重性约束两种设定下分析轨迹级分歧的可保留程度。

Result: 理论分析表明，仅约束预测性能仍可容纳范围广泛的轨迹；约束单时域多重性仅部分降低、但不能消除轨迹级多重性。在11个数据集、19种神经预测架构上的实验证实：近最优模型产生的预测轨迹存在显著变异性，且轨迹级分歧与逐时域分歧基本无关。

Conclusion: 现有多重性研究存在盲区：预测性能难以区分的模型，其隐含的时间轨迹可能根本不同，并带来后续下游影响；因此需要在轨迹层面评估与约束多重性。

Abstract: Models with near-identical predictive performance can yield substantially different predictions, a phenomenon known as predictive multiplicity. Prior work has mostly studied this at the level of individual scalar outputs. In time-series forecasting, however, predictions across horizons jointly define a trajectory, and horizon-wise comparisons can hide important differences in predictive behavior. To address this problem, we introduce temporal predictive multiplicity, a framework that characterizes disagreement over complete forecast trajectories among models with near-identical predictive performance. We show that constraining predictive performance alone can still admit a broad range of different trajectories. We further show that constraining multiplicity at individual horizons partially reduces, but does not eliminate, trajectory-level multiplicity. Experiments with 19 neural forecasting architectures on 11 datasets confirm that near-optimal models can exhibit substantial variability in the forecast trajectories they produce, and trajectory-level disagreement is largely unrelated to horizon-wise disagreement. Our framework, therefore, exposes a gap in existing multiplicity studies: models with indistinguishable predictive performance imply fundamentally different temporal trajectories, with consequential downstream effects.

</details>


### [186] [Beyond Reward Suppression: Near-Optimal Offline Attacks on Warm-Start Bandits with Bounded Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10000)
*Qirun Zeng,Manhin Poon,Xiangxiang Dai,Qixin Zhang,Jinhang Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究热身启动老虎机在离线有界攻击下，攻击者仅通过注入合法的动作-奖励对来直接推广目标臂，而非传统抑制非目标臂方式，并证明目标推广在特定条件下是必要且代价最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击通常通过抑制非目标臂来误导学习器选择目标臂，但现实中（如虚假评论）攻击者往往直接推广目标物品。本文试图弥合这一研究空白，研究离线有界攻击下目标推广的作用与代价结构。

Method: 在有界离线攻击设定下，攻击者仅能向部署前的热身历史注入合法动作-奖励对。本文理论分析针对UCB的最优阶代价攻击，证明当目标臂接近奖励下界时，任何使其在几乎所有在线轮次被选中的攻击都必须将非消失比例代价分配给目标臂；随后设计达到最优次线性代价的攻击，并刻画目标推广与非目标抑制之间的代价分配；并将攻击扩展到Thompson Sampling、ε-greedy及更广泛的老虎机算法。

Result: 理论证明目标推广是必要机制而非启发式；提出的攻击实现最优次线性代价，并明确其代价分配结构；在真实与合成数据上的实验验证了攻击有效性。

Conclusion: 目标推广应被视为有界离线攻击中的核心策略，而非仅依赖非目标抑制；该发现拓展了老虎机对抗攻击的理论与实用方法，并适用于多种主流算法。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on bandits aim to mislead a learner toward a target arm while keeping the attack cost small. Existing attacks typically achieve this by suppressing non-target arms. In practice, however, manipulation such as fake reviews often directly promotes the target item. We study this gap through bounded offline attacks on warm-start bandits, where an attacker can inject only valid action-reward pairs into the warm-start history before deployment. We show that target promotion is not merely a heuristic: when the target arm lies near the lower reward boundary, any order-optimal-cost attack against UCB that makes it selected in nearly all online rounds must allocate a nonvanishing fraction of its cost to the target arm. We then design an attack that achieves the optimal sublinear cost and characterize its allocation between target promotion and non-target suppression. We further extend the attack to Thompson Sampling, $ε$-greedy, and a broader class of bandit algorithms. Experiments on real-world and synthetic data validate the effectiveness of our attacks.

</details>


### [187] [A Drosophila Whole-Connectome Network Can Learn Human-Designed Cognitive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10014)
*Joonghui Cho,Minchan Kang,Daeshik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 以雄性果蝇MaleCNS v1.0连接组作为固定循环拓扑，每条约边学一个标量，构建有界加法与接地关系语言任务模型，显著优于度保持重连对照，表明该生物布线图可提供可复用的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生物布线图（连接组）能否作为超越其进化所对应行为之外的可复用计算基底，即其高阶连接结构是否构成通用归纳偏置。

Method: 采用单只成年雄性果蝇重建的MaleCNS v1.0连接组作为人工网络的固定循环拓扑；为有界加法任务与基于固定100词词表的受控接地关系语言任务分别训练模型，每条解剖学边仅学习一个标量参数；对照为保持有向度分布的重连（rewire）网络，并进行感官特征打乱干预。

Result: 有界加法留出集平均准确率92.77%，度保持重连为67.93%；严格配对语言端点（原文与逆序场景匹配对应描述）在四个固定接口上达61.59%，匹配重连为44.17%；在规范接口于固定21图比较中排名第一；在匹配的48组干预子集上，打乱任务定义的感官特征使得分从60.94%降至19.27%。

Conclusion: MaleCNS的高阶布线为有界加法与接地关系语言提供了可复用的归纳偏置，说明生物连接组可充当其进化行为之外的有效计算基底。

Abstract: Can a biological wiring diagram serve as a useful computational substrate beyond the behaviors for which it evolved? We use the publicly released MaleCNS v1.0 connectome, reconstructed from a single adult male Drosophila specimen, as the fixed recurrent topology of an artificial network. We train separate models for bounded addition and for a controlled grounded relational language task built from a fixed 100-word lexicon. In both models, one scalar is learned per anatomical edge. The anatomical graph reaches 92.77% mean accuracy on held-out addition, compared with 67.93% for directed degree-preserving rewires. On the strict paired language endpoint, which matches original and order-reversed scenes to their corresponding descriptions, it reaches 61.59% across four fixed interfaces, compared with 44.17% for matched rewires. At the canonical interface, it ranks first in a fixed 21-graph comparison. On the matched 48-group intervention subset, shuffling task-defined sensory features reduces its score from 60.94% to 19.27%. Together, these results show that higher-order MaleCNS wiring provides a reusable inductive bias for bounded addition and grounded relational language.

</details>


### [188] [Oscillatory Neural Dynamics over Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10018)
*Jan-Willem Van Looy,Alessandro Trenta,Alessio Gravina,Alessio Borgi,Ferdinando Zanchetta,Pietro Liò,Davide Bacciu,Rita Fioresi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出ONDA——基于算子值信息波的远程图学习框架，将二阶波动力学与学习到的层（sheaf）传输算子结合，实现层间矩阵值信息的稳健远距离传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络中增加传播深度并不保证远距离节点间产生有效影响；层神经网络（sheaf neural networks）虽通过层间的矩阵值传输增强传播表达力，但表达力本身并不自动带来有效的远程通信。

Method: ONDS让层值（stalk-valued）表示在由学习到的sheaf传输算子控制的二阶动力学下演化，将类波传播与富有表达力的局部几何相结合；并通过逐层的敏感性分析刻画远程影响。

Result: 敏感性分析表明其交叉影响永不消失（never vanishes）；在远程传播、严重图瓶颈、图迁移和异质（heterophilic）基准上，ONDA持续优于标量波传播、扩散式sheaf基线以及当前最先进模型。

Conclusion: 将波动力学与矩阵值传输耦合，可显著提升图上的有效远程传播能力。

Abstract: Effective long-range propagation remains a central challenge in graph neural networks, as increasing a model's propagation depth does not guarantee that distant nodes effectively influence each other. Sheaf neural networks enrich graph propagation through matrix-valued transport between stalks; still, this expressivity alone does not automatically imply effective long-range communication. We introduce ONDA, a long-range graph learning framework based on operator-valued information waves. Stalk-valued representations evolve through second-order dynamics governed by learned sheaf transport operators, combining wave-like propagation with expressive local geometry. We characterize long-range influence through a stalk-wise sensitivity analysis and show that the cross-influence never vanishes. Across long-range propagation, severe graph bottlenecks, graph transfer, and heterophilic benchmarks, ONDA consistently improves over scalar wave propagation, diffusive sheaf baselines, and state-of-the-art models, demonstrating the benefit of coupling wave dynamics with matrix-valued transport.

</details>


### [189] [Matching of signal, noise and hardware timescales for filtering and forecasting of correlated noise signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10037)
*Joshua Donald,Alex Gabbitas,Arthur G. T. Coveney,Sergey Savel'ev,Pavel Borisov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用纳米多孔氧化铌物理储池，研究发现噪声相关时间、储池记忆与预测时域之间的关系决定相关噪声是被滤波还是被预测，并提出储池记忆时域与预测机制指数两个指标刻画不同工作机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储池计算虽比传统机器学习更节能，且擅长处理时间相关数据，但其固有响应时间尺度固定；而现实信号同时包含确定性成分与跨多个时间尺度的随机成分，二者之间的尺度失配限制了物理储池对随机成分的处理能力，因此需要明确时间尺度匹配的规律。

Method: 以纳米多孔氧化铌作为物理储池，输入合成含噪信号以及加密货币价格波动数据，系统分析噪声相关时间、储池记忆与预测时域三者的相对关系；在此基础上引入储池记忆时域（reservoir memory horizon）和预测机制指数（forecasting regime index）两个量化指标，用于区分不同工作机制。

Result: 当噪声变化快于相关储池记忆与预测时域时，储池将其平均化（滤波）；当噪声变化较慢时，其时间结构足以支持算法化预测。即时间尺度匹配关系决定了相关噪声是被滤除还是被预测。

Conclusion: 时间尺度匹配可用于指导输入时间序列的编码方式，并引导设计能在不同时间尺度上滤波、分析与预测随机信号成分的物理储池架构。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing exploits the nonlinear dynamics of physical systems to process time-dependent data with greater energy efficiency than conventional machine learning approaches. However, physical reservoirs have fixed intrinsic response timescales, whereas real-world signals combine deterministic and stochastic components across multiple timescales. Here we show, using a nanoporous niobium oxide reservoir, synthetic noisy signals and cryptocurrency-price volatility, that the relationship among noise correlation time, reservoir memory and forecast horizon determines whether correlated noise is filtered or predicted. Noise varying faster than the relevant reservoir memory and forecast horizon is averaged by the reservoir, whereas the temporal structure of slower-varying noise is sufficient for algorithmic forecasting. We introduce the reservoir memory horizon and forecasting regime index to distinguish these operating regimes. These contributions demonstrate that timescale matching can guide the encoding of input time series and development of physical reservoir architectures that filter, analyse and predict stochastic signal components across distinct temporal scales.

</details>


### [190] [WxFM-XL: Adapting Univariate Foundation Models to Multi-Station Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10057)
*Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Rongwen Li,Hongwu Liu,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出WxFM-XL，通过跨站误差相关先验图与动态融合机制，将单变量时间序列基础模型适配到多站天气预报，实验优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单变量时间序列基础模型向多变量扩展时主要建模变量间相关性，应用于多站天气预报时忽略了站点的空间信息以及不同站点相对基础模型的误差先验差异两个关键因素。

Method: 提出WxFM-XL模型：引入跨站误差相关先验图以刻画各站点相对基础模型的误差先验；并在此基础上提出动态融合机制，自适应融合空间相关图与误差相关先验图。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该模型性能优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模站间误差先验并动态融合空间与误差两类图结构，可有效将单变量基础模型适配至多站天气预报任务。

Abstract: With the rise of univariate time series foundation models (e.g., Sundial, Timer), initial efforts have been made to extend them to multivariate settings. However, these models mainly focus on modeling correlations among variables. When they are applied to multi-station weather forecasting, two important factors are often overlooked: (1) the spatial information of stations, and (2) different error priors of different stations relative to the foundation model. In this paper, we propose WxFM-XL, a model for adapting univariate time series foundation models to multi-station weather forecasting. WxFM-XL introduces a cross-station error correlation prior graph to capture stationwise error priors with respect to the foundation model. Building on this, we further propose a dynamic fusion mechanism that adaptively integrates a spatial correlation graph with the error correlation prior graph. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that our model outperforms state of the art baselines.

</details>


### [191] [Efficient Provably Private Classification with a Tabular Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10068)
*Talal Alrawajfeh,Cristiana Diaconu,Ossi Räisä,Sebastian Rodriguez Beltran,Yuan He,John Bronskill,Richard E. Turner,Antti Honkela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrivTab 是一种内置隐私机制的表格基础模型，通过模拟数据预训练与上下文学习，将敏感行转化为可证明私密的紧凑摘要，在中等至强隐私下优于私有线性与神经网络基线，几乎无成员泄露，拟合时间降低 1 万倍，仅需单次前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据广泛支撑医学、金融、政府和科学领域的预测与决策，但常含敏感个体信息，需要兼顾准确预测与隐私保护。传统私有学习虽具形式化隐私保证，却依赖针对每个数据集的缓慢优化，在强隐私下效用损失大且难以正确应用；现有表格基础模型虽能快速适应新数据集，却缺乏形式化隐私保证，且极易受到成员推断攻击，难以用于敏感数据。

Method: 提出 PrivTab：一种易于使用的差分隐私分类表格基础模型，将隐私机制嵌入模型架构。模型在模拟数据集上预训练，利用上下文学习（in-context learning）将敏感数据行转换为紧凑的、可证明私密的摘要，即“在隐私约束下学会如何学习”，从而无需针对具体数据集进行缓慢优化，仅需单次前向传播即可完成数据集拟合。

Result: 在中等至强隐私预算下，PrivTab 优于私有线性与神经网络基线；成员泄露可忽略不计；在强隐私下仍保持良好校准的预测；数据集拟合时间降低 10,000 倍，仅需一次前向传播。

Conclusion: PrivTab 将形式化隐私、速度与易用性结合，把近期 AI 进展引入因敏感个体数据而受限的应用场景，拓展了差分隐私表格学习的实用边界。

Abstract: Tabular data underpin prediction and decision-making in medicine, finance, government and science, but often contain sensitive individual-level information, creating a need for accurate prediction while preserving privacy. Traditional private learning provides formal privacy guarantees, but requires slow dataset-specific optimisation, suffers substantial utility loss under strong privacy, and is often difficult to apply correctly. Tabular foundation models adapt rapidly to new datasets, but existing models lack formal privacy guarantees, and are highly vulnerable to membership-inference attacks, limiting their use on sensitive data. Here we introduce PrivTab, an easy to use tabular foundation model for differentially private classification that embeds a privacy mechanism within its architecture. Pretrained on simulated datasets, PrivTab uses in-context learning to transform sensitive rows into compact, provably private summaries---effectively learning how to learn under privacy. PrivTab outperforms private linear and neural-network baselines under moderate-to-strong privacy, shows negligible membership leakage, maintains well-calibrated predictions under strong privacy, and reduces dataset fitting time by 10,000 times, requiring only a single forward pass. By combining formal privacy, speed, and easy of use, PrivTab brings recent advances in AI to applications where sensitive individual-level data have limited their adoption.

</details>


### [192] [Activation-Aware Weight Tensorization: A Calibration-Time Preconditioner for Tensor-Network LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10085)
*Alessandro Beatini,Marco Maronese,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无训练的激活感知权重张量化(AWT)校准封装,通过在TT/TTN分解前对权重矩阵施加对角激活缩放,提升张量网络压缩的功能保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练后张量网络压缩(TT/TTN)替代Transformer线性层时,标准分解最小化的是权重空间的Frobenius误差,而非层激活分布下的功能误差,导致压缩后功能保真度欠佳。

Method: AWT是一种免训练的校准封装:在保持TT/TTN求解器不变的前提下,用激活导出的对角尺度对每个权重矩阵做预条件,部署时仅需在输入端做逐元素重缩放。

Result: 在Llama 3.1 8B、Ministral 8B、Qwen2.5 7B上,2-6倍压缩下AWT持续优于原生TT/TTN:单算子替换时缩小12-35%的WikiText困惑度差距,多算子Llama后缀替换时缩小27-60%;增益可迁移至HellaSwag与ARC-Challenge。消融显示对角预条件是鲁棒性-模块性权衡:稠密全协方差oracle在其自身加权目标上80/81胜出,但对角AWT在53/81例中取得更好的留出功能保真度。

Conclusion: AWT是一种有原则、模块化的预条件器,可在不修改分解求解器的固定TT/TTN压缩流程中提升功能保真度,作为鲁棒性与模块性之间的权衡,优于理论最优的稠密协方差方案在留出功能指标上的表现。

Abstract: Post-training tensor-network compression replaces Transformer linear layers with Tensor Train (TT) or Tree Tensor Network (TTN) operators, but standard decompositions minimize weight-space Frobenius error rather than functional error under the layer's activation distribution. We propose Activation-aware Weight Tensorization (AWT), a training-free calibration wrapper that preconditions each weight matrix with a diagonal activation-derived scale before an unchanged TT/TTN solver and deploys the result with only an input-side elementwise rescaling. Across Llama 3.1 8B, Ministral 8B, and Qwen2.5 7B, AWT consistently improves vanilla TT/TTN tensorization at 2-6 times compression: under single-operator replacement, AWT closes 12-35% of the WikiText perplexity gap to the dense baseline across the three model families and 2-6 times compression settings; while under multi-operator Llama suffix replacement it closes 27-60% across attention-group and all-seven-matrix settings. The gains also transfer to downstream HellaSwag and ARC-Challenge evaluations. We further show that diagonal preconditioning is a robustness-modularity tradeoff rather than a diagonal-covariance assumption: a dense full-covariance oracle wins its own weighted objective in 80/81 cases, yet diagonal AWT gives better held-out functional fidelity in 53/81 cases. Together, these results position AWT as a principled, modular preconditioner for improving functional fidelity in fixed TT/TTN compression pipelines without modifying the decomposition solver.

</details>


### [193] [Multi-Agent Coordination via Support-Preserving Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10087)
*Sangmin Lee,Youngju Na,Chanmi Lee,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线MARL中基于流的教师模型在噪声-目标配对上的缺陷，提出MoSDOT方法，通过半离散最优传输将每个噪声样本分配到单一模式，提升端点质量与路由一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线MARL通常采用生成式策略建模多模态联合行为，通过CTDE将集中式教师蒸馏为分散式单步执行器。作者发现教师训练阶段存在失效模式：标准流式教师独立地将噪声与回放目标配对，导致邻近噪声样本被路由到相互冲突的协调模式，教师生成落在有效模式之间的样本；而蒸馏损失将各局部执行器回归到教师输出在给定局部输入下的条件均值，使该误差被传递给学生而非被吸收。因此需要从教师端消除这一伪影。

Method: 提出Mode-Support Semi-Discrete Optimal Transport（MoSDOT）：将多模态回放数据归纳为具有指定容量的有限模式支撑集，并利用条件半离散最优传输在教师训练前将每个噪声样本分配给单一模式。此外研究了共享随机性变体，在执行阶段引入共享噪声分量，以揭示严格乘积式执行固有的残余差距。

Result: 在受控诊断实验和离线MARL基准上，MoSDOT提升了端点质量与路由一致性，尤其在呈现多模态联合行为的数据集上效果显著。

Conclusion: 教师端的噪声-目标配对缺陷会通过蒸馏传播至学生，MoSDOT通过模式支撑与条件半离散最优传输的噪声-模式分配予以消除；共享随机性变体则刻画了严格乘积式执行的固有残余差距。

Abstract: Offline MARL increasingly relies on generative policies to model multimodal joint behavior, typically by distilling a centralized teacher into decentralized one-step actors under the CTDE. We identify a failure mode at the teacher training stage: standard flow-based teachers pair noise with replay targets independently, so nearby noise samples can be routed toward conflicting coordination modes. The teacher then produces samples between valid modes, and because the distillation loss regresses each local actor onto the conditional mean of the teacher's output given local input, this error is not absorbed but propagated to the student. To remove this teacher-side artifact, we propose Mode-Support Semi-Discrete Optimal Transport (MoSDOT), which summarizes multimodal replay into a finite mode support with prescribed capacities and uses conditional semi-discrete optimal transport to assign each noise sample to a single mode before teacher training. We additionally study a shared-randomness variant that uses a shared noise component at execution to expose the residual gap intrinsic to strict-product execution. On controlled diagnostics and offline MARL benchmarks, MoSDOT improves endpoint quality and routing consistency, particularly on datasets exhibiting multimodal joint behavior.

</details>


### [194] [YANchor-4B: Effective Long-Horizon Reasoning in O(N) Time with O(1) Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10118)
*Huishan Ji,Hua Xu,Weiming Zhang,Qirui Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: YANchor-4B是一种通用循环模型，通过将关键记忆保留为"锚点"供后续推理检索，在O(N)生成时间与O(1)内存下实现高效长程推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理需要在可控生成成本下访问早期信息。全历史注意力导致存储与计算随序列增长，而循环压缩又会丢失精确细节，二者难以兼顾。

Method: 提出YANchor-4B，一个通用循环模型，利用多维记忆机制将关键记忆保存为锚点（ANchor），在后续推理中进行检索，兼顾O(N)时间生成与O(1)内存。

Result: 在AIME 2024--2026上取得82.93%平均pass@1、HMMT上63.64%，显著优于线性时间常数状态模型（含更大模型）；在H100上批量长生成吞吐量较Transformer与混合基线提升数倍；数十个基准测试显示其通用能力优势。

Conclusion: YANchor-4B以锚点式多维记忆机制，在长程推理效率与精度间取得良好平衡，并具备强通用能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning demands access to earlier information at a manageable generation cost. Full-history attention incurs growing storage and computation, while recurrent compression can lose precise details. Therefore, we present YANchor-4B, a general-purpose recurrent model that preserves crucial memory as ANchors for retrieval during subsequent reasoning. Beyond $O(N)$-time generation and $O(1)$ memory, YANchor enables effective long-horizon reasoning through its multidimensional memory mechanism. For example, on challenging math problems, it achieves 82.93% mean pass@1 on AIME 2024--2026 and 63.64% on HMMT, substantially outperforming linear-time, constant-state counterparts, including larger models. It also delivers several-fold higher batched long-generation throughput than Transformer and hybrid baselines on H100. Furthermore, evaluations across dozens of benchmarks demonstrate YANchor's superiority in general-purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [195] [What Can a Gaussian Process Design Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10122)
*Ivan De Boi,Marnix Van Soom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对高斯过程（GP）模型检验的"前瞻性"解读：在观测响应之前，设计本身已决定了它能产生何种矛盾。通过核矩阵零空间、Gale对偶与circuits刻画结构证据，并区分结构证据与基于先验的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP模型能与数据吻合有两种原因：其假设正确，或所选输入根本无法揭示假设的错误。作者希望在设计阶段（观测响应之前）就能区分这两种情形，从而判断模型检验的效力来源，并指导实验设计。

Method: 对有限特征构造的GP，模型蕴含无噪声响应必须满足的关系，这些关系恰为核矩阵的零空间；借助Gale对偶给出几何解释，其中一个观测对应一个向量，能暴露误差的最小观测组即circuits。对其他核，这些关系变为"软"约束，响应模式在先验下只是概率极低而非代数上不可能。标准检验结合两类证据：结构证据（来自被违反的关系，随噪声下降而无界增长）与先验证据（仅表明偏离在先验下不太可能）。并基于预测功效选择下一个输入点。

Result: 模拟中预测功效与实际拒绝率相符。按预测功效选择下一个输入点，将针对局部差异的功效从0.48提升至0.72，而按预测方差选择仅为0.51；二维网格设计包含拉丁超立方所缺失的可加性精确检验。区间两端取输入时，GP能以大可拒绝直线，但仅因截距在先验下不太可能，而非观测到曲率。

Conclusion: 检验方法本身是经典的，本文贡献在于对该检验的前瞻性解读：在观测响应之前，设计就已确定了它能产生何种矛盾。这一视角可用于评估检验效力并主动设计更具信息量的输入点。

Abstract: A Gaussian process (GP) model can agree with the data for two reasons: its assumptions are right, or the chosen inputs could never have shown that they are wrong. The distinction can be checked from the design before any responses are observed. Every model implies relations that its noiseless responses must satisfy at the chosen inputs, such as the middle value lies on the line through its two neighbours. For GPs built from finitely many features, these relations are exactly the null space of the kernel matrix. Gale duality gives them a geometric interpretation, in which each observation has a vector and the smallest groups of observations that can expose an error are the circuits. For other kernels the relations become soft: response patterns may be improbable under the prior rather than algebraically impossible. A standard test then combines two kinds of evidence. Structural evidence comes from a violated relation and grows without limit as the noise falls. Prior-based evidence only says that a departure is improbable under the prior. With all inputs at the two ends of an interval, for example, a GP can reject a straight line against a large curvature, but only because the implied intercept is improbable, never because curvature was seen. In simulations the predicted power matched the observed rejection rates. Choosing the next input by predicted power raised the power against a localised discrepancy from 0.48 to 0.72, against 0.51 when choosing by predictive variance, and a grid in two dimensions contained exact tests of additivity that a Latin hypercube lacked. The test itself is classical. The contribution is the prospective reading of that test: before observing the responses, the design already determines what kind of contradiction it can produce.

</details>


### [196] [A Unified Information-Theoretic Approach to Constrained Multi-Fidelity Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10174)
*Rikuto Matsumoto,Masanori Ishikura,Masayuki Karasuyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信息论的多目标、带约束、多保真度贝叶斯优化方法，通过最大化关于最高保真可行帕累托前沿的信息增益来联合选择评估点与保真度，并给出可处理的信息下界以构造代价感知采集函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实优化问题常同时存在多目标、约束与多保真度，现有方法难以在统一的框架下联合决定"在哪里"以及"以何种保真度"进行评估，且约束处理与保真度选择多依赖分离的启发式规则。

Method: 从统一的信息论视角出发，用观测对"最高保真可行帕累托前沿"的互信息衡量查询效用；由于该互信息不可解，借助对帕累托一致区域的下截断与上截断近似混合，推导出变分下界；并用多保真代理模型将信息传播到任意保真度，形成无需单独启发式的代价感知采集函数。

Result: 在合成问题、标准基准以及真实问题上进行了实验，在不同目标数、约束条件与保真度配置下均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将目标、约束与保真度选择统一到单一信息论采集准则中，为多保真约束多目标贝叶斯优化提供了原理清晰且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization often involves multiple objectives, constraints, and fidelity levels. We address the challenge of jointly selecting where and at which fidelity to evaluate to identify the highest-fidelity feasible Pareto frontier in this combined setting. From a unified information-theoretic perspective, we measure query utility by the information gain about this frontier, provided by an observation. Since this mutual information is intractable, we derive a variational lower bound using a mixture of under- and over-truncated approximations to the Pareto-consistent region. Multi-fidelity surrogate models propagate the information to arbitrary fidelities, yielding a cost-aware acquisition function without separate heuristics for fidelity selection or constraint handling. Experiments on synthetic, benchmark, and real-world problems demonstrate effectiveness across diverse objective, constraint, and fidelity settings.

</details>


### [197] [Robust Decentralized Fairness Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10199)
*Sayan Biswas,Jade Garcia Bourrée,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Palak,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Auditopus，一种去中心化公平性审计方法，通过多轮审计与本地降权机制抵御恶意审计者的fairwashing攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴立法要求对LLM进行合规审计（尤其公平性）。黑盒审计通常假设单一审计者拥有大规模代表性查询集，但现实中难以获得；多审计者协作可覆盖相关人群，但会引发信任问题——部分审计者可能代表LLM提供方伪造公平假象（fairwashing）。

Method: Auditopus采用无中心服务器的多轮审计：每轮每个审计者向LLM发出固定数量查询，仅向其他审计者发送累积统计向量而非明文敏感查询，聚合向量估计LLM公平性。针对单个恶意审计者可通过伪造向量操纵估计的问题，每个诚实审计者本地对累积统计向量与历史不一致的审计者进行降权。

Result: 在两个数据集、两个预训练LLM上与鲁棒聚合基线比较：面对优化发送向量使LLM显得公平的攻击者，Auditopus将审计误差相对无防御平均降低最多78%，相对鲁棒聚合基线至少降低62%；即使49%审计者为恶意，也从未让非常不公平或中度不公平的LLM被判定为公平。

Conclusion: Auditopus通过去中心化多轮审计与基于统计一致性的本地降权，有效抵御fairwashing，提升公平性审计的鲁棒性与准确性。

Abstract: Emerging legislation requires large language models (LLMs) to be audited for compliance with regulatory standards, particularly fairness. Such black-box audits typically assume a single auditor with access to a large, representative set of queries. In practice, it can be difficult for an auditor to obtain such a query set, but multiple auditors can together cover the relevant demographic groups by auditing the LLM collaboratively with their individual query sets. However, relying on multiple auditors raises a fundamental trust problem, as they may act on behalf of the LLM provider to portray a misleading appearance of fairness, i.e., fairwashing. We propose Auditopus, a novel approach for robust decentralized fairness auditing. In Auditopus, auditing proceeds in rounds without a central server. In each round, every auditor issues a fixed number of queries to the LLM, and sends only cumulative statistics vectors of its query results to other auditors instead of sensitive queries in clear. The fairness of the audited LLM is then estimated by aggregating all the vectors. We show theoretically and empirically that even a single adversarial auditor in the network can steer this estimate by fabricating the vectors it sends, making an unfair LLM appear fair. To address this threat, Auditopus has each honest auditor locally down-weight any auditor whose cumulative statistics vectors are statistically inconsistent with previous ones. We implement Auditopus and compare it to robust aggregation baselines on two datasets with two pre-trained LLMs. Against an attacker that optimizes the vectors it sends to make the LLM appear fair, Auditopus reduces audit error by up to 78% on average relative to no defense and at least 62% relative to the robust aggregation baselines. Even when 49% of the auditors are adversarial, Auditopus never lets a very unfair or moderately unfair LLM pass as fair.

</details>


### [198] [How to train your model organism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10203)
*Xilin Wang,David Bau,Byron C. Wallace*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出仅以单一"植入目标行为"目标训练模型生物体不足，提出从目标行为植入、通用能力保持与输出自然性三方面验证模型生物体，并给出基于模型合并的多目标训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型生物体（如后门、谄媚、伪相关）是评估白盒可解释性技术的关键工具。但当前普遍做法是仅以植入目标行为的单一目标来训练模型生物体，这会导致所支撑的可解释性结论缺乏泛化性。

Method: 提出包含三项目标及对应指标的验证框架：目标行为植入、通用能力保持（参数化知识与对话质量）、输出自然性（思维链CoT与激活）。用该框架重新审视两个公开的模型生物体套件；提出一种基于模型合并（model merging）的多目标训练方法；并针对临床推理中人口统计偏差构建新的模型生物体套件，比较监督微调与DPO等训练配方。

Result: （1）对话质量与CoT自然性在各训练配方下均显著退化；（2）验证指标可预测可解释性方法恢复目标行为的效果，如logit lens读出与生物体通用能力协变；（3）在新套件中，DPO训练比监督微调更接近基座模型，所提模型优化方法更好地保持了能力与自然性；（4）用调查代理审计该套件，验证指标再次与偏差恢复情况相关联。

Conclusion: 训练方法塑造了模型生物体所能支撑的可解释性结论；应综合考虑多个目标，才能借助（更真实的）模型生物体得出关于可解释性方法的可泛化结论。

Abstract: Model organisms of alignment-relevant behaviors (e.g., backdoors, sycophancy, spurious correlations) have emerged as a key tool for evaluating whitebox interpretability techniques. We argue that the prevailing practice of training model organisms to a single objective of installing the target behavior is insufficient and propose validating model organisms with respect to three objectives with associated metrics: target-behavior installation, general-capability preservation (i.e., parametric knowledge, chat quality), and output naturalness (i.e., CoT and activations).
