We re-visit two publicly released organism suites using this validation framework and show that (1) chat quality and CoT naturalness degrade substantially across training recipes, and (2) validation metrics predict how well interpretability methods recover the installed behavior, e.g., a logit lens readout covaries with an organism's general capabilities. We introduce a multi-objective training approach based on model merging to train more realistic model organisms. Finally, on a new suite of model organisms targeting demographic biases in clinical reasoning, we compare training recipes and find that DPO training stays closer to the base model than supervised finetuning, and the proposed model optimization approach better preserves capabilities and naturalness. Auditing this suite with an investigator agent, we again observe validation metrics tracking bias recovery. In sum, training methods shape the interpretability conclusions an organism supports, and we argue that one should consider multiple objectives to draw generalizable conclusions about interpretability methods using (realistic) model organisms.

</details>


### [199] [OrthoGen: A Generative Orthogonal Learner for Time-Varying Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10210)
*Tomàs Garriga,Valentyn Melnychuk,Konstantin Hess,Eduard Serrahima de Cambra,Axel Brando,Gerard Sanz,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式递归g-计算调整策略与OrthoGen（Neyman正交、双稳健生成学习器），用于估计时变治疗下的条件分布潜在结果（CDPO），并在合成、半合成和真实数据上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学中常需估计不同治疗序列下患者特异性风险，即随时间变化的条件分布潜在结果（CDPO）。但时变混杂使该任务困难，现有调整策略有限，且简单生成学习器对干扰参数估计误差敏感。

Method: 提出针对该场景的调整策略"生成式递归g-计算"：递归传播完整条件结果分布而非条件均值，直接建模关注变量而非完整轨迹。基于此构建简单生成学习器，并进一步提出OrthoGen——一个Neyman正交且双稳健的生成学习器，可实例化为归一化流、扩散模型等不同生成骨干。

Result: 理论上证明OrthoGen在适当条件下具有速率双稳健性和准神谕效率；实验上在合成、半合成及真实世界数据集上，OrthoGen均表现出高度有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次提出用于时变治疗下CDPO估计的生成式正交学习器，兼具灵活性与稳健性。

Abstract: Estimating conditional distributional potential outcomes (CDPOs) over time is important in medicine (e.g., to estimate patient-specific risks under different treatment sequences). However, this task is challenging because of time-varying confounding, yet existing adjustment strategies for this task are limited. In this paper, we aim to learn CDPOs under time-varying treatments using flexible generative models. Our contributions are two-fold. (1) We introduce a tailored adjustment strategy for our setting, namely, generative recursive g-computation. Our adjustment strategy recursively propagates full conditional outcome distributions rather than conditional means, modeling the variables of interest directly rather than full trajectories. Building on our adjustment strategy, we formulate simple generative learners for CDPO estimation. However, these learners can be sensitive to nuisance estimation errors, which motivates an orthogonal learner. (2) We thus introduce OrthoGen, a Neyman-orthogonal and doubly robust generative learner. Importantly, we show that OrthoGen further achieves rate double robustness and quasi-oracle efficiency under suitable conditions. Our learners are flexible and can be instantiated with different generative backbones (e.g., normalizing flows and diffusion models). Across experiments with synthetic, semi-synthetic and real-world datasets, we find that OrthoGen is highly effective. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to propose a generative orthogonal learner for estimating CDPOs under time-varying treatments.

</details>


### [200] [Edge Accuracy Is Not Enough: Why Dynamics-Learned Structure Fails to Transfer to Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10213)
*Nicholas Tan Jerome,Fangnian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"用丰富前向仿真数据学到的关系结构迁移到稀缺标注的逆问题"策略，证明其虽满足经典理论条件却系统性失效，并将失效归因于边准确率无法控制的近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆问题标注数据稀缺，常见思路是迁移前向仿真中学到的关系结构。既有理论表明只要边误差足够小结构就能降低样本复杂度，因此作者希望检验该策略在实践中是否真的有效。

Method: 理论层面证明近似结构在边误差满足 Δ < n² − kn 时可将样本复杂度由 O(n²) 降到 O(kn+Δ)；实验上用神经关系推断（NRI）从动力学预测中学结构，在 180 个 CFD 氢气泄漏与 180 个声学场景的源定位任务上，与任务优化的注意力基线及物理先验（Green 函数）比较。通过四组证据（增加 18 倍数据、边阈值敏感性、图重叠率、基于相关与互信息的结构估计器）排除调参因素，并提出基于 Jaccard 相似度的可迁移性检验。

Result: NRI 学到的结构使性能相对注意力基线分别下降 116% 与 201%，物理先验仅下降 69–72%；NRI 在 18 倍数据下只提升 0.5%（基线提升 16.6%，p<0.001）；对边阈值不敏感；动力学图与任务最优图仅 6% 边重叠；相关/互信息估计器相对无结构基线无可测收益，相关法明显更差。Jaccard 可迁移性检验在全部四组域/结构组合上均能区分成功与失败迁移，耗时不足一小时、仅用目标域 15–20% 数据。

Conclusion: 满足经典边准确率条件并不保证结构迁移有效，失效源于该条件无法约束的近似误差；所提 Jaccard 检验是仅在小样本上校准的启发式方法，而非经过验证的通用阈值。

Abstract: A natural strategy for inverse problems with scarce labelled data is to transfer relational structure learned from abundant forward-simulation data. We show this strategy fails systematically, even when it satisfies the standard theoretical justification for why structure should help. We prove that approximate structure provides estimation-error benefits whenever the edge error satisfies $Δ< n^2 - kn$, reducing sample complexity from $O(n^2)$ to $O(kn+Δ)$. Structure learned via Neural Relational Inference (NRI) from dynamics prediction satisfies this condition, yet on a source-localisation task across 180 CFD-simulated hydrogen-leak scenarios and 180 acoustic scenarios, it degrades performance by 116% and 201% relative to a flexible, task-optimised attention baseline, while a physics-based prior (Green's function) degrades by only 69-72%. Four independent lines of evidence show this is not a tuning failure: NRI improves only 0.5% when given 18x more training data (versus 16.6% for the task-optimised baseline, $p<0.001$); performance is insensitive to the NRI edge threshold across a wide range; the dynamics-learned graph overlaps the task-optimal graph on only 6% of edges; and two further dynamics-derived structure estimators (correlation- and mutual-information-based) show no measurable benefit over a structure-free baseline, with the correlation-based estimator performing markedly worse. We formalise this gap as a statement about approximation error that the edge-accuracy condition cannot control, and we provide a lightweight transferability test (Jaccard similarity against a partially-observed target-task graph) that separates successful from failed transfer in all four domain/structure pairs we evaluate, using under an hour of computation and 15-20% of target-domain data; we present this as a heuristic calibrated on few cases, not a validated general threshold.

</details>


### [201] [Evaluating Sequence Assembly Strategies for Differentially Private Synthetic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10222)
*Guoxiong Long,Huizhen Huang,Qikun Cai,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了差分隐私时间序列生成器输出的固定长度合成窗口在生成后如何拼接的问题，系统改变重叠率与窗口加权方案，评估其对边界连续性、统计/时间保真度及TSTR预测效用的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私时间序列生成器通常输出固定长度合成窗口，而下游预测模型需要长连续训练序列；因此生成后窗口的拼接方式会改变预测器实际接收到的合成数据，即便生成器本身不变。论文旨在弄清这一后生成拼接过程如何影响合成数据的质量与预测效用。

Method: 在四类公开数据集（ETTh1、ETTm1、Weather、Appliances）和五种预测模型上，系统变化重叠率与窗口加权方案，构建完整的五预测器拼接网格，并以匹配的TRTR（真实训练-真实测试）作为参照，评估边界连续性、统计与时间保真度以及TSTR预测效用，同时开展鲁棒性与生成器可变性分析。

Result: TSTR效用由预测器、重叠率与窗口加权方案共同决定，不同预测模型呈现不同的拼接偏好；提高重叠率通常改善边界连续性，但连续性或单项保真度指标的改善并不总能降低预测误差，说明这些诊断指标本身不足以选择拼接配置；完整拼接网格与TRTR参照刻画了这些规律，并量化了相对于真实数据训练的拼接依赖效用。

Conclusion: 合成窗口的后生成拼接是影响下游预测效用的关键环节，且最优拼接配置依赖具体预测器；连续性等单项诊断不足以指导配置选择。论文通过鲁棒性与生成器可变性分析验证了所识别的原则。

Abstract: Differentially private time-series generators commonly produce fixed-length synthetic windows, whereas downstream forecasting models often require long continuous training sequences. How these windows are assembled after generation can therefore alter the effective synthetic data presented to a forecaster, even when the trained generator remains unchanged. We study this post-generation sequence assembly process by systematically varying overlap rates and window-weighting schemes and evaluating the resulting sequences in terms of boundary continuity, statistical and temporal fidelity, and Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) forecasting utility. Across four types of public datasets (ETTh1, ETTm1, Weather, and Appliances) and five forecasting models, the results reveal a clear forecaster-dependent assembly principle: downstream TSTR utility is jointly shaped by the forecaster, overlap rate, and window-weighting scheme, leading to distinct assembly preferences across forecasting models. Increased overlap generally improves boundary continuity, but improvements in continuity or individual fidelity diagnostics do not consistently reduce forecasting error, indicating that these diagnostics alone are insufficient for selecting assembly configurations. Complete five-forecaster assembly grids, together with matched Train-on-Real-Test-on-Real (TRTR) references, further characterize these regularities and quantify assembly-dependent utility relative to real-data training. We then validate the identified principles through additional analyses of robustness and generator variability.

</details>


### [202] [From Prompts to Trees: Effective LLM-Guided Tree Generation for Few-Shot Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10227)
*Yue Qiu,Zekang Du,Yiqun Diao,Bingsheng He,Qinbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个三阶段框架，将LLM知识蒸馏为可解释决策树，在少样本表格分类中兼顾准确率、可解释性与低提示开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具备丰富世界知识与泛化能力，但直接用于表格数据分类存在推理成本高、可解释性差的问题；决策树快速透明，却在低数据场景下表现不佳。作者希望结合二者优势。

Method: 在少样本设定下，不直接让LLM生成完整决策树（不稳定且低效），而是采用三阶段范式：先提示LLM生成规则，再将规则组织成树，从而把LLM知识蒸馏进可解释决策树。

Result: 在多个真实表格数据集上，该方法相比现有基线取得更优的准确率与可解释性，且提示开销显著更低。

Conclusion: 通过规则生成加规则组织为树的三阶段蒸馏范式，可在低数据表格分类任务中有效融合LLM知识与决策树的可解释性，实现性能与效率的平衡。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) possess rich world knowledge and impressive generalization capabilities, their direct application to tabular data classification is hindered by high inference costs and limited interpretability. In contrast, decision trees are fast and transparent but often underperform in low-data regimes. In this work, we propose a novel framework that bridges these paradigms by distilling LLM knowledge into interpretable decision trees under a few-shot learning setting. Instead of directly prompting the LLM to generate full trees, which is often unstable and inefficient, we develop a three-stage paradigm that prompts the LLM to generate rules and organize the rules into a tree. Experiments on multiple real-world tabular datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy and interpretability with significantly lower prompting overhead compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [203] [ProtocolMatch: Protocol-Dependent Model Selection for Scientific Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10239)
*Lu Wei,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将科学动力学预测从单纯"架构选择"重新表述为"协议依赖的模型选择"问题，提出 ProtocolMatch 评估框架，在量子自旋动力学上系统比较循环网络、分块注意力、因果注意力与低秩线性预测器，证明预测器排序会随训练集规模、观测历史、状态刷新方式、物理惩罚与分布偏移而改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学动力学预测研究常被简化为架构选择问题，但实际部署性能还取决于观测历史长度、闭环反馈、计算预算、物理目标函数与测试分布。缺少一个计算量匹配、基于验证集选择、并保留失败案例的公平评估协议，容易得出被协议混淆的结论（如架构排序反转）。

Method: 提出 ProtocolMatch：计算量匹配（compute-matched）、验证集选择（validation-selected）、保留失败模式（failure-preserving）的评估框架。任务为驱动量子自旋动力学，使用三个独立生成的数据集；比较四类预测器：循环网络、分块注意力（patched-attention）、因果注意力、低秩线性预测器；控制变量包括观测历史长度、状态刷新/闭环反馈、系统尺寸（两自旋、四自旋、六自旋）、物理惩罚项以及驱动频率偏移。

Result: 1）在固定两自旋任务中，随训练集增大，因果注意力与循环网络的排序发生反转；2）六自旋局部可观测量比较中，线性预测器平均误差最低；3）四自旋研究中，限制观测历史使所有"刷新历史"视图变差，却使所有"闭环"视图变好；4）最新状态 MLP 在全部五个单元、所有数据集的状态刷新下误差均低于持续性基线，但其闭环排名随系统变化，并出现有限的爆炸性误差；5）物理惩罚提升目标一致性但未可靠降低预测误差；6）驱动频率偏移后，分布内区间几乎完全丧失覆盖率。

Conclusion: 科学模型选择不应只返回单一预测器，而应同时返回其评估协议，并分别报告预测精度、物理有效性与偏移分布下的可靠性。架构排序并非普适，依赖训练集规模、观测历史、反馈方式与测试分布，因此需采用协议匹配的评估与多维度报告规范。

Abstract: Scientific dynamics forecasting is often framed as an architecture choice, although deployment is also determined by observed history, rollout feedback, compute budget, physical objective, and test distribution. We formulate protocol-dependent model selection and introduce ProtocolMatch, a compute-matched, validation-selected, and failure-preserving evaluation framework. On driven quantum-spin dynamics, we compare recurrent, patched-attention, causal-attention, and low-rank linear predictors across three independently generated datasets. The causal-attention--recurrence ordering reverses as the training set grows within a fixed two-spin task, while a linear predictor has the lowest mean error in the six-spin local-observable comparison. Restricting observed history worsens every refreshed-history view but improves every closed-loop view in the four-spin study. A latest-state MLP has lower error than persistence on every dataset under state refresh across all five cells, yet its closed-loop rank varies by system and includes finite explosive errors. Physical penalties improve targeted consistency without reliably improving prediction error, and in-distribution intervals lose most coverage after a driving-frequency shift. Thus scientific model selection should return a predictor with its protocol and report accuracy, physical validity, and shifted-distribution reliability separately.

</details>


### [204] [MorphCL: Morphological Contrastive Learning for Inertial-based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10245)
*Marius Bock,Yuwei Zhang,Juergen Gall,Michael Moeller,Kristof Van Laerhoven,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MorphCL，一种面向惯性运动数据的自监督预训练框架，通过结构感知分组将全局结构建模注入对比学习，显著提升线性探测与微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴与移动设备产生的惯性数据规模庞大但缺乏标注，自监督学习虽缓解了标注需求，却忽略了大规模运动数据的全局结构，依赖随机采样批次与局部对比，在野外惯性数据中因静止、低方差行为占主导而效果受限。

Method: MorphCL基于运动分析的两大支柱——运动基元（motif）发现与领域专用特征描述子，采用结构感知分组策略，将全局结构显式注入惯性自监督学习流程。

Result: 在F1-score上，线性探测与微调结果提升最多达15个百分点；与现有基础模型相比，MorphCL预训练编码器在仅使用约1/4600训练数据的情况下，线性探测性能达到或超越这些模型。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间的定性分析显示，MorphCL学习到的表征具有形态学意义的聚类结构，能更好地区分运动学上相似的活动类别。

Abstract: Despite the ubiquity of sensors in wearable and mobile devices and the abundance of human movement data they generate, translating unlabeled recordings into foundational motion models remains an open challenge. Self-supervised learning (SSL) has alleviated the need for costly annotations, yet existing approaches leave the global structure of large-scale motion data largely untapped, relying on randomly sampled batches and local comparisons that become particularly problematic for in-the-wild inertial data dominated by stationary, low-variance behaviors. Here we introduce Morphological Contrastive Learning (MorphCL), a self-supervised pretraining framework that uses structure-aware grouping to inject explicit modeling of global structure into inertial-based SSL approaches. Building on two well-established pillars of motion analysis, the discovery of motion primitives, or motifs, and domain-specific feature descriptors, we show that MorphCL substantially improves linear probing and finetuning results of learned encoders by up to 15 percentage points in F1-score. In a comparison with existing foundation models, we demonstrate that MorphCL-pretrained encoders match or surpass them models in linear probing performance while trained on $4600\times$ less data. Qualitative analysis of the resulting embedding spaces further reveals morphologically meaningful cluster structure, with improved separation of kinematically similar activity classes.

</details>


### [205] [Stationary Bias and Extrapolation in Nonlinear Two-Timescale Stochastic Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10246)
*A. Ch. Madhusudanarao,Rahul Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究非线性双时间尺度随机逼近在常步长下的稳态均值误差，给出均匀有效的偏差展开，揭示 ε²/η 混合项并指导 Richardson-Romberg 外推的权重选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常步长随机逼近存在不为零的稳态均值误差，且时间平均无法消除。现有工作多关注线性或单时间尺度情形，对由外生有限状态马尔可夫链驱动的非线性双时间尺度递推中该误差的结构尚不清晰，尤其当慢步长远小于快步长时误差界不再一致。

Method: 在给定光滑性假设与平稳分布条件下，对外生有限状态马尔可夫链驱动的非线性双时间尺度递推推导一阶偏差展开；利用快流形坐标使协方差方程在该极限下保持正则，并给出初始化误差界（附加耦合假设），以及在加性独立噪声下的三阶矩相消更紧余项。

Result: 偏差展开中除各步长的线性项外，还出现混合贡献 ε²/η；沿幂律步长路径偏差指数不必为整数，故 Richardson-Romberg 外推需按路径匹配权重。一个可精确求解的非线性马尔可夫例子验证了系数；在等更新预算下对时序差分学习验证了局部化并比较了有限运行外推。

Conclusion: 常步长双时间尺度随机逼近的稳态偏差具有非平凡的步长依赖结构，混合项 ε²/η 对外推权重选择起决定作用，快流形坐标与三阶矩相消是获得均匀、更紧误差界的关键工具。

Abstract: Constant-step stochastic approximation generally has a nonzero stationary mean error that persists under time averaging. This paper studies that error for nonlinear two-timescale recursions driven by an exogenous finite-state Markov chain. Under stated smoothness assumptions and conditions on the stationary distribution, we derive a first-order bias expansion whose error bound remains uniform as the slow step size becomes much smaller than the fast step size. Fast-manifold coordinates keep the associated covariance equation regular in this limit. For fast step $η$ and slow step $\varepsilon$, the expansion reveals a mixed contribution $\varepsilon^2/η$ alongside terms linear in each step size. This dependence matters for bias reduction: along power-law step-size paths, the bias exponents need not be integers, so Richardson--Romberg extrapolation requires weights matched to the path. An exactly solvable nonlinear Markov example verifies the coefficients. We verify localization for temporal-difference learning and compare finite-run extrapolation at equal update budgets. For finite runs, we bound the initialization error of tail averages on both timescales under an additional coupling assumption. In the special case of additive independent noise, signed third-moment cancellation yields a sharper remainder.

</details>


### [206] [Logarithmic Regret via Passive Change Detection in Piecewise-Stationary Self-Tuning Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10250)
*A. Ch. Madhusudanarao,Rahul Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究未知含外生输入自回归系统在系数未知时刻变化下的最小方差控制，提出 PIECE-CD 算法，在固定检测间隔、稳定性与可行性等条件下实现对数级遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知 ARX 系统系数在未知时刻发生变化的变点控制问题，需要在有限扰动下兼顾检测与控制的性能，并刻画相比切换赌博机这类"未选臂不可观测"设定下变点可观测性带来的信息优势。

Method: 提出 PIECE-CD：在初始阶段和报警后主动探索，随后采用门控递推最小二乘进行控制；用能量检验比较窗口输出功率与高于噪声底的阈值来检测变化，针对不稳定控制器失配额外监测参考控制器的输入建议；输入按给定界裁剪，并控制全时域虚警率。

Result: 在固定检测间隙、稳定性与可行性条件及变点间充分间隔下，证明以至少 1-δ 的概率取得 O((C+1)log((T+1)/δ)) 遗憾，其中 T 为时域、C 为变点数；并证明对数级检测延迟，以及在裁剪经有限 burn-in 后失效的显式条件下仍保持对数遗憾。

Conclusion: 可行植物变化在利用阶段即可提供信息，因为正确可行控制器使输出仅含扰动，而变化会抬高旧控制器下的输出能量；扩展检测器在可行性条件下可覆盖具有可检测超额能量的失稳变化。

Abstract: We study minimum-variance control of an unknown autoregressive system with exogenous inputs and coefficients that change at unknown times. Under bounded independent disturbances, fixed detection gaps, stability and feasibility conditions, and sufficient time between changes, we prove \(O((C+1)\log((T+1)/δ))\) regret with probability at least \(1-δ\), where \(T\) is the horizon and \(C\) the number of changes. Unlike switching bandits, where unselected arms can change unobserved, admissible plant changes provide information during exploitation: the correct feasible controller leaves only the disturbance in the output, whereas a detectable change raises output energy under the old controller. PIECE-CD explores initially and after alarms, then uses gated recursive least squares for control. Its energy test compares windowed output power with a threshold above the noise floor; the extension to unstable controller mismatches also monitors the reference controller's input proposal. We control false alarms across the horizon and prove logarithmic detection delay. Inputs are clipped to prescribed bounds. Logarithmic regret also holds under an explicit condition ensuring that clipping becomes inactive after a finite burn-in. Under the stated feasibility conditions, the extended detector covers destabilizing changes with detectable excess energy over a fixed window.

</details>


### [207] [PairAudit: Guiding Human Review with Graph Tokens under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10260)
*Jiran Tao,Binyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAudit利用图token捕获节点间预测模式关系，通过异常关系模式发现已有预测中的潜在错误，在固定审查预算下借助人工反馈优化审查优先级，从而纠正更多错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 入侵检测器会对训练中未见的攻击给出高置信度的错误分类。人工审查可纠正这些错误，但受限于审查数量。基于不确定性的审查会忽略高置信度错误，而单靠异常分数又无法显示调整审查计划是否能纠正更多错误。

Method: 提出PairAudit：其图token捕获相连节点间的预测模式；不通过特征聚合构建新的预测器，而是利用异常的关系模式来揭示现有预测中的潜在错误；随后借助人工反馈判断这些发现是否值得调整审查优先级。

Result: 在多项安全任务实验中，PairAudit平均比基于不确定性的审查纠正更多错误，包括对未见攻击的更多错误；这些收益已计入全部审查成本，且无需重新训练检测器。

Conclusion: PairAudit在固定预算下实现更高效的错误发现与审查，优于基于不确定性的审查，且不需重训检测器。

Abstract: Intrusion detectors can confidently misclassify attacks that were not seen during training. Human review can correct these errors, but only a limited number of cases can be checked. Uncertainty-based review may overlook confident errors, while anomaly scores alone do not show whether changing the review plan will correct more errors. We introduce PairAudit to find overlooked errors and improve review under a fixed budget. Its graph tokens capture prediction patterns across connected nodes. Rather than building another predictor through feature aggregation, PairAudit uses unusual relational patterns to uncover potential errors in existing predictions. Human feedback then helps decide whether these findings justify changing review priorities. Experiments across security tasks show that PairAudit corrects more errors on average than uncertainty-based review, including more errors on unseen attacks. These gains account for all review costs and do not require retraining the detector.

</details>


### [208] [A Closed-Loop Non-Asymptotic Convergence Analysis of PPO with Learned Critics and Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10273)
*Junwei Su,Mengfan Liu,Yanyong Zhang,Chuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对PPO-Clip作为闭环actor-critic系统进行了非渐近分析，刻画了策略平稳性与critic跟踪精度，并给出显式的参数依赖与调参指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO-Clip尽管应用广泛，但仍难以调参，且critic学习、clipping与rollout复用之间的相互作用尚未被充分理解，缺乏对闭环actor-critic整体行为的理论刻画。

Method: 在显式的覆盖与critic正则性假设下，采用原始GAE与蒙特卡洛critic目标，对同步与异步PPO-Clip进行非渐近分析，刻画actor-critic耦合、非光滑概率比clipping、有限批次复用与可预测早停；针对有限分层MDP与表格critic，用实际clipped-gradient类的统一界替代完整轨迹计数。

Result: 同步与异步保证联合刻画了策略平稳性与critic跟踪精度，并显式依赖算法参数；充分耦合条件给出优化、critic跟踪、clipping与有限批次误差的统一放大界；异步情形需要延迟相关critic步长限制；已验证的增长时域族具有多项式样本复杂度，双时间尺度调度给出O(T^{-2/5})的平稳性与critic跟踪界，并显式计入新鲜rollout。

Conclusion: 该分析推进了对PPO的理解，并为调参提供了理论指导；不满足所给条件并不蕴含发散。

Abstract: Despite its widespread use, Proximal Policy Optimization with clipping (PPO-Clip) remains difficult to tune, and the interactions among critic learning, clipping, and rollout reuse remain incompletely understood. We develop a \emph{non-asymptotic} analysis of PPO-Clip as a \emph{closed-loop actor--critic} system. It captures actor--critic coupling, nonsmooth probability-ratio clipping, finite-batch reuse, and predictable early stopping under explicit coverage and critic regularity assumptions, using raw GAE and Monte Carlo critic targets. Our synchronous and asynchronous guarantees jointly characterize policy stationarity and the tracking accuracy of the learned critic, with explicit dependence on algorithmic parameters. A sufficient coupling condition gives optimization, critic tracking, clipping, and finite-batch errors a common amplification bound. The asynchronous result also requires a delay-dependent critic stepsize restriction; violating these conditions does not establish divergence. For finite layered MDPs with tabular critics, a uniform bound on the actual clipped-gradient class replaces complete-trajectory counting. A verified growing-horizon family has polynomial sample complexity, and a two-time-scale schedule gives $O(T^{-2/5})$ stationarity and critic-tracking bounds with explicit fresh-rollout accounting. These results together advance our understanding about PPO and provide theoretical guidance in tuning.

</details>


### [209] [Sparse Planning in Visual World Models via Cost Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10274)
*Yingchen Xu,Edward Grefenstette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COSTGRAD——一种免训练、目标条件化的空间token选择器，依据规划代价对每个输入token的梯度范数进行排序，从而在稀疏token世界模型中加速动作搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于token的世界模型可实现细粒度隐空间规划，但反复处理大规模空间token网格导致动作搜索代价高昂，且现有稀疏化方法多面向预测而非规划目标。

Method: COSTGRAD从下游控制目标出发，以规划代价对输入token的梯度范数作为重要性度量，对空间token排序并保留高梯度token；该方法免训练、目标条件化，可与其他稀疏化及CEM搜索缩减策略叠加。

Result: 在50%稀疏度、AdaLN条件化预测器上，COSTGRAD在四个连续控制基准中的三个上匹配或超过全token规划，环境规划步测得2.6倍墙钟加速；结合CEM搜索缩减后总加速约5倍且仍超越全token基线。消融发现架构相关失效模式：在AdaLN与concat的匹配对比中，concat保持相近的全token性能，但纯COSTGRAD相对随机选择失去优势，该差异与动作通路漂移一致——AdaLN上梯度选择移除引起的漂移小于随机移除，concat上则更大。

Conclusion: 选择器与架构的兼容性是稀疏世界模型规划的关键设计轴；COSTGRAD在AdaLN架构下有效加速规划，但需注意其在concat架构上的失效风险。

Abstract: Token-based world models enable fine-grained latent planning, but repeatedly processing large spatial token grids makes action search expensive. We introduce COSTGRAD, a training-free, goal-conditioned selector that ranks spatial tokens by the gradient norm of the planning cost with respect to each input token. By deriving importance from the downstream control objective, COSTGRAD targets tokens that matter for planning rather than merely for prediction. On AdaLN-conditioned predictors at $50\%$ sparsity, COSTGRAD matches or exceeds full-token planning on three of four continuous-control benchmarks, while giving a measured $2.6\times$ wall-clock speedup per environment planning step. Combining token sparsity with reduced CEM search increases this to a $\sim 5\times$ total speedup while still exceeding the full-token baseline. We also identify an architecture-dependent failure mode: in a matched AdaLN-vs-concat comparison, concat maintains comparable full-token performance but pure COSTGRAD loses its advantage over random selection. This difference tracks action-pathway drift: gradient-selected removal produces less drift than random removal on AdaLN, but more on concat. These results highlight selector-architecture compatibility as a design axis for sparse world-model planning. Project page and demos: https://ycxuyingchen.github.io/costgrad/

</details>


### [210] [PatchBench: Measuring Collateral Damage in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10276)
*Alexi Canesse,Mathis Le Bail,Maël Jenny,Clément Elliker,Mahammed El Sharkawy,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PatchBench 与 PatchBench-Local，用于评估越狱修复补丁的行为精确性，揭示全局指标掩盖的局部良性回退问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全补丁可能仅通过基准测试却并非良好修复。越狱修复要求只纠正特定不安全行为而不改动无关行为，但补丁可能只屏蔽评估提示、在相近有害变体上失效，或因过度拒答与原文措辞/结构相似的良性提示而压制有害行为。现有协议主要测试模型是否可被攻破，聚合指标（攻击成功率、拒答率、全局能力）无法区分选择性修复与更广泛的局部压制。

Method: 构建 PatchBench：从 37 个公开数据集的 27,870 条提示中筛选出 15,314 条英文提示，查询 8 个开源指令微调模型；结合 WildGuard 过滤、成对 Elo 排序与人工核验，保留 400 条高置信度越狱失败样本库。进一步提出 PatchBench-Local 评估协议，针对每个有害源提示生成三类局部邻居：保留恶意意图的有害变体、结构匹配的良性提示、复用关键有害词项的良性提示；评估有害邻居纠正率与良性邻居保持率，以区分选择性修复与全局局部压制。在四种激活引导方法上结合 MMLU 进行评测。

Result: 评估四种激活引导方法显示，全局能力可几乎不变，而局部良性回退严重，证实聚合指标会遗漏重要的附带损害。

Conclusion: PatchBench-Local 为开发和比较越狱修复方法提供了更精确的依据。

Abstract: An LLM safety patch can pass a benchmark while still being a poor repair. This risk is especially acute for jailbreak repairs, where the goal is to correct a specific unsafe behaviour without changing unrelated behaviours. A patch may block exact evaluation prompts yet fail on close harmful variants, or suppress harmful behaviour by over-refusing benign prompts that share its wording or structure. Existing protocols primarily test whether models can be broken, while aggregate metrics (attack success, refusal rates, global capability) cannot distinguish selective repairs from broader local suppression. To address this gap, we introduce PatchBench, a benchmark of empirically observed model-specific jailbreak failures inducing actionable harmful answers. Starting from 27,870 prompts from 37 public datasets, we curate 15,314 English prompts and query 8 open-source instruction-tuned models. Combining WildGuard filtering, pairwise Elo ranking, and manual verification, we retain a curated bank of 400 high-confidence jailbreak failures. We further introduce PatchBench-Local, an evaluation protocol testing whether a patch is behaviourally precise. For each harmful source prompt, PatchBench-Local generates three families of local neighbours: harmful variants preserving malicious intent, benign prompts with matched structure, and benign prompts reusing key harmful terms. It evaluates harmful-neighbour correction and benign-neighbour preservation, distinguishing selective repair from broader local suppression. Evaluating four activation steering methods with PatchBench-Local and MMLU shows that global capability can remain nearly unchanged while local benign regressions are severe, confirming aggregate metrics miss important collateral damage. PatchBench-Local provides a more precise basis for developing and comparing jailbreak repair methods.

</details>


### [211] [Physics-Aligned Electronic Ground-State Learning Improves Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10298)
*Eike S. Eberhard,Xaver Kainz,Viktor Kotsev,Abdulrahman Aldossary,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出观测无关的电子基态描述符模型（GSMs），通过物理约束与去除无关自由度的训练方法，在MLIPs与KS-DFT之间建立新的成本-精度帕累托前沿，大幅提升分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在分布内任务表现优异，但泛化到分布外（OOD）场景能力差；需要在MLIPs与Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论（KS-DFT）之间寻找成本-精度折中方案。

Method: 设计观测无关的电子基态描述符模型（GSMs），将学习目标与架构同KS-DFT控制方程对齐：施加物理约束、消除对非物理或无关自由度的优化压力。具体方法包括OrthoNormal-Loss（ON-Loss）与Grassmann受限占据轨道训练（GROOT），以及面向Hamiltonian GSMs的ON-Loss与残差最优规范条件感知KS方程训练（ROCKET）；并采用自洽性拒绝准则过滤外推误差。

Result: 在QM9→QM40的尺寸外推实验中，ON-Loss与GROOT使密度GSMs的能量与力MAE较此前最优分别降低79.1%与83.4%；对Hamiltonian GSMs，ON-Loss与ROCKET使最强基线的能量与力MAE分别降低99.8%与95.9%。在QMugs上使用自洽性拒绝准则，仅拒绝不到0.4%预测即达到0.07 mHa能量MAE。还展示了无标签自洽微调的高效性，并将GSMs迁移至Transition1x反应化学，能量误差低于化学精度。

Conclusion: 将GSMs的学习目标与架构同KS-DFT控制方程对齐、施加物理约束并消除无关自由度优化压力，可显著提升分布外泛化能力，推动成本-精度帕累托前沿，并在外推、误差过滤与反应化学迁移中验证有效性。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) excel at in-distribution tasks, accelerating drug and material development, yet they struggle to generalize out-of-distribution. We propose to push the cost-accuracy Pareto frontier by designing observable-agnostic electronic ground-state descriptor models (GSMs) with computational costs situated between MLIPs and Kohn-Sham density functional theory (KS-DFT). We align the learning objectives and architectures of GSMs with the governing equations of KS-DFT by enforcing physical constraints and removing optimization pressure on unphysical or irrelevant degrees of freedom. In our size-extrapolation experiments from QM9 to QM40, our combined contributions OrthoNormal-Loss (ON-Loss) and Grassmann Restricted Occupied-Orbital Training (GROOT) reach a 79.1% energy and 83.4% force mean absolute error (MAE) reduction over previous state-of-the-art density GSMs. For Hamiltonian GSMs, ON-Loss and Residual Optimal-gauge Conditioning-aware KS-Eq. Training (ROCKET) together reduce the energy and force MAEs of the strongest baseline by 99.8% and 95.9%, respectively. Using a self-consistency rejection criterion, we filter out extrapolation errors on QMugs, rejecting fewer than 0.4% of predictions while reaching an energy MAE of 0.07 mHa. Finally, we demonstrate the efficiency of label-free self-consistency fine-tuning, and transfer GSMs to reactive chemistry in Transition1x, reaching energy errors below chemical accuracy.

</details>


### [212] [Continual Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10302)
*Tommaso Marzi,Ahmed Hendawy,Jan Peters,Carlo D'Eramo,Andrea Cini,Cesare Alippi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CGMARL框架，将连续多智能体强化学习任务序列建模为属性图，并发布GRAFO基准与FROG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续多智能体强化学习方法缺乏利用任务结构信息的机制，难以促进知识迁移并缓解灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出CGMARL框架，用任务特定属性的图刻画环境动态与智能体数量；构建GRAFO基准；提出FROG，利用冻结图编码器保留历史结构信息。

Result: GRAFO揭示了图结构任务中的遗忘现象；FROG与现有持续学习方法结合后在多个CGMARL场景中显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 基于图的建模与冻结图编码器可有效提升连续多智能体强化学习的迁移能力并抑制遗忘。

Abstract: In Continual Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (CMARL), agents learn cooperative policies across sequences of tasks, aiming to adapt effectively to new tasks while preserving the ability to solve previously encountered ones. In many applications, tasks differ in their underlying structure, which can represent, for example, distinct operational conditions or target configurations (e.g., different network topologies in power grids or arrangements in formation control). Existing CMARL methods lack dedicated mechanisms to leverage this structural information when learning new tasks, failing to promote transfer and mitigate forgetting. To fill this gap, we propose Continual Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (CGMARL), a novel framework for CMARL problems in which task sequences are mapped into a series of attributed graphs, each modeling a task-specific structure. In CGMARL, each graph determines the environment dynamics (next states and/or rewards) and the number of agents for the corresponding task. Then, we present Graph-based Formation (GRAFO), the first CGMARL benchmark, and show how forgetting arises in this setting. Finally, to address this limitation, we propose Frozen Graph Encoder (FROG), a method that relies on a frozen graph backbone to preserve past structural information in graph-based CMARL policies. Experiments on GRAFO show that pairing FROG with existing CL methods substantially improves performance on multiple CGMARL scenarios.

</details>


### [213] [SemanticFold: Latent Sequence Compression SeparatesLanguage Modeling, Decodability, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10304)
*Mingyan Liu,Min Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemanticFold 前缀隐状态压缩方案，在五种模型规模上以固定目标协议评估五类端点，发现压缩对各端点的影响非单调且不存在统一的压缩阈值，即隐压缩下的能力保持没有单一标量证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究提示前缀的隐序列压缩是否保留大模型推理所依赖的能力，并检验"能力保持"能否用单一指标刻画。

Method: 提出 SemanticFold，在学习到的边界处折叠前缀隐状态。采用固定目标协议：冻结前缀分别以原生或压缩方式执行，两臂教师强制相同续写 token，以排除目标选择对似然变化的解释。在 Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-8B、SmolLM2-1.7B、Pythia-1.4B、Pythia-6.9B 上考察五类端点：固定目标负对数似然、有限标签推理准确率、线性探针可及性、开放式生成、系统级内存与延迟。并通过 NLL 分解与 MLP-only 消融分离序列缩短与残差变换的贡献。

Result: 压缩使各端点非单调变化且不共享同一压缩阈值。Qwen3-1.7B 在 R=1.7 时压缩相对原生的平均 NLL 下降 0.135（配对 bootstrap，10000 次抽样）；SmolLM2 在 R=1.2 时平均变化比原生高 0.013；两个 Pythia 检查点 NLL 基本不变。NLL 分解显示 Qwen 的有利似然主要来自残差自适应而非单纯缩短；MLP-only（仅变换不缩短）比 Full SemanticFold 的 NLL 低 0.082。线性探针准确率与 macro AUC 在各条件下绝对变化小于 0.03，置信区间跨越零。

Conclusion: 隐压缩下的能力保持不存在单一标量证书：语言模型拟合、可解码性与推理行为回答不同问题，且在同一压缩操作下可朝不同方向变化。

Abstract: We study whether latent sequence compression of prompt prefixes preserves the capabilities that large language models rely on during inference. We introduce SemanticFold, a compression scheme that folds prefix hidden states at learned boundaries, and evaluate it across five model scales: Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-8B, SmolLM2-1.7B, Pythia-1.4B, and Pythia-6.9B. We use a fixed-target protocol: a frozen prefix is executed natively or compressed, and both arms teacher-force identical continuation tokens. This design rules out target-selection explanations for likelihood changes. We examine five endpoint families: fixed-target negative log-likelihood, finite-label reasoning accuracy, linear probe accessibility, open-ended generation, and systems-level memory and latency. We find that compression moves these endpoints non-monotonically and that they do not share a single compression threshold. On Qwen3-1.7B at compression ratio R=1.7, compressed-minus-native mean NLL decreases by 0.135 under paired bootstrap with 10000 draws. On SmolLM2 at R=1.2, the mean change is 0.013 higher than native. On both Pythia checkpoints, NLL is effectively unchanged. An NLL decomposition separating sequence shortening from the learned residual transform shows that the favorable Qwen likelihood is attributable primarily to residual adaptation rather than to shortening alone. MLP-only, which applies the transform without shortening, achieves 0.082 lower NLL than Full SemanticFold. Linear probe accuracy and macro AUC change by less than 0.03 in absolute value across conditions, with confidence intervals crossing zero. We conclude that preservation under latent compression has no single scalar certificate: language-model fit, decodability, and reasoning behavior answer different questions and can move in different directions under the same compression operation.

</details>


### [214] [How Do Transformers Learn to Represent Symmetries?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10305)
*Eduardo Santos-Escriche,Valerie Engelmayer,Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究普通Transformer通过有限数据增强学习点云对称性的能力，发现不同对称群存在可学习性递增顺序，并识别出诱导不变性的可解释机制，进而将其扩展至等变函数学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的架构配合有限数据增强在几何机器学习中日益流行，但其架构、对不同对称性的不变性与增强预算之间的相互作用仍缺乏深入研究。

Method: 对普通Transformer在点云数据集上通过有限数据增强学习多种对称性进行系统研究，涵盖非保角对称、保角对称及基础保角子群（平移、旋转、缩放）；针对基础保角群进一步分析外推行为并进行结构分析以识别可解释机制；最后将分析扩展至等变函数。

Result: 识别出可学习性递增的对称群排序：非保角对称 < 保角对称 < 基础保角子群；发现诱导不变性的可解释机制，并表明这些机制可作为学习等变性的关键构件。

Conclusion: 普通Transformer能够通过有限数据增强学习多种对称性，且其近似不变性机制可进一步支撑等变性的学习，为几何机器学习中的对称性建模提供了新见解。

Abstract: Training Transformer-based architectures with finite data augmentation has become an increasingly popular approach in geometric machine learning. Despite its empirical success, the interplay between the Transformer architecture, invariance to different symmetries, and augmentation budgets remains underexplored. In this paper, we study the ability of a vanilla Transformer to learn various symmetries through finite data augmentation for point cloud datasets. We identify an ordering of increasing learnability across the following symmetry groups: (i) non-angle-preserving symmetries, (ii) angle-preserving symmetries, and (iii) base angle-preserving subgroups, such as translation, rotation, and scale. For the base angle-preserving groups, we further investigate the Transformer's extrapolation behavior and conduct a structural analysis of the trained models, allowing us to identify interpretable mechanisms that induce invariance. Finally, we extend our analysis to equivariant functions and show that the detected mechanisms for approximate invariance can also provide a key building block for learned equivariance. Our project page is available at https://transformers-learn-symmetries.github.io/

</details>


### [215] [RSIGym: A Flexible Environment for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10310)
*Fanqing Meng,Lingxiao Du,Haocheng Lu,Qiguang Chen,Ziqi Zhao,Zijian Wu,Jiayuan Zhuo,Mengkang Hu,Michael Qizhe Shieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RSIGym——一个基于"一切皆服务"(EaaS) 的智能体原生研究环境，通过可复用服务暴露训练、推理、rollout、评估与沙箱执行能力，并定义 RSI-Index 指标，让智能体在同一环境中联合优化数据、训练配置与执行 harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进(RSI)需要把已被接受的改动带入后续改进循环，而研究智能体提出的改动需要大量研究基础设施支持。现有设定常让智能体重复搭建常规基础设施，或将探索限制在单一组件上，难以在统一环境中进行个体干预研究与联合优化。

Method: 构建 RSIGym，基于 Everything as a Service (EaaS) 暴露训练、推理、rollout、评估和沙箱执行等可复用服务，并配备共享预算与权限控制，支持 Data、Harness 与 Joint 三条改进轨道。定义 RSI-Index = 在五个基准(软件工程、终端交互、数学、科学推理、技能型任务)上剩余性能差距被填补比例的平均值。在独立 Joint 运行中对比六个前沿研究模型，并开展 DSH-harness 精炼、预算变化与受限网络访问等附加实验。

Result: 在每基准运行 500 美元平台服务预算下，Opus 5 取得最高 RSI-Index 0.4809；其所选系统改善了全部五个基准，SWE-bench Verified 从 17.67% 提升至 50.33%，AIME 从 31.67% 提升至 97.78%。记录的轨迹揭示了智能体如何诊断失败并选择候选方案。

Conclusion: RSIGym 提供了智能体原生的递归自我改进研究环境与统一评价指标，验证了联合优化数据、训练设置与执行 harness 的可行性与显著收益；代码库与结果全部开源，以支持可复现性和后续研究。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires carrying accepted changes into later improvement cycles, while studying agent-proposed changes also requires substantial research infrastructure. Existing settings often leave agents to rebuild routine infrastructure or restrict exploration to individual components. We introduce RSIGym, an agent-native research environment based on Everything as a Service (EaaS). RSIGym exposes training, inference, rollout, evaluation, and sandbox execution through reusable services, with shared budget and permission controls supporting Data, Harness, and Joint improvement tracks. This design enables agents to investigate individual interventions and jointly optimize data, training settings, and execution harnesses within the same environment. We define RSI-Index as the mean fraction of the remaining performance gap closed across five benchmarks covering software engineering, terminal interaction, mathematics, scientific reasoning, and skill-based tasks. Comparing six frontier research models in independent Joint runs, Opus 5 achieves the highest RSI-Index of 0.4809 under a $500 platform-service budget per benchmark run. Its selected systems improve all five benchmarks, raising SWE-bench Verified from 17.67% to 50.33% and AIME from 31.67% to 97.78%. Additional experiments examine DSH-harness refinement, budget variation, and restricted network access, while recorded trajectories reveal how agents diagnose failures and select candidates. We open-source the full RSIGym codebase and results to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [216] [Fault-tolerant foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10311)
*Trevor McCourt,Ila R. Fiete,Isaac L. Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过4万GPU小时实验发现，LLM训练后可容忍硬件不可靠性，且误差韧性随规模增大而增强，据此推测经适当训练的LLM可能具备形式化容错能力，为在低能耗故障硬件上运行推理提供节能路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴计算机硬件常在可靠性与能效之间做出权衡，即用可靠性换取能效。作者希望验证大语言模型能否被训练以容忍这种硬件不可靠性，从而在低能耗故障硬件上运行AI推理，实现大幅节能。

Method: 在模拟的故障数字硬件上进行训练实验，累计约4万GPU小时；基于实验结果推导修改后的神经缩放定律（neural scaling laws），用以量化模型误差韧性随规模变化的趋势。

Result: LLM经训练可容忍硬件不可靠性；随着模型规模增大，其误差韧性不降反升；修改后的神经缩放定律刻画了该趋势，并表明模型学会了在"好"的纠错码内进行计算，且该相对开销无论模型多大都保持有限。

Conclusion: 作者据此推测，经适当训练的LLM可能具备形式化容错（formally fault-tolerant）能力；若成立，在低能耗故障硬件上运行AI推理有望相较现状实现大幅节能。

Abstract: Emerging computer hardware often trades reliability for energy efficiency; here we show that large-language models (LLMs) can be trained to tolerate this unreliability, and that rather than degrading, their error resilience actually increases as they grow. Modified neural scaling laws inferred from 40,000 GPU-hours of training runs on simulated faulty digital hardware quantify this trend and suggest that models learn to compute within "good" error-correcting codes, whose relative overhead remains finite no matter how large the model gets. This finding leads us to conjecture that appropriately trained LLMs may be formally fault-tolerant; if true, running AI inference on low energy, faulty hardware may be a path to substantial energy savings over the status quo.

</details>


### [217] [Thinking in Depth: Retrospective Inference for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10317)
*Hao-Run Cai,Si-Yang Liu,Zi-Jian Cheng,Kun-Yang Yu,Jin-Hao Sheng,Guo Yu,Chonghan Liu,Zhi Zhou,Jun-Peng Jiang,Lan-Zhe Guo,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Retro，一种基于"回顾式推理"的表格基础模型，通过 Attention Residuals 自适应重加权不同深度的中间表示、以及 query-conditioned Gated Attention 按查询调制注意力输出，使预测精炼更早且更广泛地分布于各层，在 TabArena、TALENT、RelArena 上位列前三并处于 Pareto 前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFMs）多依靠堆叠 Transformer 层进行上下文预测，逐层变换样本表示与比较方式。作者追踪多个强 TFMs 中单个查询的预测过程，发现预测精炼在深度上高度不均衡，且往往集中在靠后的层。这种不均衡促使作者重新思考：网络中中间表示应当如何构建与复用。

Method: 提出 Retro，将推理过程围绕两个互补操作组织：(1) 应回顾哪些中间信息——由 Attention Residuals 对不同深度的贡献进行自适应重加权；(2) 对每个查询而言，上下文更新应如何塑形——由 query-conditioned Gated Attention 在表示维度上对注意力输出进行逐元素调制。整体采用回顾式推理（retrospective inference），使网络后期阶段可显式重访并重组早期产生的中间信息。

Result: 分析表明 Retro 将预测精炼提前并使精炼更广泛地分布于各深度，不同阶段修正不同查询子集，呈现多视角精炼的模式。在 TabArena、TALENT 和 RelArena 三个基准上，Retro 均进入前三名并位于 Pareto 前沿。

Conclusion: 结果表明，直接复用中间表示是一种更充分利用 TFM 深度的实用途径。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are pretrained across diverse tabular tasks and make predictions on a new table at inference time using its labeled examples as context. Most recent TFMs perform such in-context prediction with stacked Transformer layers, repeatedly transforming how examples are represented and compared. By tracing individual queries through several strong TFMs, we find that predictive refinement is highly uneven across depth and is often concentrated in later layers. This uneven refinement motivates us to reconsider how intermediate representations are constructed and reused throughout the network. We introduce Retro, a tabular foundation model based on retrospective inference, where later stages can explicitly revisit and recombine intermediate information produced earlier in the network. Retro organizes this process around two complementary operations: which intermediate information to revisit, and how the resulting contextual update should be shaped for each query. Attention Residuals address the former by adaptively reweighting contributions from different depths, while query-conditioned Gated Attention addresses the latter by modulating the attention output element-wise across representation dimensions. Our analysis shows that Retro shifts predictive refinement earlier and more broadly across depth, with different stages revising different subsets of queries in a pattern suggestive of multi-view refinement. Across TabArena, TALENT, and RelArena, Retro ranks among the top three and lies on the Pareto frontier. These results indicate that directly reusing intermediate representations provides a practical way to better exploit depth in TFMs.

</details>


### [218] [HAN-Mamba: Hierarchical Selective State Space Networks for Multi-Scale Financial Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10323)
*Mihai Bogdan Deaconu,Ioan Daniel Pop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出HAN-Mamba：将HAN-T中的二次注意力编码器替换为选择性状态空间（Mamba）编码器，仅在融合器中保留注意力，用于短周期已实现波动率预测。在Optiver基准上以更少参数取得更低RMSPE，并支持更长高频上下文与常数时间流式更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期已实现波动率预测需要融合不同时间分辨率的信息（秒级订单簿动态到周级状态漂移），而现有分层注意力架构（HAN-T）的二次注意力编码器在计算上难以扩展、参数较多，且在延长高频上下文时性能饱和。因此需要一种既能保持分层多尺度建模优势、又能实现线性时间与高效流式推理的序列建模方案。

Method: 在HAN-T层次架构基础上，将短、中、长三个尺度流中的Transformer二次注意力编码器替换为选择性状态空间（Mamba）编码器，仅在跨尺度融合器中保留注意力，融合器输入为三令牌集合而非长序列。每个流通过循环状态进行摘要，其输入依赖门控对应波动率的两个结构特性：持续但衰减的记忆与突发状态切换。采用时间感知五折交叉验证评估。

Result: 在Optiver Realized Volatility Prediction基准上，HAN-Mamba相对HAN-T将平均RMSPE由0.1965降至0.1942，同时参数量减少33%；利用线性时间编码器将高频上下文由60个桶扩展至240个桶后，误差进一步降至0.1927，而注意力变体在此处已饱和，并支持推理阶段的常数时间流式更新。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明性能提升主要源于编码器替换，验证了层次先验在不同序列模型家族间的可迁移性，并确认置换不变注意力融合器仍是跨尺度整合的正确机制。

Abstract: Short-horizon realized volatility forecasting requires the integration of market information that evolves at incompatible temporal resolutions, from second-level order book dynamics to weekly regime drift. Our conference work introduced HAN-T, a hierarchical architecture in which scale-specific Transformer encoders process short, mid, and long-horizon streams and a learned attention fuser weighs their contributions. This article replaces the quadratic attention encoders with selective state space (Mamba) encoders while retaining attention only in the fuser, where the input is a three-token set rather than a long sequence. The resulting hybrid, HAN-Mamba, summarizes each stream through a recurrent state whose input-dependent gating matches two structural properties of volatility: persistent but decaying memory and abrupt regime shifts. On the Optiver Realized Volatility Prediction benchmark under time-aware five-fold cross-validation, HAN-Mamba improves mean RMSPE over HAN-T (0.1942 vs. 0.1965) with 33% fewer parameters. Its linear-time encoders further allow the high-frequency context to be extended from 60 to 240 buckets, reducing error to 0.1927 where the attention variant saturates, and support constant-time streaming updates at inference. Ablations attribute the gains to the encoder swap, confirm that the hierarchical prior transfers across sequence-model families, and show that the permutation-invariant attention fuser remains the correct mechanism for cross-scale integration.

</details>


### [219] [AutoAdapt: Automatic Domain Discovery Enables Low-Cost Extensibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10349)
*Josh McGiff,Salma Mekaoui,Robert Shanahan,Nikola S. Nikolov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoAdapt：通过自动发现潜在领域、并行独立训练每域LoRA适配器并进行无参数路由，实现模块化领域特化，无需全模型重训练即可达到全领域训练适配器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调模型部署环境中领域异构且持续演化，新增领域或数据通常需要昂贵的重训练成本，亟需一种无需全模型重训练的模块化扩展方案。

Method: AutoAdapt模块化框架：自动发现潜在领域，据此为每个领域独立并行训练LoRA适配器，并通过无参数路由进行组合，训练过程不修改其他适配器，从而在构造上避免领域干扰。

Result: 在14个领域特定基准及GPT-4o成对评判上，AutoAdapt达到与在全部领域上训练的LoRA适配器相当的性能；同时发现不同独立发现方法间存在特化效应收敛的证据。

Conclusion: 按域独立训练适配器可在构造上防止领域干扰，实现模块化、无分类体系的领域特化，且不造成聚合性能损失，也无需全模型重训练。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned models are deployed into environments where domains are heterogeneous and evolve, yet adding new domains or data typically requires costly retraining. We present AutoAdapt, a modular framework that incorporates new domains and data via targeted single-adapter training without modifying other adapters. The framework automatically discovers latent domains, uses them to train per-domain Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters independently in parallel and performs parameter-free routing. Across 14 domain-specific benchmarks and GPT-4o pairwise judgements, AutoAdapt achieves parity with a LoRA adapter trained on all domains without requiring full-model retraining. We also find evidence of specialisation effect convergence across independent discovery methods. Overall, training each adapter on its own domain prevents domain interference by construction, thus enabling modular, taxonomy-free domain specialisation without aggregate performance loss or full model retraining.

</details>


### [220] [ORDERS: An Empirical Study of Norm-Rank Aggregation for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10361)
*Koffka Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究个性化联邦学习配置ORDERS：共享骨干+私有残差适配器与分类器、按更新范数降序的几何权重、特征对齐、私有参数扰动。80次最终运行显示ORDERS在CIFAR-10（每客户端两类）达80.51±0.79%，仅比匹配的均匀权重对照（80.27±0.73%）高0.24个百分点，比FedPer-R1高1.49个百分点；公共本地微调后差距缩小至0.32个百分点。Sent140上74.71±0.49%，仅比事后客户端训练多数类诊断高0.69个百分点。消融证据有限且依赖端点，扰动无明确收益，参数载荷节省仅5.47%与0.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦学习常将共享表示与客户端专属预测器结合，但服务器加权规则的贡献容易被本地训练与评估选择所掩盖。作者希望厘清ORDERS这一组合配置的真实增益，特别是服务器几何加权规则是否带来实质上优于均匀加权与后处理诊断的效果。

Method: 研究ORDERS配置：共享骨干网络，客户端私有残差适配器与分类器；服务器按更新范数降序分配几何权重，对同一广播模型得到的更新做加权求和（不含顺序累加的额外优化效应）；辅以特征对齐与私有参数扰动。评估含80次最终运行（8种配置×2数据集×5个训练种子），每个数据集使用固定划分；并设置匹配的均匀权重对照、FedPer-R1基线、公共本地微调以及事后客户端训练多数类诊断。

Result: CIFAR-10（每客户端两类）上ORDERS原生平均客户端准确率80.51±0.79%，FedPer-R1为79.02±1.42%，匹配均匀权重对照为80.27±0.73%；公共本地微调后与FedPer-R1差距缩小至0.32个百分点。Sent140上ORDERS为74.71±0.49%，仅比事后客户端训练多数类诊断高0.69个百分点。消融对范数排序与特征对齐仅提供有限且依赖端点的证据，私有参数扰动无明确收益；参数载荷节省分别为5.47%和0.78%。

Conclusion: 服务器加权规则的贡献在很大程度上被本地训练与评估选择所掩盖：相对匹配均匀权重对照的增益很小，经过公共本地微调后与FedPer-R1的差距进一步收窄，Sent140上仅略高于事后多数类诊断。因此ORDERS中几何加权、对齐与扰动的独立价值证据薄弱，参数载荷节省也有限，其优势不宜被高估。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning combines shared representations with client-specific predictors, but the contribution of a server weighting rule can be obscured by local training and evaluation choices. We study ORDERS, a configuration that combines a shared backbone, a private residual adapter and classifier, geometric weights assigned by descending update norm, feature alignment, and private-parameter perturbations. The server computes a weighted sum of updates obtained from the same broadcast model; it does not obtain an additional optimization effect from sequential addition. A fully specified evaluation comprises 80 final runs: eight configurations, two datasets, and five training seeds on one fixed partition per dataset. On two-class-per-client CIFAR-10, ORDERS achieves $80.51 \pm 0.79\%$ native mean client accuracy, compared with $79.02 \pm 1.42\%$ for FedPer-R1 and $80.27 \pm 0.73\%$ for the matched uniform-weight control. After common local fine-tuning, the difference from FedPer-R1 narrows to 0.32 percentage points. On Sent140, ORDERS reaches $74.71 \pm 0.49\%$, only 0.69 points above a post hoc client training-majority diagnostic. Ablations provide limited, endpoint-dependent evidence for norm ranking and alignment, and no clear benefit from perturbations. Parameter-payload savings are 5.47% and 0.78%, respectively.

</details>


### [221] [Temporally Interpretable Differentiable Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10367)
*Eisuke Hirota,Aarav Sane,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"时间可解释性"新维度，通过动作分块与信息论树重构算法改进可微决策树，在四个仿真环境中实现高效且时间可解释的策略树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性有助于实现安全自主。可微决策树（DDT）能兼顾自动可微策略与离散树可视化，但现有DDT不适合序列决策任务：树的单时间步行为与人类多时间步规划之间存在固有错配。

Method: 1) 将"时间"作为可解释性的新维度，提出时间可解释性概念，并通过动作分块（action chunking）实现时间抽象；2) 提出两种融合动作分块的新型策略梯度算法；3) 为保持参数高效的树结构，开发基于信息论的树重构算法，在训练过程中修改树；4) 采用蒸馏的动作分块策略对DDT进行热启动。

Result: 在四个仿真环境中，从蒸馏的动作分块策略热启动的动作分块DDT效果最佳：在四个域中的三个域达到与神经网络策略相当的性能，同时最多减少80%的参数。

Conclusion: 时间可解释性为序列决策提供了新的可解释性视角，动作分块与信息论树重构相结合能获得参数高效且时间可解释的决策树，代码已开源。

Abstract: Interpretability offers a solution to safe autonomy by providing transparency into an agent's underlying decision-making model. Within sequential-decision making tasks, differentiable decision trees (DDTs) are one approach to such interpretability, maintaining automatic-differentiable policies while providing humans with a discrete tree-based visualization. Nonetheless, current implementations of DDTs are not well-suited for sequential-decision making domains, as there exists an inherent mismatch between a tree's single-timestep behavior and a human's multi-timestep planning. Our work thus introduces time as a new dimension of interpretability, coined as temporal interpretability, and demonstrates how temporal abstractions via action chunking improve it. We achieve this by first introducing two novel policy gradient algorithms that incorporate action chunking. Additionally, to maintain parameter-efficient trees, we develop an information-theoretic tree restructuring algorithm that modifies the tree during training. Across four simulation environments, we find that warm-starting action chunked DDTs from a distilled action chunked policy is the most effective way to obtain temporally interpretable trees: they match neural network policies in three of the four domains while using up to 80$\%$ fewer parameters. Our code is available at https://github.com/ei5uke/temp-interp.

</details>


### [222] [Input-Blind Controls Produce Substantial Oracle Headroom for Layer Programs in Multiple-Choice Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10368)
*Yibei Guo,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过对照实验检验自适应计算增益的来源：在共享选项顺序下，输入无关扰动产生的"headroom"（10.2-19.4个百分点）超过真实层跳过/重复程序（9.0-10.1），表明部分增益并非源自被选层计算的特异性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应计算用于按输入定制语言模型推理执行；oracle评估用已知答案估计选择灵活性带来的潜在增益。但"选择带来的增益"本身无法解释被选程序为何有效——即增益是否来自所选层计算的特定贡献，还是来自输入无关的混淆因素。

Method: 在2个模型、4,413道多选题上，比较32个层跳过/重复程序与同位置输入盲扰动。设置：与无评估提示下选定的固定动作对比；用另一提示重评选择；采用共享选项顺序、随机方向抽样、KL校准对照（含输入依赖对照）、固定字母偏移；并做轮换选项、生成答案的重述测试及安慰剂缺失说明。

Result: 共享选项顺序下，对照的headroom为10.2-11.8（Qwen3-4B-Base）和15.6-19.4（Llama-3.1-8B）个百分点，均超过真实程序的9.0和10.1（各模型三次随机方向抽样中一致）。仅答案变化率匹配，排序依赖菜单：Llama仅重复菜单下真实程序在每次抽样中领先。KL校准对照点估计偏向真实程序，校正检验不确定。固定字母偏移产生类似规模headroom；轮换选项使两家族headroom骤降，真实减对照差为1.4-2.3与3.7-4.5点，显著性依调整与参照而定。重述后搜索选择程序比他人问题选择程序保持26.0点优势，但无安慰剂对照。

Conclusion: 大量headroom可在共享选项顺序下跨提示持续存在，却无法确立被选层计算的特异性收益；无论对照排序还是KL校准对照，均不足以识别该收益。选择增益的解释需与输入无关混淆因素严格区分。

Abstract: Adaptive computation aims to improve language-model inference by tailoring execution to each input. For layer programs, oracle evaluations use known answers to estimate the potential gain from this flexibility, before a practical selector is available. However, a gain from selection does not by itself explain why the chosen programs help. This study examines this distinction using 32 layer-skipping and repetition programs on two models and 4,413 multiple-choice items. The analysis compares their gains over a fixed action selected without the evaluation prompt with those of input-blind perturbations at the same sites, re-evaluating selections on another prompt. With shared option order, the controls give 10.2-11.8 and 15.6-19.4 percentage points of headroom on Qwen3-4B-Base and Llama-3.1-8B, exceeding the real programs' 9.0 and 10.1 in all three random-direction draws per model. They match answer-change rate only, and the ordering depends on the menu: in post hoc comparisons, real programs lead on Llama's repeat-only menu in every draw. A smaller KL-calibrated comparison, including an input-dependent control, favours real programs in point estimate, with inconclusive corrected tests. Fixed letter offsets produce headroom of similar scale. Rotating options sharply reduces both families' headroom, while leaving positive real-minus-control differences of 1.4-2.3 and 3.7-4.5 points; their magnitudes and statistical support depend on further adjustments and the reference. A supplementary generated-answer test finds that search-selected programs keep a 26.0-point advantage over programs selected for other problems after rewording, without a placebo comparison. These results show that substantial headroom can persist across prompts with shared option order without establishing a benefit specific to the selected layer computation; neither ordering against these controls identifies that benefit.

</details>


### [223] [Continual Learning without Continual Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10379)
*Nikita Narayanan,Ritham Majumdarr,Sonali Parbhoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent Concept PFN，用"持续推理"替代"持续训练"：模型元训练后冻结，仅通过扩展上下文证据集来适应新类别/新领域，所有适应都体现为潜在概念空间上的后验更新而非梯度更新，从而减少遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法大多依赖持续优化，通过正则化、回放或参数扩展来防止新更新覆盖旧知识，但这些方法本质上仍需改变参数，容易造成遗忘，且通常需要任务标识、受固定词表概念限制。作者希望找到一种"参数不变"的持续适应方式。

Method: 提出 PFN（先验拟合网络）架构的 Latent Concept PFN：元训练后将模型冻结；在捕捉跨领域、跨类别语义结构的潜在概念空间上做上下文贝叶斯推理。每当新领域或新类别到来，只需将示例加入记忆，适应表现为潜在概念后验信念的更新，而非梯度更新，参数不发生变化。概念标注仅在元训练阶段使用，作为潜在空间的软锚点而非固定瓶颈；推理时结合概念标签与原始输入证据，因此可处理噪声、模糊或不完整的标注，并发现预定义概念集之外的区分。

Result: 在类别增量与领域增量学习数据集上取得有竞争力的持续学习性能，同时学到可解释的潜在概念。

Conclusion: 持续推理可替代持续训练：冻结的 PFN 模型通过扩展上下文证据即可适应新领域和新类别，同一方法无需任务标识即可同时处理领域增量与类别增量学习，减少遗忘并保持可解释性。

Abstract: Continual learning requires models to adapt to new domains and new classes while retaining prior knowledge. Many existing methods rely on continued optimization, using regularization, replay, or parameter expansion to prevent new updates from overwriting previously learned knowledge. Instead, we propose replacing continual training with continual inference: a PFN-based model that is meta-trained, and then frozen, adapting to new classes only by extending an in-context evidence set. Our model, Latent Concept PFN, performs in-context Bayesian inference over a latent concept space that captures semantic structure shared across domains and classes. As each new domain or class arrives, exemplars are added to the memory; adaptation reflects updated posterior beliefs over latent concepts rather than gradient updates. No parameters are changed, reducing forgetting. The same method handles both domain and class incremental continual learning without task identity. Concept annotations are only used during meta-training, acting as a soft anchor on the latent space rather than a fixed bottleneck. Unlike fixed-vocabulary concept methods, the model also handles noisy, ambiguous, or incomplete annotations by combining concept labels with raw input evidence to discover distinctions beyond the predefined concept set. Experiments on class and domain incremental learning datasets demonstrate competitive continual learning performance while learning interpretable latent concepts.

</details>


### [224] [ResidualQuant: KV Cache Quantization for Looped Transformers with 2-Bit Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10381)
*Heejun Kim,Junyoung Lee,SangLyul Cho,Dongsu Han,Insu Han,Sehoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ResidualQuant，利用循环 Transformer 各循环 KV 状态高度相似的特性，以最终循环 KV 为参考、其余循环用低精度残差表示，实现 INT2 级别 KV 缓存量化，显著改善精度-内存权衡并提升推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环 Transformer 通过复用共享块多次循环来提升参数效率、增加计算深度而不增加参数量，但其 KV 缓存内存仍随循环数线性增长，成为限制批大小与推理吞吐的关键瓶颈。现有 KV 缓存量化方法在激进低精度下往往精度大幅下降，因此需要一种更适合循环结构的高效量化方案。

Method: 基于“循环间 KV 状态高度相似”的观察，提出 ResidualQuant：以最终循环的 KV 状态为参考，仅用低精度残差表示其余循环的 KV；并进一步结合对残差施加最小二乘缩放与旋转，以及按循环的混合精度策略，从而在保持高效重建的同时支持低至 INT2 的精确量化。

Result: 在多个循环 Transformer 模型及数学推理、代码生成基准上，ResidualQuant 相较最先进的基于旋转的 KV 量化方法持续改善精度-内存权衡；混合精度设置下精度接近 BF16，理论 KV 存储减少 80.7%，相同内存预算下精度最高提升 13.0%。在 RTX 5090 上，KV 内存流量降低使固定批解码吞吐最高提升 2.73 倍，更小内存占用支持最多 2 倍更大批，峰值吞吐最高提升 4.15 倍。

Conclusion: 利用循环 Transformer 循环间 KV 相似性进行残差量化，可在极低精度下保持精度并大幅压缩 KV 缓存，同时带来显著的解码吞吐提升，为循环架构的高效推理提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Looped Transformers improve parameter efficiency by repeatedly applying shared Transformer blocks over multiple recurrent loops, increasing computational depth without increasing the parameter count. However, KV cache memory still scales with the number of loops, becoming a key memory bottleneck that limits batch size and inference throughput. KV cache quantization can alleviate this bottleneck, but existing methods often suffer substantial accuracy degradation at aggressive low-precision regimes. We observe that looped Transformers offer a unique opportunity: KV states across loops are highly similar. Based on this observation, we propose ResidualQuant, which uses the final-loop KV states as a reference and represents the remaining loops with low-precision residuals. Our method further combines least-square scaling and rotations applied to the residuals, as well as loop-wise mixed precision, to enable accurate quantization down to INT2 while retaining efficient reconstruction. Across multiple looped Transformer models and mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, ResidualQuant consistently improves the accuracy-memory tradeoff over state-of-the-art rotation-based KV quantization. In particular, our method retains accuracy close to BF16 under mixed-precision settings while reducing theoretical KV storage by 80.7%, achieving up to 13.0% higher accuracy than the rotation-based baseline at the same memory budget. On an RTX 5090, the reduced KV memory traffic improves fixed-batch decode throughput by up to 2.73x, while the smaller memory footprint enables up to 2x larger batches, improving peak throughput by up to 4.15x.

</details>


### [225] [Boosting and the Expressive Power of Simple Weak Learners via the $γ$-VC Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10383)
*Arthur da Cunha,Kasper Green Larsen,Liang-Yu Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过γ-VC维刻画Boosting中弱到强学习的样本复杂度，并改进VC维与γ-VC维的关系及基本概念类的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boosting能将仅略优于随机猜测的弱假设组合为高精度预测器，但其表达能力强烈依赖基类的结构，因此需要一个合适的复杂度度量来刻画这一现象。

Method: 采用Alon等（STOC 2021）提出的γ-VC维作为分析工具，研究其在弱到强学习样本复杂度中的作用，并分析经典VC维与γ-VC维的一般关系，同时对决策树桩和ℝ^d中轴平行矩形等基本概念类给出上下界。

Result: 证明了γ-VC维在常数因子（关于γ缩放）意义下刻画弱到强学习的样本复杂度；锐化了经典VC维与γ-VC维之间的一般关系；并给出了决策树桩和ℝ^d中轴平行矩形这两类基本概念类在γ-VC维上的改进上界与下界。

Conclusion: γ-VC维是刻画Boosting表达能力与样本复杂度的有效参数，本文深化了其与经典VC维的联系，并为重要概念类提供了更紧的界。

Abstract: Boosting converts weak hypotheses with a small edge over random guessing into highly accurate predictors, but the expressive power of the resulting classifier can depend strongly on the structure of the base class. We study this phenomenon through the $γ$-VC dimension introduced by Alon et al. (STOC 2021). Our first result shows that this parameter characterizes the sample complexity for weak-to-strong learning up to a constant factor scaling in $γ$. We then sharpen the general relationship between the classic VC dimension and the $γ$-VC dimension. Finally, we also give improved upper and lower bounds on the $γ$-VC dimension for the fundamental concept classes of decision stumps and axis-parallel rectangles in $\mathbb{R}^d$.

</details>


### [226] [OrBIT: Structure-Guided Embedding Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10385)
*Yunied Puig,Amit Kumar Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OrBIT，一种结构引导的嵌入表压缩框架，通过从轨道动力学中学习可复用的局部几何来约束共享码字，实现高压缩率与竞争性的率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入表压缩方法大多固定编码几何（如坐标块、低秩子空间或无限制码本）并在其内优化，本文则追问编码几何本身能否被发现，以突破固定几何带来的压缩上限。

Method: OrBIT从轨道动力学学习可复用的局部几何，用它约束一小组共享码字；全局重构残差决定固定编码预算的分配位置；冗余重叠图卡使局部误差在拼接后相互补偿。理论上分析了紧图卡几何对失真的控制、全局残差对顺序分配的指导，以及数据几何引导的细化对编解码器的改进。轨道机制最终被编译去除，仅保留紧凑解码器。

Result: 在四个LLM嵌入表上，OrBIT相对16位存储对GPT-2达到37.9倍压缩，对各7B表均超过23倍，并在率失真性能上与主流量化及低秩基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 学习得到的结构能够决定存储内容、容量分配位置以及局部信息如何全局组装，表明可变编码几何的发现是嵌入表高效压缩的有效途径。

Abstract: Embedding tables are among the largest components of modern language models. Most compression methods fix a coding geometry such as coordinate blocks, low-rank subspaces, or unrestricted codebooks, and optimize within it. We instead ask whether the coding geometry can itself be discovered. We introduce \emph{OrBIT}, a structure-guided embedding compression framework that learns reusable local geometry from orbit dynamics and uses it to constrain a small set of shared codewords. The global reconstruction residual then decides where the fixed coding budget is spent, while redundant overlapping charts let local errors compensate one another after gluing. Our theory shows how tight-chart geometry controls distortion, how the global residual directs sequential allocation, and how data-geometry-guided refinement improves the codec. The resulting orbit machinery is compiled away, leaving a compact decoder in which the learned structure governs what is stored, where capacity is allocated, and how local information is assembled globally. Across four LLM embedding tables, OrBIT achieves $37.9\times$ compression on GPT-2 and over $23\times$ on each 7B table relative to 16-bit storage, while delivering competitive rate-distortion performance against established quantization and low-rank baselines.

</details>


### [227] [Kernel Autoresearch for Open-Ended Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10394)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Feng Yin,Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Kernaut（Kernel Autoresearch），将核函数设计视为开放式模型发现，由编码智能体编写核函数程序，并通过「构造契约」保证有效性，结合质量-多样性档案与新颖性筛选，发现可泛化、可解释的核函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动核函数设计存在根本性两难：固定语法（基础核+算子）能保证有效性但限制搜索空间；无限制程序搜索虽扩展了空间却无法保证有效性。压力测试显示，22-58% 通过随机输入数值校验的 LLM 生成核函数，在改变尺度或维度后会失效。

Method: 提出 Kernaut，把核函数设计当作开放式模型发现：(1) 编码智能体将核函数写成程序；(2) 构造契约（construction contracts）确保每个被接受的核函数都是有效的；(3) 质量-多样性档案保留表现优异且行为各异的核函数；(4) 新颖性筛选引导智能体探索功能上全新的候选。

Result: 在留出的黑盒优化任务族上，发现的核函数优于在相同 episode 上元学习的深度核；由十个酶动力学速率定律发现的核函数在五个未见机制上误差低于调优的 ARD 与深度核基线。核函数是可解释程序，可被人类研究者改进：某核函数的人类改进版进一步将留出预测误差降低 5.7%、优化 regret 降低 7.8%。

Conclusion: Kernaut 通过「程序化生成+构造契约+质量多样性+新颖性筛选」的组合，突破了有效性-表达力的两难，发现的核函数编码了可复用、可泛化的归纳偏置，兼具性能与可解释性，且支持人机协同改进。

Abstract: Kernels encode the inductive bias of a wide range of machine learning models, yet automated kernel design faces a fundamental dilemma. A fixed grammar of base kernels and operators guarantees validity but limits the search to structures expressible by those building blocks. Conversely, unrestricted programs remove this limitation but no longer guarantee validity. In our stress tests, 22-58% of LLM-generated kernels that pass numerical checks on random inputs fail when evaluated at different scales or dimensions. We propose Kernel Autoresearch (Kernaut), which treats kernel design as open-ended model discovery. Coding agents write kernels as programs, while construction contracts ensure that every accepted kernel is valid. A quality-diversity archive retains high-performing kernels with distinct behaviors, and novelty screening steers agents toward functionally new candidates. Our experiments demonstrate that the discovered kernels encode reusable inductive biases that generalize to unseen tasks. On held-out black-box optimization families, a discovered kernel outperforms a meta-learned deep kernel trained on the same episodes. Furthermore, kernels discovered from ten enzyme-kinetic rate laws achieve lower error than tuned ARD and deep kernel baselines on five unseen mechanisms. The discovered kernels are also interpretable programs that human researchers can refine: a human-refined version of one further reduces the held-out predictive error by 5.7% and optimization regret by 7.8%.

</details>


### [228] [Executing Causal Structure Learning with Linear-Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10395)
*Amartya Roy,Sayar Karmakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究Transformer能否精确执行因果发现算法。作者为一种标准连续优化方法（迭代更新候选因果图并施加无环约束）显式构造了固定权重的Transformer，使前向传播精确复现单步更新，堆叠后可复现完整优化轨迹。实验表明该构造与参考更新在浮点精度上一致，并继承了参考求解器的成功与失败，从而区分了"精确执行算法"与"准确恢复因果结构"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明Transformer可以执行输入数据上的算法，但能否将其用于因果发现尚不清楚。作者希望检验Transformer能否精确执行因果发现中的迭代优化算法，并厘清"算法执行正确性"与"因果结构恢复正确性"之间的区别。

Method: 针对一种标准连续因果发现方法（反复更新候选因果图并强制无环性），显式构造一个固定权重的Transformer，使其单次前向传播精确对应该方法的一次更新，重复堆叠即可复现优化轨迹。Transformer在更新之间携带当前图与算法的乘子（multiplier）。作者证明保留乘子对精确执行是必要的，因为不同乘子值会导致不同的下一步更新；并给出在固定阶段内可预先计算达到目标精度所需更新次数、且随深度增长舍入误差有界的条件。

Result: 构造的Transformer块与参考更新在浮点精度上一致；在合成数据与七个已发表基准网络拓扑上进行算术重放时，继承了参考求解器的成功与失败。相比之下，在相同训练预算下测试的普通注意力模型无法可靠执行该更新，也难以迁移到更大的图。

Conclusion: 该工作将准确的算法执行与准确的因果恢复区分开来。梯度训练能否在该构造所属的架构类中学得这样的执行器，仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Transformers can execute algorithms on data given in their input. We ask whether they can do the same for causal discovery. We study a standard continuous method that repeatedly updates a candidate causal graph while enforcing acyclicity. We explicitly construct a fixed-weight transformer whose forward pass exactly reproduces one update of this method, so repeated blocks reproduce its optimization trajectory. The transformer carries the current graph and the algorithm's multiplier between updates. We show that retaining the multiplier is essential for exact execution, since different multiplier values can lead to different next updates. We also give conditions under which, within a fixed stage, the number of updates needed to reach a target accuracy can be computed in advance and rounding errors stay bounded as depth grows. Experiments show that the constructed block agrees with a reference update to floating-point precision, while arithmetic replay on synthetic data and seven published benchmark network topologies inherits the reference solver's successes and failures. This separates accurate algorithm execution from accurate causal recovery. In contrast, the ordinary attention models tested under our training budgets do not reliably execute the update or transfer to larger graphs. Whether gradient training can learn an executor in the architecture class of the construction remains open.

</details>


### [229] [Cross-Domain Pretraining for Steady-State Neural CFD Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10398)
*Anthony Zhou,Amir Barati Farimani,Shirley Ho,Rudy Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 跨域预训练可显著提升CFD神经代理模型的泛化能力，微调后误差降低2-3倍、样本需求减少8倍，且该增益与模型架构无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD神经代理模型能加速仿真、促进工程创新，但其核心瓶颈在于对训练集之外几何形状和应用场景的泛化能力不足，而工程场景又高度多样。现有解决方案是为特定应用重新生成数据集，但需运行昂贵的数值求解器，成本高昂。

Method: 研究跨几何、跨边界条件和跨保真度训练神经代理模型。通过跨域预训练，并与两种基线对比：从零开始训练、以及从领域特定专家模型迁移。同时探究跨域预训练在CFD代理中有效的原因，发现简单地合并稳态数据集即充分且有效。

Result: 跨域预训练提升了在留出数据集上的零样本和少样本性能，优于从零训练和领域专家迁移。微调预训练的跨域模型在相同样本量下误差降低2-3倍，达到相同误差所需样本减少8倍。该收益与架构无关，并随模型规模和预训练数据多样性提升而增强。

Conclusion: 鉴于CFD数据生成成本高昂，通过跨域预训练利用现有数据集，将是未来代理模型拓展到新问题和新用例时极具价值的策略。简单合并稳态数据集即可实现有效的跨域预训练。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for computational fluid dynamics (CFD) have the potential to greatly enhance engineering innovation through accelerating simulation. However, the primary limitation for neural surrogates is the lack of generalization to geometries and applications beyond the training set, which is significant given the diversity of engineering scenarios. Currently, this is addressed by generating a new dataset for a specific application; however, this requires running costly numerical solvers. In this work, we take a step toward addressing this by studying neural surrogates trained across different geometries, boundary conditions, and fidelities. We find that cross-domain pretraining improves zero- and few-shot performance on held-out datasets relative to both training from scratch and transferring from domain-specific experts. In particular, finetuning a pretrained, cross-domain model can achieve 2-3x lower errors at the same sample size and use 8x fewer samples to achieve the same error, compared to training from scratch. This benefit is architecture agnostic and improves with model size and pretraining dataset diversity. Furthermore, we study how and why cross-domain pretraining works in CFD surrogates, and find that simply pooling steady-state datasets is both sufficient and effective. Given the high cost of generating CFD data, leveraging existing datasets through cross-domain pretraining will likely be a valuable strategy as future surrogates expand to tackle new problems and use cases.

</details>


### [230] [Which Rollout Taught It That? BehaviorTrace and the Limits of Training-Data Attribution in Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10422)
*Amit Nautiyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在线RL微调(GRPO)中"归因"问题：能否找出教会模型新行为的训练rollout，以及如何验证归因方法的真实性。作者发布BehaviorTrace评估工具，发现多数归因信号源于混淆因素，仅触发词梯度对齐是稳健信号，并给出评估清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习使语言模型习得新行为后，能否追溯到导致该行为的训练rollout是一个重要的可解释性/归因问题；同时现有归因方法号称能做到，但缺乏可靠的真值验证手段，需要区分真实归因信号与混淆因素。

Method: 在GRPO在线RL微调上采用"植入行为"(planted behavior，已知成因)设置，发布BehaviorTrace评估框架，整合全梯度sketching，并对梯度幅度、流畅度、提升空间(headroom)、不同随机种子与生成抽样进行控制实验。测试现有估计器如GAS(重归一化TracInCP)与TRAK式估计器，未提出新方法。在Qwen2.5-1.5B上跑三个种子。

Result: 多数表观归因信号来自混淆因素；仅按梯度大小排序训练步(无行为目标)的对照达到随机水平的4.2–4.5倍，并在三个种子中的两个上匹敌或超过最佳目标估计器；在饱和checkpoint处，模型流畅度预测行为标签的能力不逊于所有梯度方法；控制流畅度后，per-rollout结果随种子和生成抽样而变，单次运行无法定论；唯一在三个种子上都成立的信号是触发词梯度与行为实际发生处构建的目标对齐。

Conclusion: RL归因评估必须控制梯度幅度、流畅度等混淆因素并考虑种子与抽样的方差，单次运行不足以得出结论；在现有设置下仅触发词梯度对齐稳健。作者据此提出RL归因评估清单，并测试(而非提出)现有估计器。

Abstract: When reinforcement learning teaches a language model a new behavior, can we find the training rollouts that taught it? And when an attribution method says it can, how do we know the answer is real? We study both questions on online RL fine-tuning with GRPO, using a planted behavior with a known cause. We release BehaviorTrace, an open evaluation harness that combines full-gradient sketching, the planted-behavior setup, and controls for gradient magnitude, fluency, headroom, and variation across seeds and generation draws. Across three seeds on Qwen2.5-1.5B, much of the apparent attribution signal comes from confounds. A control that ranks training steps by gradient size alone, with no behavior target, reaches 4.2 to 4.5 times chance and matches or beats the best targeted estimator on two of three seeds. At saturated checkpoints, model fluency predicts the behavior label at least as well as every gradient method we compared it with. Once fluency is controlled, the per-rollout results change from seed to seed and from one generation draw to the next, so a single run cannot settle the question. One signal does hold on all three seeds. The gradient of the trigger tokens aligns with a target built where the behavior actually occurs. We turn these findings into a checklist for evaluating attribution in RL. We test existing estimators, including GAS (renormalized TracInCP) and a TRAK-style estimator, and do not propose a new one.

</details>


### [231] [Q-Learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10437)
*Yonghoon Dong,Minsung Yoon,Jaehyuk Kim,Jungwoo Park,Changyeon Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SQAM 方法，通过标量伴随匹配避免流策略微调中的逐步向量-雅可比积，并引入价值惩罚，在最难的 OGBench 域及真实双臂机器人任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略（flow policies）能刻画丰富多样的动作分布，用离策略强化学习（off-policy RL）对其微调以超越示范具有吸引力。但流策略通过多步流生成动作，直接用学习到的价值函数微调并不容易。伴随匹配（adjoint matching）虽可将最终动作的价值信息回传到各流步，但需要在每一步计算向量-雅可比积（vector-Jacobian product），开销随流步数和策略规模增长。

Method: 作者观察到预训练流策略的批次平均速度雅可比集中于对角线。由此推导出闭式标量伴随（scalar adjoint），用流时间缩放最终动作处的价值梯度，消除每步的向量-雅可比积。同时发现，在标量伴随下，控制策略生成动作处的 critic 价值尤为重要。据此提出 Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching（SQAM），将标量伴随与这些动作上的价值惩罚相结合。

Result: SQAM 的提升集中在四个最难的 OGBench 域，其成功率在每个域均超过最强基线 18 至 35 个百分点。为验证其可扩展至大规模预训练策略，作者还在真实双臂机器人上微调视觉-语言-动作（vision-language-action）策略，SQAM 在全部三个任务上均优于监督微调（supervised fine-tuning）。

Conclusion: SQAM 通过标量伴随匹配显著降低了流策略离策略 RL 微调的计算成本，并在困难基准与真实机器人任务上取得一致改进，验证了其有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Flow policies capture rich and diverse action distributions, and fine-tuning them with off-policy RL to improve beyond the demonstrations has drawn growing interest. However, fine-tuning a flow policy against a learned value function is not trivial, because the policy generates its action over many flow steps. Adjoint matching offers a principled way to update the flow model itself by propagating value information from the final action back to each flow step, but it requires a vector--Jacobian product through the policy at every step, a cost that grows with the number of flow steps and the policy size. We observe that the batch-averaged velocity Jacobian of pretrained flow policies concentrates on its diagonal. Motivated by this finding, we derive a closed-form scalar adjoint that scales the value gradient at the final action by the flow time, eliminating the per-step vector--Jacobian products. We further find that controlling the critic's value at policy-generated actions is particularly important under the scalar adjoint. Based on these findings, we propose Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching (SQAM), which combines the scalar adjoint with a value penalty at those actions. SQAM's gains concentrate on the four hardest OGBench domains, where its success rate exceeds that of the strongest baseline in each domain by 18 to 35 percentage points. To test whether SQAM extends to large pretrained policies, we also fine-tune a vision-language-action policy on a real bimanual robot. SQAM improves over supervised fine-tuning on all three tasks.

</details>


### [232] [Seq-Flow: Efficient Probabilistic Forecasting with Self-Rollout Error Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10440)
*Yinan Huang,Shitij Govil,Bo Dai,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Seq-Flow 是一种条件流模型,通过 ODE 将上一时刻的预测分布输运到更新后的分布,并结合 self-rollout 训练,在少量采样步数下实现高效、可递归的科学预测更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学预测任务需要在观测陆续到来时不断更新未来轨迹的分布。传统扩散与流模型每次都从高斯噪声生成预测,采样步数多、成本高;热启动方法虽复用先前预测以降低成本,但其模型并未针对"预测更新"本身进行训练,在少步采样下质量下降。此外递归复用会带来误差累积问题,即一次预测的误差会成为后续流的初始状态误差。

Method: 提出 Seq-Flow:一个条件流模型,其 ODE 将样本从上一时刻的预测分布输运到更新后的分布,由于相邻预测差异通常较小,该输运从有信息量的分布出发,可用很少的流评估步数完成准确更新。针对递归误差累积,采用 self-rollout 训练:用模型的滑动平均副本生成预测,用于初始化后续训练更新的起点。与复用生成输出作为条件上下文的自强迫方法不同,Seq-Flow 将生成输出复用为下一段流的源分布。

Result: 在粒子加速器束流溢出预测任务上,Seq-Flow 在少量 NFE 采样预算下将 CRPS 降低 65%,同时在流体动力学预测任务上与强基线相比仍具竞争力。虽然训练时最多只用四次更新的自rollout,Seq-Flow 在超过 400 次连续更新中仍保持准确。

Conclusion: Seq-Flow 通过条件流模型的分布输运与 self-rollout 训练,解决了科学预测中少步采样与递归更新误差累积的问题,兼顾高效性与长期稳定性。代码已开源于 https://github.com/Graph-COM/Seq-Flow。

Abstract: Many scientific forecasting tasks require updating a distribution over future trajectories as new observations arrive. Conventional diffusion and flow models generate each forecast from Gaussian noise, often at the cost of many sampling steps. Warm-start methods reuse earlier predictions to reduce this cost, but their models are not trained to perform the forecast update itself, which can compromise quality under few-step sampling. In this work, we introduce Seq-Flow, a conditional flow model whose ODE transports samples from the previous forecast distribution to the updated one. Because successive forecasts often differ only modestly, this transport starts from an informative distribution and can produce accurate updates with few flow evaluations. Recursive reuse also creates a challenge: errors in one forecast become errors in the initial states of subsequent flows. We address this with self-rollout training, in which a moving average copy of the model generates forecasts that initialize later training updates. Unlike self-forcing methods, which reuse generated outputs as conditioning context, Seq-Flow reuses them as the source of the next flow. Experiments On particle-accelerator beam spill forecasting show Seq-Flow reduces CRPS by 65% under a few-NFE sampling budget, while remaining competitive with strong baselines on fluid-dynamics forecasting tasks. Although trained on self-rollouts of at most four updates, Seq-Flow remains accurate over more than 400 consecutive updates. Our code is available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/Seq-Flow.

</details>


### [233] [A Good Self-Teacher Meets the Student Where They Are: Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10447)
