*Randy Ardywibowo,Arnav Dalal,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JOLT方法，通过分析特权教师蒸馏更新与学生奖励梯度的关系，推导出教师应匹配学生能力的条件，并用KL正则化的联合训练策略提升自蒸馏效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL从结果奖励学习面临监督稀疏问题，尤其在长时程难任务上成功轨迹稀少且昂贵；On-Policy Distillation提供密集token级监督，但自蒸馏中特权信息可能使教师走捷径，导致监督与学生当前行为不匹配，即使教师表现更好也可能损害学生性能。

Method: 分析特权教师对学生更新的影响，推导教师局部蒸馏更新为学生奖励梯度正倍数的充要条件；据此设计结合结果奖励与对学生token级KL正则化的教师训练替代目标；提出JOLT，联合训练单一策略的两种角色：用KL正则目标训练的教师和用密集on-policy蒸馏训练的学生。

Result: 在数学推理、编程、工具使用和终端使用任务上，JOLT提升了训练效率与性能，且加入学生奖励后进一步提升。

Conclusion: 教师不仅要在任务上表现好，还需提供适合学生当前能力的指导；JOLT通过KL正则化的特权教师与on-policy蒸馏学生的联合训练，有效改善自蒸馏效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) from outcome rewards suffers from sparse supervision, particularly on difficult, long-horizon tasks where successful trajectories are rare and costly to generate. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers an attractive alternative by providing dense token-level supervision from a stronger teacher along the student's own generations. Self-distillation methods further remove the need for a separate teacher model by conditioning the same policy on privileged information to serve as its own teacher. However, privileged conditioning alone does not guarantee that the resulting distillation update improves the student. Indeed, privileged information can lead the teacher to solve tasks through shortcuts unavailable to the student, producing supervision poorly matched to the student's current behavior. Consequently, even a higher-performing teacher can provide guidance that degrades student performance. To address this, we analyze how the choice of privileged teacher affects the student's update. We derive a necessary and sufficient condition for the teacher's local distillation update to be a positive multiple of the student's reward gradient. Our analysis suggests that the teacher should not only perform well on the task, but also provide guidance suited to the student's current capabilities. This characterization motivates a practical teacher-training surrogate that combines outcome rewards with token-level Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization toward the student. Based on this result, we propose Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching (JOLT), which jointly trains a single policy in two roles: a privileged teacher using a KL-regularized objective, and an unprivileged student using dense on-policy distillation. Across mathematical reasoning, coding, tool use, and terminal use, JOLT improves training efficiency and performance, with further gains from student rewards.

</details>


### [234] [NeuralBES: A Differentiable, Control-Aware Emulator for Scalable Building Energy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10459)
*Ting-Yu Dai,Takuya Kurihana,Wing Yee Au,Hon Yung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuralBES 提出一种可微建筑能耗仿真器，用共享神经编码器将建筑元数据映射为物理有界的 RC 热模型参数，通过并行扫描求解线性递推并结合预测-校正回路处理恒温器非线性，在保持物理有效性的同时达到接近最强纯数据基线的精度，参数量少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求侧灵活性（负荷预测、转移与削减）依赖能在数百万异构建筑上被信任的热模型。现有工具存在硬性权衡：EnergyPlus 等高保真物理仿真器精度高但为串行计算且需逐建筑标定；纯数据驱动的序列模型可扩展却丢弃了使其预测可信的物理结构。

Method: NeuralBES 用共享神经编码器对电阻-电容（RC）热模型进行参数化：将建筑面积、建造年代、暖通类型等静态建筑元数据映射为物理有界的电容、电导和设备系数，这些系数构成标量线性递推，经对数空间并行扫描求解；预测-校正回路闭合恒温器-温度非线性，同时保持全时域梯度流。模型在 ResStock 数据集上跨三个气候区训练。

Result: 单一训练编码器即可处理异构建筑原型、年代与气候区，而黑箱基线虽能生成统计上合理但物理上不一致的轨迹。全年 rollout 中，NeuralBES 是唯一既物理有效、又与最强原始误差基线的 MAPE 相差 4 个百分点以内的数据条件模型，参数量比 Transformer 和循环基线少约一个数量级；在参数量对等的物理有效基线中，其 MAPE 比灰箱 RC 替代方案降低一半以上。

Conclusion: NeuralBES 通过将物理结构嵌入可微、可并行的 RC 参数化框架，弥合了高保真仿真器与纯数据驱动模型之间的权衡，在异构建筑上同时实现了物理有效性与高精度，并以显著更少的参数优于同类基线，适用于大规模需求侧灵活性场景。

Abstract: Demand-side flexibility i.e. forecasting, shifting, and curtailing residential energy loads, depends on thermal models trusted across millions of heterogeneous buildings. Existing tools force a hard tradeoff: high-fidelity physics simulators such as EnergyPlus are accurate but sequential and require per-building calibration, while purely data-driven sequence models scale but abandon the physical structure that makes their predictions trustworthy.
  We introduce NeuralBES (Building Energy Simulation), a differentiable emulator that resolves this tradeoff by parameterizing a resistance--capacitance (RC) based thermal model with a shared neural encoder: static building metadata such as floor area, vintage, and HVAC type is mapped to physically bounded capacitances, conductances, and equipment coefficients, which become the coefficients of a scalar linear recurrence solved via a log-space parallel scan, and a predictor--corrector loop closes the thermostat--temperature nonlinearity while preserving full-horizon gradient flow. Trained on the ResStock dataset across three climate zones, NeuralBES handles heterogeneous building archetypes, vintages, and climate zones within a single trained encoder, while black-box baselines produce statistically plausible but physically inconsistent trajectories. On the annual full-year rollout, NeuralBES is the only data-conditioned model that is simultaneously physics-valid and accurate to within 4 MAPE points of the strongest raw-error baseline, while operating at roughly an order of magnitude fewer parameters than the transformer and recurrent baselines; among physics-valid baselines at parameter parity it more than halves the MAPE of the grey-box RC alternative.

</details>


### [235] [Composing What Each Teacher Learned: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation through Teacher-Relative Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10460)
*Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Shayan Mohajer Hamidi,Xiaomin Li,Rohit Jain,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Δ-MOPD，将教师信号由"端点策略"改为"教师减基座的 logit 偏移"，并重新锚定在学生冻结初始化上，在多教师同域组合与路由域蒸馏两类设置下与端点监督对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师同策略蒸馏（MOPD）通常迁移教师的端点策略，其中混合了后训练带来的改变与教师继承自基座的偏好；作者首先揭示阻碍端点迁移的机制：继承的基座牵引可能超过后训练偏移。

Method: 提出 Δ-MOPD：迁移每个教师的"教师减基座"logit 偏移，并重新锚定在学生冻结初始化处；在保持教师选择固定的前提下，于同域组合与路由域蒸馏两种设置中与端点监督对比。

Result: 去除基座牵引可降低教师项范数比与目标-学生 KL。三个教师组合时，Δ-MOPD 较端点组合高 4.11（Math）和 1.95（五基准）分；两个教师时与端点精度相当。分阶段路由下两种顺序平均性能均更高，顺序差距由 10.50 降至 6.42 分；交错路由（每次更新仅一个教师）下两者相当。

Conclusion: 目标构建是 MOPD 中独立于教师选择的设计维度；分阶段结果提示该收益可延伸至跨训练阶段累积的信号。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) is used in two settings. In common-domain composition, several teachers score each student rollout from one prompt domain and their signals form a single target; in routed-domain distillation, prompts from different domains are assigned to the corresponding specialist. Both settings usually transfer each teacher's endpoint policy, which mixes what post-training changed with preferences inherited from the teacher's base. We introduce $Δ$-MOPD, which transfers each teacher's teacher-minus-base logit shift re-anchored at the student's frozen initialization, and compare it with endpoint supervision in both settings while holding teacher selection fixed. We first expose the mechanism that impedes endpoint transfer: inherited base pull can exceed the post-training shift. Removing it reduces the teacher-term norm ratio and target--student KL.
  Across our experiments, the results suggest that shift targets are particularly useful when teacher signals are combined at a state. With three composed teachers, $Δ$-MOPD exceeds endpoint composition by $4.11$ Math and $1.95$ five-benchmark points; with two, it matches endpoint accuracy. Under phased routing, it achieves higher mean performance in both phase orders and reduces the observed order gap from $10.50$ to $6.42$ points. Under interleaved routing, where each update involves one teacher, the two targets perform comparably. The phased results provide supporting evidence that the benefit may extend to signals accumulated across training phases. Target construction is thus an independent design axis in MOPD, complementary to teacher selection.

</details>


### [236] [Distilling Graph Geometry: Knowledge Gap from GNNs to MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10520)
*Zhewei Chen,Hao Zhu,Jiaojiao Jiang,Ahad N. Zehmakan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: G^2MLP：基于Ollivier-Ricci曲率的图几何感知蒸馏框架，在训练期按曲率分配预测级与表示级监督，解决GNN-to-MLP蒸馏中稀疏图谱欠拟合与稠密图谱过拟合两类谱失效模式，推理期保持无图MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-to-MLP蒸馏方法主要迁移节点级预测或采用基于置信度的重加权，但未明确学生应在何处保留教师由图结构诱导的几何信息。该缺失导致学生表示空间出现两类谱失效：稀疏图上的谱欠拟合（丢失边界区域附近的高能教师方向）与稠密图上的谱过拟合（保留教师经聚合已坍缩的伪方向）。

Method: 受能量加权教师-学生对齐目标启发，提出Graph Geometry-aware MLP（G^2MLP）：一个由Ollivier-Ricci曲率引导的训练期蒸馏框架。利用曲率定位两类谱误差的集中区域，并据此在预测级对齐与表示级对齐之间分配监督强度。部署模型仍为标准MLP，推理阶段无需访问图结构。

Result: 在节点分类基准上，G^2MLP持续优于无图蒸馏基线；在稀疏与稠密两种情形下均缩小了教师-学生秩差距（rank gap）；无需架构修改即可迁移至Graph Transformer教师与链接预测任务。

Conclusion: 明确并刻画了图蒸馏中的谱失效模式，并以曲率作为几何信号自适应分配蒸馏监督，实现无图推理下更优的谱对齐与泛化能力，且具备跨教师架构与跨任务的通用性。

Abstract: GNN-to-MLP distillation aims to retain the predictive accuracy of a message-passing teacher while deploying a graph-free MLP at inference. Existing methods mainly transfer node-wise predictions or use confidence-based reweighting, but they do not specify where the student should preserve the teacher's graph-induced geometry. We show that this omission leads to two spectral failure modes in the student's representation space. On sparse graphs, the student suffers from spectral underfit, missing high-energy teacher directions concentrated near boundary regions. On dense graphs, it suffers from spectral overfit, retaining spurious directions that the teacher has collapsed through aggregation. Motivated by an energy-weighted teacher-student alignment objective, we propose Graph Geometry-aware MLP (G^2MLP), a training-time distillation framework guided by Ollivier-Ricci curvature. Curvature identifies where the two spectral errors concentrate and is used to allocate supervision between prediction-level and representation-level alignment. The deployed model remains a standard MLP and requires no graph access at inference. Across node-classification benchmarks, G^2MLP consistently improves over graph-free distillation baselines, reduces the teacher-student rank gap in both regimes, and transfers without architectural changes to Graph Transformer teachers and link prediction.

</details>


### [237] [Decoupling Exploration from Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10536)
*Saif Punjwani,Micah Goldblum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ExpDis 框架，将探索与优化解耦：用带新颖性奖励的 explorer 策略采样、过滤轨迹后蒸馏到无新颖性奖励的 student 策略，多轮交替，在数学推理任务上优于 DAPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 理论上能让模型发现训练数据中不存在的新推理策略，但实践表明直接加入强新颖性激励收效有限，甚至会损害模型质量；且可验证奖励只监督模型知识与行为的狭窄部分，一旦退化难以恢复。

Method: ExpDis 将探索与优化解耦：训练一个或多个带新颖性奖励项的 explorer 策略进行探索，对其轨迹按正确性与质量筛选，再蒸馏到独立的 student 策略；student 不带新颖性奖励。上述过程重复若干轮，交替进行探索与优化。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准和两个模型家族上，相同墙钟时间预算下 ExpDis 优于 DAPO；同时观察到更好的 pass@k 扩展性，表明生成的正确解更加多样。

Conclusion: 通过解耦探索与优化，可以在不损害 student 策略的前提下大幅扩展探索规模，从而提升推理性能与解多样性。

Abstract: Modern language models undergo reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) on top of already-trained checkpoints. A key promise of RLVR is the discovery of new reasoning strategies. In principle, a model can sample novel ideas absent from its prior training data. In practice, however, augmenting RLVR with strong novelty incentives has seen limited success and can degrade model quality. Because verifiable rewards supervise only a narrow slice of the model's knowledge and behavior, such degradations are difficult to recover from. Instead, we decouple exploration from optimization in a framework we call Exploration-Distillation (ExpDis). We train one or more explorer policies with a novelty bonus in the reward, filter their trajectories for correctness and quality, and distill them into a separate student policy. The student policy is then trained without a novelty bonus. We repeat the above procedure for several rounds, alternating between exploration and optimization. This decoupling allows us to aggressively scale exploration without degrading the student policy. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two model families, ExpDis outperforms DAPO at the same wall-clock budget. Moreover, we observe improved pass@$k$ scaling, indicating that ExpDis produces models that generate more diverse correct solutions.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Phase diagram of a semiflexible magnetic polymer in three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08958)
*Linda Delimi,Arpan Dey,Jean-Charles Walter*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 半柔性磁性聚合物中自旋与链构象、刚度耦合，用Monte Carlo和变分自由能方法得到零场相图，包含多步一级相变及直接线圈-晶体转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型为体内核蛋白复合物提供最小表征，需同时容纳DNA弯曲刚性与桥连蛋白，并统一柔性磁性聚合物与半柔性均聚物两个极限情形。

Method: 每个单体带有二态自旋，耦合构象、刚度和磁性自由度；采用面心立方(FCC)晶格上的Monte Carlo模拟，辅以变分自由能上界方法，以弯曲刚度和温度为变量刻画零场相图。

Result: 低刚度下识别出线圈-球状塌缩与铁磁-顺磁耦合的一级相变；更低温度下出现无序球状体到具有类向列取向序晶体的额外一级相变；高刚度下球状体消失，呈现直接的一级线圈-晶体转变。

Conclusion: 链刚性与磁性自由度耦合共同重组了零场相图；模型连接了柔性磁性聚合物与半柔性均聚物两个极限，后者对应所有自旋对齐的强场极限。

Abstract: We study a semiflexible magnetic polymer in which each monomer carries a two-state spin, giving rise to coupled conformational, stiffness, and magnetic degrees of freedom. The model provides a minimal representation of nucleoprotein complexes in vivo, in which DNA bending rigidity and bridging proteins must be simultaneously accommodated. We employ Monte Carlo simulations on a face-centered-cubic (FCC) lattice, complemented by a variational free-energy upper-bound approach, to characterize the zero-field phase diagram as a function of bending stiffness and temperature. At low stiffness, we identify a first-order transition combining coil-to-globule collapse with a ferromagnetic-to-paramagnetic transition. At lower temperatures, an additional first-order transition separates the disordered globule from an ordered crystal characterized by nematic-like orientational order of the bond tangents. At high stiffness, the globule disappears, resulting in a direct first-order coil-to-crystal transition. These results demonstrate how chain stiffness and magnetic degrees of freedom couple to polymer conformation and reorganize the zero-field phase diagram. Our model connects the two limiting cases of flexible magnetic polymers and semiflexible homopolymers, the latter corresponding to the strong-field limit in which all spins are aligned.

</details>


### [239] [Heat Transport of the $β$-Fermi--Pasta--Ulam--Tsingou chain in the long-wave limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09405)
*Henrique Santos Lima,Matheus M. R. Poltronieri Martins,Lucas Reis e Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过长波连续介质描述与通量守恒离散化，在中尺度自由度下获得接近2/5的β-FPU链热导指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子动力学直接求解对称β-FPU链的异常热导指数面临有限尺寸交叉与热接触电阻，需要极大系统。作者希望借助连续介质模型，在中等数值自由度下可及动理学理论预测的κ∝L^{2/5}标度。

Method: 建立长波连续介质描述：非线性弹性场保留微观相互作用的立方应力项，并与Langevin热库交换热量；采用通量守恒的空间离散，使力与能量流由同一应力构造，得到内部输运估计量。

Result: 在L>8（参考分辨率下约10^3个网格节点及以上）时，拟合指数为0.399±0.004；两种更细分辨率下指数稳定，但热导振幅仍依赖分辨率。

Conclusion: 主要结果是存在可及的有限尺寸区间使指数接近2/5，而非网格间距趋于零时绝对热导收敛；该框架为研究非谐系统异常输运与非线性弹性介质能量流提供了实用途径与守恒连续介质基础。

Abstract: Resolving the anomalous conductivity exponent of the symmetric $β$-Fermi--Pasta--Ulam--Tsingou chain by molecular dynamics can require very large systems because of long finite-size crossovers and thermal-contact resistance. Motivated by this computational challenge, we derive a long-wavelength continuum description and investigate whether the kinetic-theory scaling $κ\propto L^{2/5}$ becomes accessible with a moderate number of numerical degrees of freedom. The nonlinear elastic field retains the cubic stress of the microscopic interaction and exchanges heat with Langevin reservoirs. A flux-conservative spatial discretization constructs the force and energy current from the same stress, providing a consistent interior transport estimator. For $L>8$, corresponding to approximately $10^3$ mesh nodes and above at the reference resolution, the fitted exponent is $0.399\pm0.004$. Mesh refinement supports the stability of this exponent between the two finer resolutions, although the conductivity amplitude remains resolution-dependent. The main result is thus an accessible finite-size regime near $2/5$, rather than convergence of the absolute conductivity as the mesh spacing vanishes. This formulation provides a practical route to studying anomalous transport in anharmonic systems and a conservative continuum framework for investigating energy flow in nonlinear elastic media.

</details>


### [240] [Correspondence between Asymptotic Quantum Many-Body Scars in Closed Systems and Diffusive Nambu-Goldstone Modes in Open Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09662)
*Masaya Kunimi,Taiki Haga,Masaya Nakagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文揭示了闭量子系统弱遍历破缺与开量子系统扩散流体力学之间的共同结构，建立了彩虹量子多体疤痕母哈密顿量与强耗散Lindbladian之间的谱对应关系，并将RQMBS态映射为具有SWSSB序的最大混合态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开量子多体系统中的流体力学弛豫可理解为与强-弱自发对称破缺(SWSSB)相关的扩散Nambu-Goldstone模，而闭系统中的弱遍历破缺通常被视为独立现象。作者希望寻找二者背后的统一结构。

Method: 构造自旋、费米子、玻色子系统的渐近彩虹量子多体疤痕(ARQMBS)态；在扩大的疤痕子空间中对母哈密顿量作局部Rokhsar-Kivelson型无阻挫分解；采用单模变分构造；通过向量化将RQMBS态映射为固定电荷扇区中的无穷温度最大混合态；分析有效Lindbladian的谱界与间隙标度。

Result: 单模变分构造给出最低激发能的O(k^2)上界，所得态满足正交性、能量方差消失和特征纠缠标度等ARQMBS判据。向量化后RQMBS态在固定电荷扇区对应最大混合态，在有限密度下具长程SWSSB序。在一致有界正跃迁率下，有效Lindbladian与母哈密顿量共享局部投影子，获得双边谱界及相同的间隙标度；均匀速率时两算子成正比、本征模重合。精确的二次母哈密顿量分支直接映射为扩散NG模。

Conclusion: 闭系统中的弱遍历破缺(彩虹量子多体疤痕)与开系统中的扩散流体力学之间存在精确谱对应，统一了两类系统中慢弛豫的机制。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic relaxation in open quantum many-body systems can be understood in terms of diffusive Nambu--Goldstone (NG) modes associated with strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB). Here, we find a common structure underlying weak ergodicity breaking in closed quantum systems and diffusive hydrodynamics in open quantum systems. We establish a spectral correspondence between the parent Hamiltonian of rainbow quantum many-body scar (RQMBS) states in closed systems and the effective Lindbladian of strongly dissipative open systems with strong $U(1)$ symmetry and local charge-dephasing Lindblad operators. For broad classes of spin, fermionic, and bosonic systems, we construct asymptotic RQMBS (ARQMBS) states. Within the enlarged scar subspace, the parent Hamiltonian admits a local Rokhsar--Kivelson-type frustration-free decomposition. A single-mode variational construction gives an $O(k^2)$ upper bound on its lowest excitation energy and yields states satisfying the defining ARQMBS criteria: orthogonality, vanishing energy variance, and characteristic entanglement scaling. Under vectorization, RQMBS states are mapped to maximally mixed infinite-temperature states in fixed-charge sectors, which exhibit long-range SWSSB order under finite-density conditions. Assuming uniformly bounded positive transition rates, the effective Lindbladian has the same local projectors as the parent Hamiltonian but with positive transition-dependent weights, yielding two-sided spectral bounds and the same system-size scaling of their gaps. For uniform rates, the two operators are proportional to each other and their eigenmodes coincide. Therefore, an exact quadratic parent-Hamiltonian branch maps directly to a diffusive NG mode. Our results connect different mechanisms of slow relaxation in closed and open quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [241] [Heat transport in weakly anharmonic Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09936)
*Kiratholly Nandakumar Madhav Sharma,Stefano Mossa,Jean-Louis Barrat,Markus Holzmann*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文用大规模平衡分子动力学研究弱非谐极限下的一维β-FPU链的异常热输运，通过将热流分解为声子模式贡献，揭示其长时自相关由对角模式关联主导，衰减由声子阻尼率决定，并给出热导率的标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究弱非谐区一维β-FPU链中异常热输运的微观机制，尤其是声子模式关联与阻尼率如何决定热流自相关的长时行为及热导率的尺度依赖，澄清弱非谐是否引入新的渐近输运机制。

Method: 采用大规模平衡分子动力学模拟，将热流分解为声子模式贡献，分析其长时自相关函数；从对角模式关联出发，用声子阻尼率定量刻画其衰减，并外推到热力学极限，考察对角与非对角模式关联、长波声子贡献及有限尺寸效应。

Result: 阻尼率标度为Γ_k ∝ (βT)^{4/3} k^{5/3}，含次领头k^2修正；该修正导致扩展的预渐近区C(t) ~ t^{-4/5}，随后进入渐近衰减C(t) ~ t^{-3/5}，从而给出κ(N) ~ N^{2/5} + O(N^{1/5})。弱非谐下非对角模式关联持续到特征时间τ_od ~ (βT)^{-2}，长波声子贡献需越来越大体系才能分辨。

Conclusion: 弱非谐不引入独立的渐近输运机制，而是将声子主导区间的出现推移到更长时间与更大长度尺度；异常热输运仍由声子模式关联及其阻尼率控制。

Abstract: We investigate anomalous heat transport in the one-dimensional $β$-Fermi--Pasta--Ulam--Tsingou chain in the weakly anharmonic regime using large-scale equilibrium molecular dynamics. By resolving the heat current into phonon-mode contributions, we show that its long-time autocorrelation is controlled by diagonal mode correlations, whose decay is quantitatively determined by the corresponding phonon damping rates. Extrapolation to the thermodynamic limit gives $Γ_k \propto (βT)^{4/3} k^{5/3}$, with a subleading $k^2$ correction. The latter produces an extended pre-asymptotic regime, $C(t) \sim t^{-4/5}$, before the asymptotic decay $C(t) \sim t^{-3/5}$ is reached, leading to $κ(N) \sim N^{2/5} + O(N^{1/5})$. At weak anharmonicity, off-diagonal mode correlations persist up to a characteristic time $τ_{\mathrm{od}} \sim (βT)^{-2}$, while increasingly large systems are required to resolve the long-wavelength phonon contribution. Weak anharmonicity therefore does not introduce a distinct asymptotic transport mechanism, but shifts the onset of the phonon-dominated regime to progressively longer times and larger length scales.

</details>


### [242] [Percolation on interdependent one-dimensional long-range networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10097)
*Adar Sharabi,Amir Bashan,Sergey V. Buldyrev,Guy Amit*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了两个一维长程网络构成的复用网络中互巨连通分量的涌现，给出临界值$C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$的严格下界与树近似，并揭示相变阶数随$\sigma$变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究复用（多层）结构如何影响长程网络互巨连通分量的存在性与稳定性，并与单层网络情形进行对比。

Method: 构建两个一维格点长程网络的复用模型，边连接概率随距离$r$按$Cr^{-(1+\sigma)}$（$0<\sigma<1$，$0<C\leq1$）代数衰减；推导$C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}(\sigma)$的严格下界，并采用树近似估算临界值，结合数值模拟验证。

Result: 树近似对$C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$的估计在$\sigma\leq0.5$时与模拟吻合误差在0.6%以内；复用网络显著更不稳定，$C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$最高可达单层值的2.4倍；模拟表明相变阶数依赖$\sigma$：$\sigma\leq0.35$为 abruptly 崩塌的一阶相变，$\sigma\geq0.4$为连续相变；当依赖边连接随机节点对时，所有$\sigma$下均为一级相变。

Conclusion: 复用结构显著降低长程网络的稳定性；互巨连通分量的相变性质由长程指数$\sigma$控制，且依赖连接方式（空间距离依赖 vs. 随机配对）决定相变是否恒为一阶。

Abstract: We study the emergence of a mutual giant component in a multiplex network comprised of two one-dimensional long-range networks on a lattice. The probability that two nodes on one lattice, with a distance $r$ between them, are connected with an edge falls algebraically with distance as $Cr^{-(1+σ)}$, where $0 < σ< 1$ and $0 < C \leq 1$. A mutual giant component exists if $C > C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$, where $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}(σ)$ is a critical value of $C$ that is dependent on $σ$. We find rigorous lower bounds on $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$, and a tree approximation whose estimate of $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$ agrees with simulations within 0.6% for $σ\leq 0.5$. The multiplex network is significantly less stable than the single network case, as is evident by the larger value of $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$, which is up to 2.4 times the single-layer value. Our simulations indicate that the order of the transition depends on $σ$. For $σ\leq 0.35$ the mutual giant component collapses abruptly, in a first-order phase transition, while for $σ\geq 0.4$ the transition is continuous. When the dependency links connect random pairs of nodes, the transition is first order for all $σ$.

</details>


### [243] [Vortex screening and the fate of the BKT transition with long-range couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10171)
*Guido Giachetti*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二维XY模型在长程耦合（衰减为r^{-2-σ}）下BKT相的稳定性，通过实空间重整化群方法发现稳定准长程序区间仅在σ>7/4时存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) 相在长程相互作用下的稳定性是一个尚未解决的开放问题，本文旨在回答长程耦合对QLRO态稳定性的影响。

Method: 在二维XY模型中采用耦合衰减为r^{-2-σ}的形式，发展了一套实空间重整化群处理方案，工作于准长程有序（QLRO）态的高斯流形附近。保留了完整的多体涡旋相互作用，证明在 fugacity 领头阶下，小涡旋-反涡旋对的屏蔽保持其高斯平均表示，无需对长程耦合展开；随后微扰地引入自旋波，得到局域稳定性判据。

Result: 得到局域稳定性判据：稳定的QLRO区间仅在σ>7/4时存在。对于σ<7/4，还研究了有限耦合下的Wilson-Fisher型不动点，保留的流方程预测其在并入高斯线时ν^{-1}∼√(7/4-σ)。

Conclusion: 长程耦合显著改变BKT相稳定性：只有在σ>7/4时准长程序才能稳定存在；σ<7/4时由Wilson-Fisher型不动点主导，并给出了关联长度指数发散的标度形式。

Abstract: The stability of the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless phase against long-range interactions remains an open problem. We address this question in the two-dimensional XY model with couplings decaying as $r^{-2-σ}$, by developing a real-space renormalization-group treatment near the Gaussian manifold of quasi-long-range ordered (QLRO) states. We retain the full many-body vortex interaction and show that, at leading order in the fugacity, screening by small vortex--antivortex pairs preserves its Gaussian-average representation without an expansion in the long-range coupling. Including spin waves perturbatively then yields a local stability criterion: a stable QLRO interval exists only for $σ>7/4$. We also investigate a Wilson--Fisher-like fixed point at finite coupling for $σ<7/4$, for which the retained flow predicts $ν^{-1}\sim\sqrt{7/4-σ}$ as it merges with the Gaussian line.

</details>


### [244] [Gaussian Scrooge Ensemble from Deep Thermalization in Free-Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10279)
*Angelo Russotto,Katja Klobas,Pasquale Calabrese,Bruno Bertini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在自由费米子链中研究深度热化，指出测量结果在热力学极限下对局部守恒荷的信息可忽略，从而涌现出由子系统广义吉布斯系综决定的费米高斯Scrooge系综，并给出有限温度下晚期投影系综的数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度热化揭示了测量环境后子系统出现普适纯态系综的现象，但此前研究多集中于相互作用或混沌系统；本文旨在探究非相互作用可积系统（自由费米子链）中是否能出现类似的普适投影系综，从而将热化描述从约化密度矩阵推广到条件纯态的普适统计。

Method: 采用保持高斯性且破坏粒子数守恒的局部测量方案，对自由费米子链进行测量；分析测量结果对有限子系统内局部守恒荷的部分信息在热力学极限下的渐近行为；在子系统层面直接构造费米高斯Scrooge系综，并将其与有限温度下的晚期投影系综进行数值对比。

Result: 尽管测量结果能提供有限子系统内局部守恒荷的部分信息，但该信息在热力学极限下变得可忽略（有效非揭示）；由此涌现出由子系统广义吉布斯系综决定的费米高斯Scrooge系综；数值证据表明该普适系综能描述有限温度下的晚期投影系综。

Conclusion: 研究将非相互作用可积系统的热化描述从约化密度矩阵拓展到条件纯态的普适统计，表明深度热化与普适投影系综现象可推广至自由费米子体系。

Abstract: Deep thermalization indicates the emergence of universal ensembles of pure states in a subsystem after measuring its environment. We investigate this phenomenon in a free-fermion chain using local measurements that preserve Gaussianity and break particle-number conservation. Although the measurement outcomes provide partial information about local conserved charges within a finite subsystem, we argue that this information becomes negligible in the thermodynamic limit. This effective non-revelation underlies the emergence of a fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble, determined by the subsystem's generalized Gibbs ensemble. We construct this universal ensemble directly at the subsystem level and provide numerical evidence that it describes the late-time projected ensemble at finite temperature. Our results extend the description of thermalization in non-interacting integrable systems beyond the reduced density matrix to universal statistics of conditional pure states.

</details>


### [245] [Effective-Geometry Rescaling and Universal Critical Behavior in the Anisotropic Three-State Potts Model on the Square Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10467)
*Fan Yang,Jian Gao,Lu Liu,Yuhai Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 使用Wolff单团簇蒙特卡罗与有限尺寸标度研究二维各向异性三态Potts铁磁体，发现空间各向异性只改变有效临界几何（有效纵横比），不改变三维态Potts普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究空间各向异性（耦合比λ=Jy/Jx≠1）是否会改变二维三态Potts模型的临界行为与普适类，并厘清各向异性如何影响临界几何标度。

Method: 在方格点阵上用Wolff单团簇蒙特卡罗模拟λ=0.5、0.75、1的体系，结合有限尺寸标度分析关联比、领头Fisher零点标度及累积密度；引入方向性关联比Rx、Ry与方向性FK缠绕概率刻画临界各向异性。

Result: 关联比标度与二维三态Potts普适类一致；Fisher零点标度给出ν=5/6，累积密度与α=1/3相符。λ=0.5时Rx与Ry收敛到不同临界值但共享同一关联长度指数；由缠绕概率独立测得有效纵横比ρe^□=0.6413(5)，与等径向表示理论值0.64150030接近；用理论值设定物理纵横比后Rx≃Ry恢复，整体关联比逼近各向同性方格参考值。

Conclusion: 空间各向异性改变有效临界几何（有效纵横比）但不改变三维态Potts体的普适类；方向性关联比与缠绕概率可用于定量刻画各向异性临界点。

Abstract: We study the two-dimensional anisotropic three-state Potts ferromagnet on the square lattice using Wolff single-cluster Monte Carlo simulations and finite-size scaling. For coupling ratios $λ=J_y/J_x=0.5$, $0.75$, and $1$, finite-size scaling of the correlation ratio yields critical behavior consistent with the two-dimensional three-state Potts universality class. The scaling of the leading Fisher zeros gives a correlation-length exponent consistent with $ν=5/6$, while their cumulative density is consistent with the expected specific-heat exponent $α=1/3$. We further characterize the anisotropy at criticality using directional correlation ratios $R_x$ and $R_y$ together with directional FK wrapping probabilities. For $λ=0.5$ on a physically square lattice, $R_x$ and $R_y$ approach distinct critical values while yielding a common correlation-length exponent. From the wrapping probabilities, we independently determine an effective aspect ratio $ρ_e^\square=0.6413(5)$, in close agreement with the theoretical value $ρ_e^{\square,\mathrm{th}}\simeq0.64150030$ obtained from the isoradial representation. Using the theoretical value to set the physical aspect ratio restores directional equivalence, $R_x\simeq R_y$, and brings the overall correlation ratio toward the isotropic-square reference. The results show that spatial anisotropy changes the effective critical geometry without altering the bulk three-state Potts universality class.

</details>


### [246] [Trend formation with sparse global sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10521)
*Sarath Sankar,Abhijit Sinha,Shankar Ghosh,Vikram Tripathi,Amitava Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究提出并验证了一种极简群体协调模型：自驱动个体周期性地朝随机选取的少量同伴的质心重新定向，无需感知个别邻居位置/朝向；稀疏非局域采样可使无序群体自发形成全局向列序，且采样子集越小（最少2个）排序越快，估计噪声经几何转向规则滤波成为对称破缺的驱动力。作者给出与仿真定量吻合的排序成功判据，并在最多15台差速驱动机器人上实验证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物集群与工程集群普遍面临"无中心控制器、无稠密全局通信"下实现全局协调的难题。既有研究多依赖稠密通信或对邻居位置/朝向的直接感知，缺乏对信息高效、通信受限条件下协调机制的理解。本文旨在探究：仅凭极稀疏、随机的非局域采样能否可靠产生全局有序，并厘清采样规模与频率对排序的影响。

Method: 构建极简模型：有界域内自驱动个体周期性地朝"随机选取的少量同伴的质心"重新定向，不直接感知任何个体邻居的位置或朝向。通过理论推导给出排序成功的解析判据（关于采样规模与采样频率的函数），并用数值仿真验证；进一步在最多15台差速驱动机器人组成的小型机器人集群上开展实验验证该机制。

Result: 该稀疏非局域采样规则能可靠地将初始无序群体驱动至全局对齐的向列态。缩小采样子集（直至最小2个个体）不仅不阻碍排序，反而加速有序化：由此产生的估计噪声经几何转向规则滤波后本身即成为对称破缺的驱动力。所推导的解析判据与仿真定量吻合，能预测排序在何种采样规模与频率下成功。机器人实验证实了该机制。

Conclusion: 稀疏随机采样是一种信息高效的群体协调途径，为理解动物集群行为及设计通信受限的机器人集群提供了新视角与可验证机制。

Abstract: Achieving global coordination without a central controller or dense global communication is a defining challenge for both biological collectives and engineered swarms. We introduce and analyze a minimal model in which self-propelled agents in a bounded domain periodically reorient their motions toward the centroid of a small, randomly chosen subset of their peers, with no direct sensing of any individual neighbor's position or heading. We show that this sparse, non-local sampling rule reliably drives an initially disordered population to a globally aligned, nematic state, and that shrinking the sampled subset -- down to the minimum of two agents -- accelerates ordering rather than impeding it: the resulting estimation noise, filtered through a geometric turning rule, is itself the engine of symmetry breaking. We derive an analytical criterion, in quantitative agreement with simulation, that predicts when this ordering succeeds as a function of the sampling size and sampling frequency. We confirm the mechanism experimentally in a swarm of up to fifteen differential-drive robots. These results identify sparse random sampling as an information-efficient route to collective coordination, with implications for understanding animal collectives and for designing communication-limited robotic swarms.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [FlexGrad: a root-free approach to adaptive step sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09203)
*Pablo Barros,Aaron Defazio,Vincent Guigues*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出 FlexGrad 自适应梯度优化器，同时利用累积梯度与累积平方梯度缩放步长，克服 AdaGrad 无法利用梯度相关性的缺陷，具有在线、免时域、无辅助搜索等优点，并在凸 Lipschitz 目标上达到最优渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 AdaGrad 类方法仅依赖累积平方梯度，无法利用梯度之间的相关性，导致在梯度方向持续一致时步长过小、收敛受限。

Method: FlexGrad 在步长缩放中同时使用累积梯度与累积平方梯度：噪声较大时保持 AdaGrad 类行为，梯度方向持续时允许显著更大步长。方法完全在线、无需时域信息，也无辅助搜索过程，并给出全局与逐坐标两种变体。

Result: 在凸 Lipschitz 目标上证明了 FlexGrad 达到非光滑凸优化的最优渐近收敛率；在多种凸与非凸问题上实验表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: FlexGrad 易于实现，与实用的梯度归一化方法紧密相关，在保持理论最优性的同时具备良好的实践性能。

Abstract: We introduce the FlexGrad optimizer, an adaptive gradient method that addresses a key limitation of classical AdaGrad-type methods: their inability to exploit correlations between gradients. By scaling steps using both cumulative gradients and cumulative squared gradients, FlexGrad preserves AdaGrad-like behavior in noisy regimes while allowing substantially larger steps when gradient directions are persistent. The method is fully online, horizon-free, and avoids any auxiliary search procedure during optimization. For convex Lipschitz objectives, we prove that FlexGrad attains the optimal asymptotic convergence rate for nonsmooth convex optimization, with guarantees for both global and coordinate-wise variants. FlexGrad is straightforward to implement and is closely connected to practical gradient-normalized methods. Experiments on a range of convex and nonconvex problems show that FlexGrad performs competitively across a range of optimization settings.

</details>


### [248] [Regret-Optimal Linear-Quadratic Control under Distributional Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09220)
*Lukas-Benedikt Fiechtner,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究时变分布漂移下的有限时域LQR，提出事前分布鲁棒后悔优化（DRRO），用含Gelbrich动能预算与参考矩偏离预算的时变耦合矩模糊集刻画不确定律序列，并将仿射扰动反馈综合精确化为半定规划（SDP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立扰动的概率律可能随时间漂移，传统DRO或固定律假设难以刻画这种演化不确定性；需要以“相对知晓律序列的最优因果控制器”的后悔为准则，构造能同时约束漂移速度与累积偏离的模糊集。

Method: 提出事前DRRO，最小化最坏情形超额期望成本；引入时间耦合矩模糊集：Gelbrich动能预算控制相邻时刻矩的漂移，第二预算限制相对参考矩的偏离与持续累积失配。尽管矩跨时间耦合，仿射扰动反馈综合仍精确等价于一个SDP；最优策略利用历史扰动预测由当前与未来扰动均值决定的标称LQR修正项，SDP解同时给出最坏情形矩路径及对应律。

Result: 仿射扰动反馈综合精确归约为SDP；而标准DRO的类似SDP松弛可能存在对偶间隙。组合清仓实验显示，在漂移模糊集下，所提DRRO的最坏情形后悔显著低于常见律DRRO与标称控制。

Conclusion: 通过带Gelbrich动能预算与累积失配预算的时变耦合矩模糊集，DRRO可在律序列漂移下实现可计算的鲁棒最优控制，并在实验中取得更低的最坏情形后悔；相较标准DRO，其仿射反馈综合具有精确SDP刻画优势。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon LQR with independent disturbances whose laws may drift over time. We address uncertainty in this evolution through ex-ante distributionally robust regret optimization (DRRO), which minimizes worst-case excess expected cost relative to the optimal causal controller that knows the law sequence. To specify the admissible law sequences, we introduce a temporally coupled moment ambiguity set with a Gelbrich kinetic-energy budget and a second budget limiting departures from reference moments and persistent cumulative mismatch. Despite the coupling of moments across time, affine disturbance-feedback synthesis reduces exactly to a semidefinite program. An optimal policy uses past disturbances to predict the correction to nominal LQR determined by current and future disturbance means. The SDP solution also yields worst-case moment paths and corresponding laws. For standard distributionally robust optimization (DRO), the analogous SDP relaxation can have a duality gap. Portfolio liquidation experiments show substantially lower worst-case regret over the drifting ambiguity set than common-law DRRO and nominal control.

</details>


### [249] [Fluid Mixing and Incompressible Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09235)
*Max Emerick,John Igraszek,Bassam Bamieh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将不可压缩流体混合问题表述为被动标量输运的最优控制问题，建立类比经典最优传输的几何框架，推导测地线与梯度流；指出动能目标导致不适定，而enstrophy（拟涡能）更合适，导出Euler-Poincaré测地方程并进行数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可压缩流体混合问题虽研究已久但仍有许多未解问题。作者希望回答"高效的混合流场是什么样的，它们如何行为"，借鉴最优质量传输的动态与几何方法，为混合问题建立几何理论框架，以理解高效混合流场的性质与行为。

Method: 将流体混合问题表述为被动标量输运的最优控制问题，动力学由连续性方程与不可压缩约束给出；构造类比Wasserstein度量的度量及形式Riemann结构，将问题分解为基本几何子问题并推导最优性必要条件、测地方程与梯度流；分别讨论动能与enstrophy两种控制目标，并进行数值模拟；泛化Helmholtz分解、重新推导Clebsch表示、发展类比Otto微积分的框架。

Result: 动能目标下恢复不可压缩Euler方程，但问题似乎不适定，说明动能不是合适的混合投入度量；enstrophy目标下导出体积保持微分同胚群上右不变齐次$\dot{H}^1$度量的Euler-Poincaré（EPDiff）测地方程，并给出测地线与梯度流的数值模拟。

Conclusion: 该工作为流体混合建立了类比最优传输的几何框架，表明enstrophy比动能更适合刻画混合努力，并将混合问题与Arnold几何流体力学及EPDiff方程联系起来。

Abstract: The problem of incompressible fluid mixing is long-studied, yet many questions remain open. This paper aims to address the question ``what do efficient flow fields for mixing look like, and how do they behave?'' Inspired by the dynamic and geometric approach to optimal mass transport, we formulate a version of the fluid mixing problem as an optimal control problem for transport of a passive scalar, in which the dynamics are given by the continuity equation together with an incompressibility constraint. We then develop a geometric framework around this problem which parallels that of classical optimal transport: the optimal control problem induces a metric on the set of reachable states (analogous to the Wasserstein metric), and this metric carries a formal Riemannian structure when the optimal control objective is induced by an inner product. Using this structure, we decompose our problem into a series of more fundamental geometric problems and derive (formal) necessary conditions for optimality, geodesic equations, and gradient flows for mixing. Specializing the control objective to the kinetic energy recovers the incompressible Euler equation, connecting our framework to the geometric hydrodynamics of Arnold. The resulting problem appears to be ill-posed, however, suggesting that the kinetic energy is an unnatural measure of mixing effort. Specializing instead to the enstrophy, which we argue is better suited to mixing, yields the Euler-Poincaré (EPDiff) geodesic equation for the right-invariant homogeneous $\dot{H}^1$ metric on the group of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms, and we present numerical simulations of both geodesics and gradient flows in this case. Along the way, we generalize the classical Helmholtz decomposition of vector fields, rederive the Clebsch representation of incompressible flows, and develop a parallel to the Otto calculus of optimal transport.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond Nominal Equilibria: Risk-Averse Multi-Population Mean-Field Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09244)
*Bhavini Jeloka,Siddhartha Ganguly,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"风险厌恶型多种群平均场博弈"新范式，每个种群在其余部分种群平均场流的动态可行模糊集上优化最坏情况期望收益，并建立理论性质、收缩性结果与收敛的虚拟对弈算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有平均场博弈及多种群变体虽能通过代表智能体与平均场分布建模大规模异构多智能体系统，但未显式考虑其他种群行为的不确定性，缺乏对最坏情况风险的处理能力。

Method: 采用占用测度（occupation-measure）表述，结合集值分析工具刻画模糊集的几何性质；引入熵正则化推导不动点算子的收缩性并用于学习均衡；提出风险厌恶型虚拟对弈（fictitious-play）方案。

Result: 在温和假设下证明了模糊集几何性质与新型风险厌恶多种群平均场均衡的存在性；熵正则化下不动点算子具有收缩性且可用于均衡学习；风险厌恶虚拟对弈方案的利用度（exploitability）收敛至零，尽管最坏情况目标引入额外非线性。数值实验验证了收敛性与风险厌恶行为。

Conclusion: 风险厌恶多种群平均场博弈为不确定环境下异构多智能体系统的均衡分析与学习提供了理论框架，均衡存在性、算子收缩性与算法收敛性均得到保证。

Abstract: Recent advances in mean-field games and its multi-population variants enable large-scale heterogeneous multi-agent systems to be modeled through representative agents and their associated mean-field distributions. However, existing approaches do not explicitly account for uncertainty in the behavior of other populations. To this end, we introduce a new paradigm: risk-averse multi-population mean-field games, where each population optimizes a worst-case expected reward over dynamically feasible ambiguity sets of mean-field flows of a subset of the other populations. Employing an occupation-measure formulation along with tools from set-valued analysis, we establish, under mild assumptions, several theoretical properties of the multi-population game, including the geometric properties of the ambiguity sets and the existence of a novel risk-averse multi-population mean-field equilibrium. Further, we derive contractivity results of the fixed-point operator under entropy regularization and show that it can be utilized to learn the equilibrium. Finally, we propose a risk-averse fictitious-play scheme and show that exploitability decays to zero, despite the additional nonlinearity introduced by the worst-case objective. We report several numerical experiments to illustrate convergence and risk-averse behavior.

</details>


### [251] [Consumption and Investment When Welfare Floors Change over Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09263)
*Gugyum Ha,Junkee Jeon,Takwon Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 在有限期界内研究带有随时间变化福利下限（期末为终端财富下限）的消费-投资问题，用累积乘子将其化为抛物型障碍问题，刻画干预边界的存在性、正则性与对偶性，并给出可行财富下确界及与有限承诺、内生借贷能力的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的消费-投资决策往往受制于一个随时间变化的最低福利保障（如外部期权价值），该约束在期末退化为终端财富下限。现有文献对这类时变福利下限约束下最优策略的边界结构、对偶刻画及经济含义尚缺乏系统分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 引入累积乘子将约束优化降维为抛物型障碍问题，利用障碍问题的解分析干预边界，构造对偶价值，并通过累积乘子的running supremum表示控制其矩；进一步在完全市场叠加随机收入，将福利下限解释为外部期权价值。

Result: 当相对风险厌恶系数大于1时，每一内点时刻均存在干预边界；小于1时，边界仅在福利下限的折现变换局部递增并创严格running record的时点存在，因而可消失并重新出现。证明了边界的局部正则性，构造了由障碍问题解得到的对偶价值，并验证了最优累积乘子。强对偶性给出了可行财富的精确下确界。

Conclusion: 福利下限约束下的消费-投资问题可通过抛物型障碍问题框架统一处理；干预边界的结构依赖于相对风险厌恶水平；强对偶性精确刻画可行财富下界；在含随机收入的完全市场中，该下限可解释为外部期权价值，从而将边界与有限承诺及内生借贷能力联系起来。

Abstract: We study consumption and investment over a finite horizon when continuation utility must stay above a welfare floor that changes over time, and the floor at the horizon acts as a terminal wealth floor. A cumulative multiplier reduces the problem to a parabolic obstacle problem. When relative risk aversion exceeds one, the intervention boundary exists at every interior date. When it is below one, the boundary exists exactly at the dates where a discounted transformation of the floor is locally increasing and sets a strict running record, so it can disappear and later reenter. We prove local regularity of the boundary, construct the dual value from the obstacle solution, and verify the optimal cumulative multiplier, whose moments are controlled through its representation as a running supremum. Strong duality identifies the exact lower endpoint of feasible wealth. With stochastic income in a complete market, the floor becomes the value of an outside option, which links the boundary to limited commitment and endogenous borrowing capacity.

</details>


### [252] [The Geometry of Anisotropic Dilation for Optimal Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09310)
*Carson Newman,Oscar Leong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出各向异性膨胀（anisotropic dilation）变换，可传递地作用于 Gibbs 类正则器中由径向汇总统计量 ρ_P 刻画的正则几何；给出闭式最优 profile、Jensen 间隙正向性结果及联合学习的泛化界，并实验验证其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动逆问题的核心难题是如何构造能自适应数据分布几何的正则器。已有最优正则化理论表明，在广义 Gibbs 类中，与分布 P 最匹配的正则器由单一位向相关的径向汇总统计量 ρ_P 决定，因此可通过变换数据来控制正则器几何。但尚不清楚哪些变换能以简单显式方式作用于 ρ_P，以及能否改善变分问题的优化性质或将固定基正则器适配到数据。

Method: 引入各向异性膨胀：一种向保映射，沿欧氏射线按球面上的正 profile 重缩放每个点。分析该变换族在分布上的轨道结构，推导将固定基正则器最优适配到数据的闭式 profile，并用 Jensen 间隙刻画其相对各向同性重缩放的改进；建立联合学习基正则器与各向异性 profile 的有限样本泛化界，给出 profile 对数为线性特征模型或稀疏 ReLU 网络参数化时的显式速率；据此联合参数化并学习二者，得到数据自适应正则器。

Result: 各向异性膨胀族可传递地作用在径向汇总统计量空间上：任意 Gibbs 类中的目标正则器均可由任意源分布经单一显式 profile 达到。所得改进由 Jensen 间隙支配，且在若干自然族中可证明为正。泛化界给出显式收敛速率。在受控二维族与 MNIST 去噪实验中，学习 profile 进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: 各向异性膨胀为控制正则器几何、将固定基正则器适配到数据提供了一种简单而显式的机制，并具备理论保证与实证增益，可用于变分逆问题的数据自适应正则化。

Abstract: A central question in data-driven inverse problems is how to construct a regularizer that adapts to the geometry of the data distribution. Recent work in optimal regularization shows that, within a broad Gibbs class, the regularizer best matched to a distribution $P$ is determined by a single, direction-dependent radial summary statistic $ρ_P$. This suggests a way to control regularizer geometry by transforming the data. In particular, which transformations act on $ρ_P$ in a simple, explicit way? Can they improve the optimization properties of the resulting variational problems or adapt a fixed base regularizer to data? To address these questions, we introduce anisotropic dilation, a direction-preserving map that rescales each point along its Euclidean ray by a positive profile on the sphere. Despite its simplicity, this family acts transitively on the space of radial summary statistics: any target regularizer in the Gibbs class can be reached from any source distribution by a single explicit profile. We characterize the resulting orbit structure on distributions, derive in closed form the profile that optimally adapts a fixed base regularizer to the data, and show that its improvement over isotropic rescaling is governed by a Jensen gap that is provably positive for several natural families. We also establish finite-sample generalization bounds for jointly learning the base regularizer and anisotropic profile, with explicit rates when the logarithm of the profile is parameterized by linear feature models or sparse ReLU networks. Building on this theory, we parameterize the base regularizer and anisotropic profile and learn them jointly from samples, yielding a data-adaptive regularizer for variational inverse problems. Experiments on a controlled two-dimensional family and MNIST denoising show that learning the profile further improves performance.

</details>


### [253] [Random projection with linear equalities and inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09324)
*Santanu S. Dey,Jingye Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从随机过程上确界与泛链（generic chaining）的视角重新审视随机投影，结合拉格朗日对偶给出更短、更锐利的保证，并将其从线性等式约束推广到线性不等式约束问题，关键构造是保持对偶可行性的随机投影对偶证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机投影通过用随机线性组合替代原约束来减少线性等式约束数量并近似保持最优值，但既有分析多依赖基于 Johnson–Lindenstrauss 引理的多面体设定，适用范围受限，且难以处理不等式约束。作者希望用随机过程上确界（尤其是泛链）这一更本质的工具来解释该现象，并突破原有设定。

Method: 将拉格朗日对偶与泛链（generic chaining）相结合，分析随机投影下最优值/对偶界的保持性；据此给出更简短的证明与更锋利的保证；进一步提出一种新的对偶证书随机投影构造，用于线性不等式约束问题，并证明其保持对偶可行性。

Result: 得到比基于 JL 引理与多面体设定更一般的结果：证明更短、保证更锐利且不局限于多面体情形；随机投影的不等式约束通常既非原可行域的松弛也非限制，但仍可近似保持对偶界；新构造的对偶证书投影保持对偶可行性，且与既有 JL 型构造不同。

Conclusion: 随机过程上确界与泛链为随机投影降维提供了统一且更强的理论解释，并成功把随机投影扩展到线性不等式约束场景，通过保持对偶可行性的对偶证书投影实现对偶界的近似保持。

Abstract: Random projection is a preprocessing technique for optimization that reduces the number of linear equality constraints by replacing them with random linear combinations while approximately preserving the optimal value. In this paper, we emphasize the role of suprema of random processes, and in particular generic chaining, in explaining this phenomenon. By combining Lagrangian duality with generic chaining, we obtain shorter proofs and sharper guarantees that apply beyond the typical polyhedral settings based on the Johnson--Lindenstrauss lemma. More importantly, this perspective allows us to extend random projection to problems with linear inequality constraints. Although randomly projected inequality constraints generally define neither a relaxation nor a restriction of the original feasible region, we show that random projection can nevertheless approximately preserve the dual bound. The key is a new construction for randomly projecting dual certificates that preserves dual feasibility and differs from existing constructions motivated by the Johnson--Lindenstrauss lemma.

</details>


### [254] [Nonmonotone Coderivative-Based Newton Methods for Nonsmooth Optimization with Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09364)
*Sina Kazemdehbashi,Yanchao Liu,Boris S. Mordukhovich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于余导数的牛顿型框架，用于求解无约束与有约束的非光滑优化问题。方法利用广义Hessian（次梯度映射的余导数）、自适应正则化策略与混合自适应非单调线搜索，在标准假设下实现全局收敛与局部超线性收敛，并在Lasso、逻辑Lasso和SVM等问题上验证了其效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿型算法因局部收敛速度快而成为求解优化问题的高效方法，但将其推广到非光滑优化存在困难，核心挑战在于难以融入二阶信息。为此，作者希望借助变分分析与广义微分的工具，构建适用于非光滑问题的牛顿框架。

Method: 提出基于余导数的牛顿框架，采用定义为次梯度映射余导数的广义Hessian，适用于C^{1,1}函数以及含扩展实值分量的凸复合优化问题。算法引入自适应正则化策略以处理广义Hessian为半正定的问题；同时设计混合自适应非单调（HAN）线搜索作为全局化技术。框架进一步通过前向-后向包络扩展到凸复合优化，以处理目标函数光滑部分是否强凸的情形。给出精确与不精确两种版本算法。

Result: 在标准假设下，精确与不精确版本算法均具有全局收敛性；当相关次梯度映射满足semismooth*性质时，可实现局部超线性收敛。在Lasso、逻辑Lasso与支持向量机（SVM）问题上的数值实验表明算法高效且鲁棒；与多种成熟的一阶和二阶非光滑优化方法相比，该方法在计算上具有竞争力，同时保持较强的收敛性质。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于余导数的牛顿框架为无约束与有约束非光滑优化提供了统一且有效的求解途径，兼具全局收敛与局部超线性收敛的强理论保证，并在实际问题上表现出良好的效率与鲁棒性，可作为现有非光滑优化方法的有力替代。

Abstract: Newton-type algorithms are among the most effective methods for solving optimization problems because of their rapid local convergence. However, extending these methods to nonsmooth optimization is challenging due to the difficulty of incorporating second-order information. To address this issue, we propose a coderivative-based Newton framework for solving both unconstrained and constrained nonsmooth optimization problems using tools from variational analysis and generalized differentiation. The proposed method employs generalized Hessians, defined as coderivatives of the subgradient mapping, and is applicable to both $C ^{1,1}$ functions and convex composite optimization problems with extended-real-valued components. To enhance robustness, the algorithm incorporates an adaptive regularization strategy that enables it to handle problems whose generalized Hessians are positive semidefinite. In addition, a Hybrid Adaptive Nonmonotone (HAN) line search scheme is developed as a globalization technique to improve global convergence and practical performance. Under standard assumptions, both the exact and inexact versions of the algorithm are globally convergent and achieve local superlinear convergence when the associated subgradient mapping satisfies the semismooth* property. The proposed framework is further extended to convex composite optimization through the forward-backward envelope, allowing it to handle problems with or without strong convexity in the smooth component of the objective function. Numerical experiments on Lasso, logistic Lasso, and support vector machine (SVM) problems demonstrate the efficiency and robustness of the proposed algorithm. Comparisons with several well-established first-order and second-order methods for nonsmooth optimization show that the proposed approach is computationally competitive while maintaining strong convergence properties.

</details>


### [255] [Exact and Efficient Methods for Identifying Carcinogenic Multi-Hit Gene Combinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09420)
*Rick S. H. Willemsen,Teresa L. F. Ho,Tenindra Abeywickrama*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多命中癌症驱动集合覆盖问题（MHCDSCP）中的定价子问题瓶颈，提出基于对偶信息剪枝的深度优先搜索方法，在单CPU上数秒内求得定价问题最优解，从而给出该问题首个精确算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症由约2至9个基因突变（多命中组合）驱动，但候选基因数量庞大，识别这些组合在计算上极具挑战，以往需依赖超级计算机枚举全部或大部分基因组合。近期工作将分类任务建模为MHCDSCP，并用列生成在单CPU上求解，其中反复求解的定价问题成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 将MHCDSCP的定价问题转化为枚举所有可能基因组合的搜索问题，设计专门的深度优先搜索（DFS）算法，并利用列生成产生的对偶信息对搜索空间进行大幅剪枝，从而在单CPU上实现定价问题的最优求解。

Result: 与以往需约150,000个CPU核心的超级计算机进行基因组合枚举的方法相比，该DFS算法在单CPU上数秒内即可将定价问题求解至最优，彻底消除了列生成的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过消除定价瓶颈，本文提出了MHCDSCP的首个精确方法，能够保证找到全局最优解，为多命中癌症驱动组合的识别提供了在普通计算资源上可行且具最优性保证的求解途径。

Abstract: Cancer is driven by an estimated two to nine gene mutations, known as multi-hit combinations. Due to the large number of genes, identifying these multi-hit combinations presents a computationally challenging problem, which has previously required supercomputers to enumerate all or most gene combinations. Recent work formulated this classification task as the Multi-Hit Cancer Driver Set Cover Problem (MHCDSCP) and solved it via column generation on a single commodity CPU, where a pricing problem identifies promising gene combinations (arXiv:2602.22551). The main bottleneck is the pricing problem that has to be solved repeatedly. To solve the pricing problem to optimality, we propose a dedicated depth first search method that enumerates all possible gene combinations. Although enumerating gene combinations with depth first search have been performed on supercomputers with around 150,000 CPU cores (arXiv:2603.16721), our depth first search algorithm solves the pricing problem to optimality within seconds on a single CPU by leveraging dual information to prune large parts of the search space. By eliminating this bottleneck, our approach yields the first exact method for the MHCDSCP, which is guaranteed to find a global optimal solution.

</details>


### [256] [Boundary-aware Reinforcement Learning for Hypercube State Spaces via Deterministic Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09712)
*Lijun Bo,Yijie Huang,Chenhao Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为带反射状态动力学的强化学习建立了连续时间确定性策略梯度框架，提出反射随机系统下的连续时间深度确定性策略梯度算法，并量化了理想连续时间与实际离散采样探索动力学之间的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中许多受控系统（如水库控制等）的状态受限于超立方体等有界区域，其动力学由反射随机微分方程描述。现有连续时间确定性策略梯度理论主要针对无约束或非反射状态，缺乏处理Neumann边界条件（反射边界）的框架与算法。

Method: 在适当正则性假设下，建立值函数与Neumann Bellman方程的联系；引入优势率函数（advantage-rate function），推导确定性策略梯度公式，并证明鞅刻画定理。基于此提出面向反射随机系统的连续时间深度确定性策略梯度算法，其中Neumann边界条件通过软惩罚或硬架构约束实现；同时量化理想连续时间动力学与实际离散采样探索动力学之间的偏差。

Result: 理论上证明鞅刻画定理并给出策略梯度公式；误差分析表明随着时间网格细化与探索噪声消失，离散与连续动力学间误差趋于衰减。水库控制问题实验显示该RL框架有效，且边界感知方法显著降低Neumann边界残差、提升学习稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作将连续时间确定性策略梯度推广至反射随机动力学，为有界状态空间下的强化学习提供了理论工具与算法实现，边界感知机制是提升学习稳定性与边界精度的关键。

Abstract: We develop a continuous-time deterministic policy gradient framework for reinforcement learning with reflected state dynamics, where the state process is governed by a controlled reflected stochastic differential equation on a hypercube. Under suitable regularity assumptions, we establish the connection between the value function and the Neumann Bellman equation, introduce an advantage-rate function that yields a deterministic policy gradient formula, and prove the martingale characterization theorem. Motivated by these theoretical results, we propose a continuous-time deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm for reflected stochastic systems, in which the Neumann boundary condition is imposed via either soft penalization or hard architectural constraint. We further quantify the discrepancy between the ideal continuous-time dynamics and the discretely sampled exploratory dynamics executed in practice, showing that the error decays as the time grid is refined and exploration noise vanishes. Our experiments on reservoir control problems illustrate the effectiveness of the RL framework, highlighting that boundary-aware methods substantially reduce Neumann boundary residuals and enhance learning stability.

</details>


### [257] [Curvature bounds for the cost of coherence in conditional optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09738)
*Xiaozhen Wang,Xiang Zhou,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究给定双参数条件概率测度族的联合粒子实现中，积分逐方向动能极小值与最小期望Dirichlet能量之间的差距，并在正则余面积假设下给出曲率下界，证明最小超额消失当且仅当Lie括号曲率处处为零，且在非零曲率中心处以 r^4 阶增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画一族条件概率测度在联合粒子实现下"逐方向最优"与"整体能量最优"之间的能量差距，并揭示该差距与参数空间中Lie括号曲率的内在联系。

Method: 结合局部最优传输速度的加权-Poisson刻画、精确能量-残差恒等式、弱Stokes公式以及显式传输构造；在具有紧致连通纤维与星形参数域的正则余面积假设下进行分析，并通过拟合电力需求数据的两相环面模型进行数值验证。

Result: 对任意实现给定光滑正条件测度的Sobolev映射律，导出曲率下界；最小超额在参数域上Lie括号曲率处处为零时恰好消失，而在非零曲率中心处以 r^4 阶（r→0，半边长 r 的方块、非归一化面积测度）增长；数值实验中显式耦合呈四阶行为，径向传输使有序构造的超额约减半。

Conclusion: 逐方向动能极小与联合实现的期望Dirichlet能量之间的差距由Lie括号曲率控制，曲率零处无超额、非零处呈四阶标度，为条件测度族的联合实现提供了曲率型定量刻画。

Abstract: We study the gap between the integrated direction-wise kinetic minima and the minimum expected Dirichlet energy of a joint particle realization of a prescribed two-parameter family of conditional probability measures. Under regular coarea assumptions with compact connected fibres and a star-shaped parameter domain, we derive a curvature lower bound valid for every admissible law of Sobolev maps realizing the prescribed smooth positive conditional measures. Our method combines the weighted-Poisson characterization of local optimal-transport velocities with an exact energy-residual identity, a weak Stokes formula, and explicit transport constructions. The minimum excess vanishes exactly when the Lie-bracket curvature vanishes throughout the parameter domain, whereas at a nonzero-curvature centre it is of order $r^4$ as $r\to0$ on squares of half-side length $r$ with unnormalized area measure. In a two-phase torus model fitted to electricity-demand data, numerical tests illustrate this quartic order for explicit couplings, with radial transport approximately halving the ordered construction's excess on the tested small squares.

</details>


### [258] [Necessary Conditions for Free End-Time Sweeping Process Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09750)
*Piernicola Bettiol,Jérémy Rouot,Paulin Bruneau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究由sweeping process动力学支配的自由终端时间最优控制问题，在状态方程含法锥项导致非Lipschitz、不连续的情形下，采用扰动技术与惩罚方法，分别针对时间Lipschitz和数据可测两种设定，导出了包含横截条件的必要最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优控制理论依赖状态方程的Lipschitz正则性，而sweeping process状态方程中法锥项引入对状态变量的不连续性，使标准假设失效，轨迹分析与最优性条件推导均需更精细的工具；同时自由终端时间问题还需处理额外的横截条件，因此有必要在非Lipschitz、不连续框架下建立新的必要最优性条件。

Method: 结合扰动技术与针对sweeping process定制的惩罚方法；移动集建模为满足约束资格的有限个不等式定义集之交（含正线性无关条件及Gramian矩阵对角占优性质），并引入约束资格的局部版本；分别处理动力学对时间Lipschitz连续与仅可测两种情形，对可测情形借助最大化Hamiltonian的子/超本质值概念刻画横截条件。

Result: 在Lipschitz情形下，横截条件由与最大化Hamiltonian几乎处处相等的有界变差函数表达；在可测情形下，通过子/超本质值给出合理解释，得到更强的横截条件，且这些条件还包含反映协态弧与最优端点处移动集法锥相互作用的附加项。结果适用于包括多面体等无界移动集的广泛问题，并通过两个简单示例说明所得横截条件的相关性。

Conclusion: 所得最优性系统将近期关于经典自由终端时间问题的结果推广并精化至sweeping process的非Lipschitz、不连续框架，为含一般端点约束和时变数据的自由终端时间最优控制问题提供了更完整、更强的必要最优性条件。

Abstract: This paper derives necessary optimality conditions for free end-time optimal control problems governed by sweeping process dynamics. In contrast to classical optimal control problems, the presence of the normal cone term in the state equation introduces discontinuities with respect to the state variable and prevents the standard Lipschitz regularity assumptions typically used in optimal control theory. As a result, both the analysis of trajectories and the formulation of optimality conditions require refined tools. We address problems with general endpoint constraints and time-dependent data, considering both the case in which the dynamics are Lipschitz continuous in time and the more challenging setting of merely measurable time dependence. Special attention is devoted to the additional transversality conditions associated with the free end-time variable. In the Lipschitz case, these conditions are expressed in terms of a function of bounded variation that coincides almost everywhere with the maximized Hamiltonian. In the measurable case, we show that a suitable interpretation can be obtained via the notions of sub- and super-essential values of the maximized Hamiltonian, recently introduced in the literature, leading to strengthened transversality conditions. These conditions also involve an additional term reflecting the interaction between the costate arc and the normal cone to the moving set at the optimal endpoints. Our approach combines suitable perturbation techniques with a penalization method tailored to sweeping processes. The moving set is modelled as a finite intersection of inequality-defined sets satisfying suitable constraint qualifications, including a positive linear independence condition and a diagonal dominance property of the associated Gramian matrix. We also introduce a local version of the constraint qualification condition. Our results apply to a broad class of problems, including those involving unbounded moving sets such as polyhedra. The resulting optimality system extends and sharpens recent results for classical free end-time problems to the non-Lipschitz, discontinuous framework of sweeping processes. The relevance of the transversality conditions obtained in this article is illustrated through two simple examples.

</details>


### [259] [Sparsity in control problems: Recent trends and open challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09787)
*Luca Ballotta,Geethu Josephb,Waseem Abbas,Sophie M. Fosson,Chandra R. Murthy,Masaaki Nagahara,Alex Olshevsky,Kyle Poe,René Vidal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统梳理了控制领域中在资源受限条件下处理稀疏性问题的理论、算法与应用，将碎片化的研究方向整合为统一叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制工程中始终存在高性能目标与感知、执行、控制资源有限之间的矛盾，这种张力常被形式化为带稀疏约束的设计问题。然而控制领域对稀疏性的研究分散在多个针对特定场景的独立工作线中，缺乏统一整合。

Method: 采用综述方法，汇集多条相关研究线索，涵盖执行器调度、最小能量可控性、最大免操作控制（maximum hands-off control）以及在线凸优化等控制问题，并提炼其理论基础与算法的共同主线。

Result: 构建了处理控制场景中稀疏性问题的连贯且结构化的叙事框架，把原本分散的问题与解决方案加以整合，并梳理出共同的理论与算法脉络。

Conclusion: 该综述不仅整合了稀疏控制问题的知识与方法，还讨论了开放问题与未来研究方向，其动机来自控制系统日益增长的复杂性及其与普适计算、分布式学习、网络物理安全、生态足迹等关键领域的关联。

Abstract: As in all fields of engineering, control engineers constantly face a dichotomy between achieving high performance and the limited availability of resources for sensing, actuation, and control. This tension often translates into formalizing design problems with sparsity constraints; that is, the design involves decision variables whose number, spatial reach, temporal spread, or other physical expressions are much smaller than in the ideal scenario of unconstrained optimization of a task-related performance metric. Even though this fundamental limitation underlines a plethora of methodological approaches and application domains, the control literature is fragmented into several lines of work tailored to specific scenarios. This survey fills the gap by bringing together several lines of work on handling sparsity in relevant control problems such as actuator scheduling, minimal-energy controllability, maximum hands-off control, and online convex optimization, and weaving a red thread through theoretical foundations and algorithms. Beyond establishing a coherent, structured narrative of problems and solutions for handling sparsity in control scenarios, we discuss open areas and relevant directions for future research, motivated by the growing complexity of control systems and their relations with key domains such as pervasive computing, distributed learning, cyberphysical security, and ecological footprint.

</details>


### [260] [Stochastic Optimal Control of Decoupled Reflected FBSDEs: A Variational Approach with Residual Reflection Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09867)
*Hanane Ben-Gherbal,Omar Kebiri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带反射约束（解耦正倒向随机微分方程，反射作用于倒向分量）的随机控制问题的必要最优性条件，成本泛函由反射倒向随机微分方程的初值定义。作者采用惩罚法、尖峰变分与对偶论证，得到惩罚变分关系，并在取极限后给出Pontryagin型反射变分不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射项的非光滑性使经典变分方法无法直接应用，因此需要建立适用于此类反射约束随机控制问题的必要最优性条件。

Method: 结合惩罚方法、尖峰变分（spike variations）与对偶论证，推导惩罚变分关系；其核心是将反射贡献隔离为一个残差项，从而避免对非光滑惩罚算子求导，同时保留类似经典随机最大值原理的Hamiltonian伴随结构；最后通过取极限得到反射变分不等式。

Result: 得到Pontryagin型反射变分不等式，其中障碍（obstacle）效应通过一个与反射项相关的残差贡献被显式表示。

Conclusion: 该工作为带反射约束的随机控制问题提供了保持Hamiltonian伴随结构的必要最优性条件，并明确刻画了障碍反射带来的残差影响。

Abstract: This paper studies necessary optimality conditions for stochastic control problems governed by decoupled forward--backward stochastic differential equations with reflection constraints on the backward component. The cost functional is defined through the initial value of a reflected BSDE whose lower obstacle depends on the forward state. The nonsmooth nature of the reflection term prevents the direct application of classical variational methods.
  Using a penalization approach, spike variations, and duality arguments, we derive a penalized variational relation. A key feature of our method is to isolate the reflection contribution into a residual term, avoiding the differentiation of the nonsmooth penalization operator while preserving a Hamiltonian adjoint structure analogous to the classical stochastic maximum principle. Passing to the limit yields a reflected variational inequality of Pontryagin type, where the obstacle effect is explicitly represented through a residual contribution associated with the reflection term.

</details>


### [261] [Two Answers on Heavy-Ball Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09912)
*Dmitry Pasechnyuk-Vilensky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究重球法在光滑强凸函数上的两个问题：Polyak调参下的维度一致最坏情形指数速率刻画，以及任意步长与动量下参数平面的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重球法在光滑强凸函数上的收敛速率与参数选择长期存在未明区域，Polyak调参的维度一致最坏情形速率尚无精确确定，任意步长与动量下Lyapunov区域与周期区域之间的参数区域此前保持开放。

Method: 对Polyak调参，将维度一致最坏情形指数速率表述为可插值旋转几何序列混合的最大增长因子，结合精确多项式证书、有理参数化、临界三循环处五次域精确解与验证区间延拓进行分类；对任意参数，用两个极端二次型特征多项式间夹角Θ(ω)描述相图，并给出严格证书。

Result: 最优混合构成四个代数族；两个频率即足够，五维中一个函数在每个视界达到该速率，上下界相差常数因子；方法在整个类上收敛当且仅当κ<9+4√5。相图中，Θ(ω)>ω/2时收敛，Θ(2πj/k)≤π/k时存在k周期轨道，其余为由Farey分数组织的旋转数间隙；单点严格证书可界定速率，间隙第一层中Farey分数扇形在共振端点累积，携带多谐波周期轨道且具显式扇形滞后不等式。

Conclusion: 论文精确刻画了Polyak调参重球法的维度一致最坏情形速率及其收敛阈值，并首次刻画了Lyapunov与周期区域之间开放参数区域的相图结构，通过严格证书实现阈值处收敛认证。

Abstract: We answer two questions about the heavy-ball method on smooth strongly convex functions. The first concerns Polyak's tuning. Its dimension-uniform worst-case exponential rate equals the largest growth factor of an interpolable mixture of rotating geometric sequences, and we determine this factor for every condition number. The optimal mixtures form four algebraic families; the classification combines exact polynomial certificates, a rational parametrization, an exact solution in a quintic number field at the critical three-cycle, and verified interval continuation. Two frequencies suffice, one function in dimension five attains the rate at every horizon, the upper and lower bounds differ by constant factors, and the method converges on the whole class if and only if $κ<9+4\sqrt5$, the threshold located by Badithela and Seiler. The second question concerns arbitrary step sizes and momenta, in particular the regions of the parameter plane between the computed Lyapunov and cycle regions. We describe the phase diagram through the angle $Θ(ω)$ between the characteristic polynomials of the two extreme quadratics. The method converges when $Θ(ω)>ω/2$, has a $k$-periodic orbit when $Θ(2πj/k)\leπ/k$, and the remaining regions are rotation-number gaps organized by Farey fractions. A single strict certificate at one growth exponent bounds the rate, so convergence can be certified at the threshold exponent. In the first level of the gaps, fans of Farey fractions accumulating at a resonant endpoint carry periodic orbits with several harmonics, explicit fan-lag inequalities certify convergence, and both behaviors occur in the regions left open before.

</details>


### [262] [Distances on Finite Constraint Systems and LICQ Radii in Nonlinear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09991)
*Kohei Hatano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个度量框架，用类型化约束函数的集合刻画数学规划形式，并在此定义形式之间的距离，以量化依赖形式的性质之鲁棒性。以LICQ为具体案例，定义点态LICQ半径并给出显式公式，刻画了增删单个约束的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学规划常通过代数改写、约束函数扰动、约束增删等操作被修改；有些改动保持可行集，有些则体现新的建模要求从而改变优化问题本身。因此需要一个能度量“依赖形式的性质”在形式改动下稳健程度的统一框架，弥补现有研究缺乏形式层距离度量的问题。

Method: 将形式建模为约束函数的类型化集合（typed collections of constraint functions）；在固定类型基数类内，通过最优匹配同类型约束并在底层函数空间范数下度量其差异，定义形式间距离。以此为工具研究LICQ：定义点态LICQ半径，即从可行形式到“在指定点仍可行但在该点不满足LICQ”的形式集合的距离，并在适当假设下推导显式公式。

Result: 在适当假设下得到点态LICQ半径的显式公式，并刻画了新增或删除单个等式约束或不等式约束对LICQ半径的影响。

Conclusion: 该度量框架可量化依赖形式的性质（如LICQ）对形式改动的鲁棒性，LICQ半径提供了可计算的具体指标，并揭示单约束增删的作用规律，为形式修正与建模选择提供理论依据。

Abstract: Mathematical programming formulations are routinely modified through algebraic reformulations, perturbations of constraint functions, and the addition or removal of constraints. Some of these modifications preserve the feasible set, whereas others reflect newly imposed modeling requirements and therefore change the optimization problem itself. This paper develops a metric framework for quantifying the robustness of formulation-dependent properties. Formulations are modeled as typed collections of constraint functions. Within each fixed typed-cardinality class, their distance is defined by optimally matching constraints of the same type and measuring the resulting discrepancies in a norm on the underlying function space.
  As a concrete demonstration of the framework, we study the Linear Independence Constraint Qualification (LICQ). We introduce the pointwise LICQ radius, defined as the distance from a feasible formulation to the set of formulations that remain feasible at a prescribed point but fail LICQ there. Under suitable assumptions, we derive an explicit formula for this radius and characterize the effect of adding a single equality or inequality constraint.

</details>


### [263] [A Nonlocal Stochastic Optimal Control with Elliptic-Type Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10029)
*Stefana-Lucia Anita,Luca Di Persio*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究控制通过椭圆光滑算子 $\mathcal{S}=(I-\Delta)^{-1}$ 作用的非局部随机最优控制问题，证明最优控制存在并建立 Pontryagin 型最大值原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制以非局部方式（经椭圆光滑算子）影响 Fokker-Planck 方程的系数，此类问题在种群动力学等场景中有意义，但缺乏最优控制的存在性与最大值原理刻画。

Method: 状态由受控扩散的分布刻画，等价于系数依赖 $\mathcal{S}u$ 的受控 Fokker-Planck 方程；用变分法直接方法证明最优控制存在性；通过尖峰变分（spike variations）并利用算子 $\mathcal{S}$ 的精细性质推导 Pontryagin 型最大值原理。

Result: 证明最优控制的存在性，得到 Pontryagin 型最大值原理，并给出最优控制的逐点极小化规则。

Conclusion: 在种群动力学情形下讨论了该最优控制问题的实际意义。

Abstract: We study a nonlocal stochastic optimal control problem in which the control acts through the elliptic smoothing operator $\mathcal{S}=(I-Δ)^{-1}$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$. The state is described by the law of a controlled diffusion, equivalently by a controlled Fokker-Planck equation with coefficients depending on $\mathcal{S}u$. We prove the existence of an optimal control by the direct method in the calculus of variations. We then derive a Pontryagin-type maximum principle by spike variations, relying on sharp properties of the operator $\mathcal{S}$. The result yields a pointwise minimisation rule for the optimal control. We discuss the meaning of the optimal control problem in the case of population dynamics.

</details>


### [264] [Breaking the Odd Cycle: Regularization and First-Order Optimization for the Maximum $s$-Plex Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10205)
*Eleonora Bellesso,Immanuel M. Bomze,Francesco Rinaldi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于一种新的正则化连续建模，避免了最大 s-plex 问题的伪解，并设计了块坐标一阶方法求解该问题，在理论上证明了算法的收敛性与有限支撑识别性质，实验表明正则化可避免原三次模型的支撑识别失败并快速找到高质量 s-plex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大 s-plex 问题在原三次连续建模下存在伪解，导致支撑识别失败，影响解的质量与算法可靠性。为此需要一种能规避伪解的连续建模与相应的高效求解方法。

Method: 提出一种新的正则化连续建模以避免最大 s-plex 问题的伪解；在此基础上设计定制的块坐标一阶方法（block-coordinate first-order method），将标准单纯形上的 away-step Frank-Wolfe 更新与分数 b-匹配多面体（fractional b-matching polytope）上的线性优化相结合。

Result: 理论上建立了辅助变量的有限稳定化（finite stabilization）、收敛到稳定点（stationary points）以及在适当条件下的有限支撑识别（finite support identification）。在标准基准图上的数值实验表明，正则化在实践中同样避免了原三次模型的支撑识别失败，并使所提方法能快速找到高质量的 s-plex。

Conclusion: 正则化连续建模有效规避伪解与支撑识别失败；结合 away-step Frank-Wolfe 与分数 b-匹配线性优化的块坐标一阶方法具有良好理论保证与实用效率，可快速获得高质量 s-plex。

Abstract: Building on a new regularized continuous formulation which avoids spurious solutions of the maximum $s$-plex problem, we develop a tailored block-coordinate first-order method that combines away-step Frank-Wolfe updates on the standard simplex with linear optimization over the fractional $b$-matching polytope. We establish finite stabilization of the auxiliary variables, convergence to stationary points, and finite support identification under suitable conditions. Numerical experiments on standard benchmark graphs show that, also in practice, regularization avoids the support-identification failures observed with the original cubic formulation and enables the proposed method to quickly find high-quality $s$-plexes.

</details>


### [265] [First-order $Γ$-expansion for entropic optimal transport with non-degenerate costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10257)
*Luca Nenna,Brendan Pass,Paul Pegon,Louis Tocquec*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文将熵正则化最优传输(EOT)改写为关于对偶间隙能量对应的Gibbs测度的相对熵最小化问题，利用Laplace方法的温和推广，在对非退化基代价的合适假设下，证明了Gibbs测度收敛并给出了ε→0时EOT的一阶Γ-展开，从而同时得到熵选择原理与EOT代价的o(ε)展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EOT是最优传输的核心工具，兼具理论洞察与Sinkhorn算法的高效计算。已有工作关注正则化参数ε消失时的极限行为，但现有结果对更一般的非退化基代价尚不完整，作者希望推广并系统刻画EOT的一阶渐近展开。

Method: 将对偶间隙能量引入，把EOT重写为相对于Gibbs测度的相对熵最小化；采用Laplace方法的温和扩展，在关于对偶间隙、接触集与边缘分布的合适假设下，证明Gibbs测度的收敛性，并推导非退化基代价情形下的 Γ-展开。

Result: 在所述假设下证明了Gibbs测度的收敛，得到EOT的首阶 Γ-展开，同时给出熵选择原理以及EOT代价精确到o(ε)的展开式。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了已有的EOT渐近分析，为更一般的非退化基代价提供了统一的熵选择原理与代价展开框架。

Abstract: Entropy-regularized optimal transport (EOT) has become a key tool in optimal transport, providing both theoretical insight and efficient computation via the Sinkhorn algorithm. We study the first-order \(Γ\)-expansion of EOT as the regularization parameter \(\varepsilon\) vanishes, by rewriting EOT as the minimization of a relative entropy with respect to a Gibbs measure \(\gibbs\) associated with the duality gap energy. Using a mild extension of the Laplace method, we prove the convergence of the Gibbs measures and derive this \(Γ\)-expansion for non-degenerate ground costs, under suitable assumptions on the duality gap, the contact set and the marginals. This yields at the same time an entropic selection principle and an expansion of the EOT cost up to \(o(\varepsilon)\) for this class of ground costs, generalizing existing results.

</details>


### [266] [Sharp Initialization Guarantees for the Heavy-Ball Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10369)
*Kang Liu,Quanen Wu,Zhan Sun,Mengxiao Chen,Suyan Li,Bohao Qu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究重球法的初始化问题，刻画了可保证收敛的初始化邻域，并据此提出基于梯度的预热启动规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重球法在极小点附近能加速收敛，但在一般强凸目标上可能进入持续振荡，因此需要明确如何初始化才能可靠地实现其局部加速优势。

Method: 在仅依赖曲率与正则性界的前提下，研究可保证收敛的初始化邻域；推导该邻域随动量趋近于一而收缩的规律；对Lipschitz连续Hessian给出半径与阻尼同阶的结论；通过匹配的上下界证明初始化要求无法在函数类上一致放宽；进而提出基于当前梯度的启动规则，即先用有限步梯度下降预热进入保证区域再切换为重球法。

Result: 可保证收敛的初始化邻域随动量趋近于一而收缩，在Lipschitz连续Hessian下其半径与阻尼同阶；匹配的上下界表明该初始化要求不能在函数类上一致放宽；采用对强凸二次函数最优的参数时，切换后迭代满足加速的局部渐近收敛率界。

Conclusion: 重球法的局部加速需要通过合适的初始化才能可靠获得；基于梯度的有限步梯度下降预热是进入保证区域的有效启动规则，并能实现加速的局部渐近收敛率。

Abstract: The heavy-ball method can accelerate convergence near the minimizer, but it may also enter persistent oscillations on general strongly convex objectives. How should it be initialized to reliably realize this local advantage? We study the initialization neighborhood in which convergence can be guaranteed using only curvature and regularity bounds. This neighborhood shrinks as momentum approaches one; for Lipschitz-continuous Hessians, its radius is of the same order as damping. Matching upper and lower bounds show that this initialization requirement cannot be relaxed uniformly over the function class. We then derive a startup rule based on the current gradient: a finite gradient-descent warm-up enters the guaranteed region before switching to heavy-ball. With parameters optimal for strongly convex quadratics, the subsequent iterates satisfy an accelerated local asymptotic rate bound. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/HeavyBall-811D/.

</details>


### [267] [An Optimal Second-Order Algorithm for Finite-Sum Optimization under Average Smoothness and Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10495)
*Artem Tsedenov,Dmitry Kovalev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限和凸优化问题（分量函数二阶连续可微、Hessian 均方 Lipschitz），提出随机二阶算法达到 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n+n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$ 二阶 oracle 复杂度，并证明在随机二阶线性张成假设下同类问题的下界为 $\Omega(n+n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$，上下界在对数因子内匹配，解决了该问题类最优复杂度的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限和凸优化中，随机二阶方法的最优 oracle 复杂度尚未明确；已有上界与下界之间存在缺口，需要确定该问题类的真正最优复杂度。

Method: 针对目标为凸、$n$ 个分量函数二阶连续可微且 Hessian 满足均方 Lipschitz 的有限和优化问题，设计随机二阶算法；下界分析基于随机二阶版本的一阶线性张成假设（linear span assumption），构造困难实例证明任何满足该假设的随机二阶算法的复杂度下界。

Result: 所提算法以期望 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n+n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$ 次二阶 oracle 调用找到期望精度 $\epsilon$ 的近似解；同时证明下界 $\Omega(n+n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$。两者在对数因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 本文确立了该类有限和凸优化问题的随机二阶最优复杂度为 $\Theta(n+n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$（忽略对数因子），解决了关于该问题类最优复杂度的公开问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a finite-sum optimization problem, where the objective function is convex, the $n$ component functions are twice continuously differentiable and their Hessians are mean-square Lipschitz. Our contribution is twofold. First, we develop a stochastic algorithm, which requires $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n + n^{6/7}/ε^{2/7})$ second-order oracle calls in expectation to find an approximate solution to the problem with the expected accuracy $ε$. Second, we establish a lower complexity bound of $Ω(n + n^{6/7}/ε^{2/7})$ for any stochastic second-order algorithm satisfying a stochastic second-order version of the first-order linear span assumption, which is widely adopted in the optimization literature. Our lower and upper complexity bounds match up to logarithmic factors and thus resolve an important open question of establishing the optimal complexity of this problem class.

</details>


### [268] [Decentralized SGD under Heavy-Tailed Noise: Optimal Convergence Rates and the Role of Gradient Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10527)
*Aleksandar Armacki,Haoyuan Cai,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明在重尾噪声下，采用裁剪（clipping）的分散式SGD（clipped DSGD）在光滑非凸目标上可达阶最优收敛速率，并实现智能体数量的线性加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾噪声广泛存在于现代机器学习中，梯度裁剪与归一化在集中式设定下已有较好理解，但在分散式设定下研究不足：非线性作用同时影响优化与共识。已有分散式非凸工作显示裁剪速率次优、归一化需依赖局部动量或小批量才收敛，因此本文追问：使用非线性算子的基线分散式方法能否在重尾噪声下达到最优收敛速率。

Method: 针对有界p阶矩噪声（p∈(1,2]）下的光滑非凸代价，采用clipped DSGD，即对局部梯度施加裁剪非线性。关键技术是对共识间隙（consensus gap）进行精细分析，利用裁剪的结构特性，将网络效应归入高阶项。

Result: clipped DSGD在高概率和期望意义下均达到阶最优速率，并建立了智能体数量的线性加速，这是据作者所知首次在带裁剪的分散式方法中实现。数值实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了分散式设定下裁剪与归一化的重要区别：归一化DSGD可能不收敛，而裁剪保留了梯度幅值信息，使DSGD既收敛又阶最优。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed noise has been widely observed in modern machine learning, motivating the use of methods like gradient clipping and normalization. While these methods are well understood in centralized settings, much less is known in decentralized ones, where applying a nonlinearity to local gradients affects both optimization and consensus. Recent works on decentralized non-convex optimization have studied both clipping and normalization under heavy-tailed noise, with clipping yielding suboptimal rates and normalization needing local momentum or mini-batches to converge. This raises the question: can a baseline decentralized method using a nonlinearity achieve optimal convergence rates under heavy-tailed noise? We answer affirmatively with clipped decentralized SGD ($\mathtt{DSGD}$). For smooth non-convex costs under bounded $p$-th moment noise, $p \in (1,2]$, we show that clipped $\mathtt{DSGD}$ achieves order-optimal rates both with high probability and in expectation. Moreover, we establish a linear speed-up in the number of agents, which, to our knowledge, has not been shown for decentralized methods with clipping. The key technical ingredient is a sharp analysis of the consensus gap that exploits the structure of clipping, relegating network effects to higher-order terms. Our results highlight an important distinction between clipping and normalization in decentralized settings: while normalized $\mathtt{DSGD}$ can fail to converge, clipping retains magnitude information, enabling $\mathtt{DSGD}$ to be convergent and order-optimal. Numerical experiments validate our theory.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [Trust-Region Optimization for Smooth Potential-Interaction Energies in Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08883)
*You Wan,Ting Gao,Jinqiao Duan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究Wasserstein空间中光滑势-相互作用能量的信赖域优化方法，具有二阶信息利用、L²(ρ)步长半径与Steihaug-Toint子求解器，并给出全局收敛性与复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找相互作用粒子的低能构型与近似概率分布问题可归结为Wasserstein空间中势-相互作用能量的最小化。这类能量通常非凸，因此需要利用二阶信息，同时控制局部近似的可靠性。

Method: 在有限二阶矩概率测度的Wasserstein空间上，对光滑势-相互作用能量进行信赖域优化：沿pushforward曲线构造二次模型，采用L²(ρ)步长半径，使用带显式自伴二阶变分算子的Steihaug-Toint子求解器，并通过比率检验决定接受与半径更新。

Result: 在能量下界与势/相互作用核Hessian全局有界的假设下，证明目标函数非增、Wasserstein梯度范数趋于零，并在O(ε⁻²)次外层试验（含被拒试验）内达到ε-稳定点；若势二次强制，则每个弱聚点均稳定。对经验测度，迭代等价于L²(ρ_N)内积下的有限维信赖域法，当初始目标间隙一致有界时，复杂度常数与粒子数和维数无关。数值实验包括光滑软粒子能量、非高斯目标的MMD最小化、组件消融及粒子数与维数缩放研究。

Conclusion: 该信赖域框架为Wasserstein空间中非凸势-相互作用能量最小化提供了具有全局收敛与复杂度保证的优化方法，且在经验测度情形下复杂度常数不受粒子数与维数影响，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Finding low-energy configurations of interacting particles and approximating probability distributions lead to the minimization of potential-interaction energies in Wasserstein space. These energies can be nonconvex, making it important to exploit second-order information while controlling the reliability of local approximations. We study trust-region optimization of smooth potential-interaction energies on the Wasserstein space of probability measures with finite second moment. The method uses a quadratic model along pushforward curves, an $L^2(ρ)$ step radius, and a Steihaug-Toint subsolver with an explicit self-adjoint second-variation operator. A ratio test determines acceptance and guides the radius update.
