Under a lower energy bound and globally bounded Hessians of the potential and interaction kernel, we prove that the objective is nonincreasing, the Wasserstein-gradient norms converge to zero, and an $\varepsilon$-stationary iterate is reached within $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ total outer trials, including rejected trials. If the potential is quadratically coercive, every weak accumulation point is stationary. The analysis applies to arbitrary initial measures with finite second moment. For empirical measures, the iteration is a finite-dimensional trust-region method in the $L^2(ρ_N)$ inner product, with complexity constants independent of particle number and dimension when the initial objective gaps are uniformly bounded. Numerical experiments include a smooth soft-particle energy, maximum-mean-discrepancy minimization for non-Gaussian targets, component ablations, and scaling studies in particle number and dimension.

</details>


### [270] [Careful Judge: Safe and Efficient Human-AI Collaborative Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09043)
*Chenyu Zhang,Rachel Luo,Boyi Li,Anjali Parashar,Marco Pavone,Apoorva Sharma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出CARE框架，通过校准的自适应纠偏与升级机制，在保证安全的前提下持续从人工反馈中学习，实现更高自动化并减少人工查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协同决策中人工审核可防止不安全AI决策，但每次人工判断成本高昂；将AI弃权后的人工介入仅视为一次性兜底，会错失提升未来自动化程度的机会；而让AI自适应地从选择性查询的人工反馈中学习，又会破坏针对旧模型校准的安全护栏。

Method: CARE（calibrated adaptive rectification and escalation）为端到端流水线，结合AI模型与人工审核者，保证安全且与人类对齐的决策，同时持续从人工反馈学习以提高自动化、减少查询。其核心为新型自适应校准模块，可为任意纠偏模块在每个时间步提供风险控制保证；框架具有原则性、通用性、模块化，适用于任意黑盒AI模型。

Result: 理论上证明：当AI模型训练良好且人机错位具有清晰结构时，CARE可提升查询效率。在涵盖驾驶、语言和机器人三个领域的四个安全关键真实数据集上，CARE在实现人类对齐决策的同时，相较基线减少25%–81%的人工查询。

Conclusion: CARE在保持安全护栏与人类对齐的同时，通过自适应校准与持续学习实现更高自动化并显著降低人工查询成本，为安全关键场景下的人机协同决策提供了通用且模块化的解决方案。

Abstract: In human-AI collaborative decision making, human review can prevent unsafe AI decisions, but each human judgment is costly. Treating human intervention after AI abstention as a one-off fallback misses the opportunity to improve future AI decisions for greater automation, yet AI adaptively learning from selectively queried human feedback breaks safety guardrails calibrated for old models. We approach this challenge with CARE---calibrated adaptive rectification and escalation---an end-to-end pipeline that combines AI models and human reviewers to guarantee safe, human-aligned decisions, while continuously learning from human feedback to achieve greater automation with fewer human queries. CARE is principled, general, modular, and works with any black-box AI model. Our novel adaptive calibration module guarantees risk control at every time step for any rectification module. We further show how CARE improves query efficiency when the AI model is well trained and the human-AI misalignment has a clear structure. Experiments on four safety-critical real-world datasets spanning driving, language, and robotics demonstrate that CARE achieves human-aligned decisions while reducing human queries by 25-81% relative to baselines.

</details>


### [271] [Quadratic Weak-to-Strong Generalization in Random Feature Networks via Random Matrix Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09044)
*Deborah Oliveira,Elliot Paquette*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文用随机矩阵理论分析两层随机特征网络中的弱到强泛化现象，证明在ReLU激活和球谐目标下，学生误差是教师误差的平方，达到理论下界，并刻画了不同改进情形之间的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱到强泛化是指用弱教师模型生成的标签训练强学生模型时，学生能泛化得比教师更好。该现象虽有实证观察，但缺乏理论理解，尤其是"模型强度"（宽度）如何影响泛化改进的定量刻画。本文旨在从理论上精确分析这一现象，给出泛化误差的渐近行为和改进程度。

Method: 采用两层随机特征网络，模型强度由网络宽度决定。利用随机矩阵理论的工具，推导最优训练教师和用梯度流训练学生的总体误差的确定性等价（deterministic equivalents）。针对ReLU激活和纯球谐目标，在高斯普适性假设下获得尖锐渐近结果。进一步分析更一般的停止时间和支持多个谐波次数的目标。

Result: 对于ReLU激活和纯球谐目标，学生误差按教师误差的平方缩放，即呈现二次改进（quadratic improvement），达到Medvedev et al (2025)的一般下界。对于更一般停止时间和多谐波次数目标，刻画了弱到强泛化发生的机制，并识别出二次、非二次和无改进之间的转变。

Conclusion: 弱到强泛化在随机特征网络中可被精确刻画：在特定条件下学生可相对教师获得二次误差改进且达到理论下界，而更一般的设置下改进程度取决于停止时间与目标谐波结构，存在清晰的相变边界。

Abstract: Weak-to-strong generalization is the phenomenon where a strong student model trained with labels produced by a weak teacher model is able to generalize better than the teacher. In this paper, we study this phenomenon in two-layer random feature networks where the model strength is determined by its width. Using tools from random matrix theory, we derive deterministic equivalents for the population errors of an optimally trained teacher and a student trained with gradient flow. For ReLU activation and a pure spherical harmonic target, we obtain sharp asymptotics under a Gaussian universality assumption, showing a quadratic improvement: the student error scales as the square of the teacher error. These results attain the general lower bound of Medvedev at al (2025). We also analyze how the student behaves under more general stopping times and targets supported on multiple harmonic degrees, characterizing the regimes in which weak-to-strong generalization occurs and identifying the transition between quadratic, non-quadratic, and no improvement.

</details>


### [272] [Learning Transition Kernels of Jump-Diffusion Processes with Conditional Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09045)
*Yuzhen Zhao,Yating Liu,Quentin Guibert*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究基于条件扩散模型学习时间齐次跳跃-扩散过程的转移核，给出理论误差界并在合成与真实数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是仅凭N条在高频离散时间网格上观测的独立轨迹数据，学习跳跃-扩散过程的转移核并生成新的样本路径，这在实际中具有重要需求。

Method: 方法采用条件扩散模型建模时间齐次跳跃-扩散过程的转移核，基于高频离散观测进行训练以生成新路径。

Result: 理论方面建立了条件得分估计误差以及真实生成与离散观测路径分布间KL散度的非渐近界；数值方面先在合成数据上验证理论并与Gao等（2025）方法对比，再应用于真实数据评估概率预测任务表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为跳跃-扩散过程的条件扩散建模提供了理论保障，并通过合成与真实数据实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning transition kernels for time-homogeneous jump-diffusion processes using conditional diffusion models, with the goal of generating new sample paths from training data consisting of N independent trajectories observed on a high-frequency discrete time grid. On the theoretical side, we establish non-asymptotic bounds for the conditional score estimation error and for the KL divergence between the laws of the true and generated discretely observed paths. On the numerical side, we first evaluate our method on synthetic data to assess the theoretical findings and benchmark its performance against the approach of Gao et al. (2025). We then apply our method to real-world data and investigate its performance on a probabilistic forecasting task.

</details>


### [273] [Covariate-dependent Joint Modeling of Multivariate Ordinal Preferences and Its Connections with Comparison Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09070)
*Yujie Chen,Antik Chakraborty,Anindya Bhadra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对多变量有序数据，提出联合的、依赖协变量的相邻比值马尔可夫随机场模型，避免逐属性建模与成对/列表比较带来的信息粗化，并在归一化常数不可积情形下给出极大似然推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量有序数据（如MovieLens评分、HelpSteer的LLM反馈）常伴随协变量出现。现有方法存在两个缺陷：（a）对属性单独建模而非联合建模；（b）将数据转换为成对或列表式的胜–负比较（如Bradley–Terry、Plackett–Luce）来拟合。二者都对实际观测信息造成粗化。

Method: 提出一种联合的、依赖协变量的相邻比值（consecutive ratio）马尔可夫随机场模型；证明成对或列表比较模型是该联合模型在特定约束下的特例；并针对归一化常数不可解（intractable normalizer）的情形，发展极大似然推断方法。

Result: 所提比较模型可由联合模型的受限形式导出，说明联合建模是对比较模型的推广；联合建模能够改进比较效果。

Conclusion: 联合的协变量依赖MRF建模可缓解有序多变量数据中的信息粗化问题，统一了比较类模型；并在归一化项不可解时仍可实现极大似然推断。

Abstract: Multivariate ordinal data along with covariates are commonly collected in problems ranging from alignment of language models with human preferences, as well as in recommender systems. For example, data sets such as MovieLens contain several movies rated on a scale 1--5 by human users, along with their demographic information such as age or gender. Similarly, data sets such as HelpSteer collect human feedback on several attributes such as "helpfulness" or "verbosity" of LLM response on an ordinal scale, with covariates depending on the LLM prompt--response pairs. Unfortunately, the standard approaches for modeling these data (a) look at the attributes individually rather than jointly, and (b) often convert the data into pairwise or list-wise win--loss comparisons for fitting models such as Bradley--Terry and Plackett--Luce. Both of these lead to a coarsening of what is actually observed, which we address via a joint covariate-dependent consecutive ratio Markov random field model. We also show pairwise or listwise comparison models are obtained under restrictions of our joint model, and that joint modeling improves comparisons. We also develop a maximum likelihood inference procedure even in the presence of an intractable normalizer.

</details>


### [274] [The Impact of Likelihood Tempering on the Limiting Predictive Moments of Variational Bayesian Linear Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09132)
*Ian Zhang,Thibault Randrianarisoa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究了宽贝叶斯神经网络中平均场变分推断的"先验主导"退化问题，通过温度调节似然（等价于将KL项缩放T倍），推导出单隐层线性网络在 T=τ/M^c 调度下预测分布的极限，发现预测均值与方差在不同尺度 c 处发生相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽贝叶斯神经网络中，高斯平均场变分推断存在"先验主导"现象：随着网络宽度M增大，ELBO中KL正则项压倒期望对数似然，使变分预测分布退化为先验预测分布。似然温度调节（将似然取1/T次幂，T<1）等价于将KL项缩放T倍，但温度T应随宽度M以何速率下降才能抵消这种退化、平衡两项，尚不清楚。

Method: 针对带各向同性高斯先验的单隐层线性网络，在 T=τ/M^c（τ,c>0）的调度下推导 M→∞ 时的极限预测分布，并与无温度调节的NNGP后验（精确后验的无限宽极限）进行比较，分析极限预测均值与方差随c的变化行为。

Result: 预测均值与方差在不同尺度发生相变：极限均值在 c=1/2 处偏离先验值（当τ低于显式阈值时），并在 c>1/2 时等于最小二乘预测；极限方差在 c<1 时保持先验值，c=1 时匹配NNGP方差，c>1 时趋于零。选取合适的τ,c，可分别恢复NNGP后验的均值或方差。

Conclusion: 温度随宽度的衰减速率c控制着预测分布的相变行为，均值与方差的相变点不同（分别为c=1/2和c=1），无法用单一温度调度同时匹配NNGP后验的均值与方差；但通过适当选择τ和c，可以有针对性地恢复NNGP后验的期望或方差。

Abstract: In wide Bayesian neural networks, Gaussian mean-field variational inference is prone to "prior dominance": the Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization term of the ELBO outweighs the expected log-likelihood, and the variational predictive distribution collapses to the prior predictive as the width $M$ grows. Tempering the likelihood, by raising it to the power $1/T$ for a temperature $T < 1$, is equivalent to scaling the KL term by $T$. We ask in this paper how fast $T$ must decrease with $M$ to counteract this degeneracy and strike a good balance between the two terms. For single-hidden-layer linear networks with isotropic Gaussian priors, we derive the limiting predictive distribution under schedules of the form $T = τ/M^{c}$, with constants $τ, c > 0$, as $M \to \infty$ and compare it with the untempered neural network Gaussian process (NNGP) posterior, the infinite-width limit of the exact posterior. Our main result is that the predictive expectation and variance undergo phase transitions at different scales: the limiting expectation leaves its prior value at $c = 1/2$, once $τ$ falls below an explicit threshold, and equals the least-squares prediction for $c > 1/2$, whereas the limiting variance keeps its prior value for $c < 1$, matches the NNGP's for $c=1$, and vanishes for $c > 1$. With suitable choices of $τ,c$, one can recover either the NNGP posterior expectation or its variance.

</details>


### [275] [Adjoint-Based Calibration and Optimal Control of Stochastic Multiscale Bioprocess Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09505)
*Keilung Choy,Wei Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个偏差感知的数字孪生校准与控制框架，用SDE建模多尺度生物过程，通过准似然估计与伴随灵敏度分析进行校准，量化截断偏差与不确定性传播，并开发Actor-Simulator算法实现参数更新、实验设计与策略优化，实验验证了校准精度、样本效率与控制性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向生物系统之系统（Bio-SoS）中的多尺度生物过程，数字孪生模型存在由观测稀疏、模型截断等导致的参数偏差与校准不确定性问题，且这些不确定性会传播到价值函数与控制策略性能，需要偏差感知的校准与控制一体化框架来应对。

Method: 采用随机微分方程（SDE）表示数字孪生；用准似然估计加伴随灵敏度分析从稀疏离散观测中校准；用SDE生成元矩展开刻画截断诱导的参数偏差；用前向-后向伴随量化校准不确定性向价值函数与策略性能的传播；基于参数误差分布构建策略导向的自适应实验设计与二阶高斯平均目标的不确定性感知策略优化；提出Actor-Simulator算法联合更新模型参数、选择信息性实验并优化控制策略。

Result: 刻画了探索准则的渐近行为，并推导了优化策略下物理系统的性能；数值研究显示，相比最先进基线，方法在校准精度、样本效率与控制性能上均有改进。

Conclusion: 偏差感知的数字孪生校准与控制框架可有效处理多尺度生物过程中的参数偏差与不确定性传播，Actor-Simulator算法将模型学习、实验设计与控制优化统一起来，具有更优的校准与闭环控制表现。

Abstract: We develop a bias-aware digital-twin calibration and control framework for multiscale bioprocess models within a biological systems-of-systems (Bio-SoS) paradigm. The digital twin is represented by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) model and calibrated from sparse, discrete observations using quasi-likelihood estimation and adjoint sensitivity analysis. SDE generator-based moment expansions characterize truncation-induced parameter bias, while forward-backward adjoints quantify how calibration uncertainty propagates to value functions and policy performance. The resulting parameter-error distribution supports both policy-directed adaptive experimental design and uncertainty-aware policy optimization through a second-order Gaussian-averaged objective. We characterize the asymptotic behavior of the resulting exploration criterion and derive a physical-system performance under the optimized policy. To implement these ideas, we develop an Actor-Simulator algorithm that jointly updates model parameters, selects informative experiments, and optimizes control policies. Numerical studies demonstrate improved calibration accuracy, sample efficiency, and control performance relative to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [276] [Reflected Anchored Langevin Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09522)
*Changwei Tu,Xiaoyu Wang,Yingli Wang,Xicheng Zhang,Lingjiong Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出反射锚定朗之万动力学（RALD）及对应采样算法 RALMC，用于在约束域上从不可微目标分布中采样，并给出了 2-Wasserstein 距离下的显式收敛界与迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一阶朗之万采样算法（如基于反射朗之万动力学离散化的投影朗之万蒙特卡洛）要求对数密度可微，这限制了它们在非光滑或不可微目标上的适用性。

Method: 构建反射锚定朗之万动力学（RALD）：以光滑的锚定参考势为基础，对其反射朗之万动力学的漂移项与噪声协方差同时乘以相同的状态依赖缩放因子，使其收敛到约束域上的不可微目标；再通过带投影的欧拉-丸山离散化得到 RALMC 算法。

Result: 理论证明了 RALMC 在 2-Wasserstein 距离下相对于目标分布的显式收敛界与迭代复杂度；数值实验验证了理论预测并展示了方法的实际性能。

Conclusion: RALD/RALMC 通过锚定参考势与共享缩放因子的设计，突破了可微性假设，为约束域上不可微目标的采样提供了有收敛保证的一阶朗之万方法。

Abstract: First order Langevin algorithms for constrained sampling in machine learning, such as projected Langevin Monte Carlo which are based on discretizations of reflected Langevin dynamics, require differentiable log densities that limits their applicability. This paper introduces reflected anchored Langevin dynamics (RALD), a reflected diffusion that converges to non-differentiable targets on constrained domains. The method uses a smooth anchored reference potential and multiplies the drift and noise covariance of its reflected Langevin dynamics by the same state dependent scaling factor. Its Euler-Maruyama discretization with projection gives reflected anchored Langevin Monte Carlo (RALMC) algorithm. We prove explicit convergence bounds and iteration complexity for RALMC in the 2-Wasserstein distance to the target distribution. Numerical experiments are provided to illustrate the theoretical predictions and the empirical performance of the method.

</details>


### [277] [Unpaired Canonical Correlation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09530)
*Nir Ben-Ari,Ronen Talmon,Uri Shaham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出UCCA，一种在严格无配对样本条件下学习最大相关线性投影的方法，通过建立二次分配问题（QAP）与CCA的理论联系，实现仅用无配对数据最大化真实配对的相关性，并在多模态数据集上显著优于现有无配对对齐基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCA是多视图共享空间学习的基础方法，但严格依赖配对数据，而配对数据往往难以获取甚至完全不可用，这构成重要局限。本文旨在填补传统统计多视图学习与日益增长的无配对数据学习领域之间的空白。

Method: 作者提出Unpaired CCA（UCCA）：首先建立二次分配问题（QAP）与CCA之间的理论联系；基于这些理论洞见，推导出一种仅依赖无配对数据即可最大化相关性的实用方法，学习线性投影以最大化真实潜在配对的相关性，训练过程中无需任何配对样本。

Result: 在真实世界多模态数据集上的验证表明，UCCA在恢复潜在真实相关性方面显著优于近期提出的无配对对齐基线方法。

Conclusion: UCCA是首个在严格无配对设定下学习最大相关投影的方法，填补了传统统计多视图学习与无配对数据学习之间的关键空白。

Abstract: Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA) is a fundamental method for multiview shared space learning. However, its strict reliance on paired data poses a significant limitation, as such data is often difficult to obtain or entirely unavailable. In this paper, we present Unpaired CCA (UCCA), a novel method that learns linear projections to maximize the correlation of the true underlying pairing without access to any paired samples during training. We first establish theoretical results connecting the Quadratic Assignment Problem (QAP) to CCA. Leveraging these theoretical insights, we derive a practical method to maximize correlation exclusively from unpaired data. To the best of our knowledge, UCCA is the first approach to learn maximally correlated projections in a strictly unpaired setting. We validate UCCA on real-world multi-modal datasets, demonstrating that it significantly outperforms recent unpaired alignment baselines in recovering the underlying true correlation. This work fills a critical gap between traditional statistical multiview learning and the growing field of unpaired data learning.

</details>


### [278] [Certified by Abstention: Distribution-Free Guarantees for Chain-of-Thought Verifiers at Small Calibration Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09541)
*Arjun Balaji*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分布无关的选择性保证在CoT验证器上的实际表现，发现基于弃权（abstention）的证书在小样本校准预算下会因低触发率而失效，并提出一种无单调性假设的固定序列证书以提升覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT正确性信号多用AUC比较，但实际部署需要带保证的阈值；作者想探究在数十到几百个标注问题的现实校准预算下，分布无关的选择性保证对CoT验证器能提供什么。

Method: 使用7个开源模型、5种验证器信号、37,000条带评分推理轨迹；分析(α,δ)-有效程序的弃权有效性，给出认证下限（certification floor）与Benjamini-Hochberg保形选择的格条件；提出floor-started固定序列证书。

Result: 风险已知的模拟中，标准证书至多0.3%校准抽样失败，但在触发样本中失败率最高达69%；标准证书要么不返回结果、要么返回过大接受集；不可读残差流探针的覆盖率是可读信号的2-3倍，且交叉拟合重构无法线性恢复该优势；新证书在所有模型-信号对上覆盖率均超过Bonferroni证书，在0.75π₀目标上将覆盖率从0.05提升到0.16。

Conclusion: 小样本校准下证书会因下限而弃权；所提固定序列证书在无单调性假设下有效且覆盖更高，但绝对覆盖仍小；证书无法预见部署后的分布变化，基准偏移和best-of-n选择会使接受轨迹的错误率超过目标，而经验失败频率仍低于δ，因为弃权吸收了失败。

Abstract: Signals that predict whether a chain-of-thought (CoT) trace is correct are compared by AUC, but deploying one requires a threshold with a guarantee. We ask what distribution-free selective guarantees deliver for CoT verifiers at realistic calibration budgets of tens to a few hundred labelled problems, using seven open models, five verifier signals and 37,000 graded traces. The central observation is validity by abstention: an $(α,δ)$-valid procedure that issues a certificate with probability $P_{\rm fire}$ bounds the failure probability of an issued certificate only by $δ/P_{\rm fire}$, so a certificate that rarely fires can be valid and wrong every time it is used. In a simulation with known risk the standard certificate fails in at most 0.3% of calibration draws but in up to 69% of those in which it fires. A certification floor and a lattice condition for Benjamini-Hochberg conformal selection explain why certificates abstain at these budgets, and the data bear them out: the standard certificate returns nothing or a large accepted set, and an unreadable residual-stream probe buys two to three times the coverage of the readable signals, an edge a cross-fitted reconstruction cannot recover linearly from the readable features. We then give a floor-started fixed-sequence certificate, valid without monotonicity assumptions, that covers more than the Bonferroni certificate on every model-signal pair and raises coverage at the non-vacuous target $0.75π_0$ from 0.05 to 0.16, although the floor keeps absolute coverage small. Finally, a certificate cannot see what matters after deployment: under benchmark shift the error among accepted traces tracks the new task's base error, and under best-of-$n$ selection against the verifier it rises past the target while the empirical failure frequency stays below $δ$, because abstention absorbs the failures.

</details>


### [279] [Scalable Logistic Gaussian Process Density Regression with Kinetic Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09591)
*Daniel Paulin,Ádám Jung,András A. Benczúr*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的贝叶斯条件密度估计器，基于logistic高斯过程，采用可分离协方差结构（响应方向Matérn核的截断傅里叶基+协变量方向Nyström特征），直接采样有限特征模型的潜变量场而非采用Laplace或变分近似，用于光度红移等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件密度估计需要刻画给定协变量下响应变量的完整分布（如逐星系的光度红移）。现有贝叶斯方法多依赖Laplace或变分近似，难以在保证精度同时扩展到数百万级样本；因此需要一种既保持贝叶斯推断精度又可大规模训练的估计器。

Method: 以logistic高斯过程建模对数条件密度，协方差可分离：响应方向用圆上截断傅里叶基表示的Matérn核，协变量方向用Nyström特征表示，支持带输入依赖幅度和长度尺度的非平稳核。不采用Laplace/变分近似，而是对有限特征模型的潜变量场进行采样；给定超参数时其后验强对数凹且Hessian一致有界，通过Kronecker白化坐标下的对称小批量分裂动能Langevin动力学采样。边缘似然梯度由Fisher恒等式表示为后验期望，其偏差由步长和运行长度控制；预测时对非高斯潜变量后验（而非众数附近的高斯）做平均。

Result: 在最多390万训练样本的光度红移基准上，仅用单块GPU训练，该估计器在密度和校准指标上与当前最先进的表格基础模型相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法为大规模贝叶斯条件密度估计提供了可行且有效的途径，通过有限特征logistic高斯过程与动能Langevin采样结合，在保持贝叶斯推断（覆盖非高斯后验）的同时具备良好的可扩展性和基准性能。

Abstract: Conditional density estimation targets the full distribution of a response given covariates, as required, for example, for per-galaxy photometric redshifts. We develop a scalable Bayesian estimator based on the logistic Gaussian process. The log conditional density has a separable covariance: a Matérn kernel along the response, represented in a truncated Fourier basis on a circle, and a covariate kernel represented by Nyström features, which accommodate non-stationary kernels with input-dependent amplitudes and length scales. Instead of a Laplace or variational approximation, we sample the latent field of this finite-feature model. Given the hyperparameters, its posterior is strongly log-concave with a uniformly bounded Hessian, and we draw from it by simulating kinetic Langevin dynamics with symmetric minibatch splitting in Kronecker-whitened coordinates. Marginal-likelihood gradients follow from Fisher's identity as posterior expectations. Under the conditions of our analysis their bias is controlled by the sampler's step size and run length, and the predictive averages over the non-Gaussian latent posterior instead of a Gaussian around its mode. On photometric-redshift benchmarks with up to 3.9 million training observations, trained on a single GPU, the estimator is competitive with state-of-the-art tabular foundation models on density and calibration metrics.

</details>


### [280] [Sparsifying Stochasticity, Not Capacity: Partial Stochasticity via Deep Weight Factorization of Prior Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09886)
*Marius P Linhard,Maurizio Filippone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 通过学习参数先验尺度上的深度权重分解，自动决定贝叶斯神经网络中哪些参数是随机的、哪些是确定的，从而实现通用条件密度逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯神经网络不必完全随机即可成为通用条件密度逼近器，但哪些参数应当是随机的仍是开放问题。现有混合方案（部分采样、部分优化）缺乏理论依据。

Method: 对先验尺度（参数先验的标准差）施加深度权重分解，并以最大均值差异（MMD）目标将函数先验拟合到高斯过程。当某参数的先验尺度低于阈值时，该参数变为确定性并在推断中优化，从而使正则化稀疏化的是随机性而非容量。此外为通用条件密度逼近给出可在线性时间内检验的证书，以及失败时的最小修复。

Result: 证明了常见的混合方案（采样部分参数、优化其余参数）是II型最大后验目标的随机逼近，且耦合步长会留下随步长缩小而不消失的跟踪误差。在双峰目标上，学习到的划分在所有预算下接近无约束参考且对阈值不敏感；而随机掩码在相同先验尺度下误差高出最多两个数量级。在UCI基准上，性能与全随机网络相当，同时约一半参数保持确定性。

Conclusion: 随机性的划分可以被学习而非预先设定，正则化应稀疏化随机性而非容量；该方法在理论与实验上均支持部分确定性参数的有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian neural networks need not be fully stochastic to be universal conditional density approximators, but it remains open which parameters should be stochastic. We learn this split by applying deep weight factorization to the prior scales, which are the standard deviations of the parameter priors, while fitting the functional prior to a Gaussian process with a maximum mean discrepancy objective. A parameter whose prior scale falls below a cutoff becomes deterministic and is optimized during inference, so the regularizer sparsifies stochasticity rather than capacity. We give a certificate for universal conditional density approximation that is checkable in linear time, together with a minimal repair when it fails. We further show that the common hybrid scheme of sampling some parameters and optimizing the others is stochastic approximation for a type-II maximum a posteriori objective, and that coupled step sizes can leave a tracking error that does not vanish as the step size shrinks. On a bimodal target, the learned split stays close to an unconstrained reference across all budgets and is insensitive to the cutoff, while random masks that distribute the same prior scales across layers are worse by up to two orders of magnitude. On UCI benchmarks, our method performs on par with a fully stochastic network while keeping about half of its parameters deterministic.

</details>


### [281] [Possibilistic Radial Transport for Approximate IM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09956)
*Jungeum Kim,Percy Zhai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在可能推断模型（IMs）框架下，固定显著性水平后事后来看数据检验假设依然合法，但计算代价高。作者提出可能径向传输与深度学习方法，通过最大化壳内熵实现高效摊销采样，用于覆盖/功效评估、预测检验及Bel-Pl谱假设选择，并在模拟与卵巢衰老AMH数据中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可能推断模型下，见到数据后探测假设空间仍然有效（只要显著性水平固定），但每个合理性是可能性轮廓在假设上的上确界，且轮廓需在每个查询参数值处近似，导致计算成本高昂，亟需摊销式的近似与采样方法。

Method: 提出可能径向传输，将参数的轮廓值编码为源点半径；选取最大化壳内熵的传输后，采样覆盖置信切割的参数等价于对半径截断。设计深度学习算法，在满足轮廓深度约束的同时最大化每个壳内的熵，实现摊销式采样与近似。

Result: 模拟中学习到的轮廓与置信切割的椭球近似相当或更优；覆盖率与功效能跟踪精确参考值。方法支持对新数据集做预测检验，并用于构建Bel-Pl谱以比较和选择满足给定Bel-Pl决策准则的可解释假设。在卵巢衰老场景中，使用合成AMH记录推断每位女性中位AMH水平将保持在指定参考值以上的剩余年数。

Conclusion: 所提可能径向传输与熵最大化深度学习采样器为可能推断模型提供了实用摊销方案，使覆盖与功效评估、预测检验以及基于Bel-Pl谱的假设比较和选择在实践中可行，并在模拟和AMH应用中得到验证。

Abstract: Probing the hypothesis space after seeing the data remains valid under possibilistic inferential models (IMs), provided the significance level stays fixed. The price is computation, as each plausibility is a supremum of the possibility contour over the hypothesis, and the contour itself is approximated at each queried parameter value. We propose a possibilistic radial transport, which hides the contour value of a parameter in the radius of its source point. When a transport that maximizes within-shell entropy is picked, sampling parameters covering a confidence cut becomes a matter of truncating the radius. We provide a deep learning algorithm that enforces the contour depth condition while maximizing the entropy within each shell. Our amortization makes coverage and power assessments of the learned approximation practical as well as predictive check of new datasets. We also use the sampler to construct a Bel-Pl spectrum for comparing and selecting interpretable hypotheses that satisfy a prescribed Bel-Pl decision criterion. In simulations the learned contours match or improve on ellipsoidal approximations to the cuts, while the coverage and power track the exact reference. Finally, we probe hypotheses about ovarian aging using synthetic AMH records, asking for each woman how many more years her median AMH level will remain above a specified reference value.

</details>


### [282] [Extreme Binary Classification: Extreme Value Theory for Extreme Constraint on False Negative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09984)
*Samuel Gruffaz,Muhammad Fawad,Jaakko Nevalainen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出"极端二分类"问题，要求分类器的假阴性率 α 满足 ε_{N₁}=o(1/N₁)，并给出了基于极值理论阈值自适应与样本极大值置换检验特征选择的方法，在四个数据集上优于现有方法，并以癌症筛查数据展示可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二分类研究虽广泛，但"假阴性率近乎为零"的场景（如癌症筛查等高代价漏检任务）基本未被系统探索，缺乏理论保证与专门方法。

Method: 提出极端二分类问题（FNR 约束为 ε_{N₁}=o_{N₁→∞}(1/N₁)）；方法包含两部分：(1) 基于极值理论（EVT）推导保证的阈值自适应方法；(2) 基于样本极大值置换检验的特征选择流程。

Result: 在四个规模各异的真实数据集上，方法表现优于当前最先进方法；并在癌症筛查数据集上展示了可解释性。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了极端二分类这一新问题设定，提供了具理论基础的阈值与特征选择方案，验证了有效性、实用性和可解释性。

Abstract: While binary classification is one of the most extensively studied problems in machine learning,
  the regime in which the goal is to learn a classifier with an almost zero false negative rate remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we introduce the Extreme Binary Classification problem, where the objective is to learn a classifier whose false negative rate $α$ is constrained by $ε_{N_1}=o_{N_1\to\infty}(1/N_1)$, with $N_1$ denoting the number of positive examples in the training set.
  To address this problem, we propose a threshold adaptation method theoretically grounded in guarantees derived from Extreme Value Theory, together with a feature selection procedure based on a permutation test applied to sample maxima.
  Experimental results on four real-world datasets of varying sizes demonstrate that our approach compares favorably with state-of-the-art methods.
  In addition, we illustrate its interpretability through an application to a cancer screening dataset.

</details>


### [283] [Controlling Dependence in Implicit Generative Models via Spread Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10021)
*Jiahao Yu,Song Liu,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,RuiKang OuYang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Spread Mutual Information (SMI)，通过对生成变量施加扩散核并对多噪声水平的MI加权积分，使条件/边缘密度光滑且严格正，从而将基于score差分的互信息梯度构造扩展到可能奇异的分布。实验显示SMI在依赖控制上优于同类MI方法，并与任务专用方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互信息可用于隐式生成模型中抑制或促进统计依赖，但隐式模型密度不可解导致直接评估MI困难。基于条件与边缘score差分的估计方法面临两个问题：(i) 奇异分布不一定存在所需score函数；(ii) 分布重叠差会妨碍密度比估计。因此需要一个能避开这些困难、仍可用的MI目标。

Method: SMI对生成变量施加统一的扩散核，得到跨多个噪声水平的MI加权积分。高斯扩散使条件密度与边缘密度变为光滑且严格正的，从而使由分类学习对数密度比再微分得到score差的梯度构造可扩展到原本可能奇异的分布，并缓解重叠不足问题。

Result: 在多种实验中，SMI在基于MI的方法里一致地实现了有效的依赖性控制，并与已有任务专用方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过扩散核与多噪声水平积分重构互信息目标，SMI克服了score函数不存在与密度比估计困难两大障碍，为隐式生成模型提供了一种稳健有效的依赖控制手段。

Abstract: Mutual information (MI) provides an objective for suppressing or encouraging statistical dependence in implicit generative models. However, direct MI evaluation is challenging in implicit models due to typically intractable densities. A remedy is estimating the generator gradient from the difference between conditional and marginal scores. This score difference can, in turn, be estimated by differentiating a log density ratio learned through classification. This construction nevertheless faces two difficulties: (i) singular distributions need not admit the required score functions, and (ii) poor overlap can hinder density-ratio estimation. We therefore introduce Spread Mutual Information (SMI), a weighted integral of MI across noise levels obtained by applying a common spreading kernel to the generated variable. Gaussian spreading yields smooth, strictly positive conditional and marginal densities, extending the gradient construction to distributions that may originally be singular. Across a variaty of experiments, SMI consistently achieves effective dependence control among MI-based methods and remains competitive with established task-specific approaches.

</details>


### [284] [Gaussian Equivalence for Multi-Head Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10033)
*Tomohiro Hayase,Ryo Karakida*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用随机矩阵理论建立了多头自注意力机制的"高斯等价"理论,为理解现代神经网络中的注意力机制提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头自注意力机制是现代神经网络的核心组件,但其理论研究尚不充分。作者希望为其提供严格的理论刻画,特别是揭示不同结构设计(头数分配、投影宽度、跨头共享、头内键值依赖)对输出谱的影响。

Method: 采用随机矩阵理论(RMT),证明多头自注意力存在高斯等价性:将softmax注意力替换为重新缩放的score加高斯噪声后,中心化输出的极限谱分布保持不变。该等价性还推广到依赖于key的value投影和输出投影。

Result: 推导出的极限谱定律能够分离头数分配(head allocation)与投影宽度(projection widths)的影响,并能区分保持谱的跨头共享(across-head sharing)与头内键值依赖(within-head key–value dependence)两类结构机制。

Conclusion: 所建立的高斯等价框架为多头自注意力提供了可解析的谱理论,有助于阐明其结构设计对表示/谱性质的作用机理。

Abstract: A theoretical understanding of multi-head self-attention is fundamental to the study of modern neural networks. Using random matrix theory, we establish Gaussian equivalence for multi-head self-attention: replacing softmax attention with rescaled scores plus Gaussian noise preserves the limiting spectral law of the centered output. This equivalence also covers value and output projections that depend on the keys. The resulting laws separate the effects of head allocation and projection widths, and distinguish spectrum-preserving across-head sharing from within-head key--value dependence.

</details>


### [285] [Towards Calibrated Probabilistic Forecasts for Events of Interest via Outcome-Conditional Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10076)
*Jakob Benjamin Wessel,Sam Allen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"结果条件重校准"（outcome-conditional recalibration），一种针对结果空间用户指定区域的事后校准方法，能在感兴趣区域（如极端事件）内实现有效且连续的校准，并在回归基准与电价预测任务中优于现有条件/无条件校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率预测的校准是决策有用的前提，但现有主流模型常给出失准的预测分布。常用的后处理重校准方案可能掩盖结果空间特定区域的失准问题；而极端事件等特定结果往往对决策最重要，因此要求预测在这些结果子集上也能保持校准。

Method: 提出结果条件重校准：先对预测的条件分布应用Kuleshov等（2018）的分位数重校准，再对各条件分布进行重缩放，使预测事件概率与经验发生频率匹配，从而在用户定义的每个结果区域内产生有效且连续的校准预测分布；方法简单、易实现，适用于任意预测分布。

Result: 在多个回归基准上，现有重校准方案在关注特定结果时不必然给出校准预测；本文方法相比现有条件与无条件校准方法在结果条件校准上更优，同时整体校准保持竞争力。在日前电价预测应用中，预测负价格时的校准显著改善，而预测精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 结果条件重校准为关注特定结果区域（如极端事件）的决策场景提供了一种通用且实用的后处理校准方案，兼顾区域校准质量与整体校准性能。

Abstract: Calibration is an essential requirement for probabilistic predictions to be useful for decision making. While state-of-the-art prediction methods often yield miscalibrated predictive distributions, several post-hoc recalibration schemes have been proposed to generate calibrated predictions. However, popular recalibration schemes can conceal miscalibration in specific regions of the outcome space. Since particular outcomes, such as extreme events, often matter most for decision making, probabilistic predictions should be calibrated when evaluation is restricted to these outcomes. Hence, in this paper, we introduce outcome-conditional recalibration, a post-hoc method to recalibrate probabilistic predictions on user-defined regions of the outcome space. The method is simple, easy to implement, and can be applied to arbitrary predictive distributions. It works by applying the quantile recalibration approach of Kuleshov et al. (2018) to forecast conditional distributions, before rescaling these conditional distributions so that forecast event probabilities match empirical occurrence frequencies. This produces valid and continuous predictive distributions that are calibrated within each region of interest. Across regression benchmarks, we demonstrate that existing recalibration schemes do not necessarily yield calibrated predictions when interest is on particular outcomes, and that our approach improves outcome-conditional calibration relative to existing conditional and unconditional recalibration methods, while retaining competitive calibration overall. In an application to day-ahead electricity price forecasting, the approach substantially improves calibration when predicting negative prices, at negligible cost to forecast accuracy.

</details>


### [286] [Conformal Prediction for Spatially Dependent Data via Sequential Whitening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10168)
*Ayush Baran Sen,Arkajyoti Saha*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种空间分割共形预测方法，通过额外对校准残差进行序贯条件化，并结合最近邻近似，实现任意空间设计下的精确有限样本覆盖与渐近Oracle有效性，得到更窄更稳定的区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分割共形预测依赖校准误差与目标点误差的可交换性，但在空间依赖和非随机采样几何下该假设失效。现有空间方法仅用拟合残差移除空间变异中可预测的部分，但那些只能由校准残差预测的空间变异仍残留在目标与校准误差中，从而降低区间效率与稳定性。

Method: 在拟合残差校正的基础上，额外对校准残差进行序贯条件化，去除仅由校准残差可预测的空间变异；为扩展到大规模网络，采用最近邻近似。理论上在正确的工作协方差与椭圆残差分布假设下推导精确有限样本覆盖性，并分析协方差误设下的覆盖损失，同时构造识别低覆盖风险区域的诊断方法。

Result: 在正确工作协方差与椭圆残差分布下，区间在任意空间设计下具有精确有限样本覆盖；在进一步条件下达到渐近Oracle效率；给出协方差误设时的覆盖损失上界与欠覆盖风险区域诊断。模拟数据中，区间比全局与局部化的最新方法更窄更稳定；在全国PM2.5应用中，网络内区间更窄并能识别覆盖失效风险区域。

Conclusion: 通过对校准残差的额外条件化与最近邻近似，所提方法在空间依赖场景下兼顾覆盖有效性与区间效率，并具备误设定鲁棒性和风险诊断能力，适用于大规模空间预测任务。

Abstract: Split conformal prediction uses prediction errors on held-out (calibration) data to determine how wide the prediction intervals should be. It guarantees distribution-free finite-sample coverage when these errors and the error at the target site are exchangeable. This assumption may fail under spatial dependence and nonrandom sampling geometry. Existing spatial methods use fitting residuals to remove the predictable part of spatial variation from calibration and target errors. However, the spatial variation that only the calibration residuals can predict remains in both the target and calibration errors, reducing the efficiency and stability of the interval. We address this by additionally conditioning on the calibration residuals sequentially, which scales to large networks through nearest-neighbour approximations. Under a correct working covariance and an elliptical residual law, the resulting interval has exact finite-sample coverage under any spatial design, and under further conditions it is asymptotically oracle efficient. We also bound coverage loss under covariance misspecification and develop a diagnostic that identifies regions at risk of undercoverage. In simulated data, our method produces narrower and more stable intervals than global and localized state-of-the-art alternatives. In a national PM2.5 application, it produces narrower intervals within the network and identifies regions at risk of coverage failure.

</details>


### [287] [Kinetic Langevin Meets Split Gibbs: Accelerated Posterior Sampling for Imaging Inverse Problems with Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10187)
*Dai Hai Nguyen,Duc Dung Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 RED-KLwSGS，将分裂吉布斯采样中辅助变量的更新改为一次性去噪分数驱动的欠阻尼（动能）朗之万扩散，在保持与 Langevin-within-SGS 相同单次迭代成本的同时实现非渐近 W2 收敛，并提出对两变量均使用动能朗之万扩散的 Joint-RED-KLwSGS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯成像逆问题中的分裂吉布斯采样（SGS）通过引入辅助变量将高斯数据保真项与复杂先验解耦，使数据变量可精确更新，但先验侧条件分布难以采样。现有方案存在明显缺陷：即插即用 SGS 每次迭代需运行多步扩散去噪器，代价高昂且缺乏非渐近收敛保证；Langevin-within-SGS 虽然单步便宜，但需要大量迭代才能收敛。因此需要一种既能保证收敛理论、又保持低单次迭代成本的采样方法。

Method: 提出 RED-KLwSGS：对数据变量保留精确的高斯更新，对辅助变量使用由一次性（one-shot）去噪分数驱动的欠阻尼/动能朗之万扩散进行更新，单次迭代成本与 Langevin-within-SGS 相当。针对强对数凹先验，证明了连续时间与离散时间下的非渐近 Wasserstein-2 收敛性。进一步提出 Joint-RED-KLwSGS，对数据变量与辅助变量同时施加动能朗之万扩散。

Result: 在 FFHQ 与 ImageNet 数据集上，以去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）作为扩散先验进行实验，结果表明该方法具有更快的收敛速度，并能实现高质量的图像重建。

Conclusion: 将动能朗之万扩散与一次性去噪分数结合，可在不增加单步成本的前提下为 SGS 提供非渐近收敛保证，并在扩散先验驱动的贝叶斯成像逆问题中兼顾效率与重建质量。

Abstract: Split Gibbs sampling (SGS) is a popular framework for posterior sampling in Bayesian imaging inverse problems. It decouples a Gaussian data-fidelity term from a complex prior through an auxiliary variable, so the data variable is updated exactly and only the prior-side conditional is hard to sample. Existing samplers treat this conditional in one of two ways. Plug-and-play SGS runs a multi-step diffusion denoiser at every iteration, which is expensive and lacks non-asymptotic guarantees. Langevin-within-SGS takes cheap overdamped Langevin steps but needs many iterations. We propose RED-KLwSGS, which keeps the exact Gaussian update for the data variable and updates the auxiliary variable with underdamped (kinetic) Langevin diffusions driven by a one-shot denoising score, at the same per-iteration cost as Langevin-within-SGS. We prove non-asymptotic Wasserstein-2 convergence in continuous and discrete time for strongly log-concave priors. We also introduce Joint-RED-KLwSGS, which applies kinetic Langevin diffusions to both variables. Experiments with Denoising diffusion probabilistic models as diffusion priors on FFHQ and ImageNet datasets show faster convergence and high-quality image reconstruction.

</details>


### [288] [Universal Local Error and Realized Amplification for the First-Order EDM Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10190)
*Nicolas Brosse,Arnak S. Dalalyan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在2-Wasserstein距离下分析EDM一阶确定性扩散采样器，将误差分解为局部离散化误差及其经后续学习步骤的放大，得到全局O(e^{Λ_K}/K)的离散化误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDM等确定性扩散采样器的离散化误差来源及其在采样步数增加时的衰减规律尚缺乏理论刻画，尤其是局部离散误差与学习网络导致的误差放大之间的区分不清晰。

Method: 将误差分离为局部离散化误差和误差传播/放大两部分：证明局部一步离散误差对任意有限二阶矩数据分布具有与数据分布无关的显式常数、且关于步长为二次；在高噪声段利用EDM网络参数化推导显式收缩判据；在低噪声段用采样器所输运分布上的实际放大（realized amplification）度量传播，并指出其可远小于最坏情况Lipschitz常数。

Result: 得到K步采样的全局离散化误差为O(e^{Λ_K}/K)，其中Λ_K为低噪声对数放大因子；在一维高斯混合实验上显示实测放大可解释有限采样网格上较慢的误差衰减；在预训练CIFAR-10模型上的诊断揭示相关稳定性机制但不能验证全局假设。

Conclusion: 局部离散误差具有普遍二次界，误差放大取决于学习网络；实际放大而非最坏情况Lipschitz常数决定高精度采样网格上的误差衰减，为理解扩散采样器收敛提供更紧的刻画。

Abstract: We analyze the first-order deterministic diffusion sampler of Karras et al. (2022), termed EDM, in 2-Wasserstein distance by separating two sources of error: local discretization error and its amplification by subsequent learned steps. We prove that local error admits a universal bound: for any data distribution with finite second moment, the one-step discretization error is quadratic in the step size, with an explicit constant that does not depend on the data distribution. Error propagation, in contrast, depends on the learned network. At high noise levels, we exploit the network parametrization of EDM to derive an explicit contraction criterion. At low noise levels, we measure propagation through the amplification realized on the distributions transported by the sampler; this realized amplification can be arbitrarily smaller than the worst-case Lipschitz constant. This analysis yields an $O(e^{Λ_K}/K)$ global discretization error for $K$ sampling steps, where $Λ_K$ is the low-noise log-amplification. Experiments on a one-dimensional Gaussian mixture show how measured amplification accounts for slower error decay on finite sampling grids. Diagnostics on a pretrained CIFAR-10 model illustrate related stability mechanisms without certifying the global assumptions.

</details>


### [289] [Broadly Applicable Approximate MCMC for Switching Stochastic Differential Equations Using Uniformization and Time-Conditioned Factorized Neural Likelihood Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10194)
*Shion Hosoda,Michiaki Hamada*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于均匀化与因子化神经似然估计（FNLE）的近似MCMC采样器，用于状态切换随机微分方程（SSDE）的贝叶斯推断，无需解析可解的转移密度，实验证明可恢复regime路径与参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSDE用于建模按连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）切换参数的连续时间动态，能刻画异质系统行为，应用广泛。但其贝叶斯推断仍然困难，现有SSDE推断方法适用性有限，存在噪声-free观测、单变量状态、线性漂移、状态无关扩散等限制。

Method: 方法基于均匀化（uniformization）获得CTMC的精确表示，但需要任意时间区间上的SDE转移密度；为此训练一个时间条件化的因子化神经似然估计（FNLE）模型来近似这些转移密度，进而构建近似MCMC采样器。

Result: 在合成数据实验中，方法能对三个此前方法适用性有限的SSDE模型恢复regime路径与参数；在真实数据集上也检测到一次regime转移。

Conclusion: 提出的采样器广泛适用于无需解析可解转移密度的SSDE，突破了既有方法的诸多限制，在合成与真实数据上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Switching stochastic differential equations (SSDEs) describe continuous-time dynamics whose parameters switch according to a latent regime process that follows a continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC). By allowing dynamics to change between regimes, SSDEs represent heterogeneous system behavior and have been applied across diverse fields. However, Bayesian inference for SSDEs remains difficult, and existing SSDE inference methods have limited applicability, with restrictions such as noise-free observations, univariate states, linear drift, or state-independent diffusion. In this study, we propose an approximate Markov chain Monte Carlo sampler for SSDEs using uniformization and factorized neural likelihood estimation (FNLE), a simulation-based inference method. Uniformization provides an exact representation of the CTMC but requires SDE transition densities over arbitrary time intervals. We approximate these densities by training a time-conditioned FNLE model. The resulting sampler is broadly applicable to SSDEs without requiring analytically tractable transition densities. In synthetic-data experiments, our method recovered regime paths and parameters for three SSDE models for which previous methods have limited applicability. We also applied our method to a real dataset and detected a regime transition.

</details>


### [290] [RoBART: Bayesian Additive Regression Trees with Tree-Specific Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10214)
*Jeongung Heo,Seonghyun Jeong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RoBART：为每棵树引入一个共享的Givens旋转序列，在旋转坐标系中进行轴对齐分裂，并在该网格上通过MH联合采样旋转与切点，证明后验收缩率并给出下界，说明RoBART能适应内在维数而轴对齐BART不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯加性回归树（BART）的轴对齐分裂在逼近与预测变量轴不对齐的边界时需要大量分裂，效率低；因此需要一种能自适应旋转坐标、从而适应函数内在维数与各向异性光滑性的树模型，并为其提供后验收缩的理论保证。

Method: 提出RoBART：每棵树赋予一个由所有内部节点共享的旋转，在旋转坐标下保留轴对齐分裂与常数叶；通过Metropolis-Hastings在Givens旋转序列与所得网格上的切点进行联合采样，并在叶均值积分掉的条件下建立其条件后验的可逆性。针对具有组分特定旋转和各向异性Hölder光滑性的加性函数，证明经验L2距离与噪声标准差的收敛率。此外推导后验收缩下界。

Result: 在给定的先验、设计与网格条件，且预测变量数、树数与组分数固定、组分数不超过树数的情况下，收缩率为各组分速率之和，由光滑性与所用旋转坐标数决定；下界表明存在某些函数，RoBART能适应其内在维数而轴对齐BART不能。

Conclusion: RoBART通过树内共享旋转使轴对齐分裂在最优方向上进行，理论上实现了对各向异性Hölder光滑加性函数的内在维数自适应，优于轴对齐BART，为旋转式贝叶斯树方法提供了后验收缩保证。

Abstract: Bayesian additive regression trees (BART) can require many splits to approximate boundaries misaligned with the predictor axes. RoBART assigns each tree a rotation shared by all internal nodes, retaining axis-aligned splits in rotated coordinates and constant leaves. We jointly propose a Givens rotation sequence and cutpoints on the resulting grid by Metropolis-Hastings and establish reversibility with respect to the conditional posterior with leaf means integrated out. For additive functions with component-specific rotations and anisotropic Hölder smoothness, we prove posterior contraction in empirical $L_2$ distance and for the noise standard deviation. Under the stated prior, design, and grid conditions, with fixed numbers of predictors, trees, and components and no more components than trees, the rate is a sum of componentwise rates determined by smoothness and the number of rotated coordinates used. We also establish a posterior contraction lower bound showing that there exist functions for which RoBART adapts to the intrinsic dimension but axis-aligned BART does not.

</details>


### [291] [Data Reuse in Non-Stationary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10340)
*Tomer Gafni,Garud Iyengar,Assaf Zeevi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究非平稳环境下的在线学习，目标是跟踪在有限个循环取值间突变切换的未知参数。提出 Exposure-Capped Reuse (ECR) 一类任意时间算法，结合在线变化检测、兼容性检验与"污染"控制，刻画了遗憾随不同取值数（而非变化次数）增长的区间，并给出信息论下界证明其近似极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳环境中参数会在有限个循环出现的取值间突然切换，循环性为复用历史观测以提升性能提供了可能；但信号本身的动态变化及对动态机制信息的缺失，使得"安全"复用数据存在风险。论文旨在量化该类问题中的基本权衡，揭示其与经典偏差-方差困境的相似性，并严格量化数据复用的统计"价值"。

Method: 提出一类任意时间（anytime）算法，称为 Exposure-Capped Reuse (ECR)，将在线变化检测、兼容性检验（compatibility testing）与"污染"控制（contamination control）相结合；通过限制历史数据的"暴露"程度来控制可能过时或不可靠信息的使用。

Result: 刻画了 ECR 遗憾界随不同取值数目（而非变化次数）增长的区间；推导出一个新的信息论下界，据此证明 ECR 具有近似极小极大最优性（near-minimax optimality）。

Conclusion: 论文量化了循环非平稳在线学习中数据复用的统计价值，表明合理复用历史观测可在遗憾界上获得与"取值数"而非"变化数"相关的改进，并通过下界证实该性能增益接近最优，为数据复用的统计收益提供了严格的刻画。

Abstract: We consider online learning in non-stationary environments, where the goal is to track an unknown parameter that switches abruptly between a finite set of recurring values. Recurrence opens the possibility of judiciously reusing past observations to improve algorithm performance. However, the changing nature of the underlying signal and lack of information on these dynamics may limit the ability to "safely" reuse data. In this paper we quantify some of the fundamental tradeoffs in this class of problems, and show that they bear a certain resemblance to the classical bias-variance dilemma. Specifically, we propose a class of anytime algorithms, dubbed Exposure-Capped Reuse (ECR), that combine online change detection, compatibility testing, and "contamination" control. We characterize the regime in which ECR's regret scales with the number of distinct values rather than the number of changes, and derive a novel information-theoretic lower bound that establishes the near-minimax optimality of ECR. This provides rigorous quantification of the statistical "value" of data reuse.

</details>


### [292] [Dataset Pruning from First Principles: A Label-Free Linear Programming Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10347)
*Rodrigo Schuller,Francisco Ganacim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于几何的无偏数据集剪枝框架：将无偏子集选择重构为方差最小化问题，刻画其可行域为高维凸多面体，并利用刚性运动平均下的闭式成对方差表达式构造高效顶点行走算法，无需标签或模型训练。在CIFAR-10、MNIST、CelebA上，该方法在各预算下均值测试精度不劣于均匀采样，并在小预算下优于竞争几何方法；同框架还可用于降低小批量随机梯度方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于几何的数据集剪枝方法通常假设嵌入空间中邻近点具有相似性质，这一假设缺乏理论依据且可能不成立。作者希望不依赖该假设，而是从无偏性与方差最小化的角度推导几何选择准则，从而在保证子集统计量（损失、梯度）期望无偏的前提下提升子集代表性。

Method: 将无偏子集选择形式化为方差最小化问题：无偏性保证无权重子集平均在期望上恢复全数据集平均（含固定参数下的损失与梯度）。将一类无偏子集选择算法刻画为高维凸多面体，在该多面体上最小化期望采样方差是线性目标；对刚性运动取平均后的采样方差差异具有闭式成对表达。由于多面体维度高，直接线性规划不可行，故利用闭式表达式构造高效顶点行走算法，优化方差目标的近似并保持无偏性，选择过程无需标签与模型训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、MNIST、CelebA基准上，该方法在所有评估预算下的均值测试精度均不低于均匀采样，并在若干设置（尤其小选择预算）下优于竞争几何方法。此外，同一框架在不改变批量大小的前提下，通过提高小批量内样本多样性降低随机梯度方差。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，无偏子集选择可统一为方差最小化问题，并可借助凸多面体上的高效顶点行走求解，实现无需标签和训练的几何剪枝。方法在小预算下优势明显，且框架具有通用性，可扩展至降低小批量随机梯度方差等任务。

Abstract: Dataset pruning reduces a large training set to a representative subset while preserving model performance. Existing geometry-based methods typically assume that nearby points in embedding space share similar properties. Rather than imposing this assumption, we derive geometric selection criteria by reformulating unbiased subset selection as a variance minimization problem. Unbiasedness ensures that unweighted subset averages recover full-dataset averages in expectation, including losses and gradients at fixed model parameters. Specifically, we characterize a family of unbiased subset selection algorithms as a high-dimensional polytope. In this context, minimizing the expected sampling variance is a linear objective. Differences in sampling variance, averaged over rigid motions, admit closed-form pairwise expressions. Because the polytope has high dimension, directly applying standard linear programming is impractical. We instead use these expressions to construct an efficient vertex walk that optimizes an approximation of the variance objective while preserving unbiasedness, yielding a method that requires neither labels nor model training during selection. Across CIFAR-10, MNIST, and CelebA benchmarks, our method matches or exceeds uniform sampling in mean test accuracy at every evaluated budget and outperforms competing geometric methods in several settings, particularly at small selection budgets. Beyond dataset pruning, the same framework reduces stochastic-gradient variance by increasing diversity within mini-batches while keeping the batch size unchanged.

</details>


### [293] [Safe Meta-Policy Design with Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10393)
*Wenbin Zhou,Michael Lingzhi Li,Shixiang Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线元策略方法，用于规划未来模型版本的更新时机，在提升收益与控制性能回退风险之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型可随新数据重训练，但每次部署新版本都可能用较差策略替换较好策略。作者希望在未来候选模型训练之前就规划好更新策略，兼顾改进收益与性能回退风险。

Method: 将更新调度建模为有向无环图中的路径，从历史学习轨迹中估计策略切换的价值与风险；在“预期回退更新次数”预算约束下最大化期望累计价值，并用动态规划选择调度。同时进行主导阶分析，识别策略改进的信噪比作为更新频率、等待时间与风险分配的关键驱动因素。

Result: 信噪比越高（改进越清晰），支持更早、更频繁的更新；改进越噪声化，则需要更长等待或更多风险支出。其渐近速率还揭示随时间推移实现更高安全的边际成本递减。合成数据与临床试验数据实验展示了性能—风险权衡，并与基线方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该工作将策略更新规划形式化为受风险预算约束的序贯决策问题，提供了可解释的更新调度原则，并通过实验验证了性能与风险之间的权衡。

Abstract: Models can be retrained as new data arrive, but deploying every new version risks replacing a good policy with a worse one. We study how to plan policy updates (i.e., meta-policy) before future candidates are trained, balancing the benefits of improvement against the risk of performance regression. Our offline meta-policy maximizes expected cumulative value subject to a budget on the expected number of updates that perform worse than the policies they replace. We estimate the value and risk of possible switches from historical learning trajectories, represent an update schedule as a path in a directed acyclic graph, and select a schedule using dynamic programming. A leading-order analysis identifies the signal-to-noise ratio of policy improvement as a key driver of update frequency, waiting times, and risk allocation: clearer improvements support earlier, more frequent updates, while noisier improvements call for longer waits or greater risk expenditure. Their asymptotic rates also reveal a diminishing marginal cost of achieving greater safety over time. Experiments on synthetic and clinical trial data illustrate the performance--risk tradeoff and compare our method with alternative baselines.

</details>


### [294] [Derivative Gaussian Processes on a Two-Direction Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10428)
*Hyunseok Seung,Matthias Katzfuss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种每观测梯度仅用两个方向的导数高斯过程方法，在Vecchia近似下以O(m^3)代价纳入梯度信息，精度媲美精确梯度约简方法，且时间与内存远低于函数-only基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度观测本可提升高斯过程代理模型精度，但纳入梯度的计算成本长期阻碍该优势的实现，因此需要一种低预算、高效的梯度GP构造方法。

Method: 每观测梯度仅取两个方向：一个方向关注该梯度对目标预测的直接贡献，另一个方向通过与被条件函数值的相关性聚合其间接贡献。在Vecchia近似中，每个预测条件于d维空间m个邻近输入，用至多2m个方向导数表示其md个梯度坐标，每次预测目标的稠密分解代价为O(m^3)。

Result: 对一般条件集，给出了相对使用全梯度的后验近似误差界，并刻画误差小或近似精确的条件；模拟中在相同条件集大小下精度匹配领先的精确梯度约简方法，且因代价随条件集增长更慢，可用远超精确方法内存上限的条件集，以很小的时间和内存达到更低预测误差。

Conclusion: 该方法能在利用梯度观测的同时，比仅函数GP基线消耗更少计算时间或内存，实现了高效梯度GP的实用化。

Abstract: Gradient observations promise more accurate Gaussian process (GP) surrogates, but the cost of incorporating them has long stood in the way of realizing that promise. We propose a derivative GP with a budget of just two directions per observed gradient. One direction focuses on each gradient's direct contribution to target prediction, while the other aggregates its indirect contributions through correlations with the conditioning function values. Within a Vecchia approximation, where each prediction conditions on $m$ nearby inputs in $d$ dimensions, this construction represents their $md$ gradient coordinates using at most $2m$ directional derivatives, giving $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ dense factorization cost per prediction target. For general conditioning sets, we bound the posterior approximation error relative to using full gradients and characterize when the error is small or the approximation is exact. In simulations, our method matches the accuracy of a leading exact gradient-reduction method at equal conditioning set size. Because its cost grows much more slowly with that size, it can use conditioning sets well beyond the memory limit of the exact method, reaching lower prediction error with a small fraction of the time and memory. Notably, our method can exploit gradient observations while requiring less computation time or memory than function-only GP baselines.

</details>


### [295] [Best Arm Identification for Bandits with Shifting Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10488)
*Lukas Zierahn,Wouter M. Koolen,Shubhada Agrawal,Christina Katsimerou,Dirk van der Hoeven*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究带有"均值漂移"（Shifting Means）对抗扰动的随机最佳臂识别问题，提出重要性加权算法 ISM，证明其 δ-正确且样本复杂度为 O(K(σ²+U²)Δ_min⁻² ln(1/δ))，并给出匹配的worst-case下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最佳臂识别假设各臂均值奖励随时间稳定，而本文考虑一种新的对抗扰动形式——均值漂移：仅臂间差值 Δ 稳定，共同漂移可被逐轮对抗性设定。在此设定下探索样本复杂度最小化，并揭示现有算法的失效。

Method: 作者首先证明使用广义似然比检验（GLRT）停止规则的算法（包括流行的 Track-and-Stop）在时变漂移下会失败；随后提出基于重要性权重（Importance Weights）的算法 ISM，在均值有界 U、σ²-次高斯奖励假设下进行分析。

Result: ISM 被证明为 δ-正确，样本复杂度为 K(σ²+U²)Δ_min⁻² ln(1/δ) 量级；并给出匹配（至常数因子）的worst-case下界，最后通过实验评估验证。

Conclusion: 在均值漂移对抗环境下，GLRT类停止规则不可靠，而基于重要性权重的方法能实现最优（至常数因子）的样本复杂度。

Abstract: We study the best arm identification problem in a stochastic environment with a novel form of adversarial perturbations, which we coin Shifting Means. While classically the mean rewards of the $K$ arms are stable in time, in Shifting Means only the gaps $\boldsymbolΔ$ between mean rewards are stable, while their common shift may be determined adversarially in each round. The objective of the learner is to identify the best arm with high probability while minimizing sample complexity (the fixed confidence setting). Handling shifts requires new tools: we show that algorithms employing a Generalized Likelihood Ratio Test (GLRT) stopping rule, including the popular Track-and-Stop, fail under time-varying shifts. Instead, we propose Importance Weights for Shifting Means ($\mathsf{ISM}$). Assuming means bounded by $U$ and $σ^2$-sub-Gaussian rewards, we show $\mathsf{ISM}$ to be $δ$-correct and to enjoy a sample complexity bound of order $K (σ^2 + U^2) Δ_{\min}^{-2} \ln \frac{1}δ$. We also present a matching (up to constant factors) worst-case lower bound and evaluate our results empirically.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [Optimal Linear Inviscid Damping and Vorticity Depletion for Non-monotonic shear flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08912)
*Rajendra Beekie,Shan Chen,Hao Jia*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了矩形环面上一大类谱稳定剪切流 b(y) 的最优逐点线性无粘阻尼与涡量耗竭，改进了 Kolmogorov 流的结果并去除了对数修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 Kolmogorov 流 b(y)=cos y 的线性无粘估计存在表观上的对数修正，本文旨在去除该对数修正并将其结果推广到更一般的谱稳定剪切流。

Method: 结合参数拟解（parametrix）的逐点界与一个更简单内问题的显式解，从而清晰地去除对数项。

Result: 对矩形环面上一大类谱稳定剪切流建立了最优逐点线性无粘阻尼与涡量耗竭，其中对数修正被完全去除。

Conclusion: 通过不同方法推广并改进了已有结果，为更一般剪切流的最优无粘阻尼与涡量耗竭提供了新途径。

Abstract: We give a proof of optimal pointwise linear inviscid damping and vorticity depletion for a large class of spectrally stable shear flows $b(y)$ on the rectangular torus. This extends the linear inviscid estimates in \cite{CJWZ25} for Kolmogorov flow $b(y) =\cos y$ using completely different methods. Crucially, both methods remove an apparent logarithmic correction to the depletion and inviscid damping rates. Our method combines pointwise bounds for a parametrix with an explicit solution of a simpler inner problem which makes the removal of the logarithm clear.

</details>


### [297] [A sharp spectral multiplier theorem on the Engel group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08976)
*Zhicheng Han,Zhipeng Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明Engel群上次拉普拉斯算子的谱乘子定理：在尺度不变局部H^s条件下（s>2），乘子算子为弱(1,1)型且在所有L^p（1<p<∞）上有界，并将尖锐阈值确定为2（拓扑维数的一半），局部H^2情形仍开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Engel群上次拉普拉斯算子谱乘子定理的尖锐正则性阈值，并揭示正则性条件与拓扑维数之间的关系（阈值恰为拓扑维数的一半）。

Method: 结合四阶振荡子的加权Plancherel估计，以及分解为秩二谱子空间的技术，使得参数导数在“逃逸到无穷”时能被一致控制。

Result: 在局部H^s条件（s>2）下，F(L)为弱(1,1)型且在所有1<p<∞的L^p上有界；结合已知下界，尖锐阈值被确定为2。此外，对紧支于(0,∞)的固定光滑乘子，给出了局部化核的首项与一阶修正项。

Conclusion: Engel群上次拉普拉斯谱乘子的尖锐阈值等于拓扑维数的一半（即2）；局部H^2条件下弱(1,1)估计是否成立仍未解决。

Abstract: We prove a spectral multiplier theorem for the standard sub-Laplacian on the Engel group under a scale-invariant local $H^s$ condition with $s>2$. The multiplier operators $F(\mathcal L)$ are of weak type $(1,1)$ and bounded on $L^p$ for every $1<p<\infty$. Together with the known lower bound, this identifies the sharp threshold as $2$, half the topological dimension. The proof combines a weighted Plancherel estimate for quartic oscillators with a decomposition into rank-two spectral subspaces, which controls parameter derivatives uniformly as one well escapes to infinity. For fixed smooth multipliers compactly supported in $(0,\infty)$, we also determine the leading term and first correction for the localized kernels. Whether the weak type $(1,1)$ estimate holds under the local $H^2$ condition remains open.

</details>


### [298] [Normalized nonlinear Schrödinger ground states on defected grids with unbounded defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09908)
*William Borrelli,Nicolò Cangiotti,Simone Dovetta,Lorenzo Tentarelli*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带无界缺陷的二维方格图上非线性薛定谔能量的归一化基态，揭示无界缺陷对维度交叉、基态存在性及紧性丧失的影响机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无界缺陷是否会破坏方格图上的维度交叉，以及缺陷如何通过改变图的有效维度影响归一化基态的存在性，从而理解变分问题中紧性丧失与图局部几何之间的关系。

Method: 结合小质量能量渐近分析与大质量爆破（blow-up）论证；在half-grid、quarter-grid及含无界一维通道的原型缺陷图上进行分析。

Result: 1) 无界缺陷不一定破坏维度交叉，其在half-grid和quarter-grid上持续存在；2) 在宏观二维区域与无界一维通道共存的缺陷图上，质量过小或过大时均不存在归一化基态，表明一维分量可主导变分问题并导致紧性丧失；3) 该机制对图局部几何敏感，适当添加缺陷可保持小质量不存在性，同时恢复大质量基态的存在性。

Conclusion: 无界缺陷对归一化基态存在性的影响取决于图的有效维度与局部几何；一维分量可导致紧性丧失，但通过调控局部缺陷可恢复大质量基态，说明该机制具有几何敏感性。

Abstract: We study normalized ground states for the nonlinear Schrödinger energy on two-dimensional square grids with unbounded defects. We first show that unbounded defects do not necessarily destroy the dimensional crossover, proving its persistence on the half-grid and the quarter-grid. We then consider a prototypical defected grid where a macroscopically two-dimensional region coexists with an unbounded one-dimensional channel. On this graph, normalized ground states do not exist for sufficiently small or sufficiently large masses, showing that the one-dimensional component can dominate the variational problem and induce loss of compactness. Finally, we show that this mechanism is sensitive to the local geometry of the graph: suitable additional defects preserve small-mass nonexistence but restore the existence of ground states for large masses. Our analysis combines small-mass energy asymptotics with a large-mass blow-up argument.

</details>


### [299] [Critical Sobolev Inequalities and Extremals for a Partially Fractional Grushin Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09001)
*S. Malhotra,L. Sharma,K. Sreenadh*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究部分分数阶 Grushin 型算子 \(\mathcal L_{\alpha,\beta}\)，结合各向异性伸缩与拟距离，证明各向异性柱上的分数阶 Grushin-Poincaré 不等式、临界指标的全局 Sobolev 不等式及能量空间的集中紧性原理，并刻画集中现象发生于退化超平面，进而得到最优 Sobolev 常数极值函数的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grushin 型算子在 \(x\) 方向具有分数阶扩散、在 \(y\) 方向具有退化局部扩散，其几何由各向异性伸缩决定。现有 Sobolev 型不等式与集中紧性理论多针对纯局部或非退化情形，针对此类耦合分数阶退化算子的临界 Sobolev 不等式、最优常数可达性尚未充分建立；且 \(\beta>0\) 时权重 \(|x|^{2\beta}\) 破坏 \(x\) 方向平移不变性，使集中机制更复杂。因此需要发展适配该拟距离与临界指标的分析框架。

Method: 采用各向异性伸缩 \((x,y)\mapsto(\rho x,\rho^{\alpha+\beta}y)\) 与适配拟距离；在各向异性柱上证明分数阶 Grushin-Poincaré 不等式；构造基于二进柱上 telescoping 求和的 Riesz 型位势，证明临界指数 \(2^*_{\alpha,\beta}=2Q_{\alpha,\beta}/(Q_{\alpha,\beta}-2\alpha)\) 处的全局 Sobolev 不等式，其中 \(Q_{\alpha,\beta}=n+(\alpha+\beta)m\)；随后为能量空间建立集中紧性原理，并分析集中位置的限制。

Result: 证明了一致的分数阶 Grushin-Poincaré 不等式和临界指数的全局 Sobolev 不等式；建立了相应能量空间的集中紧性原理；当 \(\beta>0\) 时，权重 \(|x|^{2\beta}\) 破坏 \(x\) 方向平移不变性，集中被限制在退化超平面 \(\Sigma=\{(0,y):y\in\mathbb{R}^m\}\) 上；该局部化性质导致最优常数 \(S_{\alpha,\beta}(n,m)\) 的极值函数存在。

Conclusion: 该工作为由分数阶扩散与退化局部扩散耦合的 Grushin 型算子建立了完整的临界 Sobolev 不等式与集中紧性框架，并揭示了权重导致的集中局部化机制，从而保证最优 Sobolev 常数可达。

Abstract: We study the partially fractional Grushin-type operator \begin{equation} \mathcal L_{α,β}u=(-Δ_x)^αu-(1+β)^2|x|^{2β}Δ_y u, \qquad (x,y)\in\mathbb{R}^n\times\mathbb{R}^m,\quad 0<α<1,\ β\ge 0, \end{equation} which couples fractional diffusion in $x$ with degenerate local diffusion in $y$. Working with the anisotropic dilations $(x,y)\mapsto(ρx,ρ^{α+β}y)$ and an adapted quasi-distance, we prove a uniform fractional Grushin-Poincaré inequality on anisotropic cylinders and, via a Riesz-type potential built from a telescoping sum over dyadic cylinders, a global Sobolev inequality at the sharp critical exponent $2^*_{α,β}=2Q_{α,β}/(Q_{α,β}-2α)$, where $Q_{α,β}=n+(α+β)m$. We then establish a concentration-compactness principle for the associated energy space. For $β>0$ the weight $|x|^{2β}$ destroys translation invariance in $x$, and we show that concentration is confined to the degenerate hyperplane $ Σ= \{(0,y):y\in\mathbb{R}^m\}$; this localization yields the existence of extremals for the sharp constant $S_{α,β}(n,m)$.

</details>


### [300] [Li-Yau and Harnack estimates for nonlinear parabolic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09009)
*Thalia Jeffres,Xiaolong Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为一类非线性抛物方程的正粘性解建立全局Harnack不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典线性热流的全局Harnack估计推广到非线性抛物方程，并在尽量少的曲率假设下获得相应结果。

Method: 利用半连续函数的最大值原理，在两个独立的时空点进行带权测地变分的比较论证。

Result: 得到正齐次一致抛物流、加权p-Laplacian热流及齐次各向同性扩散的全局Harnack不等式；无穷Laplacian热流无需曲率假设，普通加权p-Laplacian在Bakry-Émery Ricci非负下界下得到压力估计。

Conclusion: 相应的Li-Yau不等式在一阶粘性意义下成立。

Abstract: We establish global Harnack inequalities for positive viscosity solutions of several classes of nonlinear parabolic equations. The equations include positively homogeneous uniformly parabolic flows on Euclidean space and flat tori, normalized weighted $p$-Laplacian heat flows, and homogeneous isotropic diffusions on closed smooth metric measure spaces. For the normalized infinity-Laplacian heat flow, no curvature assumption is needed. For the ordinary weighted $p$-Laplacian, we obtain pressure estimates under nonnegative Bakry-Émery Ricci lower bounds. The proofs combine comparisons at two independent space-time points using the maximum principle for semicontinuous functions with weighted geodesic variations. The corresponding Li-Yau inequalities then follow in the first-order viscosity sense.

</details>


### [301] [Sharp blow-up condition for a two-species cross attraction system in 3 or more dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09078)
*Suleyman Ulusoy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究高维两物种两刺激退化趋化模型,在可积初值且具有限二阶矩与能量假设下,证明自由能解的局部存在性,并揭示解的整体存在与爆破由参数对的临界值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高维(d≥3)两物种、两刺激退化趋化系统中解的定性行为,明确决定整体存在性与爆破现象的关键临界条件,填补该模型在高维情形下自由能解理论的研究空白。

Method: 在维数d≥3下考虑含两物种与两刺激的退化趋化模型;假设初始数据可积且具有限二阶矩与有限能量;采用自由能解(即带自由能估计的弱解)框架,通过能量方法与临界值分析进行论证。

Result: 对任意质量证明了自由能解的局部时间存在性;发现解的定性行为由一组临界值决定:参数对存在一个临界值,当满足该参数对的临界性条件时存在整体时间的能量解,而当临界性条件被破坏时存在爆破的自由能解。

Conclusion: 该退化趋化模型在任意质量下均有局部自由能解,其整体存在与爆破行为由参数对相对于临界值的条件所决定,给出了区分全局适定与有限时间爆破的判据。

Abstract: We consider a degenerate chemotaxis model with two-species and two-stimuli in dimension $d \geq 3$. Under the hypothesis of integrable initial data with finite second moment and energy, we show local-in-time existence for any mass of free-energy solutions, namely weak solutions with some free energy estimates. We exhibit that the qualitative behavior of solutions is decided by a set of critical values: there is a critical value of a parameter pair in the system of equations for which there is a global-in-time energy solution and there exist blowing-up free-energy solutions under a criticality condition is violated for the parameter pair.

</details>


### [302] [Fully Nonlinear Logistic Equations with Sanctuary Regions and Nonlocal Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09271)
*Giulio Galise,Shuhei Kitano*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究有界区域上非线性非局部方程 $Iu+\mu u=k(x)u^p$ 的正解，刻画了尖锐存在区间与唯一性，并分析了上下端点处的渐近行为及两种尺度的爆破现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对含可消失系数的完全非线性非局部方程，需在一般 Caffarelli–Silvestre 意义一致椭圆算子框架下确定解的存在区间、唯一性，并理解系数在 sanctuary 区域消失时解在端点的行为。

Method: 以正主半特征值刻画尖锐存在区间，证明解的唯一性；对下端点考察解的归一化轮廓；对上端点利用 $\mu\uparrow\lambda(\Omega_0)$ 时解在 $\Omega$ 中局部一致爆破，并通过归一化与 $L^\infty$ 尺度比较证明两类极限。

Result: 证明了下端点解一致消失且归一化轮廓收敛于主特征函数；若 $k$ 在 sanctuary $\Omega_0$ 上消失，则当 $\mu\uparrow\lambda(\Omega_0)$ 时解局部一致爆破，且 $u_\mu/\|u_\mu\|_{L^\infty(\Omega)}\to\varphi_0$ 在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上一致成立，$u_\mu/\|u_\mu\|_{L^\infty(\Omega)}^{1/p}\to(I\varphi_0/k)^{1/p}$ 在 $\Omega\setminus\overline{\Omega_0}$ 上局部一致成立。

Conclusion: 该方程正解存在区间由正主半特征值尖锐刻画且解唯一；上端点爆破发生于两个不同尺度，分别对应主特征函数与 $(I\varphi_0/k)^{1/p}$ 的极限轮廓。

Abstract: We study positive solutions of $Iu+μu=k(x)u^p$ in a bounded domain, where $p>1$, the nonnegative coefficient $k$ may vanish on a sanctuary region, and $I$ is a fully nonlinear nonlocal operator uniformly elliptic in the Caffarelli--Silvestre sense. We characterize the sharp existence interval in terms of positive principal half-eigenvalues and prove uniqueness. At the lower endpoint, the solutions vanish uniformly and their normalized profiles converge to the principal eigenfunction. If $k$ vanishes on a sanctuary $Ω_0$, then, as $μ\uparrowλ(Ω_0)$, the solutions blow up locally uniformly in $Ω$. Writing $φ_0=φ(\cdot,Ω_0)$ for the normalized positive principal eigenfunction in $Ω_0$, we prove \[ \frac{u_μ}{\|u_μ\|_{L^\infty(Ω)}}\toφ_0, \qquad \frac{u_μ}{\|u_μ\|_{L^\infty(Ω)}^{1/p}} \to\left(\frac{Iφ_0}{k}\right)^{1/p}, \] uniformly in $\mathbb{R}^n$ and locally uniformly in $Ω\setminus\overline{Ω_0}$, respectively. Thus the upper-endpoint blow-up occurs on two distinct scales.

</details>


### [303] [Neumann Schrödinger Estimates in Cylindrical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09378)
*Len Meas*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在三维圆柱域上建立半经典薛定谔方程的局部时间色散估计与整体Strichartz估计，包含导数损失 ρ(q)=(3/2)(1/2-1/q)，并应用于立方非线性薛定谔方程的局部适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆柱域的部分平坦边界导致曲率沿纵向退化，产生持续的掠射（glancing）相互作用，使其区别于严格凸域；同时Neumann边界条件在谱层面表现为Airy导数零点而非Dirichlet的Airy零点，需要专门分析。

Method: 采用半经典微局部分析、振荡积分估计，以及适用于Neumann反射的Airy–Poisson求和公式，对局部化薛定谔传播子进行边界层分析；利用薛定谔相位的二次结构统一处理低频区域。

Result: 获得了局部化传播子的均匀色散界，并导出带导数损失 ρ(q)=(3/2)(1/2-1/q) 的整体Strichartz估计；进而证明了在 H^s(Ω), s>1 中，聚焦或散焦立方非线性薛定谔方程（齐次Neumann边界）的局部适定性。

Conclusion: 结果量化了部分平坦边界几何对薛定谔色散的影响，并为相应非线性动力学提供了锐利的线性框架。

Abstract: We establish sharp local-in-time dispersive estimates and global Strichartz estimates for the semiclassical Schrödinger equation in a three-dimensional cylindrical domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}$ with homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions. The partially flat boundary leads to a degeneracy of curvature in the longitudinal direction and produces persistent glancing interactions that distinguish the cylindrical setting from strictly convex domains. At the spectral level, the Neumann condition is reflected in the appearance of the zeros of the Airy derivative in place of the Airy zeros associated with Dirichlet boundary conditions.
  Using semiclassical microlocal analysis, oscillatory integral estimates, and an Airy--Poisson summation formula adapted to Neumann reflection, we obtain uniform dispersive bounds for the localized Schrödinger propagator. The quadratic structure of the Schrödinger phase yields a uniform treatment of the low-frequency regime and, combined with the boundary-layer analysis, leads to global Strichartz estimates with derivative loss $$ ρ(q)=\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac12-\frac1q\right). $$ As an application, we prove local well-posedness in $H^{s}(Ω)$, $s>1$, for the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation with either focusing or defocusing sign and homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions. These results quantify the effect of partially flat boundary geometry on Schrödinger dispersion and provide a sharp linear framework for the corresponding nonlinear dynamics.

</details>


### [304] [Interior $C^2$ Estimates for 2-Sum Hessian Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09490)
*C. Y. Ren,X. L. Sun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在正分支 Δu>0 上，对形如 σ₂(D²u)+α(x)σ₁(D²u)=f(x,u,Du)>0 的方程建立内估计 C²，条件为 α≥0 且 D²α=βI，并给出反例说明仅 L∞ 控制 α 不够。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具梯度项及一阶项扰动的 σ₂ 方程在正分支上的正则性，目标是在较一般的 α 假设下获得内 C² 估计，并厘清对 α 的最低必要条件。

Method: 引入额外变量并作四次修正，将原方程化为高一维的二次型 Hessian 方程；利用构造使含 D²u 的项相互抵消，从而可直接应用 Li–Wu 的内估计。无梯度情形另作处理，并构造例子说明仅 α∈L∞ 不足。

Result: 在 α≥0、D²α=βI 条件下，得到正分支 Δu>0 上解的内 C² 估计；无梯度情形亦获相应估计；并给出反例表明仅有 α 的 L∞ 界不能保证所需估计。

Conclusion: 通过升维与四次修正的化归，将带一阶扰动（及梯度项）的 σ₂ 方程纳入二次型 Hessian 方程框架，得到内 C² 估计；同时说明 α 的结构性假设（如 D²α=βI）不可由单纯有界性替代。

Abstract: This paper establishes an interior $C^2$ estimate for solutions of \[ σ_2(D^2u)+α(x)σ_1(D^2u)=f(x,u,Du)>0, \] on the positive branch $Δu>0$, under the assumptions $α\ge0$ and $D^2α=βI$. Using one extra variable and a quartic correction, the equation is reduced to a quadratic Hessian equation in one higher dimension. The terms involving $D^2u$ cancel, so the interior estimate of Li--Wu \cite{LW} applies. The gradient-independent case is also treated. Finally, an example shows that an $L^\infty$ bound on $α$ alone is not enough.

</details>


### [305] [Logarithmic Hessian estimates for non-uniformly elliptic equations in non-divergence form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09494)
*Jongmyeong Kim,Se-Chan Lee*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为非散度型非一致椭圆方程建立了统一的Hessian对数估计，并给出反例说明对数指数的尖锐性，同时推广到强解及Grushin型方程的正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非一致椭圆方程中解的正则性通常因椭圆性常数退化而变差，幂次型Hessian估计往往失效，需要确定可行的最优估计形式及其指数。作者旨在给出统一的Hessian估计并厘清其最优性。

Method: 证明基于凸分析中的测度估计，避免使用局部化或覆盖论证；同时构造反例排除一致幂次型Hessian估计，以验证对数指数的尖锐性（二维情形）；并将估计推广到强解。

Result: 得到非散度型非一致椭圆方程解的统一Hessian对数估计；反例表明不存在统一的幂次型Hessian估计，说明对数指数在平面上是尖锐的；估计可推广至强解。

Conclusion: 所建立的对数Hessian估计是非一致椭圆方程中在幂次估计不可行情形下的最优结果，并可用于获得Grushin型方程的正则性结论。

Abstract: We establish uniform logarithmic Hessian estimates for solutions of non-uniformly elliptic equations in non-divergence form. The proof is based on a measure estimate from convex analysis without localization or covering arguments. Moreover, we construct a counterexample that rules out uniform power-type Hessian estimates and shows the sharpness of the logarithmic exponent in the plane. Finally, we extend our estimates to strong solutions and apply them to obtain regularity results for Grushin-type equations.

</details>


### [306] [New result on Lane-Emden conjecture with exponents in a disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09526)
*Haoyang Lu,Zhitao Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究Lane-Emden系统的Liouville定理，在无增长或衰减假设条件下，证明了一个与临界双曲线相切的圆盘内（除临界点外）不存在正经典解，并扩展了已有判据未覆盖的参数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Lane-Emden猜想及Lane-Emden系统的Liouville型定理，旨在刻画使得该系统不存在正经典解的指数条件，推广和补充Busca-Manásevich、Souplet以及Li-Li-Wei等人的已有结果（对N≥5，新圆盘包含未被这些判据覆盖的非空开集）。

Method: 先利用已知Liouville判据缩减指数范围；随后构造向量场导出散度不等式，结合Sobolev嵌入定理、Gagliardo-Nirenberg插值不等式和Young不等式，得到对任意正解(u,v)的一致局部积分界∫_{B_1}(u^r v^{2p}+u^{2q} v^s)dx ≤ C(N,p,q)；最后通过伸缩(scaling)论证得到正经典解的不存在性。

Result: 证明：当N≥3、p,q>0、pq>1，且(p−2/(N−2))²+(q−2/(N−2))²≤2N²/(N−2)²时，系统在圆盘内（除p=q=(N+2)/(N−2)外）没有正经典解；无需任何增长或衰减假设，且该圆盘与临界双曲线相切。特别地，对N≥5，该圆盘包含不被相关已有判据覆盖的非空开集。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Lane-Emden系统Liouville定理的新结果，扩大了指数的可判据范围，尤其对N≥5覆盖了此前文献未涉及的非空开集，推进了对Lane-Emden猜想相关问题的理解。

Abstract: We obtain new results on Lane-Emden conjecture to prove a Liouville theorem for the Lane-Emden system $$\begin{cases} -Δu=v^p,& x\in \mathbb{R}^N,\\ -Δv=u^q,& x\in \mathbb{R}^N,\end{cases}$$ with $N\ge3$, $p,q>0$, $pq>1$, and \[ \left(p-\frac2{N-2}\right)^2+ \left(q-\frac2{N-2}\right)^2\le\frac{2N^2}{(N-2)^2}, \] the system has no positive classical solution as $(p,q)$ in the disk unless $p=q=(N+2)/(N-2)$, no growth or decay assumptions are imposed, and the disk is tangent to the critical hyperbola. Especially, for $N\ge5$, the disk contains a nonempty open set not covered by the criteria of Busca-Manásevich~\cite{BM} and Souplet~\cite{Souplet}, or by the explicit conditions of Li-Li-Wei~\cite{LLW}. After reducing the exponent range by known Liouville criteria, we first construct vector fields to derive a divergence inequality, then apply Sobolev embedding theorem, Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality, and Young's inequality to give a uniform local integral bound $\int_{B_1}(u^rv^{2p}+u^{2q}v^s)dx\le C(N,p,q)$ for suitable weights for any positive solution $(u,v)$, finally the nonexistence of positive classical solutions follows by scaling.

</details>


### [307] [Quantitative thermoviscous Darcy limits in periodic and bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09528)
*Jiaojiao Pan,Luqi Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了三维非均匀不可压缩流体在温度依赖黏性与准静态热反馈下、含周期穿孔结构的定量Darcy极限，误差以加权初始差异及关于$\varepsilon$的幂次上界给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带温度依赖黏性与准静态热反馈的非均匀不可压缩流体在多孔介质中的均质化，需明确导电率缩放如何保留热反馈，并给出定量Darcy极限误差估计。

Method: 考虑平坦环面或$C^{3,\beta}$有界域，孔洞尺寸$\varepsilon^\alpha$、间距$\varepsilon$（$1<\alpha<3$）；流体与固体导电率取$\sigma_\varepsilon^2\kappa_f,\ \sigma_\varepsilon^2\kappa_s$，$\sigma_\varepsilon^2\sim\varepsilon^{3-\alpha}$。采用独立于界面的温度估计控制黏性差异，加权Stokes残差保留系数与胞压交换子；有界情形使用无散度壁面修正与胞元散度修复，并构造固定参数微观弱解。

Result: 在足够正则的参考解与显式吸收条件下，密度、速度与温度的平方误差由加权初始差异加上$\varepsilon^{\alpha-1}+\varepsilon^{3-\alpha}$（环面）或$\varepsilon^{\alpha-1}+\varepsilon^{(3-\alpha)/2}$（有界域）控制。

Conclusion: 建立了含热反馈的多孔介质中非均匀不可压缩流的定量Darcy极限，局部强有效解需额外的收缩条件，有界域情形还需更强的边界正则性。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish quantitative Darcy limits for a three-dimensional inhomogeneous incompressible fluid with temperature-dependent viscosity and quasi-static thermal feedback. The domain is either the flat torus or a bounded domain with $C^{3,β}$ boundary, $0<β<1$. The holes have size $\varepsilon^α$ and separation of order $\varepsilon$, with $1<α<3$. Before normalization, the fluid and solid conductivities are $σ_\varepsilon^2κ_f$ and $σ_\varepsilon^2κ_s$, where $σ_\varepsilon^2\sim\varepsilon^{3-α}$. This scaling preserves the thermal feedback in the limit. For a sufficiently regular reference solution and under an explicit absorption condition, the squared density, velocity and temperature errors are bounded by the weighted initial discrepancy together with $\varepsilon^{α-1}+\varepsilon^{3-α}$ on the torus and $\varepsilon^{α-1}+\varepsilon^{(3-α)/2}$ in a bounded domain. An interface-independent temperature estimate controls the viscosity discrepancy, and a weighted Stokes residual retains the coefficient and cell-pressure commutators. The bounded comparison uses a solenoidal wall correction and cellwise divergence repair. We also construct fixed-parameter microscopic weak solutions. The local strong effective solutions require a separate contraction condition in both settings and stronger boundary regularity in the bounded case.

</details>


### [308] [Shortest Paths in Domains with Crack-Induced Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09540)
*Phiet Dau The,Phan Thanh An*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将含裂纹区域的最短路径问题建模为带裂纹状态约束的Hamilton–Jacobi方程，其中裂纹结合部分对应变分不等式（裂纹为可触及可沿行但不可穿越的双侧障碍），通过轨迹法建立比较原理与值函数唯一性，并给出滑移/照亮两种机制及尖端衍射律的完整几何刻画，数值模拟验证了闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含裂纹域中的最短路径问题涉及低维状态约束（裂纹两侧唇），经典Hamilton–Jacobi理论中Crandall–Ishii–Lions加倍技术在裂纹处失效，因此需要一个能处理此类障碍约束的适定性与几何刻画框架。

Method: 将问题表述为携带裂纹状态约束的Hamilton–Jacobi方程；在裂纹结合部分以变分不等式形式刻画（切向允许传播、禁止穿越）；采用基于轨迹的论证替代经典加倍技术，建立比较原理并证明值函数唯一性；由最优性条件推导自由边界及滑移/照亮机制的几何结构。

Result: 建立了比较原理与值函数唯一性；变分不等式在裂纹上诱导出自由边界，将滑移机制（最优轨迹沿裂纹有限段运行）与照亮机制（直接射线横向到达裂纹）分离；完整刻画两种机制，包括掠射（切向）接触、规则裂纹点无反射及裂纹尖端满足衍射律；在可精确求解构型上的正问题模拟精确复现三种机制与尖端衍射律，与闭式解精度一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为含裂纹的最短路径/Hamilton–Jacobi问题提供了严格的适定性理论与几何描述，表明裂纹尖端衍射等波前行为可纯由最优性导出，并被数值验证。

Abstract: We formulate the shortest-path problem in a domain containing a crack as a Hamilton--Jacobi equation carrying a state constraint on the crack, which on the binding part of the crack takes the form of a variational inequality: the crack is a two-sided barrier that may be touched and followed but not traversed, so that it acts as a lower-dimensional state constraint, carried separately by each of its two lips, which admits tangential propagation but forbids crossing. We establish a comparison principle and uniqueness of the value function through trajectory-based arguments that circumvent the failure of the classical doubling technique of Crandall, Ishii, and Lions at the crack. The variational inequality induces a free boundary on the crack separating a sliding regime, where optimal trajectories run along the crack over a finite segment, from an illuminated regime, where the crack is reached transversally by direct rays; we give a complete geometric characterization of both regimes, including grazing (tangential) contact, the absence of reflection at regular crack points, and a diffraction law at crack tips, all derived from optimality alone. A simulation of the forward problem on an exactly solvable configuration reproduces the three regimes and the tip-diffraction law to the accuracy of the closed-form solution.

</details>


### [309] [Faber-Krahn inequality for the first Dirichlet eigenvalues of the logarithmic Hardy operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09578)
*Ying Wang,Tao Zhang,Wanwan Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究对数 Hardy 算子 \(\mathcal{L}_\mu=(-\Delta)^{\ln}+2\mu\log\frac{1}{|x|}\) 的 Dirichlet 特征值 Faber--Krahn 不等式。证明 \(\mu\in(-1,0)\) 时球最优，\(\mu>0\) 时不等式失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Faber--Krahn 不等式描述固定体积下 Dirichlet 第一特征值的最优集合。本文将该问题推广到含对数 Hardy 位势的非局部算子 \(\mathcal{L}_\mu\)，探究其最优形状及参数 \(\mu\) 对不等式成立性的影响。

Method: 在有界 Lipschitz 区域 \(\Omega\subset\mathbb{R}^N\)（含原点）上考察 \(\mathcal{L}_\mu\) 的 Dirichlet 特征值。对 \(\mu\in(-1,0)\)，通过分析证明固定体积下以原点为心的球最小化第一特征值，且等式仅对该球成立。对 \(\mu>0\)，构造显式的双球加细管区域反例。

Result: 对 \(\mu\in(-1,0)\)，建立 Faber--Krahn 不等式：固定体积下以原点为心的球最小化第一特征值，等式当且仅当区域为该球时成立。对 \(\mu>0\)，通过显式双球加细管构造证明该不等式失效。

Conclusion: 对数 Hardy 算子的 Faber--Krahn 不等式成立与否取决于参数 \(\mu\) 的符号：负区间 \(\mu\in(-1,0)\) 具有球最优性，而正区间 \(\mu>0\) 则发生失效。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is to study Faber--Krahn inequality of
  Dirichlet eigenvalues $\{ λ^{\ln,μ}_{k}(Ω)\}_{k\in \N}$
  for the logarithmic Hardy operator \[ \mathcal{L}_μ=(-Δ)^{\ln}+2μ\log\frac{1}{|x|} \] on a bounded Lipschitz domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^{N}$ containing the origin, with $μ>-1$. We establish a Faber--Krahn inequality for $μ\in(-1,0)$, the ball centered at the origin minimizes the first eigenvalue at fixed volume, with equality only for that ball. Finally, we show this inequality fails for $μ>0$ via an explicit two-ball-and-tube construction.

</details>


### [310] [Regularity Estimates for Quasilinear Elliptic Equations with Measure Data and Matrix-Valued Weight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09594)
*Sun-Sig Byun,Xiatong Li,Yeonghun Youn*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带有限Radon测度和退化矩阵权$\mathbb{M}$的拟线性椭圆Dirichlet问题解的存在性与局部梯度正则性；在对$\mathbb{M}$施加小的log-BMO条件下，证明SOLA存在并建立局部Calderón-Zygmund估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典椭圆正则性理论通常要求系数或权满足较强可积性/BMO条件；对于退化矩阵权与测度数据的拟线性问题，SOLA的存在性及梯度加权正则性尚需在更弱（小log-BMO）假设下加以刻画，以扩展适用范围并刻画权与数据的定量关系。

Method: 对权$\mathbb{M}$假设小log-BMO条件，选取逼近问题（正则化数据/系数）构造近似解序列；利用紧性与弱收敛论证，取极限得到SOLA；随后借助分数极大函数与Calderón-Zygmund型加权估计技术，对加权梯度$|\mathbb{M}|^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\mathbb{M}Du$建立局部可积性/估计。
