Result: 在$\mathbb{M}$满足小log-BMO条件时：(1) 证明有限Radon测度数据的拟线性椭圆Dirichlet问题存在SOLA；(2) 建立局部Calderón-Zygmund估计，将$|\mathbb{M}|^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\mathbb{M}Du$的局部可积性上界由分数极大函数$\mathcal{M}_1(\mu)^{\frac{1}{p-1}}$控制。

Conclusion: 小log-BMO条件足以保证测度数据拟线性椭圆问题的SOLA存在，并获得权-梯度组合量的局部正则性估计，说明该条件在退化加权情形下是有效且可用的正则性框架。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the existence and local gradient regularity of solutions to a quasilinear elliptic Dirichlet problem with finite Radon measure and a degenerate matrix-valued weight. Under a small log-BMO condition on $\mathbb M$, we first prove the existence of a SOLA (solution obtained by a limit of approximations) and then establish a local Calderón-Zygmund estimate for $|\mathbb{M}|^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\mathbb{M} Du$ in terms of the fractional maximal function $\mathcal{M}_1(μ)^{\frac{1}{p-1}}$.

</details>


### [311] [Stability of optimal transport on sub-Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09688)
*Bang-Xian Han,Zhuo-Nan Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究平方Carnot–Carathéodory距离下最优传输的稳定性，证明了Riemannian与fat次Riemannian流形上映射的sharp $W_1^{1/4}$稳定性，以及John域上Kantorovich势的对数$L^2$稳定性，并给出势的Hölder指数最优范围及相应下界构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画最优传输在次Riemannian几何中的稳定性，明确映射与Kantorovich势相对于$W_1$扰动的最优正则性指数，并揭示次Riemannian结构（步数、秩等）对稳定性的影响。

Method: 在完备Riemannian与fat次Riemannian流形上，假设源支撑有界与密度上界，证明最优传输映射的sharp $W_1^{1/4}$估计；在John域与双侧密度界下，对源附近equiregular的完备光滑次Riemannian流形及任意紧目标，利用Kantorovich泛函的定量凹性得到势的对数$L^2$稳定性。映射估计源于对偶缺陷的强制性，势估计源于Kantorovich泛函的定量凹性。并通过三原子例子与构造光滑传输族给出指数上界与步数$s$相关的$t^{1/s}$退化。

Result: 映射获得sharp的$W_1^{1/4}$稳定性；势在John域上获得对数$L^2$稳定性，在two-generating结构上得到任意$0<α<1/2$的Hölder指数，在Riemannian、fat设定及所有step-two Carnot群上得到指数$1/2$。三原子例子表明：在水平秩至少为2的任意光滑次Riemannian流形上，映射与势相对于$W_1$的指数分别不超过$1/4$与$1/2$。并构造出在步长$s$的equiregular区域中，$W_1$目标扰动为$t$阶时映射扰动为$t^{1/s}$阶的光滑传输族。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了次Riemannian最优传输稳定性的最优指数刻画：映射指数$1/4$与势指数$1/2$在一般光滑次Riemannian流形上不可改进，且稳定性随步长增加出现$t^{1/s}$型退化；证明机制分别建立在对偶缺陷强制性与Kantorovich泛函定量凹性之上。

Abstract: We study the stability of optimal transport for the squared Carnot--Carathéodory distance. We prove the sharp $W_1^{1/4}$ stability estimate for optimal transport maps on complete Riemannian and fat sub-Riemannian manifolds, assuming bounded source support and an upper density bound. On John domains with two-sided density bounds, we obtain logarithmic $L^2$ stability of Kantorovich potentials on complete smooth sub-Riemannian manifolds that are equiregular near the source, with arbitrary compact targets. For potentials, we obtain every Hölder exponent $0<α<1/2$ on two-generating structures, and the exponent $1/2$ in the Riemannian and fat settings and on all step-two Carnot groups. Three-atom examples show that the map and potential exponents with respect to $W_1$ cannot exceed $1/4$ and $1/2$, respectively, on any smooth sub-Riemannian manifold of horizontal rank at least two. We also construct smooth transport families whose target variation of order $t$ in $W_1$ produces map variation of order $t^{1/s}$ in an equiregular region of step $s$. The map estimates follow from coercivity of the duality defect, while the potential estimates follow from quantitative concavity of the Kantorovich functional.

</details>


### [312] [Strichartz estimates on two-dimensional irrational tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09689)
*Ming Wang,Yuanjia Xiong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了二维环面上色散关系 $n_1^2+\alpha n_2^2$（$\alpha>0$ 任意）的 $L^4$ Strichartz 估计，损失由归一化平方傅里叶系数的 Shannon 熵及其支撑集基数 $S$ 控制，在固定时间区间上得到 $(\log\#S)^{1/2}$ 的范数损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为无理色散情形提供 Herr–Kwak 基数估计的对应结果，并将 Bourgain–Demeter 在频率盒上 $N^\varepsilon$ 的损失改进为 $(\log N)^{1/2}$。

Method: 综合运用加权关联（incidence）估计、光滑局部化以及熵递归（entropy recursion）技术。

Result: 对任意 $\alpha>0$ 建立了 $L^4$ Strichartz 估计，损失由 Shannon 熵与支撑集基数控制，得到 $(\log\#S)^{1/2}$ 的范数损失，且常数对 $\alpha$ 局部一致。

Conclusion: 该结果为无理色散情形下频率支撑基数型 Strichartz 估计给出了熵控制框架，改进了已有对数损失的最优界。

Abstract: We prove an $L^4$ Strichartz estimate for the dispersion $n_1^2+αn_2^2$ on $\mathbb T^2$, for every $α>0$. The loss is controlled by the Shannon entropy of the normalized squared Fourier coefficients and the cardinality of their support $S$. In particular, we obtain a norm loss of $(\log\#S)^{1/2}$ on fixed time intervals, with constants locally uniform in $α$. This provides an irrational-dispersion counterpart of the cardinality estimate of Herr and Kwak [Forum Math. Pi (2024)] and improves the $N^\varepsilon$ loss of Bourgain and Demeter [Ann. of Math. (2015)] to $(\log N)^{1/2}$ on frequency boxes. The proof combines weighted incidence estimates, smooth localization, and an entropy recursion.

</details>


### [313] [Carleman Estimates for Critical Electromagnetic Schrödinger Operators on Conic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09721)
*Baiyang Yin,Fang Zhang,Junyong Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文为锥奇异空间上带标度临界电磁奇异势的薛定谔算子建立了L^p-L^q型Carleman估计，并由此证明唯一延拓性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异空间（如锥形空间）上的色散型偏微分方程研究具有重要意义，而带奇异势的电磁薛定谔算子的Carleman估计及唯一延拓性此前尚缺乏针对标度临界势与锥奇点的系统性结果。

Method: 在度量为g=dr^2+r^2h、X=C(Y)为闭黎曼流形(Y,h)上乘积锥的d≥3维锥奇异空间上，采用二次权φ(r)=1/4(log r)^2，建立L^p-L^q型Carleman估计：‖e^{τφ}u‖_{L^{2d/(d-2)}(X)}≤C‖e^{τφ}H_{A,V}u‖_{L^{2d/(d+2)}(X)}，且关于τ一致。

Result: 证明了该电磁薛定谔算子在锥奇异空间上带标度临界电磁奇异势的Carleman估计，估计常数关于τ一致；当截面Y=S^{d-1}时，作为应用进一步证明了该算子的唯一延拓性质。

Conclusion: 该Carleman估计为锥奇异空间上奇异电磁薛定谔算子的唯一延拓性提供了有效工具，并可推广至更一般的奇异空间几何与分析问题。

Abstract: We establish an $L^p$--$L^q$-type Carleman estimate for the Schrödinger operator $H_{A,V}$ with scaling-critical electromagnetic singular potentials on a conical singular space $(X,g)$ of dimension $d\geq3$, where the metric is $g=dr^2+r^2h$ and $X=C(Y)=(0,\infty)_r\times Y$ is a product cone over the closed Riemannian manifold $(Y,h)$. More precisely, we prove $$ \|e^{τφ}u\|_{L^{2d/(d-2)}(X)}\le C\,\|e^{τφ}H_{A,V}u\|_{L^{2d/(d+2)}(X)} $$ with the quadratic weight $φ(r)=\tfrac14(\log r)^2$, uniformly in $τ$. As an application of this Carleman estimate, we prove the unique continuation property for this electromagnetic Schrödinger operator when the cross-section is $Y=\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$.

</details>


### [314] [Rigidity of stationary black holes with small ADM angular momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09729)
*Alexandru F. Radu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了ADM角动量足够小的光滑渐近平坦稳态真空黑洞的全局刚性，回答了Alexakis-Ionescu-Klainerman提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alexakis、Ionescu和Klainerman此前的工作需要假设分岔球处稳态场足够小，本文旨在将该小性假设替换为无穷远处角动量足够小，从而推进稳态真空黑洞的全局刚性问题。

Method: 将真空度规变换为一族Einstein-Maxwell度规，在其极限端比较零聚焦与紧致极大超曲面上的面积-电荷不等式（通过沿该族连续延拓构造），由等号成立确定变换后的度规及其稳态场，再通过逆变换恢复Kerr度规；并利用两个Kerr恒等式给出所需视界估计。

Result: 证明了在全局正则性、分岔和极大超曲面假设下，ADM角动量足够小（阈值依赖于固定几何界）的光滑渐近平坦稳态真空黑洞具有全局刚性；并建立了任意角动量下无穷远处的Kerr刚性。

Conclusion: 文章以无穷远处角动量的小性替代分岔球处稳态场的小性，给出了此类稳态真空黑洞全局刚性的证明，并最终导出Kerr度规。

Abstract: We prove global rigidity of smooth asymptotically flat stationary vacuum black holes with sufficiently small ADM angular momentum, under global regularity, bifurcation, and maximal-hypersurface assumptions, with a threshold depending on fixed geometric bounds. This answers the question of Alexakis, Ionescu, and Klainerman concerning replacement of their smallness assumption on the stationary field at the bifurcation sphere by smallness of the angular momentum at infinity. The main step establishes Kerr rigidity near infinity for arbitrary angular momentum. We transform the vacuum metric into an Einstein--Maxwell family and compare null focusing at its limiting end with the area--charge inequality on compact maximal hypersurfaces, constructed by continuation through the family. Equality determines both the transformed metric and its stationary field, from which the inverse transformation recovers Kerr. Two Kerr identities then give the horizon estimate required for the global conclusion.

</details>


### [315] [Strong transport noise prevents self-intersection in 2D fluid-structure interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09811)
*Dominic Breit,Prince Romeo Mensah*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维粘性不可压缩流体与边界上弹性壳的相互作用：壳由完全弹性线性化梁方程建模，并证明在结构方程中加入充分强的传输噪声后，结构在任意给定终止时刻前、以任意小于1的规定概率不会发生自交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体-结构相互作用中，即便对完全弹性板这一理想情形，如何长期排除结构的自交仍是一个公开问题。作者希望借助噪声的正则化/去奇异化效应，绕开确定性框架下的困难，从概率意义上保证结构不发生自交。

Method: 考虑二维粘性不可压缩流体与占据流体域边界的弹性壳耦合，壳采用完全弹性线性化梁方程建模；在结构方程中引入物理动机明确且足够强的传输噪声（transport noise），并在给定终止时刻与规定概率的框架下分析系统的自交行为。

Result: 证明加入充分强的传输噪声后，结构在任意给定终止时刻之前发生自交的概率可被压到任意小于1的规定水平，即自交以一种可控的概率方式被排除。

Conclusion: 结果表明噪声可作为一种有效机制，在概率意义下避免弹性结构在流体相互作用中的长期自交，为这一流体-结构相互作用公开问题提供了新的随机化处理思路。

Abstract: We consider the interaction of a viscous incompressible fluid with a flexible elastic shell occupying the boundary of the fluid's domain in two space dimensions. The shell is modelled by a perfectly elastic linearised beam equation. It is an open problem in fluid-structure interaction, even for the idealised case of a perfectly elastic plate, if a self-intersection of the structure can be excluded in the long run. We show that by adding a physically well-motived and sufficiently strong transport noise in the structure equation, the self-intersection does not occur before any given end-time with any prescribed probability smaller than 1.

</details>


### [316] [Self-similar solutions to the steady Navier-Stokes equations in arbitrarily high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09840)
*Changfeng Gui,Hao Liu,Yun Wang,Chunjing Xie*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将稳态Navier-Stokes方程 $(-1)$-齐次解的存在性从 $4\leq n\leq16$ 推广到所有高维空间，去除了维数限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作[2]仅在 $4\leq n\leq16$ 维建立了无小性假设的 $(-1)$-齐次解存在性，本文旨在去除这一维数限制，将结果推广至所有更高维。

Method: 在 $\mathbb R^n\setminus\{0\}$ 中研究带 $(-3)$-齐次局部Lipschitz力的稳态Navier-Stokes方程的 $(-1)$-齐次解。核心创新是采用源-通量方法(source-flux method)，并将Wang-Yang[28]的压力加权恒等式推广到齐次情形，得到球面上总压头的维数无关先验估计；再结合[2]中径向速度与总压头的结构关系，闭合所有维数下的先验估计。

Result: 建立了球面上总压头的维数无关先验估计，并据此在所有高维（无维数上限）证明了自相似解即 $(-1)$-齐次解的存在性。

Conclusion: 通过源-通量方法与压力加权恒等式的齐次化推广，成功移除维数限制，实现了所有高维下稳态Navier-Stokes方程 $(-1)$-齐次解的存在性。

Abstract: We study $(-1)$-homogeneous solutions of the steady Navier-Stokes equations in $\mathbb R^n\setminus\{0\}$ with $(-3)$-homogeneous, locally Lipschitz forces. Previous work [2] established the existence of $(-1)$-homogeneous solutions without any smallness assumption in dimensions $4\leq n\leq16$. In this paper, we remove the dimension restriction and obtain the existence of self-similar solutions in all higher dimensions. The new ingredient is a dimension-independent a priori estimate for the total head pressure on the sphere, obtained by adapting the source-flux method and the weighted identity of the pressure of Wang and Yang [28] to the homogeneous setting. This combines with the structural relation between the radial velocity and the total head pressure used in [2] to close the a priori estimates for the solutions in all dimensions.

</details>


### [317] [Observable sets for the Schrödinger equation with potential $\lvert x\rvert$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09850)
*Lu Chen,Haolin Liu,Hongyu Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维薛定谔方程（算子 $H=-d^2/dx^2+|x|$，定义在 $L^2(\mathbb R)$ 上）的可观测集问题，证明可测集在每个正时刻均可观测当且仅当它是 thick 集；且对固定观测时间，可观测常数在所有具有给定 thickness 参数的可测集上一致成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Gengsheng Wang 针对该非谐振子提出的关于 thick 集与可观测集之间关系的公开问题。

Method: 基于算子 $H=-d^2/dx^2+|x|$ 生成的一维薛定谔方程，研究其可测观测集，并借助 thickness 参数刻画可观测性，进一步分析固定观测时间下可观测常数对给定 thickness 参数的一致估计。

Result: 证明可测集在每个正时刻均可观测当且仅当该集合为 thick 集；并证明对每个固定观测时间，可观测常数在所有具有给定 thickness 参数的可测集上一致。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了该非谐振子薛定谔方程的可观测集，即 thick 性与全时刻可观测性等价，解决了 Wang 提出的公开问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study observable sets for the one-dimensional Schrödinger equation associated with the operator $H=-d^2/dx^2+|x|$ on $L^2(\mathbb R)$. We prove that a measurable set is observable at every positive time if and only if it is thick. For each fixed observation time, the observability constant is uniform over all measurable sets with prescribed thickness parameters. The theorem answers the question concerning the relationship between thick and observable set raised by Gengsheng Wang [12] for this anharmonic oscillator.

</details>


### [318] [Nonnegative Viscosity and Weak Solutions to Trudinger's Parabolic Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09905)
*Peter Lindqvist,Mikko Parviainen,Jarkko Siltakoski*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究方程 ∂t(|u|^{p-2}u) = div(|∇u|^{p-2}∇u) 的粘性解与弱解之间的关系，证明至少对非负函数二者等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性解与弱解是偏微分方程中两套独立的解概念，二者关系在一般情形下并不清楚，本文旨在厘清该方程下两种解概念的联系。

Method: 通过理论分析，考察该非线性抛物型方程（涉及 p-拉普拉斯型算子）的粘性解与弱解定义，并证明其等价性。

Result: 证明了至少对于非负函数，粘性解与弱解是等价的概念。

Conclusion: 该方程在非负函数范围内，粘性解与弱解可相互对应，两种解概念等价。

Abstract: We study the relation between viscosity solutions and weak solutions of the equation $$\partial_t\left(|u|^{p-2}u\right)\,=\, \operatorname{div}(|\nabla u|^{p-2}\nabla u).$$ We prove that at least for non-negative functions these are equivalent concepts.

</details>


### [319] [Asymptotic stability of the 2D Boussinesq equations around Couette flow in infinite channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10005)
*Qionglei Chen,Jiakun Jin,Zhen Li,Changxing Miao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带Navier-slip边界条件的二维Boussinesq系统在无穷通道中Couette流附近的稳定性阈值，通过构造双层频率相关的时间分解并利用增强耗散效应，证明了在初始扰动满足特定小性条件下全局渐近稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boussinesq系统在Couette流附近的稳定性受温度-涡量耦合诱导的非线性回声链影响，传统方法难以处理此类耦合效应，需要新的分解框架来刻画稳定性阈值。

Method: 构造双层频率相关的时间分解，两层分别对应方程的无穷与有限叠加原理；充分利用每一分解层次上的增强耗散效应来处理非线性回声链。

Result: 若初始扰动满足‖ω^in‖_{H^4∩L^1_xH^4_y}≤cν^{1/3}且‖θ^in‖_{H^5∩L^1_xH^5_y}≤cν^{2/3+}，则Boussinesq系统存在全局渐近稳定解。

Conclusion: 该方法为具有耦合效应的流体力学方程提供了一种有效的分析途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the stability threshold of the two-dimensional Boussinesq system around the Couette flow in an infinite channel $\mathbb{R}\times[-1,1]$ under Navier-slip boundary condition. To address nonlinear echo chains induced by temperature-vorticity coupling, we construct a two-level frequency-dependent temporal decomposition, with the two levels matched to the infinite and finite superposition principles for the equation, respectively. By fully exploiting the enhanced dissipation effect at each decomposition level, we prove that if the initial perturbation $(ω^{in}, θ^{in})$ around the Couette flow satisfies $\|ω^{in}\|_{H^4_{x,y}\cap L^1_xH^4_y}\leq cν^{\frac{1}{3}}$, and $\|θ^{in}\|_{H^5_{x,y}\cap L^1_xH^5_y}\leq cν^{\frac{2}{3}+}$, the Boussinesq system admits a globally asymptotically stable solution. It should be emphasized that the method in this paper provides an effective approach for the hydrodynamic equations with coupling effects.

</details>


### [320] [Well-posedness of Strong Solutions for a Thermodynamically Consistent Diffuse-interface Model for Incompressible Two-phase Flows with a Soluble Surfactant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10009)
*Maurizio Grasselli,Bohan Ouyang,Hao Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究含可溶表面活性剂、不可压缩黏性牛顿流体二元混合物的热力学一致扩散界面模型，证明其强解的局部与全局适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Abels-Garcke-Grün 模型推广至含可溶表面活性剂、且具浓度依赖迁移率的情形，填补由常数迁移率到一般情形的强解理论空白。

Method: 在无滑移速度边界与相场变量及化学势齐次 Neumann 边界条件下，针对奇异势建立强解理论；采用能量方法与适定性的严格分离性分析。

Result: 三维得到局部强解，二维得到全局强解；二维在自然增长假设下强解与纯相严格分离并保证唯一性；三维在初值严格分离时获得局部唯一性。

Conclusion: 结果将 Abels-Garcke-Grün 模型的强解结论从常数迁移率推广到浓度依赖迁移率且与表面活性剂耦合的一般情形。

Abstract: We consider a thermodynamically consistent diffuse-interface model for binary mixtures of incompressible viscous Newtonian fluids with a soluble surfactant. This hydrodynamic system generalizes the Abels-Garcke-Grün model for two-phase flows with unmatched densities by incorporating an additional Cahn-Hilliard equation for the surfactant concentration. We study the strong well-posedness of the initial-boundary value problem with physically relevant singular potentials, subject to a no-slip boundary condition for the fluid velocity and homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions for the phase-field variables and the corresponding chemical potentials. First, we establish the existence of local-in-time strong solutions in three dimensions and global-in-time strong solutions in two dimensions. In the two-dimensional case, we further show that, under natural growth assumptions on the singular potentials, strong solutions remain strictly separated from the pure phases, thereby guaranteeing uniqueness. In the three-dimensional case, we establish local-in-time uniqueness of strong solutions, provided that the initial data are strictly separated from the pure phases. Our results extend previous work on strong solutions to the Abels-Garcke-Grün model from the special case of constant mobility to the more general scenario with concentration-dependent mobilities and interactions with a surfactant.

</details>


### [321] [A priori Dirichlet energy bounds for positive Trudinger--Moser critical points on simply connected planar domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10086)
*Hongtao Hu,Qiaoran Wu,Wenming Zou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明二维光滑单连通有界域上带指数平方非线性问题 \(-\Delta u=\theta u e^{u^2}\) 的正解具有仅依赖区域的 Dirichlet 能量上界，进而排除了大能量下 Trudinger–Moser 泛函的非负约束临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 \(\theta u e^{u^2}\) 型指数平方非线性 Dirichlet 问题在二维情形下的能量有界性，以及 Trudinger–Moser 泛函在大预设能量处是否存在约束临界点。

Method: 结合共形变换后得到的加权 Pohozaev 恒等式、归一化测度的集中分析以及局部径向比较；比较假设由局部积分估计得到，无需先验能量界；再利用外部 Green 位势估计排除无界能量序列。

Result: 证明所有正解的 Dirichlet 能量被仅依赖 \(\Omega\) 的常数界住；因此 Trudinger–Moser 泛函在足够大的预设能量处不存在非负约束临界点。

Conclusion: 该问题在二维单连通域上具有统一能量界，并由此得到 Trudinger–Moser 泛函大能量非负约束临界点的非存在性。

Abstract: \noindent Let \(\Om\subset\R^2\) be a smooth bounded simply connected domain. We prove that all positive solutions of \(-Δu=θue^{u^2}\), with \(θ>0\) and zero Dirichlet boundary data, have Dirichlet energy bounded by a constant depending only on \(\Om\). Consequently, the Trudinger--Moser functional has no nonnegative constrained critical points at sufficiently large prescribed energies. The proof combines a weighted Pohozaev identity obtained after conformal change of variables, concentration analysis of normalized measures, and local radial comparison. The comparison hypotheses are obtained from local integral estimates, without an a priori energy bound. An estimate for the exterior Green potential then excludes sequences of unbounded energy.

</details>


### [322] [Sharp Hilbert transform norms on Schatten classes: classification and optimal spectral corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10158)
*Zhouyu Long*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明Hilbert变换在$L^p(\mathbb{R};\mathcal{S}_p)$上的范数与有序Hilbert Schur乘子在$\mathcal{S}_p$上的范数均为$C_p=\cot(\pi/(2p^*))$，其中$p^*=\max\{p,p/(p-1)\}$，从而证明Gohberg-Krupnik尖锐Volterra猜想，并同时解决Riesz-Titchmarsh猜想与Laeng猜想5.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Hilbert变换与Schur乘子的完全有界范数仅有非尖锐估计，Gohberg-Krupnik尖锐Volterra猜想、Riesz-Titchmarsh猜想及Laeng猜想5.7长期未决；本文旨在给出统一的最优范数刻画与有限维实现。

Method: 采用基于Heinävaara迹联合谱测度的谱缺陷恒等式，含两个非负余项；对有限符号实岭和刻画迹不等式，并将最小齐次超调和优函数识别为逐点最小加性特征值修正；相关角锥由非负位势刻画，并由有限幂零矩阵加权角测度的弱-*极限实现。

Result: $L^p(\mathbb{R};\mathcal{S}_p)$上Hilbert变换与$\mathcal{S}_p$上有序Hilbert Schur乘子范数均为$C_p=\cot(\pi/(2p^*))$；标量Hilbert变换与Schur乘子的规范完全有界范数相同；同时有限维实现解决了Riesz-Titchmarsh猜想与Laeng猜想5.7（所有移位）；范数恒等式推广到具$\ell^p$直和值域的有限族实Hilbert变换pencil。

Conclusion: Hilbert变换与Schur乘子的尖锐范数由$C_p=\cot(\pi/(2p^*))$精确给出，统一证明了多个经典猜想，并通过谱缺陷与角锥刻画将算子范数问题与迹不等式及超调和优函数理论联系起来。

Abstract: For every $1<p<\infty$, the Hilbert transform on $L^p(\mathbb{R};\mathcal{S}_p)$ and the ordered Hilbert Schur multiplier on $\mathcal{S}_p$ have norm $C_p=\cot(π/(2p^*))$, where $p^*=\max\{p,p/(p-1)\}$. The scalar Hilbert transform and the Schur multiplier have the same canonical completely bounded norm. This proves the Gohberg-Krupnik sharp Volterra conjecture. A simultaneous finite-dimensional realization also resolves the Riesz-Titchmarsh conjecture and Laeng's Conjecture 5.7 for all shifts. The norm identities extend to finite families of real Hilbert-transform pencils with $\ell^p$ direct-sum ranges.
  The proof uses a spectral-defect identity with two nonnegative remainders, based on Heinävaara's tracial joint spectral measure. It classifies trace inequalities for finite signed real ridge sums and identifies the least homogeneous superharmonic majorant as the pointwise least additive eigenvalue correction. The associated angular cone is characterized by nonnegative potentials and realized by weak-* limits of weighted angular measures of finite nilpotent matrices.

</details>


### [323] [Collapse and saturation for radial nodal solutions of a Lorentz--Minkowski mean curvature equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10166)
*Alberto Boscaggin,Francesca Colasuonno,Ricardo Ziegele*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究Lorentz–Minkowski平均曲率算子在球内径向Dirichlet问题的解在μ→+∞时的渐近行为，证明先验二分性（解要么一致趋于0，要么收敛到满足|u∞'|=1的分片仿射极限），并证明在适当指定零点数时两种情形均可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画Minkowski平均曲率方程在参数μ趋于无穷时解的渐近结构与零点性质，厘清是否所有解都退化为饱和的分片仿射轮廓，或存在趋于零的小解，从而完善对该奇异/非一致椭圆型问题的解集理解。

Method: 在径向设定下研究带参数λ, μ≥0的Minkowski平均曲率方程Dirichlet问题。首先证明关于规定零点数解族的先验二分性；再基于已有文献[A. Boscaggin, F. Colasuonno, R. Ziegele, arXiv:2607.15956 (2026)]的多重性结果，细化其中的shooting构造，用于获得一致趋于0的小解族，并通过backward shooting论证构造收敛到饱和分片仿射轮廓的大解族。

Result: 建立了μ→+∞时规定零点数解的先验二分性：解要么一致收敛到0，要么一致收敛到分片仿射极限函数u∞，且|u∞'|=1几乎处处成立并保持相同零点性质；进一步证明在适当指定零点数时两种替代情形都实际发生：存在一致趋于0的小解族，也存在收敛到饱和分片仿射轮廓的大解族。

Conclusion: 结果表明Minkowski平均曲率方程的径向Dirichlet解在μ→+∞时呈现两类截然不同的渐近行为——小解趋于零、大解趋于饱和分片仿射轮廓，且通过精细的shooting构造可在指定零点数下实现两种情形，深化了对该非线性方程解集结构的认识。

Abstract: We consider the Dirichlet problem for the Lorentz--Minkowski mean curvature operator in a radial setting, $$ -\operatorname{div}\left(\frac{\nabla u}{\sqrt{1-|\nabla u|^2}}\right) = λu + μg(u) \quad \text{in } \mathcal B_R, \qquad u = 0 \quad \text{on } \partial\mathcal B_R, $$ on a ball of radius $R$ centered at the origin $\mathcal B_R \subset \mathbb{R}^N$, where $λ, μ\geq 0$ are parameters, and the nonlinearity $g$ satisfies suitable growth assumptions at zero. We first prove an a priori dichotomy for the asymptotic behaviour, as $μ\to +\infty$, of solutions with a prescribed number of zeros: either they converge uniformly to $0$, or they converge to a piecewise affine limit function $u_\infty$, with $|u_\infty'|=1$ a.e., having the same nodal properties. We then show that, when the number of zeros is appropriately prescribed, both alternatives are actually realized. More precisely, building on the multiplicity result in [A. Boscaggin, F. Colasuonno, and R. Ziegele, \emph{Positive and nodal solutions for the Minkowski mean curvature equation: multiplicity and asymptotics}, arXiv:2607.15956 (2026)], we refine the shooting construction therein to obtain a family of small solutions converging to zero and, through a backward shooting argument, a family of large solutions converging to a saturated piecewise affine profile.

</details>


### [324] [A proof of the fractional Lane--Emden conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10188)
*Linfeng Mei,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明分数阶Lane--Emden系统在$\frac1{p+1}+\frac1{q+1}>\frac{n-2s}{n}$时不存在正的整解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将最近为经典Hénon--Lane--Emden系统发展的局部化势核virial与源层区间压力方法推广到分数阶Lane--Emden系统，在无径向对称、全局有界、有限能量或无穷远处衰减假设下证明Liouville定理。

Method: 采用局部化势核virial方法和源层区间压力法。核心观察是Riesz核与Newton核具有相同的对数导数结构，仅$n-2$换成$n-2s$。对两个本质非局部问题特殊处理：当$2s\le1$时单个核梯度项局部绝对不可积，需先合并两方程再取对角极限；当$2s>n-1$时核在直线上的限制无穷远处不可积，区间比较须用非负扩展积分表述。随后建立局部化virial恒等式、一维区间对不等式及平均源层估计，得到一致局部能量界。

Result: 对任意$n\ge2$、$0<s<1$、$p,q>0$，只要$\frac1{p+1}+\frac1{q+1}>\frac{n-2s}{n}$，系统$(-\Delta)^s u=v^p$、$(-\Delta)^s v=u^q$（$u,v>0$于$\mathbb{R}^n$）无严格正整解。

Conclusion: 在无附加对称性或衰减假设下，分数阶Lane--Emden系统在次临界参数范围满足Liouville型不存在性；方法通过势核virial-源层框架统一处理分数阶情形，并借亚临界标度使局部能量发散导出矛盾。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$, $0<s<1$, and $p,q>0$. We prove a Liouville theorem for the fractional Lane--Emden system \[
  (-Δ)^s u=v^p,\qquad (-Δ)^s v=u^q, \qquad u, v>0
  \qquad\text{in }\R^n. \] More precisely, we show that the system does not have a strictly positive entire solution whenever \[
  \frac1{p+1}+\frac1{q+1}>\frac{n-2s}{n}. \] No radial symmetry, global boundedness, finite-energy assumption, or prescribed decay at infinity is required. Our proof adapts the localized potential-kernel virial and source-layer interval-pressure method recently introduced for the classical Hénon--Lane--Emden system. The basic reason the method also works in the fractional setting is simple: the Riesz kernel associated with $(-Δ)^s$ has the same logarithmic derivative structure as the Newton kernel, with $n-2$ replaced by $n-2s$. However, two genuinely nonlocal points require special treatments. When $2s\le1$, the individual kernel-gradient terms are not locally absolutely integrable, so the two equations must be combined before the diagonal limit is taken. When $2s>n-1$, the kernel restricted to a line is not integrable at infinity, and the interval comparison must therefore be formulated using nonnegative extended integrals. After these issues are resolved, a localized virial identity, a one-dimensional interval-pair inequality, and an averaged source-layer estimate yield a universal local energy bound. The subcritical scaling then forces the same energy to diverge, giving the desired contradiction.

</details>


### [325] [The stability of Sharp Poincaré and Brascamp--Lieb inequalities with full monomial weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10337)
*Nguyen Lam,Guozhen Lu,Andrey Russanov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究满单项式权高斯测度的曲率-维数条件与泛函不等式，证明其满足积分曲率-维数条件 ICD(2,∞)，并由此建立最优常数为 2 的 Poincaré 不等式、Brascamp–Lieb 不等式的稳定性及改进的 Beckner 不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果表明单项式权高斯测度满足逐点曲率-维数条件 CD(1,∞)，但仅能对部分单项式权导出最优常数为 1 的 Poincaré 不等式，无法覆盖满单项式权情形。作者希望建立更适应的积分曲率-维数条件，从而获得满单项式权下的最优不等式及其稳定性。

Method: 首先证明满单项式权高斯测度满足积分曲率-维数条件 ICD(2,∞)；利用该条件证明最优常数为 2 的 Poincaré 不等式与 Brascamp–Lieb 不等式稳定性；将部分结果推广到凸锥上的齐次高斯测度；结合 Poincaré 不等式与超压缩性推导改进的 Beckner 不等式；发展 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 型生成元的 Laguerre 谱分解，导出 Poincaré 亏损的精确恒等式；进一步建立 Brascamp–Lieb 不等式的 L^2 与加权梯度稳定性估计。

Result: 得到满单项式权下的最优 Poincaré 不等式（常数 2）及其梯度稳定性；Brascamp–Lieb 不等式稳定性；改进的 Beckner 不等式在端点处恢复最优 Poincaré 与对数 Sobolev 常数；Poincaré 亏损的精确恒等式；当所有单项式指数大于 1 时，Brascamp–Lieb 不等式的 L^2 与加权梯度稳定性估计（含显式常数），其中 L^2 估计在次调和假设下可推广到对数凹齐次权；在尺度不变 Hessian 条件下得到改进的积分曲率-维数界，并确定了径向类中 Poincaré 不等式的最优常数。

Conclusion: 积分曲率-维数条件 ICD(2,∞) 是研究满单项式权高斯测度泛函不等式的有效工具，可统一导出最优 Poincaré 不等式、Brascamp–Lieb 稳定性、改进 Beckner 不等式及谱分解下的精确亏损恒等式，并可推广至凸锥齐次高斯测度与对数凹齐次权。

Abstract: It was known that the Gaussian measures with monomial weights satisfy the sharp pointwise curvature-dimension condition $CD(1, \infty)$, and consequently they allow us to derive the sharp constant $1$ for the Poincaré inequality only with partial monomial weights. In this paper, we first establish the Gaussian measures with full monomial weights satisfy the integrated curvature-dimension condition $ICD(2, \infty)$ which is sufficient for us to prove the sharp Poincaré inequalities with optimal constant $2$ and the stability of the Brascamp--Lieb inequality for full monomial Gaussian measures. Several of these results are extended to homogeneous Gaussian measures on convex cones. Using the sharp Poincaré inequality and the hypercontractivity, we obtain an improved Beckner inequality that recover the sharp Poincaré and logarithmic Sobolev constants at the endpoints. By developing a Laguerre spectral decomposition of the associated Ornstein--Uhlenbeck type generator, we derive an exact identity for the Poincaré deficit and obtain sharp gradient stability for the Poincaré inequality with full monomial weights. We further establish $L^2$ and weighted gradient stability estimates for the Brascamp--Lieb inequality with explicit constants when all monomial exponents exceed $1$. The $L^2$ estimate extends to log-concave homogeneous weights under a subharmonicity assumption. Finally, we obtain an improved integrated curvature-dimension bound under a scale-invariant Hessian condition and identify the sharp constant for the Poincaré inequality in the radial class.

</details>


### [326] [Uniqueness and stability of planar shock wave to 2D compressible Euler equations in the vanishing viscosity limit of compressible Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10341)
*Geng Chen,Jeffrey Cheng,Alexis F. Vasseur,Yi Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为二维可压缩Navier-Stokes方程（密度依赖粘性）引入全局弱解框架，首次建立了对物理相关的平面激波解的“消失粘性极限”选择准则，证明其在近平面初值扰动（激波方向扰动可任意大）下的唯一性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩Euler方程在平面激波初值下可能有无穷多熵弱解（Chiodaroli-De Lellis-Kreml），因此需要从粘性极限（Navier-Stokes）角度给出选择准则，以确定物理相关的唯一解。此前缺乏二维情形下全局时间弱解框架及相应的极限选择结果。

Method: 在R×T上构造二维可压缩Navier-Stokes（密度依赖粘性）的全局弱解概念；基于Bresch-Noble-Vila的T²相对κ-熵，建立二维Navier-Stokes弱解与一维平均初值强解之间的相对1/2-熵；弥合二维Euler表述的相对1/2-熵与Kang-Vasseur在拉格朗日坐标下的一维消失粘性极限激波解之间的结构鸿沟，从而得到选择准则。

Result: 在近平面初值出发的Navier-Stokes解的不可压极限类中，证明二维Euler平面激波解的唯一性与稳定性，且激波方向扰动可任意大；成功获得首个消失粘性极限选择准则。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至R×T²的三维极限选择准则，以及更复杂的平面Riemann解和相互作用波型。

Abstract: It is well known that the compressible Euler equations with planar shock initial data may admit infinitely many entropy weak solutions, as shown by Chiodaroli, De Lellis and Kreml [Comm. Pure Appl. Math. (2015)]. In this paper, we introduce a suitable notion of global-in-time weak solutions to the two-dimensional (2D) compressible Navier-Stokes equations with density-dependent viscosities in $\mathbb{R} \times \mathbb{T}$. Within this solution framework, we establish the first vanishing viscosity limit selection criterion for the physically relevant planar shock solution to the 2D compressible Euler equations. More precisely, we prove the uniqueness and stability of the 2D Euler planar shock solution within a class of inviscid limits of Navier-Stokes solutions emanating from near-planar initial data while the shock direction perturbation can be arbitrarily large. Starting from the relative $κ$-entropy for 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations in $\mathbb{T}^2$ by Bresch, Noble and Vila [ESAIM Proc. Surveys (2017)], we first establish the relative $\frac12$-entropy between 2D weak solution of compressible Navier-Stokes equations in $\mathbb{R} \times \mathbb{T}$ and one-dimensional (1D) strong solution for averaged initial data in $\mathbb{R}$. Then we achieve the desired vanishing viscosity limit selection criterion by successfully bridging the fundamental structural gap between 2D Eulerian formulation of the original Navier-Stokes system in the relative $\frac12$-entropy and 1D vanishing viscosity limit to a shock solution of Euler equations in Lagrangian coordinates by Kang and Vasseur [Invent. Math. (2021)]. The framework developed herein can be applied to the three-dimensional limit selection criterion in $\mathbb{R} \times \mathbb{T}^2$, as well as to more complex planar Riemann solutions and interacting wave patterns.

</details>


### [327] [Stability for a formally determined Lorentzian inverse problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10413)
*Spyridon Filippas,Lauri Oksanen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究洛伦兹流形上波动方程 $(\Box_g+q)u=0$ 中时空依赖势 $q$ 的反问题，在零锥叶层假设下证明了形式确定版本问题的Lipschitz稳定性，并由此得到洛伦兹Calderón问题中 $q$ 的唯一性，方法基于利用畸变平面波对Bukhgeim–Klibanov方法的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹流形上波动方程势函数的反演以及洛伦兹Calderón问题是几何反问题中的重要课题。已有结果多依赖较强或较特殊的几何条件，稳定性（尤其是Lipschitz型稳定性）与唯一性的适用范围仍有待扩展。本文旨在较一般的零锥叶层几何假设下建立稳定性与唯一性。

Method: 采用Bukhgeim–Klibanov方法的改进版本：引入畸变平面波（distorted plane waves）作为特殊解构造Carleman型估计与球面平均/时间反演论证，在零锥叶层（null-cone foliation）假设下处理形式确定（formally determined）的反问题，从而导出 Lipschitz 稳定性估计。

Result: 在零锥叶层假设下证明了形式确定反问题的Lipschitz稳定性；作为推论，得到洛伦兹Calderón问题中势 $q$ 的唯一性。

Conclusion: 零锥叶层为洛伦兹波动方程势反演提供了有效几何框架，畸变平面波结合改进的Bukhgeim–Klibanov方法可获得Lipschitz稳定性并推出唯一性，拓展了洛伦兹Calderón问题的结果。

Abstract: We consider the inverse problem of recovering a potential $q$, depending on space and time, in the wave equation $(\Box_g+q)u=0$ on a Lorentzian manifold. We prove Lipschitz stability for a formally determined version of this problem under a \textit{null-cone foliation} assumption. As a consequence, we obtain uniqueness in the Lorentzian Calderón problem for $q$ under this assumption. Our approach is based on a modification of the Bukhgeim--Klibanov method using distorted plane waves.

</details>


### [328] [Asymptotic Stability of Domain Walls for One Dimensional Schrödinger Map with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10458)
*Ze Li,Zifan Wang,Lifeng Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维含常数Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）的Schrödinger映射中静态畴壁小扰动的长时间动力学，证明了畴壁在平移与旋转对称意义下的渐近稳定性（模参数收敛、辐射以t^{-1/2}的尖锐速率衰减），并证明辐射呈现带显式对数相位修正的修正散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为含DMI的一维Schrödinger映射的静态畴壁，其扰动方程含导数非线性，线性化算子存在阈值共振，这使经典方法难以直接处理。作者希望在加权Sobolev空间中刻画小扰动长时间行为，并厘清导数损失与共振对稳定性和散射的影响。

Method: 在加权Sobolev空间中分析扰动方程的长时间动力学；针对线性化算子的阈值共振和方程中的导数非线性，采用几何约化（geometric reduction）来克服导数损失，并利用非线性相互作用中的结构性抵消。

Result: (i) 证明了畴壁模平移与旋转对称的渐近稳定性：相应的调制参数随时间趋于无穷而收敛，辐射以尖锐速率t^{-1/2}衰减；(ii) 证明了辐射呈现修正散射，其显式对数相位修正由长程三次相互作用产生。

Conclusion: 含DMI的一维Schrödinger映射静态畴壁的扰动具有渐近稳定性与修正散射行为；所发展的几何约化与结构性抵消技术可有效处理导数非线性与阈值共振带来的困难，为该类色散方程的长时间分析提供了途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the long time dynamics of small perturbations of static domain walls in weighted Sobolev spaces for the one dimensional Schrödinger map with constant Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction. The perturbation equation contains derivative nonlinearities, and its linearized operator has a threshold resonance. The results are twofold. (i) We prove the asymptotic stability of the domain wall modulo the translation and rotation symmetries. More precisely, the corresponding modulation parameters converge as time tends to infinity, while the radiation decays at the sharp rate $t^{-1/2}$. (ii) We prove that the radiation exhibits modified scattering, with an explicit logarithmic phase correction generated by the long range cubic interaction. The proof develops geometric reduction to overcome the loss of derivatives and exploits structural cancellations in the nonlinear interactions.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [329] [Numerical Bogoliubov approximation of bosonic many-body quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08956)
*Yoann Le Hénaff,Christian Lubich,Peter Pickl*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种求解平均场标度下N≫1玻色子含时薛定谔方程的算法，计算复杂度与粒子数N无关，基于Bogoliubov理论拟设，结合Dirac-Frenkel变分原理与动态低秩近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子动力学数值算法的计算复杂度随粒子数N增长，难以处理N≫1的多体玻色子系统。本文旨在构造一种复杂度不依赖于N的数值方法，利用大N渐近理论（Bogoliubov理论）来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 分为两部分。第一部分：按Bogoliubov理论用Hartree方程演化凝聚体函数，并利用Dirac-Frenkel含时变分原理推导Bogoliubov拟设中激发函数的非线性运动方程；通过利用玻色对称性，证明所得到的近似波函数（除按频率正比于N旋转的含时全局相位外）其导数界与N无关。第二部分：构造复合算法数值逼近（模去相位的）波函数，对运动方程进行适当分裂，并对基本双粒子激发函数采用动态低秩近似，即用单粒子函数乘积的线性组合表示；低秩激发函数由基更新与Galerkin（BUG）积分器时间演化，该积分器对小奇异值稳健并支持秩自适应。

Result: 所提算法的计算复杂度独立于粒子数N；数值结果验证了算法性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明借助Bogoliubov拟设与动态低秩技术可构造复杂度与N无关的量子动力学数值算法，为大规模玻色子系统的含时模拟提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We propose an algorithm for the numerical solution of the time-dependent Schrödinger equation for N >> 1bosons interacting via a two-body interaction potential in the mean-field scaling regime. Unlike existing numerical algorithms for quantum dynamics, the proposed algorithm has a computational complexity that is independent of the number N of particles. It is based on an ansatz for the wave function inspired by the Bogoliubov theory, which is an asymptotic theory for large N.
  In a first part, we follow Bogoliubov theory in evolving the condensate function by the Hartree equation, but we use the Dirac--Frenkel time-dependent variational principle to derive nonlinear equations of motion for the excitation functions in the Bogoliubov ansatz. By carefully exploiting the bosonic symmetry, the so obtained approximate wave functions are shown to have derivatives bounded independently of N apart from a time-dependent global phase factor which rotates with a frequency proportional to N.
  In the second part, we numerically approximate the wave function (modulo the phase) by a composite algorithm, which employs an appropriate splitting of the equations of motion and uses a dynamical low-rank approximation of the fundamental two-particle excitation function by linear combinations of products of single-particle functions. The low-rank excitation function is evolved in time by a basis update and Galerkin (BUG) integrator that is robust to small singular values of the coefficient matrix and allows for rank adaptivity. Numerical results are provided to assess the performance of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [330] [Convergence of isoparametric BGN methods for mean curvature flow of closed surfaces with high-order finite elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08972)
*Yifei Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了闭曲面平均曲率流的等参BGN方法的收敛性：当有限元次数k≥6且时间步长τ=O(h²)时，数值曲面在流形距离下以h²阶收敛到精确曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为平均曲率流的等参Barrett--Garcke--Nürnberg（BGN）方法建立严格的收敛性理论，弥补该数值方法缺乏收敛性证明的空白。

Method: 证明依赖两个关键工具：（1）通过在时间上做展开构造近似流，使其插值满足BGN方法并具有改进的缺陷项；（2）利用切向测试函数的代数恒等式导出切向误差的精细估计。

Result: 对于次数k≥6的有限元和时间步长τ=O(h²)，数值曲面在流形距离意义下以h²阶收敛到精确曲面。

Conclusion: 等参BGN方法对闭曲面平均曲率流是收敛的，且达到最优的h²收敛阶。

Abstract: We prove the convergence of the isoparametric Barrett--Garcke--Nürnberg (BGN) method for mean curvature flow of closed surfaces. For finite elements of degree $k\ge6$ and time steps $τ=O(h^2)$, the numerical surfaces converge to the exact surfaces in the manifold distance with order $h^2$. The proof relies on two ingredients. First, we construct approximation flows by an expansion in time, whose interpolations satisfy the BGN method with an improved defect. Second, we derive a refined estimate for the tangential error from an algebraic identity for tangential test functions.

</details>


### [331] [Polynomial Difference Schemes with Energy and Momentum Multipliers on Orthogonal Uniform Meshes: Stencil Restrictions and Monge--Ampère-Type Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08979)
*E. I. Kaptsov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文研究一维流体动力学型方程的非线性多项式守恒差分格式：证明9点模板上不可能同时保持能量与动量守恒，并在更大模板上构造出近似Monge–Ampère型方程的二阶格式，给出齐次Monge–Ampère方程的五守恒律、D4不变格式及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有经验表明，正交均匀网格上采用9点模板的非线性多项式格式，在以u_t、u_x的标准差分近似作为乘子时，无法同时保持能量与动量的差分守恒律；作者希望严格证明这一现象，并探讨在更大模板上是否存在可同时守恒的格式。

Method: 对9点模板，针对任意次数的多项式给出不可能性证明；对更大模板，采用直接法的差分模拟，在多项式次数有界的条件下搜索并构造守恒格式；作为副产品，构造齐次Monge–Ampère方程的五守恒律格式。

Result: 9点模板上对任意次数多项式证明无法同时保持能量与动量守恒；在更大模板上，有界次数的此类格式仅存在于足够大的模板上，且其逼近的二阶方程为Monge–Ampère型；所得齐次Monge–Ampère格式具有五个守恒律、保留微分方程多数对称性，并在离散二面体群D4下不变；数值试验证实其二阶逼近精度，且守恒律在精确解上以同阶精度成立。

Conclusion: 9点模板的守恒不可能性具有一般性；要同时保持能量与动量守恒需借助更大模板，但会引入Monge–Ampère型二阶方程；所得D4不变五守恒律格式在理论性质与数值表现上均良好。

Abstract: Experience with invariant finite-difference schemes for one-dimensional hydro\-dynamic-type equations for a function $u(t,x)$ suggests that nonlinear polynomial schemes on orthogonal uniform meshes with the 9-point stencil cannot simultaneously preserve difference analogues of the energy and momentum conservation laws, with the multipliers given by the standard difference approximations of $u_t$ and $u_x$. For the 9-point stencil this hypothesis is proved for polynomials of arbitrary degree. On larger stencils it is investigated by means of the difference analogue of the direct method: for polynomials of bounded degree, such schemes are found to exist only on sufficiently large stencils, and the second-order equations they approximate are of Monge--Ampère type. As a by-product, a scheme for the homogeneous Monge--Ampère equation is obtained that has five conservation laws, admits most of the symmetries of the differential equation, and is invariant under the discrete dihedral group $D_4$. Numerical tests on exact solutions confirm its second order of approximation and show that the conservation laws are satisfied on these solutions with the same order of accuracy.

</details>


### [332] [Sum of Squares and Non Sum of Squares Regimes for the Toeplitz Bottcher Wenzel Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08980)
*Wenqi Zhu,Ping Nie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文否定了 László 关于 Toeplitz 矩阵对 Böttcher–Wenzel 型为平方和的猜想，构造了充分大阶数下非平方和的显式反例族，并给出两类正结果及 N≤50（Lean 4 验证至 20 阶）的 SoS 可表示性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Toeplitz 矩阵是刻画平移不变性的最简结构模型，广泛出现于卷积、平稳协方差模型与平移不变算子离散化。Böttcher–Wenzel 型是衡量交换子不等式间隙的非负四次型，László 猜想在 Toeplitz 矩阵对情形下该四次型对任意阶数都是平方和。澄清该猜想是否成立，对结构化矩阵与半代数几何交叉问题具有重要意义。

Method: 构造一类显式的角支撑 Toeplitz 矩阵对（仅保留两侧最外侧对角线），证明其 BW 型在充分大阶数下不是平方和。证明将全部 Gram 表示归约为一个有限复检验：其 Gram 不变量部分产生严格负的极限方向，而依赖 Gram 的剩余贡献趋于零，从而排除一切 Gram 表示；并给出阶数的显式充分阈值。

Result: 反例：存在显式的角支撑 Toeplitz 对族，当阶数充分大时 BW 型不是平方和，并给出显式阶数阈值，因而 László 猜想为假。正结果一：若两个 Toeplitz 因子之一为对称或反对称，则对应 BW 型对任意阶数均为 SoS，另一因子可为任意实 Toeplitz 矩阵。正结果二：对无限制 Toeplitz 对，证明 2≤N≤50 时均可 SoS 表示，并通过 Lean 4 形式化验证至 20 阶。

Conclusion: László 猜想在一般 Toeplitz 对下不成立；SoS 性质是否成立取决于因子的对称性结构与阶数，存在清晰的否定机制与若干可保证 SoS 的正则情形。

Abstract: Toeplitz matrices are among the simplest structured models of translation invariance, arising naturally in convolution, stationary covariance models, and discretizations of translation-invariant operators. For a pair of real matrices, the Böttcher--Wenzel form (BW form) is a nonnegative quartic measuring the gap in the corresponding commutator inequality. László conjectured that, when both matrices are Toeplitz, this quartic is a sum of squares for every matrix order. We show that the conjecture is false. More precisely, we identify an explicit family of corner-supported Toeplitz pairs, retaining only the outermost diagonals on the two sides, for which the associated BW form is not a sum of squares at all sufficiently large orders. The proof reduces the complete family of Gram representations to a finite complex test: its Gram-invariant part develops a strictly negative limiting direction, while the remaining Gram-dependent contribution vanishes. We also give an explicit sufficient threshold for the matrix order. The negative result is complemented by two positive regimes. If either Toeplitz factor is symmetric or skew-symmetric, then the corresponding BW form is SoS for every order, with the other factor allowed to be an arbitrary real Toeplitz matrix. For unrestricted Toeplitz pairs, we further prove SoS representability for every $2\le N\le50$, with formal Lean 4 verification through order 20.

</details>


### [333] [JAXIGA - A software framework for GPU-enabled and differentiable isogeometric analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09083)
*Cosmin Anitescu,Yongjie Jessica Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: JAXIGA：基于JAX的开源等几何分析库，将网格构建与可追踪编译/微分的计算分离，统一实现Galerkin、能量最小化与配点法，并通过伴随法求灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等几何分析中几何精确表示与高阶连续性需求强烈，但现有实现难以同时获得JIT编译、自动微分与GPU加速；同时需要统一框架比较Galerkin、能量最小化与配点三种方法，并高效求解灵敏度问题。

Method: 基于JAX构建库：网格构建与计算分离，计算部分被追踪以便编译与微分，使几何求值、组装与求解器可组合进单一JIT程序并支持一阶反向模式导数；从同一逐点能量密度导出Galerkin、能量最小化与配点三种方法；采用一次伴随问题求解收敛解的灵敏度；引入分层抽取实现变分方法的局部细化，并使用光滑样条空间处理四阶问题。

Result: 数值结果与解析解及独立的FEniCSx、JAX-FEM计算对比一致。GPU基准（36,448个未知量）中组装速度为主机CPU的24倍；CG求解器同样运行于GPU时，总求解时间由31.8秒降至1.38秒，结果与直接解法一致到5×10⁻¹¹。

Conclusion: 求解器选择与编译复用对整体计算成本的降低至关重要；JAXIGA验证了在等几何分析中结合JIT编译、自动微分与GPU加速的有效性与准确性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present JAXIGA, an open-source library for isogeometric analysis based on JAX. The library separates the construction of the mesh from the computations which are traced for compilation and differentiation. This allows geometry evaluation, assembly and the supported solvers to be combined in a just-in-time compiled program, for which first-order reverse-mode derivatives can be computed. We consider three numerical methods obtained from a single pointwise energy density. The Galerkin and energy-minimization methods use the same discrete potential and have the same stationary points, while collocation enforces the corresponding strong form. The sensitivities of a converged solution are obtained by solving one adjoint problem, and their accuracy depends on the tolerances of both the primal and adjoint problems. We also describe hierarchical extraction for local refinement of the variational methods and the use of smooth spline spaces for fourth-order problems. The numerical results are compared with analytical solutions and independent FEniCSx and JAX-FEM computations. For the reported GPU benchmark with 36,448 unknowns, assembly is twenty-four times faster than on the host CPU. When the conjugate-gradient solver is also run on the GPU, the total solution time is reduced from 31.8 to 1.38 seconds, and the result agrees with the direct solution to $5\times10^{-11}$. These results show that the choice of solver and the reuse of compilation are important for obtaining an overall reduction in computational cost.

</details>


### [334] [Improving Learning of Green's Functions Through Kernel Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09101)
*Beiji Chen,Qiang Du,Kui Ren,Tian-Yi Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该工作分析了一个学习自伴二阶椭圆算子解算子（等价于Green函数）的框架，通过减去Green函数的奇异部分，将学习L^{-1}转化为学习边界上的L-调和延拓算子，并在RKHS间进行算子学习分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习椭圆算子解算子（或Green函数）是科学计算与算子学习的核心问题。直接学习L^{-1}面临奇异性和收敛速率受限的困难，因此需要一种能规避奇异性、提升学习速率的方法。

Method: 减去Green函数的奇异部分（解析已知或由显式Laplace参数矩阵给出），将L^{-1}的学习重构为学习边界上的L-调和延拓算子；将该过程表述为再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）间的算子学习；并通过迭代分解将框架扩展到半线性问题。

Result: 核分解策略将代数输入速率提升两个数量级；在诱导边界数据一致解析正则性与兼容输出空间条件下，输出速率从代数升级为指数；在半线性问题中，唯一学习对象仍为线性算子，改进的线性速率直接传递到非线性迭代。

Conclusion: 通过减去奇异部分并在RKHS间进行算子学习，可显著提升解算子学习的收敛速率（输入速率提升两个数量级、输出速率升级为指数），且该优势可经迭代分解延伸到半线性问题，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: This work analyzes a framework for learning the solution operator, or equivalently, the Green's function, of a general self-adjoint second-order elliptic operator $\cL$. Subtracting the singular part of the Green's function, either known analytically or supplied by an explicit Laplace parametrix, recasts the learning of $\cL^{-1}$ as learning an $\cL$-harmonic extension operator on the boundary. We formulate the process as operator learning between reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs) to show that the kernel decomposition strategy raises the algebraic input rate by two orders and, under uniform analytic regularity of the induced boundary data together with compatible output spaces, upgrades the output rate from algebraic to exponential. We extend the framework further to semilinear problems via an iterative decomposition in which the only learned object remains a linear operator, ensuring that the improved linear rates translate directly to the nonlinear iteration. Numerical experiments are provided to validate the theoretical development.

</details>


### [335] [High-order entropy stable isothermal wall boundary condition for the compressible Navier--Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09249)
*Mohammed Sayyari,Nail K. Yamaleev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SBP-SAT算子的熵稳定等温无滑移壁面边界条件，填补了文献空白，并在标准基准问题上验证了其保结构、设计精度和熵平衡特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵稳定边界条件对保证数值格式满足离散熵不等式至关重要。已有的绝热壁边界条件在Parsani 2014和Dalcin 2019中被提出和推广，但尚无针对等温壁边界条件的SBP-SAT熵稳定形式，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用分部求和(SBP)与同时近似项(SAT)算子，针对可压缩Navier-Stokes方程构造新的等温无滑移壁面边界条件，在保持稳定性的同时实现设计精度，并确保壁面处熵产生的正确符号。

Result: 所提方法在标准可压缩流动基准问题上得到验证，展示了保结构（结构保持）和设计精度特性，并能在离散层面模拟Navier-Stokes方程的熵平衡。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出了SBP-SAT框架下等温壁面边界条件的熵稳定公式，满足熵不等式并保持设计精度，为可压缩Navier-Stokes数值模拟提供了新的可靠边界处理手段。

Abstract: Entropy stable boundary conditions are critical for ensuring that the corresponding numerical scheme satisfies the discrete entropy inequality, thus, mimicking the second law of thermodynamics for the compressible Navier-Stokes equations discretely. Provably entropy-stable adiabatic wall boundary conditions were introduced in Parsani 2014~\cite{parsani2014entropy}. These discrete wall boundary conditions were further generalized in Dalcin 2019~\cite{dalcin2019conservative} to a moving adiabatic solid wall or a wall with a prescribed heat flux for the compressible Navier--Stokes equations discretized by using summation-by-parts (SBP) and simultaneous-approximation-term (SAT) operators. Currently no entropy-stable formulation using SBP-SAT operators for isothermal wall boundary conditions is available in the literature. This paper presents a new isothermal no-slip wall boundary conditions that: 1) enforces the $T|_{y=0}=T^{wall}$ and $\vec{v}|_{y=0}=\vec{v}^{wall}$ conditions with the design order of accuracy while maintaining stability, 2) provides the correct sign of entropy production at the wall, and 3) mimics the entropy balance of the Navier-Stokes equations at the discrete level. The structure-preserving and design-order properties of the proposed methodology are demonstrated and verified on standard benchmark problems for compressible flows.

</details>


### [336] [Convergence of repeated-pole rational approximation of the exponential on infinite sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09251)
*Fei Xue,Tianqi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文研究在扇形区域 Σ_β 上用仅有单一有限极点 s=-q/h（重数至多 m）的有理函数逼近 e^{-hz} 的最优一致误差 E_{m,β}(q)。在线性缩放 q=αm 下，通过 Möbius 映射转化为紧致透镜域上的多项式逼近，利用 Faber 积分、分数幂围道变形与双鞍点展开，证明了尖锐的双侧阶估计 E_{m,β}(αm) ≍ m^{-1/2} H_β(α)^m，并给出指数因子 H_β(α) 在非合并区间上的唯一极小点与最小值，恢复了 β=0 时的经典半直线结果 √2-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有理逼近 e^{-z} 在扇形上的误差由"指数因子 + 代数前因子"共同决定，但其精确阶与最优极点位置（缩放参数 α）在非合并区间上缺乏统一、尖锐的刻画。该文旨在：1) 给出 E_{m,β}(αm) 的 sharp 双侧阶；2) 确定指数因子 H_β(α) 的最优缩放 α_β^* 及最小指数率，从而推广经典的半直线（β=0）结论；3) 将逼近论结果连接到投影 shift-and-invert Arnoldi 的收敛界，并厘清"扇形一致律"与单矩阵实际计算之间的区别。

Method: 1) 设定 Σ_β={re^{iθ}: r≥0, |θ|≤β/2}，误差 E_{m,β}(q) 为以 s=-q/h（重数≤m）为唯一有限极点的有理函数一致逼近 e^{-hz} 的最优误差；2) 取线性缩放 q=αm（α>0 固定），经 Möbius 变换把问题约化为紧致透镜域上的多项式逼近；3) 引入 γ_β=2-β/π 与鞍点合并阈值 α_c(γ_β)，在 0<α<α_c 上定义指数鞍作用 H_β(α)∈(0,1)；4) 用精确的边界 Faber 积分、全局分数幂围道变形和双鞍点渐近展开推导上下界；5) 对 H_β(α) 求极小并做数值-range 估计与有限次极点搜索、非正规实验。

Result: 在 0<α<α_c(γ_β) 的紧致子集上一致成立 sharp 双侧阶 E_{m,β}(αm) ≍ m^{-1/2} H_β(α)^m（前因子 m^{-1/2}，指数因子 H_β(α)^m）。在非合并区间上，H_β(α) 有唯一极小点 α_β^*=1/[γ_β sin(π/(2γ_β))]，对应最小值 tan(π/(4γ_β))；当 β=0 时退化为经典半直线因子 tan(π/8)=√2-1。数值-range 估计给出与矩阵维数无关的投影 shift-and-invert Arnoldi 上界；有限次极点搜索与非正规实验表明扇形一致律与单个矩阵计算之间存在差异。

Conclusion: 该文在非鞍点合并区间上完整刻画了扇形区域上单极点有理逼近 e^{-hz} 的尖锐误差阶：误差由代数前因子 m^{-1/2} 与指数因子 H_β(α)^m 乘积控制，并确定了指数因子的唯一最优缩放 α_β^* 及最小指数率 tan(π/(4γ_β))，统一并推广了经典半直线结果。方法上，Faber 积分、分数幂围道变形与双鞍点展开的结合为这类带约束极点逼近提供了可复用的分析框架。应用上给出了与矩阵维数无关的 projected shift-and-invert Arnoldi 上界，同时提醒扇形一致律不能直接等同于单个矩阵的实际收敛行为。

Abstract: For $h>0$, degree $m\ge0$, $0\leβ<π$, and $q>0$, let $Σ_β=\{re^{iθ}:r\ge0,\ |θ|\leβ/2\}$ and let $E_{m,β}(q)$ denote the best uniform error for approximating $e^{-hz}$ on $Σ_β$ by rational functions whose only possible finite pole is $s=-q/h$, with multiplicity at most $m$. For the linear scaling $q=αm$ with a fixed $α>0$, a Möbius map reduces the problem to polynomial approximation on a compact lens. Write $γ_β=2-β/π$ and let $α_c(γ_β)$ denote the saddle-coalescence threshold. For $0<α<α_c(γ_β)$, let $H_β(α)\in(0,1)$ be the exponential of the real saddle action. An exact boundary Faber integral, a global fractional-power contour deformation, and a two-saddle expansion yield the sharp two-sided order $E_{m,β}(αm)\asymp m^{-1/2}H_β(α)^m$, uniformly on compact subsets of $0<α<α_c(γ_β)$. On this noncoalescing interval, the exponential factor $H_β(α)$ has the unique minimizer $α_β^*=1/[γ_β\sin(π/(2γ_β))]$ and minimum $\tan(π/(4γ_β))$, recovering the classical half-line factor $\tan(π/8)=\sqrt{2}-1$ at $β=0$. Numerical-range estimates give matrix dimension-independent upper bounds for projected shift-and-invert Arnoldi, while finite-degree pole searches and nonnormal experiments illustrate the distinction between the sector-uniform law and individual matrix computations.

</details>


### [337] [Shifted tapered-pole approximation on finite sectors: an exact asymptotic and dimension control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09256)
*Fei Xue,Tianqi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究闭单位半径扇区上 $g(z)z^\alpha(\log z)^m$ 的显式 lightning-plus-polynomial 逼近,给出分支一致误差界、精确主导常数及显式表示维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本扇区根指数收敛率已知,但需要回答三个更精细的问题:网格平移下的统一分支误差界、主导常数的精确刻画,以及额外极点与多项式压缩下的显式表示维数。

Method: 采用单侧 Abel--Poisson 公式与无极点缺口轮廓获得分支误差界;对 $z^\alpha$ 在稠密平衡区做顶点尺度重标定;用速率匹配的额外表示极点带加多项式压缩。

Result: 得到对 $(0,1)$ 紧子集内网格平移一致、对任意固定对数幂成立的分支误差界;识别出指定平移序列的正有限主导常数;表示维数为 $N+2\sqrt{2\alpha\lambda_\sigma N}+O(1)$,且前 $N$ 个锥化极点不变;稍微过分辨的混合序列亦保持主导常数。

Conclusion: 结论针对所述构造的精确渐近与维数性质,不涉及扇区极小极大逼近或总计算复杂度;匹配维数实验(含凹入扇区)验证了极点分配。

Abstract: We study explicit lightning-plus-polynomial (LP) approximation of $g(z)z^α(\log z)^m$ on a closed unit-radius sector, where $0<α<1$, $m$ is fixed, and $g$ is analytic nearby. Since the basic sector root-exponential rate is already known, we address three finer questions. First, a one-sided Abel--Poisson formula and a pole-free notched contour give a branchwise error bound that is uniform for grid shifts in compact subsets of $(0,1)$ and valid for every fixed logarithmic power. Second, for $z^α$ in the dense and balanced regimes, an exact vertex-scale rescaling identifies a positive finite leading constant for a specified shifted sequence. Third, a rate-matched band of additional representation poles followed by polynomial compression gives the explicit representation dimension $N+2\sqrt{2αλ_σN}+O(1)$ while leaving the first $N$ tapered poles unchanged. A slightly overresolved hybrid sequence also preserves the exact leading constant. Matched-dimension experiments, including reentrant sectors, illustrate the allocation. These exact-asymptotic and dimension statements concern the stated constructions, not sector minimax approximation or total computational complexity.

</details>


### [338] [Convergence of kernel and neural-network methods for Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations on unbounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09299)
*Yumiharu Nakano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为无网格配置法（Wendland核）与PINN两类非单调近似HJB方程方法，从经验平方残差出发推导了误差界：主方法是把经验损失转化为逐点残差控制以用于粘性解误差估计，并量化离散/采样误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无界空间域上的HJB方程，其粘性解误差估计通常需要逐点（一致）残差控制，而实际训练/配置只能得到经验平方残差；需要建立从经验残差到逐点残差控制的桥梁，并量化核方法离散化误差与神经网络采样误差，从而给出可计算的误差界。

Method: 考虑两类非单调、无网格近似：（1）Wendland核配置法，在native space范数约束下；（2）光滑激活函数的PINN，在Sobolev范数约束下。核心步骤是由经验损失恢复逐点残差控制，并估计离散化/采样误差。证明技术结合：经验到总体估计、将L2残差转为sup范数界的插值不等式、以及由域截断导致误差的受控扩散估计。核方法在经典与native space正则性下证明紧集上一致收敛，并给出唯一有界连续粘性解的后验定量误差界，误差界由fill distance控制；神经网络方法在经典Sobolev正则性下证明紧集上依概率一致收敛，给出高概率定量误差界，误差界由网络规模和样本量控制。收敛所依赖的正则性假设是在粘性适定性之外的额外假设。

Result: 核方法：紧集一致收敛，得到由fill distance控制的后验定量误差界；神经网络：紧集上依概率一致收敛，得到由网络与样本规模控制的高概率定量误差界。一维和二维数值实验验证了方法与估计。

Conclusion: 在无界域上，对核配置法与PINN求解HJB方程，可从经验平方残差推出粘性解误差的定量界；误差由fill distance或网络/样本规模控制。该框架统一了经验到总体估计、L2到sup插值与域截断扩散误差，所加正则性假设独立于粘性适定性。数值实验支持理论结果。

Abstract: We derive error bounds from empirical squared residuals for two non-monotone, mesh-free approximations of Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations on unbounded spatial domains: collocation with Wendland kernels under a native space norm constraint, and physics-informed neural networks with smooth activations under a Sobolev-norm constraint. The main step is to recover the pointwise residual control needed for viscosity-solution error estimates from the empirical loss, while quantifying the discretization or sampling error. For the kernel method, we prove uniform convergence on compact sets under classical and native space regularity and derive an a posteriori quantitative error bound for the unique bounded continuous viscosity solution. For the neural-network method, we prove uniform convergence on compact sets in probability and give a quantitative high-probability error bound under classical Sobolev regularity. The bounds are governed by the fill distance for kernels and by the network and sample sizes for neural networks. The proofs combine empirical-to-population estimates, an interpolation inequality that turns an $L^2$ residual into a sup norm bound, and a controlled diffusion estimate for the error caused by truncating the domain. The regularity assumptions used to obtain convergence from approximation properties are additional to viscosity wellposedness. Numerical experiments in one and two space dimensions illustrate the methods and the estimates.

</details>


### [339] [An energy--stable ALE--FEM with compensated tangential velocity for Navier--Stokes free--boundary flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09301)
*Nuo Lei,Weiying Zheng,Na Zhu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种能量稳定的ALE有限元格式，用于带表面张力驱动自由边界的不可压缩Navier-Stokes流动，核心是无乘子的补偿切向速度公式，用离散切向曲率残差重分布边界节点，松弛参数取α=γ₀/μ，理论证明与数值实验验证其稳定性与网格质量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带表面张力驱动自由边界的不可压缩流动在ALE有限元中常因网格变形导致质量下降甚至计算失效，且切向网格重分布常需引入拉格朗日乘子或额外约束，增加复杂度并可能破坏能量稳定性。因此需要一种无需乘子、且能在粘性与毛细效应之间取得平衡的切向速度处理方式，以保持能量稳定性和网格质量。

Method: 构造ALE有限元格式，采用无乘子的补偿切向速度公式：通过离散切向曲率残差驱动边界节点重分布。松弛参数由粘性-毛细平衡确定，取α=γ₀/μ（γ₀为表面张力系数，μ为动力粘度）。对全离散格式进行适定性、能量耗散性及与半离散格式时间一致性的理论证明，并与BGN方法对比测试。

Result: 全离散格式被证明适定、能量耗散，并与半离散格式时间一致；数值实验表明方法在一系列μ和γ₀取值下均稳健；相对BGN方法在网格质量保持上更优，尤其在小时间步长情形下。

Conclusion: 该无乘子补偿切向速度ALE有限元格式在保证能量稳定与时间一致性的同时，改善了表面张力驱动自由边界流动中的网格质量，尤其适用于小时间步长场景，为粘性-毛细问题提供了一种简洁且稳健的数值方案。

Abstract: We propose an energy-stable arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) finite element formulation for incompressible Navier-Stokes flows with surface-tension-driven free boundaries. The key ingredient is a multiplier-free compensated tangential velocity formulation, which redistributes boundary nodes via the discrete tangential curvature residual. The relaxation parameter governing the artificial tangential velocity is scaled according to the viscous-capillary balance, yielding the natural choice $α= γ_0/ μ$, where $γ_0$ is the surface tension coefficient and $μ$ is the dynamic viscosity. We prove that the fully discrete scheme is well-posed, energy-dissipative, and temporally consistent with its semi-discrete counterpart. Numerical results confirm the robustness of the proposed method across a range of $μ$ and $γ_0$ values. Furthermore, the method exhibits superior mesh quality preservation compared with the BGN method, particularly in the small-time-step regime.

</details>


### [340] [Fully Deterministic Shallow Neural Networks with Localized Activations. Part I: Construction and Optimal Sobolev Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09338)
*Ran Bi,Weibing Deng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文在有界Lipschitz区域上构造固定Gaussian与tanh-difference激活的浅层神经网络，通过确定性规则预设隐藏参数并建立最优H^s→H^m逼近率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究独立于目标函数、由显式确定性规则生成的浅层神经网络能否达到H^s空间中的最优逼近率，并厘清实现该逼近率所需的外侧偏移裕度条件。

Method: 在Ω⊂R^d上以频率分辨率M构造N_M≍M^d个固定特征张成线性空间V_M；取M≍N^{1/d}满足N_M≤N，构造有界线性算子A_N:H^s→V_M。方向采用显式球面求积节点，偏移采用Gauss-Legendre节点或等距中点；分析截断ridge积分与去除外侧中点节点所需的偏移裕度。

Result: 对任意u∈H^s(Ω)与整数0≤m<s，得到‖u−A_Nu‖_{H^m}≤C N^{−(s−m)/d}‖u‖_{H^s}，C独立于u、N、m；Sobolev宽度下界说明该率在维数≤N且独立于目标的线性空间中是最优的。外侧偏移裕度可趋于零；Gaussian需超越sup|x|的O(M^{−1}log M)裕度，tanh-difference需O(M^{−1}(log M)^2)。二维与三维数值实验检验L^2与H^1逼近。

Conclusion: 固定激活的浅层网络配合确定性预设参数即可实现Sobolev空间中的最优逼近率，且所需外侧偏移裕度极小（对数级修正），为显式构造高效逼近算子提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We construct shallow neural networks with fixed Gaussian and tanh-difference activations on bounded Lipschitz domains $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\ge2$. For each $s>0$, explicit deterministic rules prescribe all hidden parameters independently of the target $u\in H^s(Ω)$. At frequency resolution $M$, the $N_M\asymp M^d$ prescribed features span a linear space $V_M$. For every sufficiently large integer $N$, we choose $M\asymp N^{1/d}$ with $N_M\le N$ and construct a bounded linear operator $A_N:H^s(Ω)\to V_M$ such that \[
  \|u-A_Nu\|_{H^m(Ω)}
  \le C N^{-(s-m)/d}\|u\|_{H^s(Ω)} \] for every $u\in H^s(Ω)$ and every integer $0\le m<s$, with $C$ independent of $u$, $N$, and $m$. Sobolev width lower bounds show that this rate is optimal among linear spaces of dimension at most $N$ chosen independently of the target. We use explicit spherical cubature nodes for the directions and Gauss-Legendre nodes or equispaced midpoints for the offsets. We show that the exterior offset margin can shrink to zero as $M\to\infty$ while preserving the approximation rate. A margin beyond $\sup_{x\inΩ}|x|$ of order $M^{-1}\log M$ for the Gaussian and $M^{-1}(\log M)^2$ for the tanh difference suffices to truncate the ridge integral and remove exterior midpoint nodes. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions examine $L^2$ and $H^1$ approximation.

</details>


### [341] [Fully Deterministic Shallow Neural Networks with Localized Activations. Part II: Condition Number Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09357)
*Ran Bi,Weibing Deng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文证明了确定性浅层网络（Gaussian与tanh-difference激活）连续Sobolev Gram矩阵的正定性与谱条件数上界，上界为exp(CN^{2/d}/log(N+2))，并给出匹配下界说明指数尖锐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究浅层网络特征族在Sobolev内积下Gram矩阵的条件数增长，关乎最优Sobolev逼近构造（Part I）的数值稳定性，需给出定量谱条件数界限并判断其最优性。

Method: 在有界Lipschitz区域上用完整H^m内积构造raw与列归一化Gram矩阵；使用齐次微分算子分离方向群，结合一维系数恢复估计；对midpoint与Gauss-Legendre偏移分析；在固定椭球上构造匹配下界。

Result: 对任意固定非空有界Lipschitz域（d≥2）与m≥0，充分大N时raw与列归一化Gram矩阵谱条件数≤exp(CN^{2/d}/log(N+2))，C与N无关；midpoint偏移与适当角序列下，固定椭球上的匹配下界显示该指数在给定域类上对raw Gaussian及raw/归一化tanh-difference矩阵是尖锐的；二维方形上的L^2实验展示条件数增长与归一化效应。

Conclusion: Gaussian与tanh-difference激活的浅层网络Sobolev Gram矩阵具有可控的次指数条件数增长，其上界指数N^{2/d}/log N为最优，列归一化不能改变该指数但影响常数与实验表现。

Abstract: We prove positive definiteness and quantitative condition-number bounds for continuous Sobolev Gram matrices of deterministic shallow networks with Gaussian and tanh-difference activations. The prescribed feature families include the optimal Sobolev approximation constructions of Part I. On every fixed nonempty bounded Lipschitz domain in $\R^d$, $d\ge2$, and for every fixed integer $m\ge0$, the raw and column-normalized Gram matrices in the full $H^m$ inner product have spectral condition numbers bounded by $\exp(CN^{2/d}/\log(N+2))$ for sufficiently large $N$, where $N$ is the feature count and $C>0$ is independent of $N$. The upper bounds hold for midpoint and Gauss-Legendre offsets. For midpoint offsets and a suitable angular sequence, matching lower bounds on fixed ellipsoids establish sharpness of this exponent over the stated domain class for raw Gaussian matrices and both raw and normalized tanh-difference matrices. The proof combines homogeneous differential operators that isolate direction groups with one-dimensional coefficient-recovery estimates. Two-dimensional $L^2$ experiments on a square illustrate condition-number growth and normalization effects.

</details>


### [342] [Structure-preserving full- and low-rank exponential methods for the optimal control of Lindblad equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09403)
*Hao Chen,Alfio Borzi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个求解Lindblad方程最优控制的统一框架：由Pontryagin最大值原理导出连续最优性系统，用序贯二次Hamiltonian（SQH）方法求解，并基于二阶指数中点传播子进行数值实现，兼顾全秩与低秩格式，且给出严格误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lindblad主方程控制的开放量子系统最优控制，需要反复求解前向与伴随演化方程；在大Hilbert空间下，这要求数值方法同时具备保结构、高精度、计算高效，并能与不可微优化技术兼容。

Method: 1）从Pontryagin最大值原理导出连续最优性系统，采用序贯二次Hamiltonian（SQH）方法求解；2）以时间依赖Hamiltonian下的二阶指数中点传播子离散前向与伴随Lindblad方程；3）发展保持密度矩阵Hermitian性与半正定结构的全秩格式，以及显著降低存储与计算成本的低秩格式；4）为全秩与低秩的前向、伴随传播子建立严格误差估计。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论预测的收敛阶，展示了低秩近似的有效性，并说明了所提出的FREM-SQH与LREM-SQH算法在光滑与不可微控制代价的最优控制问题中的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为Lindblad方程最优控制提供了一个结构保持、精度可控且计算高效的统一求解框架。

Abstract: Optimal control of open quantum systems governed by the Lindblad master equation requires the repeated solution of forward and adjoint evolution equations. For large Hilbert spaces, these computations demand numerical methods that are structure-preserving, accurate, and computationally efficient, while remaining compatible with nonsmooth optimization techniques. In this work, a unified framework for optimal control of Lindblad equations is presented. The continuous optimality system is derived from the Pontryagin maximum principle and solved by a sequential quadratic Hamiltonian (SQH) method. Its numerical realization is based on second-order exponential midpoint propagators for the forward and adjoint Lindblad equations with time-dependent Hamiltonians. Full-rank schemes preserving the Hermitian and positive-semidefinite structure of the density matrix are developed together with low-rank formulations that substantially reduce storage requirements and computational cost. Rigorous error estimates are established for the full- and low-rank forward and adjoint propagators. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence rates, demonstrate the effectiveness of the low-rank approximations, and illustrate the performance of the proposed FREM-SQH and LREM-SQH algorithms for optimal control problems with smooth and nonsmooth control costs.

</details>


### [343] [A hat function based positive contact discontinuity capturing Boltzmann scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10419)
*Balwinder Singh,S. V. Raghurama Rao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文基于Perthame提出的紧支撑帽函数分布，构造并分析了一种基于固有速度的接触间断捕捉Boltzmann格式，简化了数值分析并直接证明了正性，扩展至二阶精度后通过一维和二维基准无粘流动问题验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maxwell速度分布函数的适当矩可恢复气体动力学Euler方程的守恒变量与通量，但Maxwellian在速度空间中具有无限支撑，给基于它的动理学格式数值分析带来困难。因此，寻找既能保持关键矩又具有紧支撑的替代分布函数具有重要价值。

Method: 采用Perthame（1990）引入的紧支撑帽函数作为替代分布函数，构造基于固有速度（peculiar velocity）的接触间断捕捉Boltzmann格式；利用帽函数在速度空间的紧支撑特性简化数值分析，直接证明格式的正性，并在不破坏一阶格式保正性的前提下将格式扩展至二阶精度。

Result: 帽函数的紧支撑显著简化了所提格式的数值分析，实现了正性的直接证明；二阶扩展格式保持了保正性，并在多个一维和二维基准无粘流动问题上进行了测试。

Conclusion: 该工作表明基于帽函数分布构造的Boltzmann格式在保持Euler方程关键矩的同时，可有效简化数值分析并保证正性，为接触间断捕捉动理学格式提供了一种可行的替代方案，并具备扩展至二阶精度的能力。

Abstract: The suitable moments of the Maxwell velocity distribution function recover the conserved variable and flux vectors of the Euler equations of gas dynamics. However, the Maxwellian has infinite support in velocity space, which poses challenges for the numerical analysis of kinetic schemes based on it. Although Maxwellian is a natural choice, it is not the only distribution function that preserves these key moments. In his seminal paper, Perthame (1990) introduced a compactly supported hat function as an alternative. This paper presents the formulation and analysis of a contact discontinuity capturing Boltzmann scheme based on peculiar velocity, constructed using the hat function. Owing to the compact support of the hat function in velocity space, the numerical analysis of the proposed scheme is considerably simplified, allowing for a direct proof of its positivity. The scheme, extended to second-order accuracy without compromising the positivity-preserving property of its first-order counterpart, is tested on several benchmark inviscid flow problems in one- and two-dimensions.

</details>


### [344] [Leapfrog Crank--Nicolson decoupling of wave--heat-type problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09603)
*Benjamin Dörich,Julian Dörner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对耦合波-热系统，提出一种二阶数值格式：波部分用leapfrog、热部分用Crank–Nicolson，耦合采用Strang分裂式，使整体耦合显式，仅受双曲型CFL条件约束而非严格的抛物型限制；在抽象空间离散框架下推导全离散误差估计，并用数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合波-热系统广泛出现于带记忆的粘弹性、有限热速的热弹性波传播以及Maxwell方程与色散材料律耦合等应用。已有研究中波问题常用显式leapfrog，热方程受抛物型时间步限制，二者耦合时若处理不当会引入更严格的时间步约束，因此需要设计兼顾显式性与二阶精度、且不受抛物型限制的数值格式。

Method: 构造二阶格式：波部分采用leapfrog时间积分，热部分采用Crank–Nicolson步进；耦合以Strang分裂（Strang-splitting）方式组织，使整体格式在耦合项上保持显式，仅需双曲型CFL条件。同时提出一个抽象空间离散框架，覆盖一大类波-热系统，并兼容conforming与stabilized non-conforming离散；基于一般误差分解推导全离散误差估计。

Result: 在所要求的双曲型CFL条件下，得到了全离散格式的误差估计，从理论上证明该格式为二阶收敛且不受抛物型时间步限制。针对模型应用的数值实验确认了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提leapfrog–Crank–Nicolson + Strang分裂耦合的二阶格式能有效求解波-热耦合系统，在保持显式耦合的同时避免严格抛物型时间步约束；抽象离散框架与误差分析具有一般性，适用于多种应用与非一致离散情形。

Abstract: This paper proposes and analyzes a numerical method for coupled wave-heat systems that arise, for example, in visco-elasticity with memory, thermo-elastic wave propagation with finite thermal speed, and Maxwell's equations coupled to dispersive material laws. Motivated by the widespread use of explicit leapfrog schemes for wave problems and the parabolic time-step restriction for heat equations, we design a second-order scheme that combines leapfrog time integration for the wave part with a Crank--Nicolson step for the heat part. The coupling is arranged in a Strang-splitting--type fashion so that the overall scheme remains explicit in the coupling and is subject only to a CFL condition of hyperbolic type, rather than the more restrictive parabolic constraint.
  We introduce an abstract space discretization that covers a broad class of wave--heat systems and accommodates both conforming and stabilized non-conforming discretizations. Using a general error decomposition, we derive error estimates for the fully discrete scheme under the desired CFL condition. Numerical experiments for our model applications confirm the theoretical results.

</details>


### [345] [A locking-free virtual element method for the three-dimensional quasi-static electroporoelasticity equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09646)
*Jianguo Huang,Wenxuan Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对三维准静态电孔弹性方程，构造并分析了一种无锁定的虚拟元方法，在多面体网格上实现了压力、电场、磁场和位移场的离散，并通过加密网格上的Stokes型空间和精确序列消除了锁定现象、降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准静态电孔弹性问题涉及多物理场耦合，传统有限元方法在多面体网格上处理困难，且位移场存在由Lamé常数λ引起的体积锁定现象，导致误差估计依赖于λ。因此需要一种适用于一般多面体网格、且对λ鲁棒（无锁定）的数值方法，同时希望降低多场耦合系统的计算成本。

Method: 采用最低阶节点、边、面及Stokes型虚拟元空间分别离散压力、电场、磁场和位移场，时间离散采用后向Euler格式，使格式适用于一般多面体网格。为克服锁定现象，在加密网格$\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_h$上定义Stokes型空间，构造满足$÷(\mathrm{I}_s\bm v)|_K=Π^K_0÷\bm v$的插值算子$\mathrm{I}_s$，从而使误差估计与Lamé常数λ无关。利用精确序列$\curl\bm V^e_h=\bm V^f_{h,0}$，显式更新磁场，将四场系统降为三场系统，降低计算代价。

Result: 误差估计不依赖于Lamé常数λ，即方法无锁定；通过显式更新磁场，四场耦合系统被约化为三场系统，计算成本降低；数值实验验证了所提方法的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的无锁定虚拟元方法适用于一般多面体网格上的三维准静态电孔弹性问题，兼具对λ鲁棒的误差估计与较低的计算成本，数值实验证实了其有效性。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a locking-free virtual element method for the three-dimensional quasi-static electroporoelasticity equations. The pressure, electric, magnetic, and displacement fields are discretized by the lowest-order nodal, edge, face, and Stokes-like virtual element spaces, respectively, so that the scheme works on general polyhedral meshes, while the temporal discretization is carried out by a backward Euler scheme. The locking phenomenon is overcome by defining the Stokes-like space on a refined mesh $\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_h$, on which we can construct an interpolation operator $\mathrm{I}_s$ satisfying $÷(\mathrm{I}_s\bm v)|_K=Π^K_0÷\bm v$, which renders the error estimates independent of the Lamé constant $λ$. Moreover, since the exact sequence $\curl\bm V^e_h=\bm V^f_{h,0}$ holds, the magnetic field is updated explicitly, reducing the four-field system to a three-field one and lowering the computational cost. Numerical experiments are provided to demonstrate the performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [346] [Anisotropic Lagrange interpolation on exact spherical triangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09686)
*Hiroki Ishizaka*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文研究由弦三角形径向投影得到的精确测地球面三角形上的线性 Lagrange 插值，通过将单元映射分解为仿射映射与径向修正，把平面各向异性插值估计严格迁移到球面，并证明所得局部误差估计关于单元尺寸、形状与球半径为一致常数，且方向性因子达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面有限元中常用的弦三角形与真实测地三角形存在差异：弦三角形带各向异性，径向投影引入径向畸变；直接借用平面各向异性插值估计会混淆这两类误差来源，并错误地用直径替代真实方向尺度，导致角度依赖与常数不最优。作者希望建立精确的球面恒等式，把弦各向异性与径向畸变分离，从而得到方向尺度与几何因子显式、常数一致且最优的局部误差估计。

Method: 将精确测地球面三角形上的线性 Lagrange 单元映射分解为仿射映射与径向修正两部分，以分离弦各向异性与径向畸变；推导曲面测度、Dirichlet 能量与方向导数的精确恒等式；利用这些恒等式把平面各向异性插值估计逐项迁移到球面；构造四族显式三角形，用以量化用直径替代两个弦方向尺度所造成的损失，并检验最大角因子的位置与指数；第四族三角形进一步验证径向迁移因子平方根的最优性。

Result: 得到保留两个弦方向长度尺度、几何因子显式的球面局部误差估计，常数独立于单元尺寸、形状与球半径；梯度估计中最大角因子只乘在较长的方向尺度上。四族显式三角形表明：(i) 用直径替代方向尺度会造成可量化的损失，并确定最大角依赖的尖锐形式；(ii) 在常数与单元无关的两项方向界内，最大角因子不能移到较短的尺度上，其指数也不能降到 1 以下；(iii) 第四族证明径向迁移因子的平方根最优，同时保持相同的迁移方向尺度。局部估计可推广到相容球面三角剖分，并给出 mean-zero Laplace–Beltrami 问题协调近似在精确积分下的各向异性能量误差估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为球面线性 Lagrange 插值提供了方向尺度显式、常数最优的局部误差理论，澄清了弦各向异性与径向畸变的相互作用，并可直接用于球面 Laplace–Beltrami 问题的各向异性能量误差分析。

Abstract: We study linear Lagrange interpolation on exact geodesic spherical triangles obtained by radial projection of chordal triangles. Factorising the element map into an affine map and a radial correction separates chordal anisotropy from radial distortion. We derive exact identities for the surface measure, Dirichlet energy, and directional derivatives, and use them to transfer planar anisotropic interpolation estimates to the sphere. The resulting local error estimates retain the two chordal directional length scales and keep the geometric factors explicit, with constants independent of the element size, shape, and sphere radius. In the gradient estimate, the maximum-angle factor multiplies only the longer directional scale. Four explicit triangle families quantify the loss caused by replacing these scales with the diameter and establish the sharp maximum-angle dependence. They also show that, within the same two-term directional bound with a constant independent of the element, the maximum-angle factor cannot be moved to the shorter scale or have its exponent reduced below one. The fourth family proves the optimality of the square-root radial-transfer factor while retaining the same transported directional scale. The local estimates extend to compatible spherical triangulations. As an application, they yield an anisotropic energy-error estimate for the conforming approximation of the mean-zero Laplace--Beltrami problem with exact integration.

</details>


### [347] [Operator preconditioning of the Costabel FEM-BEM coupling for the time-harmonic Maxwell transmission problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09765)
*Quang Huy Nguyen,Van Chien Le,Kristof Cools*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将带 Lipschitz 界面的时谐 Maxwell 传输问题的 Costabel FEM-BEM 耦合改写为迹空间上的等价形式，并基于 Maxwell 单层边界积分算子构造块对角预条件子，证明预处理矩阵谱条件数一致有界，数值实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Costabel FEM-BEM 耦合的离散系统条件数差、难以高效迭代求解，且原耦合形式不便直接在迹空间上做算子预条件分析，因此需要等价改写并构造稳健预条件子。

Method: 引入迹提升算子与 Steklov--Poincaré 算子，将耦合问题重构在迹空间乘积上；代数上消去内部 FEM 未知量，得到含 FEM 内部块 Schur 补的离散系统；在频率避开共振集的假设下证明其与 Costabel 耦合在连续与离散层面等价；借助算子预条件框架，由 Maxwell 单层边界积分算子诱导的 Galerkin 矩阵构造块对角预条件子。

Result: 证明了重构耦合与 Costabel 耦合在连续和离散层面等价；预处理矩阵的谱条件数一致有界；数值实验展示了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 所提等价改写与块对角预条件子为 Lipschitz 界面时谐 Maxwell 传输问题提供了理论稳健、数值有效的求解方案。

Abstract: This paper derives an equivalent formulation of the Costabel FEM-BEM coupling for the time-harmonic Maxwell transmission problem with a Lipschitz interface, and constructs a block-diagonal preconditioner for the resulting discrete system. The reformulated coupling is posed on a product of trace spaces and is obtained by introducing a trace lifting operator and the Steklov--Poincar{\' e} operator. At the algebraic level, it corresponds to eliminating the interior FEM unknowns, resulting in a discrete system that involves the Schur complement of the interior FEM block. Assuming that the frequency lies outside the resonance sets, we prove that the reformulated coupling is equivalent to the Costabel coupling at both the continuous and discrete levels. Using the operator preconditioning framework, we build a preconditioner from the Galerkin matrix induced by the Maxwell single layer boundary integral operator. The spectral condition number of the preconditioned matrix is shown to be uniformly bounded. Numerical experiments illustrate the practical effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [348] [An arbitrarily high-order accurate and locally energy/entropy-stable space-time method for a class of nonlinear wave systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09797)
*Michael Dumbser,Ilaria Perugia,Enrico Zampa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一类在非结构单纯形网格上、保持结构且任意高阶精度的时空格式，用于非线性声波传播系统；格式满足局部守恒、局部能量/熵平衡以及速度场旋度对合的弱保持，并给出数学证明与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一类非线性声波传播系统（非线性体现在状态变量与对偶变量的本构关系中，微分算子仍为标准一阶声波结构），需要构造在高阶精度下仍能保持守恒性、能量/熵平衡及旋度对合等结构性质的非结构网格时空离散格式。

Method: 关键要素包括：i) 空间采用间断Galerkin（DG）离散，其中对偶变量为能量对守恒变量导数在DG空间上的L2投影；ii) 以对偶变量表达的数值通量与气泡加权的单元局部粘性正则化；iii) 时间方向采用连续-间断Petrov-Galerkin方法。

Result: 在完全离散层面，格式实现：I) 状态变量的局部守恒；II) 每个时空单元上的局部能量/熵平衡，具有一致的单值数值能量/熵通量与非负局部能量/熵耗散，且去掉稳定项时能量精确守恒；III) 即使存在间断并激活激波捕捉项时，速度场旋度对合仍被弱保持。这些性质先经数学证明，再通过数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一类兼顾任意高阶精度与结构保持性质的时空DG格式，可同时保证局部守恒、熵稳定及旋度对合的弱保持，适用于非结构单纯形网格上的非线性声波传播问题。

Abstract: We present a new class of structure-preserving, arbitrarily high-order accurate space-time schemes using unstructured simplicial meshes in space for a class of nonlinear acoustic wave propagation systems, in which the nonlinearity is encoded in the constitutive relations between the state and dual variables, while the differential operators retain the standard first-order acoustic wave structure. At the fully discrete level, the schemes satisfy the following properties: I) local conservation of the state variables; II) a local energy/entropy balance on each space-time element, with a consistent, single-valued numerical energy/entropy flux and nonnegative local energy/entropy dissipation, and exact energy conservation when the stabilization terms are omitted; III) weak preservation of the curl involution of the velocity field, even in the presence of discontinuities and with the shock-capturing terms activated. The key ingredients of the new schemes are: i) a discontinuous Galerkin (DG) discretization in space, in which the dual variables are computed as the L2 projections onto the DG space of the energy derivatives with respect to the conserved variables; ii) numerical fluxes and a bubble-weighted, element-local viscous regularization expressed in terms of the dual variables; iii) a continuous-discontinuous Petrov-Galerkin method in time. Properties I)-III) are first proved mathematically and are then verified by suitable numerical tests.

</details>


### [349] [UnCut FEM with PHT-splines on unfitted T-meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09909)
*Falai Chen,Nuo Lei,Buyang Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于PHT样条的非拟合有限元方法，求解隐式边界（水平集）椭圆PDE，在背景网格上以L²残差最小化实现，无需切割单元几何构造与非平凡数值积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解边界由水平集隐式描述的椭圆PDE时，传统非拟合有限元需在边界单元构造曲线切割子区域并做复杂数值积分，实现困难且成本高。作者希望利用样条空间的高光滑性规避这些几何与积分难题。

Method: 基于分层T网格上的多项式样条（PHT样条），通过最小化PDE在物理域扩展邻域上的L²残差来构造格式；全部实现于背景网格，采用标准逐单元积分；并用基于残差的后验误差指示子驱动分层T网格的局部自适应细化。

Result: 格式产生对称正定线性系统，避免了边界单元中曲线切割子区域的几何构造及相关复杂数值积分；建立了数值格式的适定性，并推导出H¹范数下的最优先验误差估计；数值实验验证了理论结果，展示了方法的精度与效率。

Conclusion: 所提PHT样条非拟合有限元方法在保持最优收敛阶的同时显著简化了隐式边界PDE的实现，并可通过残差型后验指示子实现局部自适应，具有精度与效率优势。

Abstract: We propose a novel unfitted finite element method based on polynomial splines over hierarchical T-meshes (PHT-splines) for elliptic partial differential equations (PDEs) posed on domains whose boundaries are described implicitly by a level-set function. Exploiting the enhanced smoothness of the spline space, we formulate the method by minimizing the $L^2$ residual of the PDE over an extended neighborhood of the physical domain. This formulation allows the method to be implemented entirely on the background mesh using standard element-wise quadrature. It yields a symmetric positive-definite linear system and avoids the geometric construction of curved cut subregions in boundary elements, as well as the associated nontrivial numerical integration. We establish well-posedness of the numerical scheme and derive optimal \emph{a priori} error estimates in the $H^1$ norm. In addition, we employ a residual-based \emph{a posteriori} error indicator to guide local adaptive refinement of the hierarchical T-meshes. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical results and demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [350] [Neural Sampling with Reweighted Normalizing Flows via the Wasserstein--Fisher--Rao JKO Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10278)
*Chenguang Duan,Johannes Hertrich,Gabriele Steidl*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文提出基于WFR几何下JKO格式的神经采样算法，证明精确迭代指数收敛（无需log-concavity或LSI等假设），并用重加权归一化流参数化传输与反应分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从未归一化Boltzmann密度中采样是统计与机器学习中的核心问题，现有神经采样方法多依赖log-concavity或对数Sobolev不等式等结构假设，限制了在多模态等复杂目标上的理论保证与实用性。

Method: 采用Wasserstein--Fisher--Rao几何下的Jordan--Kinderlehrer--Otto（WFR JKO）格式，将演化分解为传输与反应两部分；理论侧证明任意固定步长下精确WFR JKO迭代对目标的指数收敛性；实现侧用重加权归一化流（reweighted normalizing flows）分别参数化传输与反应分量，构建神经网络实现。

Result: 理论上证明了在无log-concavity、无对数Sobolev不等式等结构假设下，任意固定步长的精确WFR JKO迭代随迭代次数趋于无穷时指数收敛到目标分布；数值实验在具有挑战性的多模态目标上展示了所提方法的良好性能。
